Conclusion: 晶面选择被确立为与化学取代同等重要的设计参数。作者鉴定了可用于多种（光）电化学半反应的特定组分，并指出MChX固溶体与常见光伏接触材料的能带对齐关系仅限于空穴选择性接触，为后续器件设计提供了明确指导。

Abstract: Pnictogen chalcohalide (MChX; M=Bi,Sb; Ch=S,Se; X=I,Br) solid solutions combine earth-abundant constituents, tunable band gaps ($1.2$-$2.1$ eV), and strong optical absorption, making them attractive for solar energy conversion. Yet their vast compositional space has so far prevented a systematic assessment of how band-edge positions vary with stoichiometry and surface termination. Here, we combine first-principles density functional theory with machine learning to predict the valence and conduction band-edge positions of $\mathrm{Bi}_x\mathrm{Sb}_{1-x}\mathrm{S}_y\mathrm{Se}_{1-y}\mathrm{I}_z\mathrm{Br}_{1-z}$ solid solutions across their full compositional range on the two most stable surfaces, (010) and (011). We find that the valence-band maximum and conduction-band minimum can be tuned by more than $1$ eV through composition alone, and shift by up to $0.6$ eV between the two surface terminations for a same composition despite their nearly degenerate formation energies, establishing facet selection as a design parameter on par with chemical substitution. Guided by these results, we identify specific compositions capable of driving hydrogen, ammonia, methane, hydrogen peroxide, and oxygen (photo)electrochemical half-reactions, and show that several electron- and hole-transport contact materials commonly used in photovoltaic devices align with MChX solid solutions only as hole-selective contacts.

Abstract: Semi-metallic noble-metal dichalcogenides are promising materials for infrared optoelectronics, yet the origin and tunability of their nonlinear optical responses remain poorly understood. Inspired by the identified chemistries, we further realize 11 previously unexplored rare-earth-based M2CT2 MXenes (M= Pr, Nd, Sm, Gd, Tb, Ho, and Tm). TL;DR: 本研究利用基于原子团簇展开（atomic cluster expansion, ACE）的Fe-H机器学习势（MLIP），通过在300 K下使用质心约束路径积分分子动力学的量子平均力重新标记训练构型，构建了核量子修正的PACE势（NQC-PACE），无需额外的密度泛函理论计算即可描述α-Fe中氢的俘获、扩散及晶界偏聚等核量子效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢脆（hydrogen embrittlement）的理解依赖于缺陷处氢扩散与俘获的原子级描述，而氢作为金属中最轻的溶质，即使在室温下也表现出显著的核量子效应（nuclear quantum effects）。然而，对这些效应的显式处理计算成本极高，限制了复杂环境中的大规模模拟，因此需要一种高效且准确的方法来在大尺度模拟中纳入核量子效应。

Method: 本文基于原子团簇展开的高性能实现（PACE）构建了覆盖多种Fe-H环境的机器学习原子间势（MLIP），并在300 K下通过质心约束路径积分分子动力学（centroid-constrained path-integral molecular dynamics）获取量子平均力，用其重新标记支持该势可迁移性的训练构型，从而在不增加密度泛函理论（DFT）计算的前提下构建核量子修正的PACE势（NQC-PACE）。

Result: NQC-PACE在父级PACE的框架下成功描述了核量子效应对α-Fe中氢在空位、位错、表面及一般晶界处俘获行为、H-H相互作用以及扩散过程的影响。巨正则蒙特卡洛/分子动力学（Grand-canonical Monte Carlo/Molecular dynamics）模拟表明，核量子效应显著增强了氢在一般晶界处的偏聚行为，其俘获趋势与实验更加吻合，该增强源于开放且各向异性软局域环境的选择性量子稳定化。

Conclusion: 该框架利用有限温度量子平均力重新标记MLIP所覆盖的构型空间，为轻元素量子效应显著影响的复杂材料体系提供了大规模分析能力，展示了一种无需额外DFT计算即可将核量子效应纳入大尺度模拟的有效途径。

Abstract: Atomistic descriptions of hydrogen diffusion and trapping at defects are essential for understanding hydrogen embrittlement. As the lightest solute in metals, hydrogen exhibits nuclear quantum effects that alter these processes even at room temperature. Explicit treatment of such effects is computationally demanding, limiting large-scale simulations of complex environments. Here, we use an Fe-H machine-learning interatomic potential (MLIP) based on the performant implementation of the atomic cluster expansion (PACE), covering diverse Fe-H environments, and relabel the training configurations underpinning its transferability with quantum mean forces from centroid-constrained path-integral molecular dynamics at 300 K. This yields a nuclear-quantum-corrected PACE (NQC-PACE) without additional density functional theory calculations. At parent PACE, NQC-PACE describes nuclear quantum effects on hydrogen trapping at vacancies, dislocations, surfaces and general grain boundaries, H-H interactions, and diffusion in alpha-Fe. Grand-canonical Monte Carlo/molecular dynamics simulations show nuclear quantum effects markedly enhance hydrogen segregation at general grain boundaries and trapping behaviour in closer agreement with experimental trends. This enhancement arises from selective quantum stabilisation of open, anisotropically soft local environments. Our framework uses finite-temperature quantum mean forces to relabel the configurational space covered by an MLIP, enabling large-scale analysis of complex materials where light-element quantum effects matter.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗场X射线显微镜中的弱束成像可以分辨体块晶体中的单个位错，但传统上从对比度中确定伯格斯矢量需要对手动前向模拟进行比对，过程繁琐且依赖人工。因此需要一种自动化的方法来简化并加速位错识别。

Method: 在几何光学模拟的面心立方铝孤立位错图像上训练物理信息卷积神经网络，并将晶体学约束融入学习过程，网络从弱束积分摇摆曲线图像中识别伯格斯矢量。

Result: 在合成测试数据上模型准确率约为93%；在实验交叉滑移案例中，约束模型将72.7%的逐层预测归因于参考伯格斯矢量。

Conclusion: 模拟训练的物理信息卷积神经网络代表了向DFXM中自动化位错识别迈出的一步。

Abstract: Weak-beam imaging in dark-field X-ray microscopy (DFXM) can resolve individual dislocations in bulk crystals, but assigning Burgers vectors from the resulting contrast typically requires manual comparison with forward simulations. Here, we train a physics-informed convolutional neural network (CNN) on geometrical optics simulations of isolated dislocations in face-centred cubic (FCC) aluminium, incorporating crystallographic constraints into the learning pro- cess. The model identifies Burgers vectors from weak-beam integrated rocking-curve images. On synthetic test data, the model achieves an accuracy of approximately 93%. In an experimental cross-slip case, the constrained model as- signs 72.7% of the layer-wise predictions to the reference Burgers vector. These results show that simulation-trained, physics-informed CNNs represent a step toward automated dislocation identification in DFXM.

</details>

### [69] [Probability-Preserving Transformer for the Time-Dependent Schrödinger Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15112)
*Mushtaq Ali,Muzamil Tariq,Niaz Ali Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将含时薛定谔方程（TDSE）概率守恒作为硬约束（hard constraint）的Transformer架构，该架构在时间演化中天然保证幺正性（unitarity），无需反复重训练，并在物理精确性与计算效率上均优于传统软约束（soft constraint）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法求解含时薛定谔方程（time-dependent Schrödinger equation, TDSE）计算代价高昂，Transformer模型虽可作为替代方案，但标准实现依赖于软约束，无法严格保证概率守恒（probability conservation）。

Method: 引入一种Transformer架构，将TDSE概率守恒设计为硬约束，使时间演化过程中的幺正性在结构层面得到内在保证，从而无需重复重训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，硬约束方法不仅在物理上精确（physically exact），而且在计算性能上也优于传统软约束方法。

Conclusion: 硬约束Transformer架构在求解TDSE时兼具物理精确性与计算高效性，为量子动力学模拟提供了优于软约束方法的可行方案。

Abstract: Solving the time-dependent Schrödinger equation (TDSE) via traditional numerical methods is computationally intensive. Transformer models offer a compelling alternative, but standard implementations rely on soft constraints that cannot rigorously guarantee probability conservation. Here, we introduce a Transformer architecture that enforces TDSE probability conservation as a hard constraint. The design intrinsically ensures unitarity across temporal evolution without requiring repeated retraining. Our empirical findings show that this hard-constraint approach is not only physically exact but also computationally superior to conventional soft-constraint methods.

</details>


### [70] [BDIP-Net: Dual-Interaction Graph Learning for Property Prediction of Bilayer Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14640)
*An Vuong,Chen Zhao,Jin Hu,Shui-Qing Yu,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个机器学习框架，用于高效构建和预测堆叠双层材料（stacked bilayer materials）的结构与性质。框架包含基于MatterSim-D3的结构优化流程和名为BDIP-Net的图神经网络（graph neural network），后者通过显式建模层内与层间相互作用实现更准确的物性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 堆叠双层材料具有丰富且依赖堆叠方式的性质，但传统计算发现依赖昂贵的DFT优化，而现有机器学习模型在物性预测时往往不能显式区分不同相互作用类型（如强层内键合与弱层间范德华相互作用）。

Method: 提出一个机器学习框架：其一，采用基于MatterSim-D3的结构优化流程，以显著降低的计算成本从单层构件和堆叠构型生成具有DFT质量的双层结构；其二，引入BDIP-Net（Bilayer Dual-Interaction Potential Network），一种通过相互作用特异性势表示和自适应消息融合显式建模层内与层间相互作用的图神经网络。

Result: 在BiDB、HetDB和SAMBA数据集上（涵盖同质双层、异质双层及扭转双层体系）评估后，MatterSim-D3流程能紧密复现DFT-PBE-D3优化的结构；BDIP-Net在双层物性预测上 consistently 优于现有图神经网络和基于势的方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的机器学习框架能够以较低的计算成本高效生成DFT质量的双层结构，并通过显式建模层内与层间相互作用提升双层材料物性预测的准确性，为堆叠双层材料的计算发现提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Stacked bilayer materials exhibit rich stacking-dependent properties driven by the interplay between strong intra-layer bonding and weak inter-layer van der Waals interactions. The computational discovery of such materials is challenging because accurate structure generation typically relies on expensive DFT-based optimization, while existing machine-learning models often fail to explicitly distinguish different interaction types during property prediction. To address these challenges, we propose a machine-learning framework for efficient construction and property prediction of stacked bilayer materials. The framework employs a MatterSim-D3-based structural optimization workflow to generate DFT-quality bilayer structures from monolayer building blocks and stacking configurations at substantially reduced computational cost. For property prediction, we introduce BDIP-Net (Bilayer Dual-Interaction Potential Network), a graph neural network that explicitly models intra-layer and inter-layer interactions through interaction-specific potential representations and adaptive message fusion. We evaluate the proposed framework on BiDB, HetDB, and SAMBA, encompassing homobilayers, heterobilayers, and twisted bilayer systems. Results show that the MatterSim-D3-based workflow closely reproduces DFT-PBE-D3 optimized structures, while BDIP-Net consistently outperforms existing graph neural network and potential-based approaches for bilayer property prediction.

</details>


### [71] [Unraveling the Size Determination Mechanism of Nanocrystal Synthesis via Interpretable Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14734)
*Kai Gu,Haizheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出完全白盒（white-box）神经网络NanoEQL来解读纳米晶体合成的尺寸决定机制，通过八个算子拟合合成过程中的数学方程，并利用温度门控注意力池化策略评估不同前驱体的权重，最终将最终纳米晶尺寸描述为由三个标量构成的线性方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型虽能预测纳米晶体尺寸与形状，但其黑盒（black-box）特性阻碍了对底层合成机制（synthesis mechanism）的深入理解，研究者需要一种既能预测又具有可解释性的方法。

Method: 在EQL架构基础上引入八个算子替代标准激活函数以拟合纳米晶体合成中的数学方程，其中三个平滑算子解决了奇异算子在零点处的梯度爆炸问题；同时开发了温度门控注意力池化（temperature-gated attention pooling）策略，将浓度驱动和反应活性驱动的化学合成机制编码到温度门中。

Result: NanoEQL模型表明最终纳米晶尺寸可由包含三个标量的线性方程描述，分别代表纳米结晶能力（-Zp）、生长能力（Zrea）和外部输入电位（-Zops）。

Conclusion: 这些可解释标量不仅推动了纳米晶体合成的理性设计，也为通过白盒机器学习（white-box machine learning）解读化学反应机制建立了通用范式。

Abstract: Deep learning models of nanocrystal synthesis enable the prediction of size and shape by encoding precursors and reaction conditions. However, their black-box nature hinders gaining deep insights into the underlying synthetic mechanisms. Here, we develop the Nanocrystal Equation Learner (NanoEQL), a fully white-box neural network to unravel the size determination mechanisms of nanocrystal synthesis. Building on the EQL architecture, eight operators are introduced to replace standard activation functions to fit the mathematical equations in nanocrystal synthesis. Among these operators, three smoothed operators address the gradient explosion of singular operators at zero. To evaluate the weights of different precursors, we develop a temperature-gated attention pooling strategy that encodes concentration-driven and reactivity-driven chemical synthesis mechanisms into the temperature gate. The NanoEQL model illustrates that the final nanocrystal size can be described by a linear equation composed of three scalars representing nanocrystallization capability (-Zp), growth capability (Zrea), and external input potential (-Zops). These interpretable scalars not only advance the rational design of nanocrystal synthesis but also establish a generalizable paradigm for deciphering chemical reaction mechanisms through white-box machine learning.

</details>


### [72] [Sparse Prototype Code Underlies Classification and Prediction Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15632)
*Yehonatan Avidan,Daniel D. Lee,Haim Sompolinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现现代AI模型（视觉、音频、语言处理）在分类任务中共享一种普遍的表示几何（representational geometry）：类内变异性并非随机，其分类器相关成分与自身类别质心（centroid）及竞争类别质心存在强结构化关联。基于此，作者推导出解析的平均场理论（mean-field theory），仅需真实类与竞争类质心坐标的少数变量及类别半径的全局重整化，即可跨架构和模态精确预测分类准确率，并与稀疏特征提取方法（如稀疏自编码器）建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表示（neural representations）已成为研究现代AI模型内部机制的核心工具，但其高维结构复杂且难以解释，缺乏对不同模型与模态间共享规律的统一理论框架。

Method: 作者首先通过实证分析揭示分类任务中表示空间的普遍几何规律——类内变异性与类别质心之间存在结构化相关；随后构建解析的平均场理论，以真实类与竞争类质心坐标的变异性为核心变量，并结合类别半径的非高斯统计全局重整化（global renormalization）来校正理论预测。

Result: 该理论能够跨不同架构和模态准确预测分类准确率；相关几何量随模型规模增大而系统性提升，与准确率提升同步；理论具有稀疏性（sparsity），仅需真实类及最强竞争的少数质心坐标即可精确预测，与稀疏自编码器（sparse autoencoder）的稀疏特征提取方法相一致。

Conclusion: 深度网络中的分类由嵌入在完整高维表示空间中的稀疏、质心对齐（centroid-aligned）结构所支配，这为神经表示提供了一种简约且可预测的统一理论。

Abstract: Neural representations have become a central tool for studying the internal mechanisms of modern AI models, yet their complex high-dimensional structure makes them difficult to interpret. We show that classification tasks give rise to a universal representational geometry, shared across state-of-the-art models in vision, audio, and language processing. The key structure is that within-class variability is not random in representation space. Instead, its classifier-relevant component has strong and structured correlations with the class's own centroid and with the centroids of its competing classes. Building on this observation, we derive an analytical mean-field theory governed mainly by the variability along true-class and rival-class centroid coordinates, together with a global renormalization of the class radius that compensates for the non-Gaussian statistics of real representations. The theory accurately predicts classification accuracy across architectures and modalities. The relevant geometric quantities improve systematically with model scale, mirroring the observed gains in accuracy. A striking feature of the theory is its sparsity: accurate prediction requires only a small set of centroid coordinates associated with the true class and its strongest rivals - connecting our framework to sparse-feature extraction approaches such as sparse autoencoders. Together, these results provide a parsimonious predictive theory of neural representations and suggest that classification in deep networks is governed by a sparse, centroid-aligned structure embedded within the full high-dimensional representation space.

</details>

### [73] [Tracking Chirality during Molecular Motor Photoisomerization via Simulated Time-Resolved Circular Dichroism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15330)
*Leonardo Biancorosso,Ali Hassanali,Mauro Stener,Emanuele Coccia,Marta Monti,Gonzalo Díaz Mirón*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在非绝热分子动力学系综中模拟时间分辨电子圆二色谱（TRCD）的框架，并将其应用于第二代分子马达的光异构化过程。研究表明，TRCD能够区分两种光产物的立体化学分支路径，为实时探测分子手性的形成提供了可直接测量的光谱信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快光谱技术虽广泛用于研究光诱导过程，但对手性（chirality）信息几乎不敏感。瞬时吸收光谱只能捕捉整体激发态动力学，无法区分不同的光产物路径，亟需一种能够解析立体化学分支的手性敏感探测手段。

Method: 建立模拟时间分辨电子圆二色谱（time-resolved electronic circular dichroism, TRCD）的理论框架，并将其嵌入非绝热分子动力学（nonadiabatic molecular dynamics）的系综采样中。该方法随后被应用于第二代分子马达（molecular motor）的光异构化过程。

Result: TRCD响应能够分辨立体化学分支：回归稳定P异构体（右手螺旋）的轨迹在可见区保持独特的手性光谱带，而形成M异构体（左手螺旋）的轨迹在穿过圆锥交叉（conical intersection）时转变为手性暗态。这种不对称性构成了立体化学分支的可直接测量特征。

Conclusion: TRCD提供了一种实时探测分子手性形成的光谱探针，为未来的时间分辨电子圆二色谱实验提供了具体预测，弥补了传统超快吸收光谱无法区分立体化学分支的不足。

Abstract: Ultrafast spectroscopic techniques are widely used to investigate photoinduced processes, yet they remain largely blind to molecular chirality. Here we introduce a framework for simulating time-resolved electronic circular dichroism (TRCD) along an ensemble of nonadiabatic molecular dynamics, and apply it to the photoisomerization of a second-generation molecular motor[1]. While transient absorption captures the overall excited-state dynamics, it cannot distinguish the two photoproduct pathways. The TRCD response, by contrast, resolves the stereochemical branching: trajectories returning to the stable P isomer (right-handed helix) retain a distinct chiroptical band in the visible region, whereas those forming the M isomer (leftl-handed helix) become chiroptically dark as they twist through the conical intersection. This asymmetry constitutes a directly measurable signature of the stereochemical branching, offering a real-time probe of the formation of molecular chirality and concrete predictions for future TRCD experiments.

</details>

### [74] [Riesz--Laurent representation of black-hole scattering and sourced response at exceptional points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14752)
*Okuto Morikawa,Shoya Ogawa,Takuya Hirose*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用复标度Regge–Wheeler–Zerilli预解式（complex-scaled resolvent）在黑洞例外点（exceptional point, EP）处将两个准正则模（quasinormal modes）处理为一个孤立的二阶Riesz簇，通过零阶和一阶轮廓矩（contour moments）构造精确的成对预解式，无需标记单独模或构造归一化Jordan链；并证明透射系数与灰体因子在EP处保持实解析性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑洞例外点处，两个准正则模合并，其各自的留数变得病态，导致传统的近简并模态拟合方法失效。作者旨在避免人为假设近简并拟合，而是直接从解析框架构造响应，以获得物理上确定、与模标记无关的量。

Method: 从复标度的Regge–Wheeler–Zerilli预解式出发，将两个合并模视为一个孤立的秩二Riesz簇，利用其零阶和一阶轮廓矩构造两边及EP处的精确成对预解式；矩决定二阶EP处Laurent算子中的简并极与二重极算子；进一步比较源-观察者矩阵元与Jost双零点展开的系数以验证其一致性。

Result: 在EP处，尽管模分解是奇异的，固定实频率的透射和灰体因子仍是EP处实解析的，条件是该簇保持孤立、互补预解式正则且无极点到达物理轴；Laurent算子的源-观察者矩阵元定义有限且归一化无关的振幅，并确定因果振铃（ringdown）中的常数项和线性时间项；这些系数与Jost双零点展开的系数相等。

Conclusion: 两个簇矩提供了与模标签无关的数据，从中可同时推导散射响应和驱动响应；这些系数是所指定物理响应的算子定义的系数，而非拟合参数。

Abstract: At a black-hole exceptional point (EP), two quasinormal modes coalesce and their separate residues become ill-conditioned. Rather than postulating a near-degenerate modal fit, we derive the response constructively from the complex-scaled Regge--Wheeler--Zerilli resolvent, treating the modes as one isolated rank-two Riesz cluster. Its zeroth and first contour moments determine an exact pair resolvent on both sides of, and at, the EP, without labeling the individual modes or constructing a normalized Jordan chain. At a second-order EP, these moments determine the simple- and double-pole Laurent operators. Although the modal decomposition is singular, fixed-real-frequency transmission and the greybody factor remain real-analytic through the EP, provided that the cluster remains isolated, the complementary resolvent is regular, and no pole reaches the physical axis. Source--observer matrix elements of the Laurent operators define finite, normalization-independent amplitudes and fix both the constant and linear-in-time terms in the causal ringdown. Their equality with the coefficients from the Jost double-zero expansion shows that they are operator-defined coefficients of the specified physical response, rather than fitting parameters. Thus two cluster moments provide mode-label-free data from which both scattering and driven responses follow.

</details>


### [75] [New metric useful for studies of gravitational radiation from accelerating black holes of type D with any cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14919)

Abstract: Magnetic reconnection has been widely recognized as an important mechanism for extracting energy from rapidly rotating black holes. We find that magnetic reconnection can also efficiently extract energy from slowly rotating ModMax black holes. The results indicate that the charge parameter $Q$ and screening factor $γ$ exhibit a competing effect on the fundamental characteristic quantities of the ModMax black hole. Furthermore, for the extremal ModMax black hole, a larger $Q$ and a smaller $γ$ lower the minimum allowed spin parameter $a$. Subsequently, we analyze the parameter space $(r,a)$ for energy extraction in circular and plunging orbits. A larger $Q$ and a smaller $γ$ reduce the region for energy extraction and decrease the minimum spin parameter $a$ required for energy extraction. For circular orbits, the minimum allowed spin parameter is $a\simeq 0.50071$, while for plunging orbits, this threshold further decreases to $a\simeq0.22407$. This proves that energy extraction from lower spin ModMax black holes is theoretically feasible. Finally, we compare the energy extraction region, power, and efficiency between circular and plunging orbits. It is found that these quantities in plunging orbits are always higher than those in circular orbits, indicating that energy extraction from plunging orbits via magnetic reconnection may be more efficient.

Abstract: In this paper, we derive static magnetic brane solutions in quasi-topological gravity, considering the presence of power-law Maxwell nonlinear electrodynamics. However, there exists a conic singularity with a deficit angle, which depends solely on the parameters $q$, $n$, and $s$ (where $s$ is the nonlinear parameter). In addition, in order to obtain finite solutions at infinity, the parameter $s$ of the power-law Maxwell theory is constrained to the range $1/2 < s \leq 2$. It is also observed that, for $ρ$ approaching $r_+$, the solutions $f(ρ)$ are dependent on the values of parameters $q$ and $n$, and for larger values of $ρ$, the solutions depend on the coefficients of Lovelock and quasi-topological gravities, namely $λ$, $μ$, and $c$. Result: braiding产生热流和压强各向异性：类时梯度有径向和切向热流，类空梯度有径向热流及混合各向异性。开域类空梯度的应力张量呈零尘埃形式。Hawking–Ellis分类中，类时区域为Type I（正判别式）、Type II（判别式零超曲面）、Type IV（负判别式）；类空区域在径向热流平方等于混合各向异性平方时出现额外Type II和Type III退化。能量条件排除了Type III和IV，且对类空梯度要求所有径向热流和各向异性为零。

Conclusion: 可容许的类空proper braiding必须是对角形式，而类时区域的能量条件仅约束总热流的幅值。该研究为braiding修改引力在物理解释和模型筛选方面提供了清晰的分类框架。

Abstract: We investigate the fluid interpretation, Hawking--Ellis classification, and energy conditions of the proper kinetic gravity braiding contribution to the stress tensor of minimally coupled scalar fields for timelike, spacelike, and open-region null scalar gradients. Using a $2+1+1$ decomposition adapted to the gradient, we derive the associated effective fluid variables. Unlike k-essence, braiding generates heat fluxes and pressure anisotropies: radial and tangential heat fluxes for timelike gradients, and a radial heat flux plus mixed radial--tangential anisotropies for spacelike gradients. These quantities depend only on the normal fundamental scalars and the two-dimensional accelerations, despite the larger set of variables appearing at intermediate stages. For open-region null gradients, the proper-braiding stress tensor has null-dust form. We obtain the complete Hawking--Ellis classification: the timelike sector is Type I for positive discriminant, Type II on the nontrivial discriminant hypersurface, and Type IV for negative discriminant; the spacelike sector has the same generic branches and additional Type II and Type III degeneracies when the squared radial heat flux equals the squared mixed anisotropy. The open-region null sector is Type II for nonzero null-dust density and vanishes in the zero-density case. Finally, the standard energy conditions further restrict the Type I and generic Type II sectors. The null energy condition excludes Types III and IV and, for spacelike gradients, every nonzero radial heat flux or mixed anisotropy. Thus admissible spacelike proper braiding is diagonal, whereas in the timelike sector the energy conditions bound the total heat flux.

</details>

Abstract: We characterize the gravitational radiation present in the general Plebański-Demiański type~D solution with cosmological constant $Λ$ of any sign. In addition, we recast a recent parametrization of this family of metrics into a new form by a simple homothety, correctly identifying $Λ$, and study the existence and regularity of the possible axes of symmetry, searching for a relation between deficit angles and radiation.

Method: 作者在任意渐近平直的静态球对称黑洞背景上精确求解静态无质量狄拉克方程（massless Dirac equation），并在视界处施加正则性条件（regularity），由此推导出静态费米子TLN的闭合公式，进而从该公式推出TLN为零的充要条件。

Abstract: The static tidal Love numbers (TLNs) of $4$-dimensional black holes vanish for bosonic perturbations but are generically nonzero for fermions, with rare exceptions. This follows from a closed formula for the static fermionic TLN of any asymptotically flat black hole, obtained by solving the static massless Dirac equation exactly on an arbitrary such spacetime and imposing regularity at the horizon. As applications, we analyze the Simpson--Visser regular black hole and the loop-quantum-gravity remnant black holes, whose extremal configurations lead to vanishing fermionic yet nonvanishing bosonic TLNs.

Abstract: Black holes embedded in a dark matter background represent an important candidate model that extends the standard vacuum case, characterizing the strong gravitational environments at galactic centers and the gravitational influence of dark matter. This study focuses on dark matter halo-modified Kerr black holes and systematically investigates the observational characteristics of their surrounding thin accretion disks. The dark matter halo is modeled by a general double power-law density profile, whose integrated mass $M_D(r)$ modifies the radial mass function of Kerr metric to $m(r) = M + M_D(r)$. Based on this spacetime, we analyze the observational properties of their thin accretion disk, including the radiative flux, temperature, differential luminosity, and spectral luminosity as a function of radius, with respect to the black hole spin and dark matter halo parameters. Furthermore, by combining ray-tracing methods, we simulate the bolometric image of the thin accretion disk under different black hole spins, dark matter halo parameters, and viewing inclinations. Abstract: In February 2016 the LIGO and Virgo collaborations reported the discovery of gravitational waves in merging black holes, with the conclusion that their observational data did not show any violation of general relativity. The joint LIGO and Virgo team presented an upper limit on the graviton mass, using tests of modified gravitational dynamics. Yukawa-type gravitational potential corrections are often used as generic finite-range parametrisation in modified gravity contexts, including massive-gravity inspired models. Yukawa corrections are derived by semi-empirical approach, in the framework of f(R) gravity, a type of modified gravity which generalises Einsteins general relativity. Contrary to this approach, we use a classical Newtonian gravitational potential corrected by nonlinear effects in order to obtain a characteristic wavelength of the nonlinear density wave. This wavelength is then phenomenologically identified with an effective graviton Compton wavelength, leading to a model-dependent estimate of an effective graviton mass within the galaxy scale. This phenomenon is therefore a gravitational analogue of the Aharonov-Bohm effect, in which a field confined to an inaccessible region influences the quantum phase of particles propagating in a field-free region.

Abstract: We investigate the temperature encoding process of two-level probes in de Sitter spacetime within the framework of open quantum systems. For a static probe separated by a finite distance from a freely falling observer, both the intrinsic Gibbons--Hawking temperature and the position-dependent Unruh temperature are encoded in the probe state. We find that the encoding rate of the effective temperature is equal to the sum of the encoding rates of the intrinsic temperature and the Unruh temperature associated with the probe's inherent acceleration. Interestingly, when the intrinsic temperature is sufficiently small, the encoding rate of the intrinsic temperature initially increases with the Unruh temperature, reaches a maximum, and then decreases to zero. Thus, a nonzero inherent acceleration does not necessarily suppress the encoding of the intrinsic temperature. Instead, there exists an optimal inherent acceleration at which its encoding rate is maximized. Furthermore, we investigate the encoding rate of the Unruh temperature and the minimum total probe time required to estimate it with sufficient precision as functions of the separation distance. We find that the required total probe time and the corresponding number of probes remain experimentally feasible even when the probes are located relatively far from the cosmological horizon, with a radial position as large as $0.1$ times the horizon radius.

Abstract: The effect of deflection of a light signal that propagates through the gravitational field of a solar system body at rest is considered in the case that the source and the observer are at a finite distance from the body. The observer is assumed to be located somewhere nearby the Earth, for instance at Lagrange point L2 of the Sun-Earth system, while the spatial position of the celestial light source is arbitrary. The gravitational fields in the exterior of the bodies are described by the full set of time-dependent mass-multipoles and spin-multipoles of these bodies. So the gravitating bodies can be of arbitrary shape, inner structure and rotational motion. The unit tangent vector of the light trajectory at the position of the observer is determined in the 1PN and 1.5PN approximation of the post-Newtonian (PN) scheme. A simplified expression for the unit tangent vector is obtained, where all terms are neglected that together contribute less than 10 nano-arcseconds in light deflection for all astrometric configurations between source, body and observer. It is shown that in the case of an axi-symmetric body the unit tangent vector of the light ray as well as the light deflection are given in terms of Chebyshev polynomials. This fact allows for determining the effect of light deflection in the gravitational fields of the bodies up to any order of the mass-multipoles and spin-multipoles. It is shown that the total light deflection, that is the angle of light deflection where source and observer are located at infinite spatial distance from the body, represents an upper limit of the effect of light deflection. These investigations are aiming at astrometric measurements on the sub-micro-arcsecond level of accuracy.

Abstract: The effective-one-body (EOB) framework, originally formulated within the post-Newtonian (PN) expansion, is central to modeling and interpreting gravitational-wave signals. Recent developments have incorporated the post-Minkowskian (PM) expansion into EOB theory. In this work, we focus on the bound state dynamics in the PM approximation up to the third order and construct the core ingredients of EOB: the effective metric. We examine two correspondence strategies, one based on the radial action variable and the other on the precession angle. We demonstrate that the radial action variable provides a consistent correspondence, which is further verified by the correspondence based on the precession angle. Building on these results, we adopt an isotropic gauge with a Schwarzschild-like parametrization to fix the remaining freedom. Within this parametrization, the effective metric coefficients are determined at 3PM order. Our results provide a consistent effective metric for the bound state dynamics.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了闵可夫斯基时空中无质量标量波的共形散射（conformal scattering），在紧致化双零坐标中将其表述为包含空间无穷远邻域的Goursat特征初值问题，并提出一种数值方法，可计算自由传播、Pöschl–Teller势散射及半线性波方程三种物理场景，实现了全局二阶收敛，且能准确捕捉自散焦Kerr非线性效应。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形散射问题的数值求解具有挑战性，尤其是在处理空间无穷远和零无穷远边界时。作者希望建立一种简单高效的方法，使渐近辐射可以通过构造直接访问，从而研究标量波在闵可夫斯基时空中的全局演化与散射性质。

Method: 将物理波方程在紧致化双零坐标（compactified double-null coordinates）中表述为Goursat特征初值问题，纳入空间无穷远邻域。对多极数ℓ=0,1采用显式模板沿空间方向平均，对ℓ≥2则采用隐式模板沿时间方向平均以保证数值稳定性；对ℐ⁺上的辐射数据使用Richardson外推提升收敛阶。

Result: 全局二阶收敛在ℓ=0,1下获得；ℓ≥2时需隐式模板保证稳定。空间无穷远的奇异性将ℐ⁺辐射数据的收敛降至一阶，但Richardson外推将有效收敛阶提升至约1.5。PT散射情形中相移计算准确；半线性情形中成功捕捉到自散焦Kerr非线性的物理特征，包括自相位调制和频谱展宽。

Conclusion: 紧致化双零框架被证明简单且高效，为共形散射的全局演化提供了一种有前景的数值方法。

Abstract: We study the conformal scattering of massless scalar waves in Minkowski spacetime. The conformal scattering problem is formulated as a Goursat (characteristic initial-value) problem of the physical wave equation in compactified double-null coordinates, including the neighborhood of spatial infinity $i^0$. As null infinities $\mathcal{I}^\pm$ lie on the domain boundary by construction, asymptotic radiation is directly accessible. We consider three physical scenarios: free wave propagation, scattering off a Pöschl--Teller (PT) potential, and the semi-linear $|φ|^{n-1}φ$ wave equation. For multipole numbers $\ell =0,1$, an explicit stencil, averaging along the spatial direction, yields globally second-order convergent results. For $\ell \ge 2$, an implicit stencil averaging along the temporal direction is required for numerical stability. Although the singular $i^0$ reduces the convergence of the radiation data on $\mathcal{I}^+$ to first order, Richardson extrapolation enhances the effective convergence rate to approximately $1.5$. For PT scattering, our method accurately computes scattering quantities, notably the phase shifts induced by the potential. In the semi-linear case, our method captures the physical signatures of a self-defocusing Kerr nonlinearity, including self-phase modulation and spectral broadening. The compactified double-null framework proves to be simple and efficient, suggesting a promising approach to the global evolution of conformal scattering.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 研究者首先分析若干已知虫洞解（如单向虫洞），考察其从静态推广为时间依赖形式时是否引发奇异性；随后构造无奇异性的动力学虫洞解，并基于无鬼场的双标量场模型重建对应的拉格朗日量。研究还分析了能量条件（energy conditions）在动力学虫洞中的满足情况，并通过模拟光子环（photon ring）结构的演化来比较动力学与静态解的观测特征。

Result: 研究表明，大多数已知的虫洞解在时间依赖化后确实会产生奇异性，但存在可构造的无奇异性动力学虫洞解。重建得到的双标量场拉格朗日量不包含鬼场，且能量条件对于奇异动力学虫洞可能仍然成立。光学模拟显示动力学物体周围的光子环结构随时间演化，呈现若干与静态解不同的特征。

Conclusion: 通过观测光学外观——尤其是光子环结构的演化，有望实现对动力学超紧凑天体时间演化的测量，这为区分动力学虫洞与静态解提供了可行的观测途径。

Abstract: Some dynamical spacetime solutions that describe ultra-compact objects are analysed. We show that by turning a static spacetime that describes a one-way wormhole into a dynamical time-dependent one, a singularity might arise in general. Several well-known wormhole solutions are studied, which are shown to become singular when promoted to a time-dependent wormhole. However, we show that one can construct a dynamical wormhole free of singularities. The corresponding Lagrangian that holds such dynamical spacetimes is also reconstructed, based on a two-scalar-field model free of ghosts. Energy conditions are also analysed, which might remain satisfied for a dynamical -singular- wormhole. Finally, the evolution of the optical appearance of such dynamical objects is simulated and compared to static solutions. Some interesting features emerge that point to a way to measure the time evolution of dynamical objects by looking at the photon ring structure around the object.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用Hamilton-Jacobi隧穿形式，将quintessence场视为微扰贡献，推导事件视界、隧穿概率及Hawking温度；随后对Bekenstein-Hawking熵引入对数与逆熵量子修正，获得二阶修正热力学量。

Conclusion: quintessence与高阶量子修正共同增强热力学稳定性，并显著有利于稳定黑洞残骸的形成。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate Hawking radiation and second order quantum corrected thermodynamics of a Vaidya-Bonnor black hole surrounded by quintessence. Within the Hamilton-Jacobi tunneling formalism, analytical expressions for the event horizons, tunneling probability, and Hawking temperature are derived by treating the quintessence field as a perturbative contribution. The classical thermodynamic quantities, including entropy, heat capacity, enthalpy, Helmholtz free energy, and Gibbs free energy, are then obtained and analyzed. Quantum thermal fluctuations are incorporated through logarithmic and inverse-entropy corrections to the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, leading to second-order corrected thermodynamic quantities. Their effects on thermal stability and phase transitions are examined in detail. Furthermore, analytical expressions for the quantum-corrected remnant radius and remnant mass are derived, showing that the combined effects of quintessence and quantum corrections can prevent complete black hole evaporation and lead to the formation of a stable remnant. Our results show that both quintessence and higher-order quantum corrections enhance thermodynamdymic stability and significantly favor the formation of stable black hole remnants.

</details>

Method: 采用解析克拉奇波形框架，在长期轨道演化中包含暗物质诱导的动力学摩擦（dynamical friction）和吸积（accretion）效应；使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样进行贝叶斯参数估计，并辅以费舍尔矩阵（Fisher matrix）分析。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) provide sensitive probes of the astrophysical environments surrounding massive black holes. We investigate the inference of dark-matter (DM) spikes with eccentric EMRI gravitational waves within the analytic-kludge waveform framework, including DM-induced dynamical friction and accretion in the secular orbital evolution. We perform Bayesian parameter estimation using Markov-chain Monte Carlo sampling, complemented by Fisher-matrix analyses. We find that steeper DM spikes produce stronger accumulated waveform modifications and substantially improve the measurement of the spike slope $α_{\rm DM}$. More importantly, the constraints exhibit a nonmonotonic dependence on orbital eccentricity. At moderate eccentricity, higher harmonics help break the degeneracy between $e_{\rm LSO}$ and $α_{\rm DM}$, whereas this correlation can persist or the parameter recovery can deteriorate for highly eccentric systems. These results demonstrate that the harmonic structure of eccentric EMRIs can provide valuable information for probing DM distributions around massive black holes with future LISA observations.

### [97] [Perturbative solutions for the bumblebee field in a Schwarzschild background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15988)

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定史瓦西（Schwarzschild）背景时空上，受洛伦兹破坏四次势（Lorentz-violating quartic potential）作用的矢量场的平衡构型及其线性扰动行为。通过要求背景场为实且有限，导出了参数的允许范围，并确定了单极子扰动增强的临界情形，同时给出了多极扰动在事件视界和空间无穷远处的渐近表达式。

Method: 在固定史瓦西背景上引入含洛伦兹破坏四次势的矢量场，分析其平衡构型与线性扰动（linear perturbations）。通过构造一个二次函数（quadratic function）来刻画平衡解和球对称扰动的定性行为，并结合背景场为实且有限的要求推导参数约束，识别单极（monopole）扰动增强的临界情形。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹破坏四次势对史瓦西背景上的矢量场平衡与扰动行为有显著影响，可通过参数约束和临界条件刻画其稳定性与增强效应，并为该理论框架下的黑洞周边物理提供定量预测。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the behavior of a vector field subject to a Lorentz-violating quartic potential on a fixed Schwarzschild background. We analyze the equilibrium configuration of the field and its linear perturbations. We show that the equilibrium solution and spherically symmetric perturbations are governed by a quadratic function whose properties determine their qualitative behavior. By requiring the background field to be real and finite, we derive bounds on the allowed parameters and identify critical cases in which the monopole perturbations become enhanced. We further derive asymptotic expressions for multipole perturbations near the event horizon and at spatial infinity.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克星作为可能的致密天体（compact object）候选体，其内部可能存在暗物质积累，而传统单流体模型忽略了这一成分。为更真实地刻画夸克星的物理性质，需在两流体框架下研究暗物质对恒星结构及其旋转和潮汐响应的具体影响。

Method: 采用两流体框架，将暗物质与普通物质视为仅通过引力相互作用的独立理想流体，推广广义Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程。普通物质分别使用色-味-锁定（color-flavor-locked）和MIT袋模型（MIT bag model）状态方程建模，暗物质则使用自相互作用玻色凝聚（self-interacting bosonic condensate）和费米子状态方程。固定暗物质比例为5%，变化普通物质的袋常数，并扩展至一阶旋转效应，计算了参考系拖拽方程（frame-dragging equation）和转动惯量，同时探索旋转与潮汐效应之间的普适关系（universal relations）。

Result: 发现5%的暗物质比例可导致恒星半径、引力质量和潮汐形变度相对传统单流体夸克星特征产生显著偏离。同时，旋转和潮汐力的响应即使在包含暗物质成分的情况下仍保持一致。理论结果与来自GW事件和NICER任务的当前观测数据相吻合。

Conclusion: 包含暗物质的两流体夸克星模型在恒星性质上与传统单流体模型存在可观测的差异，且该模型与当前多信使天体物理（multi-messenger astrophysics）观测数据一致，表明暗物质成分在致密星建模中不可忽视。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine the stellar properties of quark stars containing dark matter, focusing on both non-rotating and slowly rotating configurations. By employing a two-fluid framework, we formulate the generalized Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations, treating dark matter and ordinary matter as independent perfect fluids that interact solely through gravitational forces. We model ordinary matter using the color-flavor-locked and the MIT bag model equation of state, while for dark matter, we apply a self-interacting bosonic condensate along with fermionic equation of state. By considering a dark matter fraction of $f_\x=5\%$ and varying the bag constant for ordinary matter, we investigate how dark matter accumulation affects global stellar features such as maximum gravitational mass, radius, and dimensionless tidal deformability. Further, we extend our analysis to first-order rotational effects, calculating the frame-dragging equation and the influence of moments of inertia on the two-fluid system. We also explore universal relations, particularly the connection between rotation and tidal effects, to understand how the inclusion of dark matter affects the relationship between rotational and the tidal deformability. Our findings indicate that a dark matter fraction of $f_\x=5\%$ can lead to notable deviations from the traditional single-fluid quark star characteristics in stellar radius, gravitational mass and tidal deformability. Moreover, the response to rotational and tidal forces remains consistent even in the inclusion of the dark matter component. Finally, we compare our theoretical results with current observational data from GW events and the NICER mission, thereby demonstrating that our developed two-fluid models are consistent with these observations.

</details>


### [99] [Exceptional points of the Schwarzschild spectrum under local potential perturbation: The Princess and the Pea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16283)
*Mohamed Ould El Hadj,Sam R. Dolan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究史瓦西黑洞在加入局部狄拉克δ函数扰动的Regge-Wheeler势后，其引力扰动准正则模（quasinormal modes，QNMs）在频率谱中出现的奇异点（exceptional points，EPs）现象。发现存在无穷多离散EP族，它们随径向距离增大而趋近于未扰动的QNM泛音频率；通过渐近分析和数值方法，系统研究了EP附近的平方根劈裂、避免交叉、迟滞、几何相、绝热不变量等多种现象，并验证了有限宽度高斯扰动下EP结构的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞准正则模在频率谱中可能在奇异点处合并，此前对这一现象的研究尚不充分。作者旨在系统地研究史瓦西黑洞引力扰动中由局域势扰动诱导的奇异点及其物理现象学。

Method: 以幅值为ε、位于半径r₀处的狄拉克δ函数扰动Regge-Wheeler势；对大r₀进行渐近分析；采用奇异点附近的二次近似配合高精度数值方法；并将δ函数替换为有限宽度高斯扰动以验证结构稳定性。

Result: 对实数ε，发现无穷多族离散奇异点，每族在大r₀极限下趋近于不同的未扰动QNM泛音频率；奇异点在乌龟坐标（tortoise coordinate）中渐近等间距分布，其幅值ε可极小而随未扰动QNM复频率指数衰减。还发现相邻QNM泛音在固定r₀下迁移于复平面并在奇异点处合并，高阶泛音移动更远；高斯宽度变化可将孤立奇异点扩展为参数空间中的奇异线，且弱扰动下时间域响应仍保持微扰性。

Conclusion: 局域势扰动下黑洞QNM谱可被显著重构，出现丰富的奇异点现象，但对应的时域响应仍保持微扰性——这一对比通过"豌豆公主"故事的重新诠释被捕捉，暗示谱结构对扰动的敏感性未必意味着物理观测量的同等敏感。

Abstract: Black-hole quasinormal modes (QNMs) may coalesce at exceptional points (EPs) in the frequency spectrum. We study EPs and their phenomenology for gravitational perturbations of a Schwarzschild black hole whose Regge-Wheeler potential is perturbed by a localized delta-function of amplitude $ε$ at radius $r_0$. For real $ε$, we find infinite families of discrete EPs. Each family approaches a distinct unperturbed QNM overtone frequency as $r_0$ grows large. Under perturbation at fixed $r_0$, we find that neighboring QNM overtones migrate in the complex plane to meet at these EPs, with the higher overtone moving further. We derive large-$r_0$ asymptotics, finding that EPs have (asymptotically) equal spacing in the tortoise coordinate, and that EP amplitudes $ε$ can be exceedingly small, decaying exponentially according to a rule governed by the complex frequency of the unperturbed QNM. Using quadratic approximations around EPs and high-precision numerical methods, we investigate a range of phenomena in the model: square-root splittings, avoided crossings and hyperbolae, hysteresis and geometric phases generated by encircling an EP, adiabatic invariants, deformed Cassini ovals and cusp-like trajectories, as well as the lemniscate geometry of the excitation factors. We then replace the delta-function perturbation by a finite-width Gaussian, verify the persistence of the EP structure, and show that varying the width extends isolated EPs into exceptional lines in parameter space. Finally, we study the response to initial data and find that, although a weak perturbation can substantially reconfigure the QNM spectrum, the corresponding time-domain response remains perturbative. This contrast is captured by a re-interpretation of the tale of The Princess and the Pea.

</details>


### [100] [Charge-Induced Pole Cancellation and Horizon Transitions in Scale-Dependent Gravitational Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16341)
*Ghulam Muhammad,Syed Zaheer Abbas,Muhammad Sajjad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文构造了尺度依赖引力（scale-dependent gravity）中的带电Oppenheimer-Snyder型坍缩模型，通过Israel-Maxwell衔接条件将空间平坦的FLRW内部时空与带电尺度依赖外部时空在类时薄壳上匹配。研究表明，电荷会定性改变尺度依赖坍缩的奇点和视界结构，并在负极点参数区间内提供视界屏蔽（horizon shielding）的模型级证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在研究尺度依赖引力框架下带电物质坍缩的动力学行为，特别是电荷如何影响引力坍缩产生的奇点与视界结构。

Method: 通过将空间平坦的FLRW内部几何与带电尺度依赖外部几何在类时薄壳上匹配来构建带电Oppenheimer-Snyder型坍缩模型；电荷被限制在恒星表面以保持内部均匀各向同性；外部几何由Bianchi相容的有效源支持，壳层动力学遵循Israel-Maxwell衔接条件，并用正压表面状态方程闭合壳层系统。

Result: 对于负尺度依赖参数，外部时空包含有限半径边界。根据电荷量将解分为三个区域：当0≤q²<x_s时，曲率奇点处出现负极点，外部含一个外视界，单调坍缩在到达x_s前穿过该视界；当q²=x_s时，分子分母同时为零消除了曲率极点；当q²>x_s时，曲率奇点以正极点形式存在，并存在局部外向径向零分支，外部时空可能包含两个简单视界、一个退化视界或无视界。

Conclusion: 电荷定性改变了尺度依赖引力坍缩的奇点和视界结构，并在负极点参数区间内为视界屏蔽提供了模型层面的证据。

Abstract: We construct a charged Oppenheimer-Snyder-like collapse model in scale-dependent gravity by matching a spatially flat FLRW interior to a charged scale-dependent exterior across a timelike thin shell. The electric charge is confined to the stellar surface, preserving interior homogeneity and isotropy. The exterior geometry is supported by a phenomenological Bianchi-consistent effective source, while the shell dynamics follow from the Israel-Maxwell junction conditions. A barotropic surface equation of state closes the shell system, with a charged-dust shell as the minimal realization. For a negative scale-dependent parameter, $\tildeω<0$, the exterior contains a finite-radius boundary $x_s$ defined by $D(x_s)=0$. Charge separates the solutions into three regimes. For $0\le q^2<x_s$, the lapse develops a negative pole at a curvature singularity, the physical exterior contains one outer horizon, and a representative monotonic collapse crosses this horizon before reaching $x_s$; no future-directed locally outgoing radial null branch emerges from the singular boundary. At $q^2=x_s$, simultaneous zeros of the numerator and denominator cancel the curvature pole, although the prescribed running coupling remains singular. For $q^2>x_s$, the curvature singularity persists with a positive pole and locally outgoing radial null branches exist. Depending on the physical extremality condition $x_e>x_s$, the exterior may contain two simple horizons, one degenerate horizon, or no horizon. These results show that charge qualitatively changes the singular and horizon structure of scale-dependent collapse and provide model-level evidence for horizon shielding in the negative-pole regime.

</details>


### [101] [Limiting cases of second-order moments of relativistic stars and their universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16376)
*Koutarou Kyutoku*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了研讨会“从夸克到中子星：kHz引力波洞察”的内容，探讨了相对论星体（relativistic stars）的转动惯量（moment of inertia）、潮汐形变（tidal deformability）和自旋感应四极矩（spin-induced quadrupole moment）的极限情形。作者提出长度尺度层次结构可能是这些二阶矩之间普遍关系的关键，并考察了不可压缩相对论星体的极刚极限以及弱场极限下的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清相对论星体的二阶矩（转动惯量、潮汐形变、自旋感应四极矩）在极端条件下的行为，特别是探索这些量之间普遍关系（universality）背后的物理机制。传统文献中可能未明确呈现相对论潮汐形变计算与牛顿对应量之间的显式联系，本文希望填补这一空白。

Method: 首先提出长度尺度层次结构（hierarchy of the length scale）作为这些二阶矩之间普遍性的关键假设，进而将不可压缩相对论星体（即史瓦西内部解，Schwarzschild's interior solution）作为可能的极刚极限（stiff limit）候选进行考察；其次推导弱场极限（weak-field limit）下的极限形式，并演示相对论潮汐形变计算与传统牛顿对应量之间的关系。

Result: 给出了极刚极限（不可压缩星体情形）和弱场极限下转动惯量、潮汐形变与自旋感应四极矩的极限形式；明确展示了相对论潮汐形变计算如何与牛顿对应结果相关联，这一关系在既有文献中可能未被显式呈现。

Conclusion: 长度尺度的层次结构可能是理解相对论星体二阶矩之间普遍关系的关键；不可压缩星体作为极刚极限的候选，以及弱场极限下与牛顿力学的对应关系，为这些普遍性关系提供了新的理论约束和验证途径。

Abstract: Extending the work presented in a workshop ``From Quarks to Neutron Stars: Insights from kHz gravitational waves'', we discuss some limiting cases of the moment of inertia, tidal deformability, and spin-induced quadrupole moment for relativistic stars. First, conjecturing that a hierarchy of the length scale is the key to proposed universality among these second-order moments, we revisit the relation for incompressible relativistic stars (known as Schwarzschild's interior solution) as a candidate of the possible stiff limit. Second, we present the limiting form for the weak-field limit. In particular, we demonstrate how relativistic computations of tidal deformation are related to the traditional Newtonian counterpart, which might not have been presented explicitly in the literature.

</details>


### [102] [Exceptional lines of Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter black hole surrounded by a thin shell of matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16521)
*Liang-Bi Wu,Yu-Sen Zhou,Ming-Fei Ji,Xia-Yuan Liu,Wen-Tao Fu,Li-Ming Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究被静态物质薄壳包围的Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞的准正态模（QNM）中的例外线（EL）。对于共形标量扰动，作者推导了QNM条件，发现两个模式简并形成例外点（EP），扩展参数空间后该简并构成连续例外线。光谱响应沿该线具有内在方向性，平方根分支结构使传统线性参数化在该线附近失效，因此构建了适应例外线的参数化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正态模是黑洞时空的重要谱特征，而例外点/例外线在开放量子系统和非厄米物理中具有深刻意义。以往研究多关注单独例外点，而例外线作为连续简并结构在黑洞QNM中的性质尚未被充分探索，特别是其方向性光谱响应和参数化问题有待阐明。

Method: 通过对共形标量扰动在壳内外解之间进行匹配，推导出被薄壳包围的Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞的QNM条件。通过扩展参数空间使两个QNM之间的模式置换形成连续例外线，并引入谱灵敏度各向异性矩阵K来定量描述参数空间中谱分裂的方向依赖性，最后构建例外线适应的参数化方案以处理平方根分支的非解析结构。

Result: 发现高阶泛音对壳和背景参数变化尤为敏感。QNM分裂满足|ω₊−ω₋|=C_{\hat u}√ε+o(√ε)，其中C_{\hat u}=(ûᵀKû)^{1/4}，K为谱灵敏度各向异性矩阵。例外线的切向方向是K的零方向，导致沿例外线方向的前导平方根分裂消失，而法平面内两个主方向表现出不同的灵敏度。传统线性QNM参数化在例外线附近奇异，因此构建了结合线局部几何与非解析分裂的适应参数化。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了黑洞QNM中例外线的连续简并结构及其内在方向性光谱响应，表明例外线附近QNM的低阶线性参数化不适用，需要采用非解析的适应参数化方案。这一发现对理解黑洞谱的非厄米性质和参数化建模具有重要含义。

Abstract: We study the exceptional line (EL) in the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter black hole surrounded by a static thin shell of matter. For a conformally scalar perturbation, we derive the QNM condition by matching the interior and exterior solutions across the shell and show that higher overtones are particularly sensitive to variations of the shell and background parameters. A mode permutation between two QNMs reveals an exceptional point (EP). After extending the parameter space, this degeneracy forms a continuous EL. We show that the spectral response near the line is intrinsically directional. For a perturbation in parameter space $ε\widehat{\mathbf u}$, the QNM splitting is $|ω_+-ω_-| =C_{\widehat{\mathbf u}}\sqrtε+o(\sqrtε)$, with $C_{\widehat{\mathbf u}}=(\widehat{\mathbf u}^{T}\mathbf{K}\widehat{\mathbf u})^{1/4}$ and $\mathbf{K}$ is so-called spectral sensitivity anisotropy matrix. The tangent direction of EL is a null direction of $\mathbf{K}$, so that the leading square-root splitting vanishes along the exceptional line, whereas the two principal directions in the normal plane exhibit different sensitivities. Furthermore, the square-root branch structure makes conventional linear QNM parametrizations singular near an EL. We therefore construct an EL adapted parametrization which incorporates both the local geometry of the line and the nonanalytic QNM splitting.

</details>


### [103] [Quasinormal Modes of Black Holes Embedded in Dark-Matter Halos: Analysis of Dehnen(1, 4, 5/2) and DC14 Density Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16525)
*Gaurav Rachh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了静态球对称黑洞被暗物质晕（dark-matter halo）包裹时，晕对黑洞准正则模（quasinormal mode, QNM）谱的影响。作者考察了Dehnen (1,4,5/2)和DC14两种双幂律密度轮廓，计算了晕对度规函数及轴向引力微扰有效势的贡献，并提取了ℓ=2多极的基模QNM频率。结果显示晕的尺度半径（scale radius）rs对QNM谱影响最大，而密度归一化因子影响较小；Prony方法与六阶WKB结果高度一致（相对差异<0.04%）。这是首个针对嵌入DC14暗晕中黑洞的QNM分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正则模是引力波天文观测中探测黑洞周围环境的重要工具，而暗物质晕作为星系尺度普遍存在的结构，其对黑洞QNM谱的影响此前未被系统量化。特别是DC14轮廓作为经由星系形成模拟校准的密度模型，此前尚无针对其嵌入黑洞的QNM研究。

Method: 从两种双幂律密度轮廓（Dehnen (1,4,5/2)与DC14）出发，计算暗晕对静态球对称黑洞度规函数的贡献及相应的轴向引力微扰（s=2）有效势，随后分别使用Prony方法与六阶WKB近似提取ℓ=2多极的基模QNM频率，并对比两种方法的计算结果。

Result: 晕的尺度半径rs是影响QNM谱最主要的晕参数，它重塑了光环（light ring）附近的有效势垒，并使QNM频率的实部和虚部均发生偏移；密度归一化对Dehnen轮廓影响较小，但对DC14轮廓影响更显著，这与不同X值对应的内部密度结构差异有关。Prony与六阶WKB方法在全部参数范围内的相对差异低于0.04%。

Conclusion: QNM谱对周围的暗物质环境敏感，不同密度轮廓会通过其内部结构参数差异性地改变QNM频率；该工作提供了研究黑洞环境效应的一致数值框架，并为未来引力波观测约束暗晕性质提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate how a surrounding dark-matter halo imprints itself on the QNM spectrum of a static, spherically symmetric black hole, considering two physically distinct density profiles: the Dehnen (1,4,5/2) and DC14 profiles. Both follow double-power-law forms, while the shape parameters of the DC14 profile are calibrated to galaxy-formation simulations and vary with the stellar-to-halo mass ratio $X=\log_{10}(M_*/M_{\rm halo})$, which controls the inner density structure of the halo. For both profiles, we compute the halo contribution to the metric function and the resulting effective potential governing axial gravitational ($s=2$) perturbations, and extract the fundamental QNM frequencies for the $\ell=2$ multipole. Our results show that the halo scale radius $r_s$ has the largest influence on the QNM spectrum among the halo parameters explored, reshaping the effective potential barrier near the light ring and shifting both the real and imaginary parts of the QNM frequencies. The density normalization, by contrast, plays a comparatively minor role for the Dehnen profile but is more consequential for DC14, reflecting the different inner structures associated with its varying $X$ values. The Prony-extracted frequencies show excellent agreement with the sixth-order WKB results, with relative differences remaining below $0.04\%$ across the parameter ranges considered. To our knowledge, this constitutes the first QNM analysis of a black hole embedded in a DC14 dark-matter halo. These results demonstrate the sensitivity of the QNM spectrum to the surrounding dark-matter environment and provide a consistent numerical framework for studying such environmental effects.

</details>


### [104] [Quasi-local Hamiltonian for generalized Kerr-Schild black holes in the Iyer-Wald formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16534)
*M. A. Jaraba,T. L. Campos,M. C. Baldiotti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于 Iyer-Wald 形式的协变相空间方法（covariant phase space formalism），为广义 Kerr-Schild（GKS）黑洞提出一种拟局域哈密顿量（quasi-local Hamiltonian）。在平直闵可夫斯基（Minkowski）背景上的球对称时空中，该表述可还原 Misner-Sharp 质量，并能自然地扩展到旋转几何及渐近反德西特（AdS）黑洞情形，从而建立 Kerr-AdS 热力学的几何拟局域表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Misner-Sharp 质量仅适用于球对称时空，难以直接推广到旋转几何。本文旨在借助 GKS 结构提供一种统一的拟局域哈密顿量框架，使其既能回收球对称情形下的既有结果，又能拓展至旋转黑洞与时空背景带有宇宙学常数的情形。

Method: 采用 Iyer-Wald 形式的协变相空间方法，基于广义 Kerr-Schild 结构构造拟局域哈密顿量；针对渐近 AdS 黑洞引入背景 AdS 能量扣除（renormalization）程序。

Result: 该拟局域哈密顿量在闵可夫斯基背景上的球对称时空中精确还原 Misner-Sharp 质量；由于框架仅依赖 GKS 结构，可将质量定义直接扩展到旋转时空；在渐近 AdS 情形下自然涌现出背景能量的重整化减法，并据此建立了 Kerr-AdS 热力学的几何拟局域表述。

Conclusion: 该工作为拟局域质量提供了一种基于 GKS 结构的统一框架，将 Misner-Sharp 质量推广至旋转与时空气体和 AdS 背景，并为 Kerr-AdS 热力学建立了几何拟局域（geometric quasi-local）描述。

Abstract: Grounded in the covariant phase space approach of the Iyer-Wald formalism, we propose a quasi-local Hamiltonian for generalized Kerr-Schild (GKS) black holes. Evaluated for spherically symmetric spacetimes over a flat Minkowski background, our formulation recovers the Misner-Sharp mass. Because this framework relies solely on the GKS structure, it provides a straightforward extension of the Misner-Sharp mass to rotating geometries. We further extend our analysis to asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS) black holes, where a renormalization procedure to subtract the background AdS energy naturally emerges. By employing this technique, we establish a geometric quasi-local formulation of Kerr-AdS thermodynamics.

</details>


### [105] [Non-exotic traversable wormholes in Einstein-Chern-Simons gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16723)
*Mauricio Cataldo,Fernando Goméz,Paola Meza,Cristian Quinzacara,Patricio Salgado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在五维爱因斯坦–陈–西蒙斯（Einstein-Chern-Simons, EChS）引力框架下研究可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole）解，证明在喉部（throat）处径向零能量条件（Null Energy Condition, NEC）当且仅当耦合参数满足αl²≤−r₀²时才成立，且在足够负的耦合下四种标准能量条件可同时满足。利用体积积分量子（Volume Integral Quantifier, VIQ）进一步推导出临界耦合的精确闭式表达式，并验证所有结果在l→0极限下连续回归广义相对论（GR）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论下的可穿越虫洞通常需要违反能量条件（energy conditions），而EChS引力作为广义相对论的一种规范理论扩展，引入了由αl²参数化的高阶曲率修正，为在物理上更可接受的能量条件下实现可穿越虫洞提供了潜在的替代框架。

Method: 采用Morris-Thorne度规与各向异性流体假设，推导虫洞喉部能量密度、径向压力和横向压力的精确表达式；对任意形状函数（shape function）进行解析分析，并以幂律形状函数族b(r)=r₀(r₀/r)ⁿ、Φ=0作为具体示例；利用VIQ定量评估整体NEC违反程度。

Result: 径向NEC在喉部满足的充要条件为αl²≤−r₀²，在αl²=−r₀²时饱和、在αl²<−r₀²时严格满足，且与形状函数无关。在后者区域中能量密度严格为正，全部四种标准能量条件可同时满足。径向压力在喉部为负且由几何固定，独立于α、b(r)与Φ(r)。EChS修正使负NEC积分幅值相对于GR减小，并给出了VIQ为零的精确临界耦合αl²_crit，满足层级关系αl²_crit<−r₀²<0。

Conclusion: EChS引力能够在不违反任何能量条件的情况下支持可穿越虫洞解，喉部NEC满足与整体非负VIQ是相容但不同的条件，二者均可在EChS框架中实现。所有结果在l→0时连续回到GR极限，确认了框架的一致性。

Abstract: We investigate traversable wormhole solutions in five-dimensional Einstein-Chern-Simons (EChS) gravity, a gauge-theoretic extension of General Relativity that introduces a higher-curvature correction parametrized by the combination $αl^2$, where $α$ is a dimensionless coupling and $l$ is a length scale. Working with the Morris-Thorne metric and an anisotropic fluid, we derive exact expressions for the energy density, radial pressure, and lateral pressure at the wormhole throat. We show that the radial Null Energy Condition (NEC) is satisfied at the throat if and only if $αl^2\leq-r_0^2$, being saturated at $αl^2=-r_0^2$ and strictly satisfied for $αl^2<-r_0^2$, independently of the shape function. In the latter regime, the energy density is strictly positive and the full NEC, Weak, Strong, and Dominant Energy Conditions can be simultaneously satisfied. These results are established analytically for arbitrary shape functions and illustrated concretely for the power-law family $b(r)=r_0(r_0/r)^n$, $Φ=0$, for which all four standard energy conditions are satisfied at the throat for all $n>0$ when the EChS coupling is sufficiently negative. We further show that the radial pressure is negative and geometrically fixed at the throat, independently of $α$, $b(r)$, and $Φ(r)$. Using the Volume Integral Quantifier (VIQ), we establish that the EChS correction reduces the magnitude of the negative integrated NEC contribution relative to GR, and derive an exact closed-form critical coupling $αl^2_{\rm crit}$ at which the VIQ vanishes. The hierarchy $αl^2_{\rm crit}<-r_0^2<0$ shows that NEC satisfaction at the throat and a non-negative global VIQ are compatible but distinct conditions, both achievable within EChS gravity. All results reduce continuously to those of GR in the limit $l\to0$, confirming the consistency of the framework.

</details>


### [106] [On the (Non)Unitarity with respect to the Clock of a Dynamical Local Observer and the Einstein Equivalence Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16762)
*Kengo Shimada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究相对于局部观测者时钟所定义的内部时间的量子演化幺正性。在固定弯曲背景中，内部时钟演化一般是非幺正的，量子层面违反爱因斯坦等效原理；而在全微分同胚不变（diffeomorphism-invariant）的动力学引力理论中，微分同胚不变性使得采用观测者中心坐标成为可能，在部分约化的相空间上，时钟演化具有幺正性，从而在量子领域也保持了等效原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力中时间问题的核心议题——局部观测者视角下内部时钟时间的量子演化性质，并考察量子层面等效原理（equivalence principle）是否依然成立。

Method: 将观测者建模为同时携带时钟和物质场探测器的相对论粒子（relativistic particle），首先在固定弯曲背景中分析，再在完全微分同胚不变的动力学引力理论中分析；利用微分同胚不变性采用观测者中心坐标（observer-centric coordinates），在部分约化的相空间上考察进化生成元与时钟哈密顿量的线性关系。

Result: 固定弯曲背景下，相对内部时钟时间的演化一般是非幺正的（nonunitary），意味着量子层面违反爱因斯坦等效原理；而在微分同胚不变的引力理论中，微分同胚生成元之一在部分约化相空间上与时钟哈密顿量呈线性关系，在有效量子场论假设下，相对内部时钟的演化是幺正的（unitary）。

Conclusion: 微分同胚不变性在塑造局部观测者视角下的关系性幺正演化（relational unitary evolution）中起着根本性作用，它保证了在量子领域中等效原理依然成立。

Abstract: We investigate the unitarity of quantum evolution relative to an internal time defined by a local observer's clock. The observer is modeled as a relativistic particle carrying both a clock and a matter-field detector, analyzed first on a fixed curved background and subsequently within a fully diffeomorphism-invariant theory of dynamical gravity. In the former case, we find that evolution with respect to the internal clock time is generally nonunitary, implying a violation of the Einstein equivalence principle at the quantum level. In contrast, in the latter case, diffeomorphism invariance allows us to adopt observer-centric coordinates without loss of generality. On the resulting partially-reduced phase space, one of the diffeomorphism generators becomes linear in the clock Hamiltonian, generating a relational evolution that is consistent with the remaining diffeomorphism constraints. Assuming that an effective quantum field theory exists to be consistent with the diffeomorphism invariance, these features ensure unitary evolution relative to the internal clock, thereby preserving the equivalence principle even in the quantum regime. Our results highlight the fundamental role of diffeomorphism invariance in shaping relational unitary evolution from the perspective of a local observer.

</details>

### [107] [Proposal of a consistent value for the mean excitation energy of liquid water using advanced modeling, detailed simulations and precission proton Bragg curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15368)
*Pablo de Vera,Isabel Abril,Flávio Matias,Julian M. B. Shorto,Hélio Yoriyaz,Rafael Garcia-Molina*

Main category: physics.med-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过两种互补的理论方法（MELF-GOS与TDDFT-Penn）计算液态水的质子阻止本领，并利用模拟代码SEICS评估布拉格曲线，最终确立了液态水的平均激发能I值为79.4 eV，为质子治疗中的射程预测提供了坚实的物理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子治疗的精度高度依赖于液态水（液态水，liquid water）的阻止本领（stopping power）与平均激发能I值，但挥发性液体实验测量困难，导致这些值长期存在争议。本文利用液态水与非晶冰在同质密度下阻止本领的等价性，寻求可靠估算I值的途径。

Method: 采用两种互补的理论方法：MELF-GOS（Mermin能量损失函数-广义振子强度）方法基于微扰介电形式论（dielectric formalism），在足够高能量下作为精确参考；而新型非微扰TDDFT-Penn（含时密度泛函理论-Penn）方法确保在低于阻止最大值能量区间的准确性。随后将这些阻止本领值纳入模拟代码SEICS（固体中高能离子与团簇模拟），评估1至230 MeV范围内的质子布拉格曲线。

Result: 通过与可用的精确深度-剂量（depth-dose）测量数据对比，确立了液态水的一致I值为79.4 eV，并验证了两种理论方法在不同能量区间的互补准确性。

Conclusion: 确定I = 79.4 eV为液态水的平均激发能值，为改善质子治疗计划中的射程预测提供了坚实的物理基础。

Abstract: Protontherapy precision depends on accurately knowing the stopping power of liquid water, as it is the most abundant component of soft body tissues. Difficulties when working with volatile liquids have led to a long-standing debate regarding the values of the stopping power and the mean excitation energy $I$ of liquid water. The equivalence between the stopping power of liquid water and amorphous ice per unit mass density across all clinically relevant proton energies opens the possibility to obtain a reliable estimation of the $I$-value of liquid water. In this work, we employ two complementary theoretical methodologies to assess the proton stopping power of water. The MELF-GOS (Mermin Energy Loss Function - Generalized Oscillator Strengths) approach, based on the perturbative dielectric formalism, serves as an accurate reference for sufficiently high energies, while the novel non-perturbative TDDFT-Penn (time-dependent density functional theory - Penn) method ensures accuracy for energies even below the stopping maximum. These stopping-power values were incorporated into the simulation code SEICS (Simulation of Energetic Ions and Clusters through Solids) to evaluate proton Bragg curves from $1$ to $230$ MeV. Comparisons with available precise depth-dose measurements provide critical insights that help establish the consistent value $I = 79.4$ eV for liquid water, providing robust physical grounds for improving range prediction for treatment planning.

</details>

### [108] [$\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ Theta Subgroup Structure of Maxwell theory in the Lattice Villain Hamiltonian Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25332)
*Shoto Aoki,Yoshio Kikukawa,Toshinari Takemoto*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文在哈密顿量 Villain 形式下研究了带 theta 项的格点麦克斯韦理论的二元性结构，证明了 S 与 T² 变换在算子层面实现了 SL(2,ℤ) 的 theta 子群，并将分析拓展至带电/带磁扇区，最后通过规范 U(1) 1-形式对称性的 ℤ_N 子群得到非可逆缺陷，其融合规则复现了 Tambara–Yamagami 结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考虑到奇数阶 Chern–Simons 理论依赖于自旋结构的选择，完整的 SL(2,ℤ) 二元性需引入费米子自由度，因此本文聚焦于玻色子理论，研究其 theta 子群对偶结构。

Method: 在哈密顿量 Villain（Hamiltonian Villain）公式化下构建格点麦克斯韦理论，从算子层面构造 S 与 T² 变换以检验 SL(2,ℤ) 的 theta 子群结构；通过破坏 Gauss 约束与 Bianchi 恒等式引入电、磁荷扇区，并利用规范 U(1) 1-形式对称性的 ℤ_N 子群构造非可逆缺陷。

Result: S 变换在算子层面交换电、磁荷；T² 变换实现 Witten 效应（Witten effect）；所构造的非可逆缺陷的融合规则复现了预期的 Tambara–Yamagami 结构。

Conclusion: 玻色性格点麦克斯韦理论中的 theta 子群对偶可以在算子层面得到完整实现，且相关非可逆缺陷呈现标准的 Tambara–Yamagami 融合代数，为格点规范理论中的二元性结构提供了具体构造。

Abstract: We study the duality structure of lattice Maxwell theory with a theta term in the Hamiltonian Villain formulation. Reflecting the fact that odd-level Chern--Simons theory depends on a choice of spin structure, a complete realization of the full $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ structure would require fermionic degrees of freedom.
  We therefore restrict our analysis to the bosonic theory and focus on the theta subgroup generated by the $\mathcal{S}$ and $\mathcal{T}^{2}$ transformations. We construct these transformations at the operator level and show that they realize the theta subgroup structure of $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb Z)$. We also extend the analysis to sectors with electric and magnetic charges, introduced as violations of the Gauss-law constraint and the Bianchi identity, respectively. We show that the $\mathcal S$ transformation exchanges electric and magnetic charges, while the $\mathcal T^2$ transformation realizes the Witten effect. Finally, we discuss a related non-invertible defect obtained by gauging a $\mathbb Z_N$ subgroup of the global $\mathrm{U}(1)$ $1$-form symmetry, and show that its fusion rule reproduces the expected Tambara--Yamagami structure.

</details>


### [109] [Configurational Temperature in the 3D XY Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16677)
*Kutloano Nkojoana,Anosh Joseph*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了构型温度估计量（configurational temperature estimator）作为三维虚化学势XY模型的蒙特卡洛和朗之万（Langevin）模拟诊断工具的有效性。在8³格点上对β=0.2–0.7范围内的模拟表明，该估计量在对称相中与理论值1一致，但在有序相出现系统偏差，主要归因于有限尺寸效应和离散化伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在涉及真实化学势的格点场论模拟中，符号问题（sign problem）会使传统验证方法失效，因此需要仅依赖于场构型的内禀一致性诊断工具，以评估采样算法的正确性与热化程度。

Method: 将构型温度估计量作为诊断工具，在8³格点上对带虚化学势的三维XY模型进行数值模拟；分别采用实朗之万动力学（real Langevin dynamics）和Metropolis蒙特卡洛（Metropolis Monte Carlo）算法，在耦合常数β=0.2–0.7范围内测量作用量密度和构型温度估计量。

Result: 在较小β下，两种算法得到的作用量密度与强耦合展开（strong-coupling expansion）预言高度一致；构型温度估计量在对称相中与理论值1良好吻合，但在有序相中出现系统偏差，主要归因于小格点上有限尺寸效应与离散化伪影。

Conclusion: 构型温度估计量能有效评估格点场论模拟中的热化与算法正确性，是应对真实化学势下符号问题的重要诊断工具。

Abstract: We investigate the configurational temperature estimator as a diagnostic tool for Monte Carlo and Langevin simulations of the three-dimensional XY model with an imaginary chemical potential. This estimator depends only on the field configurations. It provides a stringent internal consistency check for numerical sampling algorithms. We perform simulations using both real Langevin dynamics and the Metropolis Monte Carlo algorithm on an $8^3$ lattice across a range of coupling values, $β= 0.2 - 0.7$. Our results for the action density are in excellent agreement with strong-coupling expansion predictions at small $β$, providing an important validation of both simulation approaches. The measured value of the configurational temperature estimator shows good agreement with its expected value of unity in the symmetric phase. However, systematic deviations appear in the ordered phase. We attribute these deviations primarily to finite-size effects and discretization artifacts associated with the relatively small lattice volume. Our results demonstrate that the configurational temperature estimator provides a valuable diagnostic for assessing thermalization and algorithmic correctness in lattice field theory simulations. Such diagnostics are particularly important in preparation for studies at real chemical potential, where sign problems arise and conventional validation methods become less reliable.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum Simulation of QCD in Axial Gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16783)
*Xiaojun Yao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了3+1维SU(3)非阿贝尔规范理论与基本费米子动态耦合的量子模拟。通过采用轴向规范（axial gauge）的格点哈密顿量，作者给出了体系所需的量子比特数上界，并分析了基于Trotter化、量子傅里叶变换和Jordan-Wigner变换的时间演化量子算法，证明模拟格点QCD实时动力学所需的量子资源随体积、能量、时间、精度和裸哈密顿参数呈多项式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔规范理论（non-Abelian gauge theory）的实时动力学模拟在经典计算机上极具挑战，量子模拟有望提供指数级加速。现有工作多集中于低维或简化模型，在3+1维SU(3)规范理论（如格点QCD）上建立可操作的量子模拟方案及其资源估算尚不充分，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用轴向规范下的格点哈密顿量以避免Gauss定律约束；将规范场的时间分量解析求解为独立场自由度与格点正则化Green函数的表达式。规范场自由度在局域场基下表示，并通过局域量子傅里叶变换（quantum Fourier transform）高效转换到共轭动量基。基于Trotter化、量子傅里叶变换和Jordan-Wigner变换构建显式量子电路，推导了达到能量E和精度ε所需量子比特数的严格上界，以及每个Trotter步中CNOT门和单比特旋转门的复杂度标度。

Result: 量子比特数上界为16n_A V + 12n_f V，其中n_A为每个格点独立规范场所需的量子比特数，其表达式中包含体积、能量、精度、裸耦合常数和费米子味数等参数。每Trotter步的CNOT门和单比特旋转门数量均标度为O(n_A^4 V^{4/3}) + O(V^{5/3})（固定费米子味数n_f≤6）。

Conclusion: 在轴向规范框架下，模拟3+1维格点QCD实时动力学所需的量子资源随体积、能量、时间、精度及裸哈密顿参数呈多项式标度，这表明利用量子计算机模拟真实尺寸的格点QCD动态演化在资源上具有可行性。

Abstract: We study quantum simulation of SU(3) non-Abelian gauge theory dynamically coupled with fundamental fermions in $3+1$ dimensions by employing the lattice Hamiltonian in axial gauge that avoids Gauss's law constraints. The temporal component of the gauge field is analytically solved in terms of independent field degrees of freedom and a lattice regulated Green's function. The axial gauge condition is trivially maintained in time evolution, even under Trotterization. The gauge field degrees of freedom are expressed in the local field basis and can be efficiently transformed into the canonical conjugate momentum basis by local quantum Fourier transforms. We prove the number of qubits needed for describing all states up to an energy $E$ with an accuracy $ε$ on a lattice of volume $V$ at bare coupling $g$ is bounded as $16n_A V + 12n_f V$, where $n_A \approx \log_2 (\frac{64 E' V^{4/3}}{π^2ε} + \frac{32\sqrt{2}g n_f E'^{1/2} V^{7/6}}{\sqrt{3}π^3ε^{1/2}} ) $ is the number of qubits needed for each independent gauge field per site with a shifted energy $E'$, and $n_f$ denotes the number of fermion flavors. We then analyze a quantum algorithm for time evolution that is based on Trotterization, quantum Fourier transform, and Jordan-Wigner transformation, for which quantum circuits can be explicitly constructed under arbitrary gauge field truncation and digitization. We find the numbers of CNOT and single-qubit rotation gates both scale as $O(n_A^4 V^{4/3}) + O(V^{5/3})$ per Trotter step for fixed $n_f\leq 6$. We conclude that quantum resources needed for simulating real-time dynamics of lattice QCD scale polynomially with volume, energy, time, accuracy, and bare Hamiltonian parameters.

</details>


### [111] [Jump-Diffusion Stochastic Quantization for Euclidean Lattice Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16451)
*Alexander Rothkopf*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过引入跳跃-扩散过程（jump-diffusion processes）构造了马尔可夫意义下随机量子化（stochastic quantization）的自然推广，利用Lévy跳跃路径高效穿越势垒景观，从而改善甚至恢复遍历性（ergodicity），并应用于解决2维U(1)规范理论中的拓扑冻结（topological freezing）基准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的随机量子化方法基于扩散过程，其连续路径在存在势垒的action景观中难以有效穿越，导致遍历性丧失和采样效率低下。本文旨在推广随机量子化框架，利用具有非连续路径的跳跃扩散过程来克服这一局限。

Method: 构造跳跃-扩散过程的随机量子化推广，其中跳跃路径由Lévy飞行（Lévy flights）描述。论文探索了多种构建高效跳跃更新（jump updates）的策略，并将这些策略部署到2维U(1)规范理论中以解决拓扑冻结问题。

Result: 跳跃机制在存在势垒的action景观中能够实现高效探索，相比传统扩散方法可改善甚至恢复遍历性。相关策略在2维U(1)规范理论的拓扑冻结基准问题上得到实际应用验证。

Conclusion: 跳跃-扩散过程为随机量子化提供了一种自然推广框架，显著增强了采样方法在复杂势垒景观下的遍历性表现，为规范场论中拓扑冻结等难题提供了新的解决途径。

Abstract: We construct the natural generalization of stochastic quantization (in the Markovian sense) by considering jump-diffusion processes. This class of stochastic processes exhibits non-continuous paths, so-called Lévy flights. In the presence of jumps, action landscapes with barriers can be efficiently explored, improving and even restoring ergodicity where traditional diffusion approaches become inefficient. We explore different strategies for constructing efficient jump updates, which we deploy to address the benchmark problem of topological freezing in 2d U(1) gauge theory.

</details>
