### [112] [Electronic structure and magnetic correlations in the epitaxially strained bilayer nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_{7}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14810)
*I. V. Leonov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过DFT+动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）方法，研究了电子-电子关联和外延应变对双层Ruddlesden-Popper镍氧化物La₃Ni₂O₇（LNO）正常态电子结构、准粒子带重整化、费米面和磁关联的影响，发现轨道选择性局域化、应变敏感性和Lifshitz相变等关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究镍氧化物超导体（nickelate superconductor）中电子-电子关联和外延晶格应变对其正常态电子性质的影响，以理解其磁性关联和可能的超导机制。

Method: 采用DFT+动力学平均场理论（DFT+dynamical mean-field theory）组合方法，对La₃Ni₂O₇的晶体结构施加不同外延应变，计算正常态电子结构、准粒子带重整化（quasiparticle band renormalization）、费米面（Fermi surface）和磁关联（magnetic correlations）。

Result: Ni 3d带呈现显著的轨道选择性重整化和强非相干性，表明Ni x²-y²和3z²-r²态接近轨道选择性局域化（orbital-selective localization）。拉伸应变和中度压缩应变（约-2%）增强磁关联；约-4%的大压缩应变引发Lifshitz相变，表现为γ费米面片的消失，伴随近完全占据的浅平带（Ni 3z²-r²键合轨道特征），导致磁关联急剧下降。

Conclusion: LNO中自旋-电荷密度波条纹不稳定性（spin-charge-density-wave stripe instability）由费米面嵌套（Fermi surface nesting）驱动；压力和应变均可有效调控自旋-电荷密度波有序，从而增强自旋涨落（spin fluctuations）。

Abstract: Using the DFT+dynamical mean-field theory method we study the effects of electron-electron correlations and epitaxial strain of the crystal structure on the normal-state electronic structure, quasiparticle band renormalizations, Fermi surface, and magnetic correlations of the bilayer Ruddlesden-Popper nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ (LNO). Our results exhibit a remarkable orbital-selective renormalization and strong incoherence of the Ni $3d$ bands, pointing to the proximity of the Ni $x^2-y^2$ and $3z^2-r^2$ states to orbital-selective localization. The electronic properties of LNO show a high sensitivity to the in-plane strain. We note that both a tensile and a moderate compressive strain (up to about $-2$\%) yield a significant enhancement of magnetic correlations compared to the unstrained LNO. Under a large compressive strain of about $-4$\%, we observe a Lifshitz transition characterized by the disappearance of the $γ$ Fermi surface sheet, which is associated with a nearly fully occupied, shallow flat-band of the bonding Ni $3z^2-r^2$ orbital character. As a result, we observe a sharp decrease of magnetic correlations, implying suppression of superconductivity. Overall, our results support the picture of spin- and change-density-wave stripe instability driven by the Fermi surface nesting in LNO. Our results suggest that both pressure and strain can effectively tune (suppress or enhance) spin-change-density-wave ordering, giving rise to enhanced spin fluctuations.

</details>


### [113] [Antiferroquadrupolar Order in Altermagnetic CoF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14889)
*Daniel Halliday,Laura Pöysti,Chung Xu,Daniel A. Mayoh,Didier Wermeille,Dharmalingam Prabhakaran,David R. Bowler,Roger D. Johnson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在CoF₂（一种具有金红石晶体结构和Γ点反铁磁性的d波交错磁性（altermagnetism）化合物）中，结合共振弹性X射线散射、对称性分析和第一性原理计算，直接观测到反铁四极子（antiferroquadrupolar）电荷序，并证明该电荷序是交错磁自旋劈裂的微观起源，从而为交错磁序参量分解为磁和电荷自由度提供了实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论研究表明交错磁体（altermagnets）中非相对论性自旋劈裂电子态的微观起源与隐藏的电荷序相关联，但该电荷序的实验研究十分有限。

Method: 综合运用共振弹性X射线散射（resonant elastic X-ray scattering）、对称性分析（symmetry analysis）和基于第一性原理的电子结构计算（ab initio calculations），对金红石（rutile）结构、Γ点反铁磁的d波交错磁化合物CoF₂进行电荷序表征。

Result: 直接观测到电荷序并将其鉴定为反铁四极子型；电子结构计算表明该反铁四极子序产生特征性交错磁自旋劈裂，从而建立交错磁序参量分解为磁和电荷自由度的实证证据。

Conclusion: 证明反铁四极子序是CoF₂中交错磁性的微观起源，该结论可推广至更广泛的金红石族交错磁体；同时，本方法适用于一般交错磁研究，共振弹性X射线散射可作为直接探测支撑自旋劈裂电子态的电荷多极子的手段。

Abstract: Altermagnets host non-relativistic spin-split electronic states whose microscopic origin is theoretically linked to a hidden charge order, yet experimental studies of this charge order are limited. We therefore investigate charge order within CoF$_2$, a $d$-wave altermagnetic compound with a rutile crystal structure and $Γ$-point antiferromagnetism. Combining resonant elastic X-ray scattering, symmetry analysis and ab initio calculations, we directly observe charge ordering and identify it as antiferroquadrupolar in nature. Via electronic structure calculations, we show that the experimentally observed antiferroquadrupolar order gives rise to the characteristic altermagnetic spin-splitting, thereby establishing empirical evidence for the decomposition of the altermagnetic order parameter into magnetic and charge degrees of freedom. We hence demonstrate that antiferroquadrupolar order is the microscopic origin of altermagnetism in CoF$_2$, with implications to the wider family of rutile altermagnets. Furthermore, our approach is applicable to studying altermagnetism in general, having demonstrated that resonant elastic X-ray scattering can serve as a direct probe of the charge multipoles that underpin spin-split electronic states in these materials.

</details>


### [114] [Low temperature magnetic structure and lattice response in SmCuAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14920)
*M. G. Kim,C. Neupane,Y. Yu,R. Acevedo-Esteves,C. Nelson,D. Evans,E. D. Mun,D. F. Agterberg,J. -W. Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用高分辨率同步辐射X射线衍射（synchrotron X-ray diffraction）和Sm L₂与L₃边X射线共振磁散射（XRMS）研究了单晶SmCuAs₂的结构与磁性。研究发现该化合物在8 K至室温间保持四方对称性，在Néel温度附近出现磁弹性耦合引起的晶格参数异常，并确定其共度反铁磁结构（传播矢量q = (0,0,0.5)），Sm磁矩位于ab面内并按++--方式沿c轴堆叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究SmCuAs₂的结构与磁性性质，特别是理解其电阻率极小值（resistivity minimum）现象的微观起源，并比较同族RECuAs₂化合物（RE = Pr, Nd, Gd）以揭示磁矩取向、磁阻挫与低温度输运行为之间的关联。

Method: 采用高分辨率同步辐射X射线衍射进行温度依赖的结构表征，并在Sm的L₂和L₃吸收边实施X射线共振磁散射（XRMS）测量来确定磁结构。

Result: SmCuAs₂在8 K至室温区间维持四方对称性；在电阻率极小值（T ≈ 30 K）以下出现晶格参数反常，c轴晶格常数在Néel温度附近呈现平台及随后的增长，表明存在磁弹性耦合（magnetoelastic coupling）。XRMS确定其为共度反铁磁结构，传播矢量q = (0,0,0.5)，Sm磁矩位于ab平面内，沿c轴呈++--堆叠。

Conclusion: 与Pr、Nd和Gd相应化合物的对比表明，面内磁矩取向及相关的磁阻挫（magnetic frustration）在电阻率极小值的形成中起关键作用；该族化合物中自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与磁弹性耦合的差异是调控低温输运行为的重要因素。

Abstract: We investigated the structural and magnetic properties of single-crystalline SmCuAs$_2$ using high-resolution synchrotron X-ray diffraction and X-ray resonant magnetic scattering (XRMS) at the Sm $L_2$ and $L_3$ edges. Temperature-dependent diffraction measurements confirm that SmCuAs$_2$ maintains its tetragonal symmetry from room temperature down to 8 K, with lattice parameters showing anomalous behavior below the resistivity minimum ($T \approx$ 30 K). Notably, the \textbf{c}-axis lattice parameter exhibits a plateau and subsequent increase near the Néel temperature, indicating magnetoelastic coupling. XRMS measurements reveal a commensurate antiferromagnetic structure with a propagation vector \textbf{\textit{q}} = (0, 0, 0.5). Our measurement shows that the Sm moments are aligned within the \textbf{\textit{ab}} plane and arranged in a $++--$ stacking along the \textbf{\textit{c}}-axis. Comparison with related \textit{RE}CuAs$_2$ compounds (\textit{RE} = Pr, Nd, and Gd) suggests that in-plane moment orientation and associated magnetic frustration play a key role in the emergence of the resistivity minimum. Differences in spin-orbit and magnetoelastic coupling across the series highlight their importance in governing low-temperature transport behavior.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum Many-Body Scars, Magnon-Pair Condensation, and Hilbert Space Fragmentation in an Anisotropic Heisenberg Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15017)
*J. Y. Liu-Sun,Z. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由两个相同二分次晶格（bipartite sublattices）构成的自旋-1/2各向异性海森堡模型（anisotropic Heisenberg model），发现由受限谱生成代数（RSGA）生成的一组精确本征态构成量子多体疤痕（quantum many-body scars），这些疤痕态是呈现离域长程序（ODLRO）的磁振子对凝聚（magnon-pair condensate）。在次晶格间相互作用的共振点，模型精确映射到混合自旋-1与自旋-0的XY模型，引出希尔伯特空间碎裂（Hilbert space fragmentation, HSF），从而建立了量子多体疤痕、磁振子对凝聚与希尔伯特空间碎裂自然共存的精确可解平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体疤痕、非平衡态物理与希尔伯特空间碎裂等概念近年来受到广泛关注，但此前缺乏一个同时涵盖这些现象的精确可解平台。本文旨在构建一个统一的理论框架，在其中所有这些现象能够自然共存并被精确分析。

Method: 作者考虑双二分次晶格上的自旋-1/2各向异性海森堡模型，利用受限谱生成代数构造精确本征态族作为量子多体疤痕态；在次晶格相互作用共振点处，通过精确映射将模型转化为混合自旋-1和自旋-0的二分量XY模型，并据此分析其动力学约束与希尔伯特空间结构。

Result: 发现疤痕态表现为磁振子对凝聚态，具有亚广延纠缠熵（subextensive entanglement entropy）和离域长程序。共振点处的映射将模型分解为按自旋构型标记的独立子哈密顿量，每个自旋-0粒子作为动力学约束与邻居隔离，从而产生涌现的希尔伯特空间碎裂。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个精确可解的模型平台，使量子多体疤痕、具有离域长程序的磁振子对凝聚以及希尔伯特空间碎裂三种现象在其中自然共存，为深入理解这些非平衡态与量子关联现象提供了理想的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate a spin-$1/2$ anisotropic Heisenberg model on a lattice consisting of two identical bipartite sublattices. A family of exact eigenstates generated by the restricted spectrum generating algebra (RSGA) constitutes quantum many-body scar states, characterized by subextensive entanglement entropy and supporting. These scar states are magnon-pair condensates exhibiting off-diagonal long-range order (ODLRO). At the resonance point of the inter-sublattice interaction, the model exactly maps onto a mixed spin-$1$ and spin-$0$ XY model on a bipartite lattice, which decomposes into independent sub-Hamiltonians labeled by all possible spin configurations. Each spin-$0$ particle is dynamically isolated from its neighbors and acts as a kinetic constraint, giving rise to emergent Hilbert space fragmentation (HSF). Our work establishes an exactly solvable platform in which quantum many-body scars, magnon-pair condensation exhibiting off-diagonal long-range order, and Hilbert space fragmentation naturally coexist.

</details>


### [116] [Zero-point theorems in quantum many-body physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15542)
*Yuan Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子自旋系统相图中能隙（spectral gap）必然存在的零点（zero point）的零点型论证，并将其推广到多参数哈密顿量族，提出了统一推广的猜想，为任意高维量子自旋模型中的临界现象提供了不依赖具体模型的普适约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零点定理仅考虑单参数情形，缺乏对多参数相图结构的系统分析。作者旨在推广该定理，在不对低能动力学或响应做任何具体假设的前提下，仅利用参数边界上的模型无关变换关系，为量子自旋模型中的临界现象提供普适约束。

Method: 提出几种基于各维数量子自旋系统相图中能隙必然存在零点的零点型论证；考虑多参数哈密顿量族以扩展仅含单参数的传统零点定理；类比零点定理，仅沿参数边界施加模型无关的变换关系，不指定任何低能动力学或响应；并进一步给出一系列统一推广的猜想。

Result: 所得结果对任意高维量子自旋模型中临界现象的可能相关算子（relevant operators）给出了强有力且普适的模型无关约束。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了系统性的多参数零点型论证框架，为高维量子自旋系统的临界行为研究建立了不依赖具体微观模型的通用约束工具。

Abstract: We propose several zero-point type arguments based on the inevitable zero point(s) of a spectral gap in the quantum spin system phase diagrams in various dimensions. We consider multi-parameter families of Hamiltonian extending the conventional zero-point theorem that includes only one parameter. Analogously to the zero-point theorem, we only impose model-independent transformation relations along the parameter boundary, rather than specifying any low-energy dynamics or response. We further give a series of conjectures, which generalize our statements in a uniform way. Our results give powerful and universal model-independent constraints on the possible relevant operators for critical phenomena in quantum spin models in arbitrary high dimensions.

</details>


### [117] [Accessing Gapped Chiral Phase with Auxiliary-Assisted PEPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15732)
*Sen Niu,Rui-Zhen Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种辅助辅助框架（auxiliary-assisted framework），通过将物理手性系统与一个时间反演伙伴（time-reversed partner）耦合，构建非手性扩大表示（non-chiral enlarged representation），从而克服无限投影纠缠对态（PEPS）在手性有隙相描述中产生虚假长程关联的问题。该框架在自由费米子Chern绝缘体和相互作用手性自旋液体中均获得干净的有隙关联函数和有限传递矩阵关联长度，并通过层分辨动量投影恢复预期的手性纠缠边界谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限键维PEPS虽然能捕捉手性相（chiral phases）的许多局域和拓扑性质，但会普遍产生虚假的长程幂律型关联，使得无限PEPS能否忠实描述二维手性有隙相（chiral gapped phases）存在长期争议。

Method: 将物理手性系统与一个时间反演伙伴系统相互嵌入耦合，构成非手性扩大表示，通过受控退耦恢复物理手性扇区；使用层分辨动量投影（layer-resolved momentum projection）提取手性拓扑信息。

Result: 在自由费米子Chern绝缘体和相互作用手性自旋液体两种系统中，所得到的PEPS均展现出干净的有隙关联函数和有限的传递矩阵关联长度，与直接手性PEPS表示中的人工长程尾形成对比。物理与辅助系统之间的耦合因有限纠缠效应而很小，仅影响短程局域量；通过层分辨动量投影恢复了预期的普适手性纠缠边界谱。

Conclusion: 该研究通过改变表示方式而非直接研究手性纯态，为使用有限键维PEPS访问有隙手性相提供了一条实用途径。

Abstract: It has been controversial whether infinite projected entangled pair state (PEPS) can faithfully describe chiral gapped phases in two dimensions or not. Finite-bond-dimension PEPS can capture many local and topological properties of chiral phases, but generically develop spurious long-range power-law like correlations. We introduce an auxiliary-assisted framework that bypass this obstruction by embedding the physical chiral system together with an auxiliary time-reversed partner, yielding a non-chiral enlarged representation whose physical chiral sector is recovered with controlled decoupling. For both a free-fermion Chern insulator and an interacting chiral spin liquid, the resulting PEPS show clean gapped correlation functions, and a finite transfer-matrix correlation length, in contrast with the artificial long-range tail of direct chiral PEPS representations. There exist small and negligibly coupling between the physical and auxiliary systems due to the finite entanglement effect, which only affect short-ranged local quantities. Despite the nonchiral enlarged representation, the chiral topological information remains encoded in the entanglement. Using layer-resolved momentum projection, we recover the expected universal chiral entanglement boundary spectrum. Our study provides a practical route to access gapped chiral phases with finite bond-dimension PEPS by changing the representation problem rather than directly studying the chiral pure state.

</details>


### [118] [Linear response functions from inhomogeneous dynamical mean-field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15800)
*Don Rolih,Fabian B. Kugler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非均匀动力学平均场理论（inhomogeneous dynamical mean-field theory）计算静态晶格磁化率的新方法，该方法通过系统对外场的响应来绕过双粒子顶点函数。在方形晶格Hubbard模型的磁化率计算中，采用数值重整化群（numerical renormalization group）作为杂质求解器，结果与标准顶点方法一致，且能达到其他方法难以企及的低温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动力学平均场理论计算静态晶格磁化率通常需要处理双粒子顶点函数，这一过程计算复杂且对杂质求解器有较高要求，限制了可达到的温度范围。作者希望找到一种绕过顶点函数的替代方案，以扩展方法的适用范围并降低低温计算的难度。

Method: 方法利用系统对外加场的响应来计算静态晶格磁化率，从而避免直接计算双粒子顶点函数。该方法基于非均匀动力学平均场理论框架，并与数值重整化群杂质求解器相结合，应用于方形晶格Hubbard模型的磁性测量。

Result: 所提出的方法在方形晶格Hubbard模型的磁化率计算中取得了成功，计算结果与采用标准顶点方法获得的结果吻合良好。此外，该方法能够达到使用其他方法难以访问的低温度。

Conclusion: 由于避免了顶点函数且与几乎所有杂质求解器兼容，该方法的通用性强，能够实现低温度下的静态晶格磁化率计算，为强关联电子体系的磁性研究提供了一种可行且高效的新工具。

Abstract: We present a method for calculating static lattice susceptibilities with inhomogeneous dynamical mean-field theory. The method utilizes the response of the system to an external field and thereby circumvents two-particle vertex functions. We demonstrate the success of our approach for the magnetic susceptibility of the square-lattice Hubbard model using the numerical renormalization group as an impurity solver and show that it compares well with the results obtained using the standard vertex approach. As it avoids vertex functions and is compatible with virtually any impurity solver, our method is able to reach low temperatures that are hard to access with other methods.

</details>


### [119] [Topological Altermagnetic Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15811)
*Jasmin Bedow,Nitin Kaushal,Marcel Franz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个典型的Lieb晶格关联交变磁体模型中，伊辛自旋-轨道耦合（Ising spin-orbit coupling）稳定的交变磁性拓扑相。通过Hartree-Fock和精确对角化方法，作者确立了量子自旋霍尔效应（quantum spin Hall effect）与交变磁自旋序在每原胞平均电子密度为2和4时于参数空间大范围共存，并探讨了边缘的磁结构对拓扑边缘模电子行为的影响，证明其对反演对称破缺项的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnet）作为一种兼具反铁磁与自旋劈裂特性的新型磁性材料，其与拓扑能带结构（topological band structure）的耦合机制尚不明确。本文旨在探究关联Lieb晶格模型中，伊辛自旋-轨道耦合如何稳定交变磁性拓扑相，并考察边缘磁结构对拓扑边缘态的调控作用。

Method: 采用Hartree-Fock近似与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）两种互补的数值技术处理电子关联效应，在Lieb晶格模型中研究交变磁体的伊辛自旋-轨道耦合效应，并系统地扫描参数空间以及两种平均电子密度填充情形，同时考察边缘磁构型对拓扑边缘态的响应。

Result: 在每原胞平均电子密度为2和4时，宽参数范围内观测到量子自旋霍尔效应与交变磁自旋序的共存。边缘磁结构能够显著影响拓扑边缘模的电子行为，且这些边缘模对反演对称性破缺（inversion-symmetry breaking）项保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 交变磁性拓扑相可在关联Lieb晶格模型中由伊辛自旋-轨道耦合稳定实现，其拓扑边缘模具有对反演对称性破缺的鲁棒性，为交变磁体与拓扑物理的融合提供了可行的模型平台。

Abstract: We study the emergence of altermagnetic topological phases stabilized by Ising spin-orbit coupling in a prototypical Lieb lattice model of a correlated altermagnet. Treating the electron interactions within the Hartree-Fock and the exact diagonalization techniques, we establish the co-existence of a quantum spin Hall effect with altermagnetic spin order over a wide region of the parameter space for average electron densities of 2 and 4 per unit cell. We explore how the magnetic structure along edges influences the electronic behavior of the associated topological edge modes, demonstrating their robustness against inversion-symmetry breaking terms.

</details>


### [120] [Transport-Noise Witnesses of Electronic Multipartite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16028)
*Shuhan Ding,Prakash Sharma,Zecheng Shen,Jiang-Xiazi Lin,Sergei Urazhdin,Yao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于输运测量的协议，利用平衡态噪声谱（equilibrium noise spectrum）作为实验可观测的量来证认固体材料中多体纠缠电子态，该方法适用于极低温条件下传统光谱探针无法触及的材料体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子间的纠缠是强关联量子材料区别于传统金属和半导体的核心特征，验证固体材料中相互作用电子间的内在纠缠，对于量子物态分类和基于材料的量子技术发展都具有重要意义。然而在极低温条件下，传统光谱探针难以用于纠缠证认。

Method: 作者引入一种基于输运的纠缠见证（entanglement witness）协议，以平衡态噪声谱作为可实验观测的观测量。所构造的电流噪声经过适当的积分、对称化及投影处理后满足一个可由微观模型参数推导出的上界，且该上界对电子基的选择具有不变性。该方法是对近期基于散射的纠缠见证方案的拓展，使其适用于超低温材料体系。

Result: 该方法在多个典型体系中进行了基准测试，包括扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）、扭转双层MoTe₂和Hubbard模型，成功证认了分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulating state）中的纠缠特性。

Conclusion: 该输运协议将散射型纠缠见证方法推广至超低温材料，为那些传统光谱手段无法适用但预计存在候选纠缠态的体系提供了纠缠认证的新途径。

Abstract: Entanglement among particles is a defining feature of strongly correlated quantum materials, distinguishing them from conventional metals and semiconductors. The ability to certify intrinsic entanglement among interacting electrons in solid-state materials is important not only for classifying quantum states of matter, but also for developing material-based quantum technologies. Here, we introduce a transport-based protocol for witnessing multipartite entangled electronic states, based on the equilibrium noise spectrum as an experimentally accessible observable. The appropriately integrated, symmetrized, and projected current noise obeys an upper bound that can be derived from microscopic model parameters and is invariant with respect to the choice of electronic basis. We benchmark this framework in several paradigmatic systems, including twisted bilayer graphene, twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, and Hubbard models, certifying entanglement in the fractional Chern insulating state. The method extends recently developed scattering-based entanglement witnesses to ultralow-temperature materials, where conventional spectroscopic probes are inaccessible but candidate entangled states are expected to arise.

</details>


### [121] [Non-Equilibrium Instantaneous Approximation and Dipole Forbidden d-d Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16219)
*Marco Marino,Lasse Sternemann,Mirko Cinchetti,Frithjof B. Anders*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于泵浦驱动关联体系的瞬态近似方法（instantaneous approximation），基于格林函数在相对时间与平均时间上的尺度分离，结合一次性Hubbard I级动力学平均场（DMFT）构造，将局域关联壳层的驱动动力学纳入格点格林函数，并推导了名义上偶极禁戒的d-d激发的有效耦合；该方法成功再现了FePS3中时间分辨光电子能谱的主要定性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间与角度分辨光电子能谱可直接探测关联材料中泵浦诱导的电子结构变化，但其理论描述通常需要计算上非常耗时的双时间非平衡计算，这限制了该技术对实验结果的定量解释与预测能力。

Method: 该方法基于格林函数在相对时间变量上的快速衰减与在平均时间上的缓慢演化之间的分离，引入瞬态近似；结合一次性Hubbard I级别的动力学平均场构造，将局域关联壳层的驱动动力学并入格点格林函数同时保留动量分辨；并利用密度泛函理论推导的紧束缚模型与超胞展开（supercell unfolding）程序，应用于顺磁与反铁磁FePS3。

Result: 对泵浦驱动d-d跃迁的FePS3体系，计算得到的动量分辨谱能够再现近期时间分辨光电子能谱实验中第一和第二d-d跃迁激发后观察到的主要定性特征。

Conclusion: 所提出的高效框架为描述强关联材料在泵浦驱动下的瞬态电子结构与光致响应提供了一条实用途径，避免了昂贵的双时间非平衡计算；同时推导出的d-d激发的有效耦合机制揭示了偶极允许d-p跃迁与p-d杂化组合对光学响应的贡献。

Abstract: Time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy provides direct access to pump-induced changes in the electronic structure of correlated materials, but its theoretical description generally requires computationally demanding two-time non-equilibrium calculations. We introduce an instantaneous approximation for pump-driven correlated systems, based on a separation between the rapid decay of the Green's functions in the relative time variable and their slower evolution in the average time. Combined with a one-shot dynamical mean-field construction at the Hubbard I level, the method incorporates the driven dynamics of the local correlated shell into the lattice Green's function while retaining momentum resolution. We also derive an effective coupling for nominally dipole-forbidden d-d excitations. It arises from a dipole-allowed d-p transition followed by p-d hybridization; beyond the instantaneous limit, the same process produces an energy-dependent vertex correction to the optical response. As a proof of principle, we apply the framework to paramagnetic and antiferromagnetic FePS3, using a density-functional theory derived tight-binding model together with a supercell unfolding procedure. The calculated momentum-resolved spectra reproduce the main qualitative features observed after excitation of the first and second d-d transitions in recent time-resolved photoemission experiments.

</details>


### [122] [Non-invertible Lattice 1-Form Symmetries for Non-Abelian Topological Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16520)
*Rafael Flores-Calderón,Frank Pollmann,Michael Knap*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究在有限非阿贝尔群的量子双元格模型（quantum double lattice models）中精确构造了电、磁和双木一号形式对称算子，展示了它们构成完整的非局域诊断代数，并将基态确立为非可逆一号形式对称性自发破缺的微观实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阿贝尔拓扑相可由形成群结构的一号形式对称性（1-form symmetry）组织，而基于有限群的非阿贝尔拓扑相则需要由不可逆融合代数（non-invertible fusion algebra）支配的一号形式对称性算子来描述，这一说法此前缺乏精确的微观构造与验证。

Method: 在有限非阿贝尔群G的量子双元格模型D(G)中，于晶格不动点（lattice fixed point）处直接构造电场、磁场及双木（dyonic）一号形式算子，并系统计算其融合（fusion）与粘接（gluing）性质，再投影到无缺陷拓扑子空间（defect-free topological subspace）以提取拓扑变形特性。

Result: 构造的一号形式算子共同形成拓扑希尔伯特空间（topological Hilbert space）的完整非局域诊断代数；利用这些算子可明确确定任意有限群G下的柱面与环面基态子空间（ground-state subspaces）；微观融合与粘接性质经投影后显现出拓扑变形规律。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔量子双元模型的基态是可逆非一号形式对称性自发破缺（spontaneous non-invertible 1-form symmetry breaking）的具体微观实现，所提供的算子语言可用于在量子处理器上刻画此类拓扑态。

Abstract: Higher-form symmetries generalize conventional global symmetries and act on lower-dimensional submanifolds of a quantum system. While Abelian topological phases can be organized by 1-form symmetries that form a group, non-Abelian topological phases based on finite groups require 1-form symmetry operators governed by non-invertible fusion algebras. In this work, we make this statement precise in quantum double lattice models $\mathcal D(G)$ for finite non-Abelian groups $G$. We construct the electric, magnetic, and dyonic 1-form operators directly at the lattice fixed point and show that together they form a complete nonlocal diagnostic algebra for the topological Hilbert space. Using these operators, we explicitly determine the cylinder and torus ground-state subspaces for arbitrary finite $G$. Furthermore, we calculate the microscopic fusion and gluing of the 1-form symmetries and show that their topological deformation properties emerge after projection to the defect-free topological subspace. Our results establish ground states of non-Abelian quantum double models as a concrete microscopic realization of spontaneous non-invertible 1-form symmetry breaking and provide an operator language that may be useful for characterizing such states in quantum processors.

</details>


### [123] [Scarred discrete time crystal in a periodically driven dimerized spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16616)
*Davood Marripour,Saeed S. Jahromi,Jahanfar Abouie*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过数值研究周期性驱动的二聚化自旋链，证明了系统中量子多体疤痕（QMBS）诱导弱遍历性破缺，从而涌现出疤痕离散时间晶体（SDTC）相，并表明该相是长寿命亚稳态动力学相而非严格稳定的热力学相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在周期驱动的相互作用Floquet系统中，本征态热化假设（ETH）预期系统会热化，但作者希望探究是否存在例外情形——即量子多体疤痕能否诱导弱遍历性破缺，并进一步驱动时间晶体相的出现。

Method: 采用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）方法，对周期驱动的二聚化自旋链进行数值分析，包括Floquet能级统计、纠缠熵和本征态保真度等诊断量，并对多种初始态构型进行测试与有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 识别出嵌入热谱中的低纠缠态流形（具有半泊松统计特征），这些疤痕态支持周期加倍的鲁棒次谐波振荡；SDTC响应在多种初始态下保持稳定，且有限尺寸标度表明时间晶体寿命随系统尺寸增长，但预计在超出数值可达的更大系统尺寸下因疤痕-热连续谱杂化而趋于饱和。

Conclusion: SDTC应被刻画为长寿命的亚稳态动力学体系而非严格稳定的热力学相，该工作为理解无无序体系中周期性驱动与约束多体动力学之间的相互作用提供了综合框架。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of a scarred discrete time crystal (SDTC) phase in a periodically driven dimerized spin chain. While generic interacting Floquet systems are expected to thermalize according to the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH), we demonstrate that this system hosts quantum many-body scars (QMBS) that induce a regime of weak ergodicity breaking. Through an analysis of Floquet level statistics, entanglement entropy, and eigenstate fidelity, we identify a manifold of low-entanglement states characterized by semi-Poisson statistics embedded within an otherwise thermal spectrum. These scarred states support robust subharmonic oscillations with period doubling, signaling the spontaneous breaking of discrete time-translation symmetry. We show that the SDTC response is robust against a variety of initial state configurations, demonstrating its stability beyond fine-tuned conditions. A finite-size scaling analysis reveals that the time-crystalline lifetime grows with system size within the range accessible to our exact-diagonalization calculations. However, drawing on the general phenomenology of approximate many-body scars, we expect that hybridization between Floquet scars and the thermal continuum will eventually curtail this growth, causing the lifetime to saturate at system sizes beyond our current numerical reach. This characterizes the SDTC as a long-lived metastable dynamical regime rather than a strictly stable thermodynamic phase, providing a comprehensive framework for understanding the interplay between periodic driving and constrained many-body dynamics in disorder-free systems.

</details>


### [124] [Resolving the Magnetic Ground State and Field-Induced Transitions in Magnetic Dirac Semimetal Candidate EuMnSb$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16724)
*Yiu-Fung Chiu,Jian-Rui Soh,Sanjay Sharma,J. Alberto Rodríguez-Velamazán,John Singleton,Eugen Weschke,Oleksandr Prokhnenko,Oksana Zaharko,Dharmalingam Prabhakaran,Stephen J. Blundell,Paul A. Goddard,Andrew T. Boothroyd*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用极化与非极化中子衍射、脉冲场X射线磁圆二色及脉冲场磁强计，在高达30 T的磁场下研究了磁性拓扑半金属（magnetic topological semimetal）EuMnSb₂的磁结构，确定了零场下Eu与Mn子晶格的磁序，并通过平均场模型估算了主导的Eu–Eu及Eu–Mn交换相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性拓扑半金属中磁结构与电子拓扑性质的耦合机制尚不清晰，理解其在强磁场下的磁行为对于揭示磁性与电子拓扑之间的相互作用至关重要。

Method: 采用极化与非极化中子衍射、脉冲场X射线磁圆二色（x-ray magnetic circular dichroism）以及脉冲场磁强计等多种实验手段，在最高30 T的磁场条件下系统表征EuMnSb₂的磁结构；并结合平均场模型（mean-field model）描述关键磁行为。

Result: 确定了零场下Eu和Mn子晶格的磁结构；发现低于2 T的磁场诱导磁相变仅对应Eu自旋磁序的改变，Mn自旋磁序未受可探测扰动；在接近Eu自旋饱和场处观察到额外的磁相变。

Conclusion: 平均场模型能描述磁场下磁行为的关键特征，并据此估算出主导Eu–Eu及Eu–Mn交换相互作用的大小，这些相互作用是磁性与电子拓扑性质耦合的关键。

Abstract: The magnetic structure of a magnetic topological semimetal EuMnSb$_2$ is investigated in fields up to 30 T using polarized and unpolarized neutron diffraction, pulsed-field x-ray magnetic circular dichroism and pulsed-field magnetometry. We determine the zero-field magnetic structures of the Eu and Mn sublattices, and find that magnetic transitions induced by applied fields below 2 T correspond to changes in the magnetic order of the Eu spins alone without detectable perturbation to the order of the Mn spins. An additional magnetic transition is observed at fields close to the saturation field for the Eu spins. We present a mean-field model which describes key features of the magnetic behavior and allows us to estimate the dominant Eu--Eu and Eu--Mn exchange interactions responsible for the coupling between magnetism and electronic topology.

</details>
