### [125] [Floquet-Liouville Theory for Strongly Driven Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14966)
*Kamran Akbari,Stephen Hughes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非平庸的Floquet–Markov广义主方程（F-GME），在准能量（quasienergy）基下统一处理相互作用诱导与驱动诱导的非微扰修饰，并证明其与时间无关的修饰基主方程在稳态布居和发射谱上可产生定性差异，揭示了时间无关耗散描述在多光子Floquet跃迁中的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动量子系统通常假设环境耗散耦合到静态能量跃迁，但该近似在强驱动或非共振情形下的有效性尚不清楚。为获得更自洽的量子理论方法，需要一种能平等处理内部与外部非微扰修饰的耗散描述框架。

Method: 在准能量基下构建非平庸的Floquet–Markov广义主方程（F-GME），将相互作用诱导（内部）与驱动诱导（外部）的非微扰修饰置于同等地位。随后将其应用于两个谐波驱动的开放量子系统（二能级系统及耦合二能级系统），并与时间无关的修饰基主方程进行比较。

Result: 即使对于平坦的谱密度（flat spectral density）和弱耗散场景，F-GME与时间无关的修饰基主方程仍可给出定性不同的稳态布居和发射谱。通过对耗散进行分解，发现驱动辅助边带过程经Floquet扩展空间准能量通道衰减，且这些通道可通过非平庸耦合杂化为集体Floquet–Liouville模式，主导可观测的谱共振。

Conclusion: 时间无关的耗散描述会错误地将准能量分辨的衰减途径压缩为静态能隙，从而对多光子Floquet跃迁赋予不正确的权重。F-GME框架为识别需要Floquet一致耗散的区域提供了系统性诊断工具，并阐明不同常用主方程方法之间差异的物理根源。

Abstract: Periodically driven quantum systems are commonly modeled using master equations constructed in the eigenbasis of an undriven Hamiltonian, implicitly assuming that environmental dissipation couples to static energy transitions even under strong time-periodic driving. The validity of this approximation beyond weak or near-resonant driving remains poorly understood. To address the need for a more self-consistent quantum theory approach, we formulate a nonsecular Floquet--Markov generalized master equation (F-GME) in the quasienergy basis, treating interaction-induced (internal) and drive-induced (external) nonperturbative dressing on an equal footing. We subsequently investigate dissipation in two minimal driven open quantum systems---a harmonically driven two-level system and a harmonically driven coupled-two-level-system---each weakly coupled to a Markovian bath. Comparing the F-GME to a time-independent dressed-basis master equation, we show that even for a flat-bath spectral density and weak dissipation, the two approaches can yield qualitatively different steady-state populations and emission spectra. We resolve dissipation into drive-assisted sideband processes decaying via Floquet extended-space quasienergy channels, and show these channels can hybridize through nonsecular couplings into collective Floquet--Liouville modes governing observable spectral resonances. This analysis demonstrates that time-independent dissipative descriptions can incorrectly weight multiphoton Floquet transitions by collapsing quasienergy-resolved decay pathways into static energy gaps. The F-GME framework provides a systematic diagnostic for identifying regimes where Floquet-consistent dissipation is essential and clarifies the physical origin of discrepancies between commonly used master-equation approaches.

</details>


### [126] [An error-mitigated quantum annealing solution for the weighted Max-Cut problem on a cubic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15094)
*Y. S. Yang,P. Tyson,A. B Murphy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究加权Max-Cut问题在113节点、混合符号随机边权的三次元晶格（cubic lattice）上的求解，提出一种新的误差缓解量子退火方法SEMO（spin-error mitigation for optimisation），并定量验证其在QPU可嵌入的混合符号边权实例上获得比标准D-Wave QA、BQM混合求解器、模拟退火和禁忌搜索更短的耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权Max-Cut问题是典型的NP-hard问题，具有实际应用价值；同时量子退火（quantum annealing）在离散优化中的效率受噪声和误差限制，需要探索误差缓解策略以提升求解性能和时间优势，特别是在时间敏感的优化场景中。

Method: 在113节点的三次元晶格上构造混合符号随机边权的加权Max-Cut实例，并设定边权上界固定、下界变化以考察计算难度变化。提出基于自旋误差缓解的量子退火方法SEMO，并将其与标准D-Wave量子退火、D-Wave BQM混合求解器、模拟退火（simulated annealing）及禁忌搜索（Tabu search）等基线进行求解时间对比。

Result: 定量结果表明，在固定边权上界条件下，边权下界越负，问题计算难度越大。在QPU可嵌入的混合符号边权Max-Cut实例中，SEMO误差缓解量子退火在求解时间上显著优于标准D-Wave QA、D-Wave BQM、模拟退火和禁忌搜索基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的误差缓解量子退火方法有望提升量子退火的求解效率和适用范围，可推广至其他可转换为QUBO或Ising形式的离散优化问题，其求解时间优势对时间关键型优化应用具有重要影响。

Abstract: The weighted Max-Cut problem is an NP-hard problem with application implications. It is investigated on a cubic lattice with 113 nodes and mixed-signed random edge weights. For a fixed upper bound on edge weights, it has been demonstrated that the computational difficulty increases as the lower bound on edge weights becomes more negative. The solution time for the problem using a novel error-mitigated quantum annealing approach is compared with standard D-Wave quantum annealing (QA) and BQM hybrid solvers, as well as various classical solvers. For the QPU-embeddable weighted Max-Cut instances with mixed-signed edge weights, it has been quantitatively demonstrated that the SEMO (spin-error mitigation for optimisation) error-mitigated quantum annealing achieved substantially shorter time-to solution than standard D-Wave QA, D-Wave BQM, simulated annealing and Tabu search baselines. The error-mitigated quantum annealing approach presented in this article potentially elevates the efficiency and application scope of quantum annealing and would be applicable in solving other discrete optimisation problems that can be formulated as QUBO or Ising instances. The promising solution time advantage would be particularly impactful for time-critical optimisation applications.

</details>


### [127] [On the zeros of the stellar representation in the discrete cylinder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15237)
*Nicolas Fabre*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在离散圆柱相空间（discrete cylinder phase space）中构建了基于零点数目的态星形分布层级（number-of-zeros hierarchy），发现该层级与量子计算实用性相关：轨道角动量（OAM）本征态位于层级底部而包裹相干态（wrapped coherent state）携带无穷多个零点，表明"相干态"在不同相空间几何中并不必然意味着经典性。通过L^r范数、逆参与比（inverse participation ratio）和Wehrl熵等扩展性测度，发现零点层级与局域化层级并不完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散圆柱相空间为扭曲光子（twisted photons）、约瑟夫森结（Josephson junctions）及某些玻色子码（bosonic codes）提供了自然物理背景，在此背景下理解态的量子计算有用性与结构特性具有重要性。作者旨在建立该系统下态星形分布的零点层级，并考察其与量子计算实用性、态局域化特性之间的关系，进而揭示不同相空间几何中"相干态"概念的深层结构差异。

Method: 作者在离散圆柱相空间上对态星形分布（stellar distribution）构建基于零点数目的层级结构，通过分析态密度的零点秩（rank）来分类量子态。利用L^r范数、逆参与比和Wehrl熵三种互补的扩展性（spread）测度，在Husimi密度（Husimi density）框架下比较各层级态的局域化程度，并识别保持或改变零点数目的操作。

Result: 结果表明，轨道角动量本征态和相位本征态（phase eigenstate）无零点，位于层级底部，与平面相空间中的相干态在零点秩为零和非负Wigner分布方面具有数学相似性。然而圆柱的相干态（包裹相干态）却携带无穷多个零点，是量子性最强的态。通过L^r范数、逆参与比和Wehrl熵测量，发现零点层级与局域化层级并非总是对应：包裹相干态虽然具有无穷多零点，却可能比OAM本征态更局域化。

Conclusion: 离散圆柱相空间中"相干态"与经典性之间的传统关联被打破，说明"相干态"概念的语义在不同相空间几何下需要重新审慎理解。零点层级反映了态在量子计算中的潜在有用性排序，但不一定等同于其在Husimi密度下的空间局域化程度，两种层级结构需作为互补的刻画工具使用。

Abstract: The discrete cylinder phase space offers a natural setting for twisted photons, Josephson junctions, and certain bosonic codes. In that context, we build a number-of-zeros hierarchy for the stellar distribution of states on the discrete cylinder phase space, tied to usefulness for quantum computation. The orbital angular momentum (OAM) and phase eigenstate, having no zeros, sits at the bottom of the hierarchy and share mathematical properties with the coherent state on the plane phase space, both sharing zero rank and a positive Wigner distribution. This reveals a structural peculiarity of the cylinder: its coherent state---the wrapped coherent state---is instead the most quantum, carrying infinitely many zeros, so "coherent state" need not signal classicality across different phase-space geometries. We identify the operations that preserve or change the zero count, giving explicit access to every rank of the hierarchy. We then ask whether this hierarchy also governs localization. Using the $L^r$ norm, the inverse participation ratio, and the Wehrl entropy as complementary measures of spread in the Husimi density, we find that the two hierarchies need not track one another: the wrapped coherent state can be more localized than the OAM eigenstate even though it carries infinitely many zeros.

</details>


### [128] [A minimal qutrit counterexample to Conjecture 4.9 of Lesniewski and Ruskai](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15464)
*Domingos S. P. Salazar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推翻了Lesniewski和Ruskai关于单调黎曼度量（monotone Riemannian metric）在单位随机映射（unital stochastic map）下收缩系数等于无迹子空间上Hilbert–Schmidt收缩的猜想，通过一个由双随机3×3矩阵诱导的纠缠破缺qutrit通道给出反例，同时所有归一化单调度量的精确下界为8896/20007。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在验证或反驳Lesniewski和Ruskai提出的猜想，即单位随机映射下单调黎曼度量的收缩系数与无迹子空间上的Hilbert–Schmidt收缩系数相等。

Method: 构造了一个由双随机3×3矩阵诱导的纠缠破缺qutrit通道，选择一个忠实对角态（faithful diagonal state）和交换的无迹切向量，同时对每个归一化单调度量计算精确下界。

Result: 该反例对所有归一化单调度量给出精确下界η^{Riem}_κ(Φ_K) ≥ 8896/20007 > (62+2√61)/225 = Λ₂(Φ_K†Φ_K)，表明猜想在维度三及以上不成立。反例在最大交换子代数上是完全经典的。另一方面，Hiai和Ruskai的定理确立了该猜想对所有单位qubit映射成立，因此矩阵全代数的极小反例维度为三。

Conclusion: Lesniewski–Ruskai猜想在维度三及以上被否定，维度二是猜想的成立上限，这表明维度三是该猜想成立的最小全矩阵代数维度。

Abstract: Lesniewski and Ruskai conjectured that the contraction coefficient of every monotone Riemannian metric under a unital stochastic map equals the Hilbert--Schmidt contraction on the traceless subspace. We disprove the conjecture with an explicit entanglement-breaking qutrit channel induced by a doubly stochastic $3\times3$ matrix. A faithful diagonal state and a commuting traceless tangent give, simultaneously for every normalized monotone metric, the exact lower bound $η^{\mathrm{Riem}}_κ(Φ_K)\ge 8896/20007>(62+2\sqrt{61})/225=Λ_2(Φ_K^{\dagger}Φ_K)$. The counterexample is entirely classical on a maximal abelian subalgebra. A theorem of Hiai and Ruskai establishes the conjectured identity for all unital qubit maps, so dimension three is minimal among full matrix algebras.

</details>


### [129] [Local observable errors from truncating interaction tails in gapped quantum lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15576)
*Kangle Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对有能隙的量子格点哈密顿量在截断空间尾部时引起的局域可观测量基态期望值误差进行了严格的界估计。研究发现该误差受每点附近被丢弃的相互作用强度控制，而非被省略哈密顿量的整体范数；在能隙保持开放的条件下，得到的误差界对系统尺寸一致成立，并可推广到热力学极限和孤立低能区的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值模拟中常用有限范围截断来近似长程相互作用系统，但截断误差如何定量控制尚不清楚。关键在于理解截断对基态局域物理性质的影响，以及误差随截断范围衰减的速率，从而判断有限范围截断何时能忠实复现能隙长程系统的局域物理。

Method: 通过对被丢弃的相互作用尾部进行逐点（per-site）强度控制而非整体范数控制，建立截断误差的严格上界。利用能隙保持开放的条件推导系统尺寸无关的均匀界，并借助自同构等价（automorphic equivalence）方法获得超多项式衰减相互作用的直接无限体积估计。对于 d 维空间中衰减为 r^{-p} 的两体相互作用，推导出误差界 O(R^{-(p-d)})，其中 R 为截断范围。

Result: 证明了误差收敛速率与相互作用尾部的衰减方式一致：幂律相互作用对应代数衰减，超多项式相互作用对应超多项式衰减，指数衰减相互作用对应任何严格更小速率的指数衰减。在 d 维空间中，对衰减为 r^{-p}（p>2d）的两体相互作用，得到了 O(R^{-(p-d)}) 的误差界，并通过构造一个有能隙的非平移不变实例证明了该标度是最优的。

Conclusion: 有限范围截断在能隙长程系统中可以忠实复现局域物理，前提是截断过程中能隙保持开放。该结果量化了截断误差与相互作用衰减速率之间的关系，为数值模拟中长程相互作用系统的有限截断提供了严格的理论依据。

Abstract: We bound the error in ground-state expectations of local observables caused by truncating the spatial tails of a gapped quantum lattice Hamiltonian. We show that the error is controlled by the interaction strength discarded near each site, rather than by the extensive norm of the omitted Hamiltonian. If the gap remains open along a path that removes the interaction tail, the resulting bounds are uniform in system size and extend, under suitable assumptions, to thermodynamic-limit ground states. The convergence rate reflects the decay of the interaction tail: it is algebraic for power-law interactions, superpolynomial for superpolynomial interactions, and exponential at any strictly smaller rate for exponentially decaying interactions. For superpolynomial interactions, we also derive a direct infinite-volume estimate using automorphic equivalence. The results extend to isolated low-energy sectors and parity-even fermionic systems. For two-body interactions decaying as $r^{-p}$ in $d$ dimensions, we prove an error bound $O(R^{-(p-d)})$ for $p>2d$, where $R$ is the truncation range. We construct a gapped non-translation-invariant example that saturates this scaling, showing that the bound is optimal for the general class considered. Our results quantify when finite-range truncations faithfully reproduce the local physics of gapped long-range systems.

</details>


### [130] [Geodesic Quantum $f$-Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15833)
*Ángela Capel,Pablo Costa Rico*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入测地线量子f-散度（geodesic quantum f-divergence）$D_f^t$，由标准与极大相对模算子之间的仿射不变测地线构造而来。文中证明了对所有有限算子凸生成元的量子数据处理不等式（data processing inequality, DPI），刻画了等式成立的充分性类（Petz类与BS类），证明了测地线量子马尔可夫链的坍缩现象，并给出了参数单调性、连续性界及每单位成本容量等额外结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息论中已有多种散度（如Umegaki相对熵、Belavkin-Staszewski相对熵、Petz-Rényi散度等），但缺乏统一的测地线框架来连接这些对象。本文旨在通过仿射不变测地线构造一类统一的量子f-散度，使其在极限情形恢复已知的重要散度族，并系统性地研究其数据处理不等式与等式刻画。

Method: 在标准相对模算子与极大相对模算子之间构建仿射不变测地线，将其代入f-散度生成元构造出参数化散度族$D_f^t$。通过对数生成元和对数生成元分别获得测地线相对熵和$(t,\alpha)$-Rényi散度。文中利用算子凸函数理论与量子信息处理方法证明DPI及其等式条件，并通过有序条件互信息构造分析测地线量子马尔可夫链的坍缩。

Result: 主要结果有三：其一，对所有有限算子凸生成元和所有$t\in[0,1]$，$D_f^t$满足数据处理不等式（DPI），并由此导出测地线相对熵及$(t,\alpha)$-Rényi散度在$0<\alpha<1$或$1<\alpha\leq2$范围内的DPI。其二，在可逆态下，等式成立时的生成元在$0\leq t<1$恰好刻画Petz充分性类，而在$t=1$时变为一般更大的极大（BS）类。其三，可逆测地线量子马尔可夫链在$0\leq t<1$时等价于普通量子马尔可夫链，而在$t=1$时为可逆BS量子马尔可夫链。此外还确定了参数单调性区间、与$(\alpha,z)$族的交集、连续性界及每单位成本容量解释。

Conclusion: 测地线量子f-散度族为量子信息量提供了统一的几何框架，在$t=1$处呈现相位过渡，使得充分性类与马尔可夫链结构均发生显著扩张。这一框架不仅统一了Petz与BS两类散度族，还揭示了参数$t$对量子信息处理性质（如数据压缩与信道容量）的定量影响。

Abstract: We introduce the geodesic quantum $f$-divergences $D_f^t$, $0\leq t\leq1$, obtained from the affine-invariant geodesic between the standard and maximal relative modular operators. They reduce to the classical $f$-divergence for commuting states. The logarithmic generator yields geodesic relative entropies joining the Umegaki and Belavkin-Staszewski entropies, while the power generators yield $(t,α)$-Rényi divergences joining the Petz and geometric families.
  Our first main result is data processing of $D_f^t$ for every finite operator-convex generator $f$ and every $t\in[0,1]$. In particular, this gives DPI for the geodesic relative entropies and for the $(t,α)$-Rényi divergences when $0<α<1$ or $1<α\leq2$. Our second main result identifies equality in the DPI: for invertible states, every equality-determining operator-convex generator has, at each nonmaximal parameter $0\leq t<1$, exactly the Petz sufficiency class; at $t=1$, this changes to the generally larger maximal, or BS, class, which also coincides with the equality class of the divergences associated with the quadratic generator for every $t$. Our third main result is the corresponding collapse of invertible geodesic quantum Markov chains: for each of the three ordered conditional-mutual-information constructions, the $t$-quantum Markov chains are precisely the quantum Markov chains for $0\leq t<1$, whereas at $t=1$ they are the invertible BS quantum Markov chains, a class that can be strictly larger.
  We also determine parameter-monotonicity regimes and the intersections with the $(α,z)$ family. We prove strengthened data-processing and reconstruction estimates; we compare the three conditional orientations; we establish continuity bounds under positive lower-eigenvalue assumptions together with complementary discontinuity results; and we give a capacity-per-unit-cost interpretation.

</details>


### [131] [Information Geometry of the Geodesic Quantum $f$-Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15916)
*Ángela Capel,Pablo Costa Rico*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由测地线量子f散度（geodesic quantum f-divergences）引入的微分、统计与几何结构，通过内插相对模算子（relative modular operator）与交换子Radon-Nikodym导数构造新的量子信息度量，并与Petz-Hasegawa度量进行比较，同时利用Busemann函数为统计框架赋予正算子锥中的几何意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在系统性地分析测地线量子f散度在微分几何、统计推断与几何结构三方面产生的后果，探索插值参数t在新几何中的物理与数学含义，并建立其与已有量子信息度量之间的联系。

Method: 对于可逆态ρ与算子凸函数f，计算测地线量子f散度的Hessian并得到诱导单调量子信息度量g_{ρ,t}^{(f)}的显式公式；通过内插相对模算子Γ_t在态σ的参考纯化中定义正则有限二元实验(p_t,q_t)，构造对数似然累积函数Ψ_{ρ,σ}(t,s)；最后利用Busemann函数在正定算子锥中赋予该统计框架几何意义。

Result: 给出了诱导单调量子信息度量的显式公式；将该度量与Petz-Hasegawa度量进行了比较，并揭示了插值参数t在新几何中的含义；在t=0时恢复Nussbaum-Szkoła分布，在t=1时恢复Matsumoto构造。

Conclusion: 测地线量子f散度统一了两种已知的量子信息构造（Nussbaum-Szkoła与Matsumoto），并借助Busemann函数建立了统计框架与正算子锥几何之间的内在联系，为量子信息几何提供了新的统一视角。

Abstract: We study the differential, statistical, and geometrical consequences generated by the geodesic quantum $f$-divergences introduced in [14], which are constructed by interpolating the relative modular operator and the commutant Radon-Nikodym derivative using a geodesic with parameter $t\in [0,1]$. For an invertible state $ρ$ and an operator convex function $f$, we compute the Hessian and obtain an explicit formula for the induced monotone quantum information metric $g_{ρ,t}^{(f)}$. Furthermore, we also compare these metrics with the Petz-Hasegawa metric and find the meaning of the interpolation parameter $t$ in this new geometry. We next show that the interpolation of relative modular operators $Γ_t$ in the reference purification of a state $σ$ defines a canonical finite binary experiment $(p_t,q_t)$, and introduce a log-likelihood cumulant function $Ψ_{ρ,σ}(t,s)$, recovering the Nussbaum-Szkoła distributions at $t=0$ and the Matsumoto construction at $t=1$. Finally, using Busemann functions, we endow this statistical framework with a geometric meaning in the cone of positive operators.

</details>


### [132] [Universal Frame Potential Hierarchy in Critical Projected Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15467)
*Hui-Huang Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Tomonaga-Luttinger液体在平衡量子临界点处的投影系综（projected ensemble）结构，发现其态重叠矩（state overlap moments）呈现普适的非线性层次结构，且主要标度指数不依赖于连续变化的Luttinger参数，该普适性源于复制边界共形场论（replica boundary conformal field theory）中结果锁定（outcome locking）机制与复制膜（replica branes）几何约束的组合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域测量将多体波函数转化为条件量子态的统计系综，在混沌系统中这些系综的高阶矩可用于诊断量子态随机性的出现，但它们在平衡量子临界点处的结构尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者利用复制边界共形场论分析Tomonaga-Luttinger液体的投影系综态重叠矩；通过结果锁定机制将活跃的复制交换（replica swaps）合并为单个集体旋转扇区，并利用相交的紧致复制膜消除领先阶相互作用依赖的零模贡献；同时采用矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）计算跨越相互作用的XXZ临界相，以及自由费米子直接计算作为独立验证。

Result: Tomonaga-Luttinger液体的投影系综展现出态重叠矩的普适非线性层次结构，其领先标度指数与连续变化的Luttinger参数无关；矩阵乘积态计算与自由费米子直接计算均独立确认了相互作用的独立性和非线性层次结构。

Conclusion: 量子临界点处的投影系综存在超越具体哈密顿量参数的普适非线性矩结构，其标度行为由几何机制（复制交换的集体旋转与复制膜的相交）决定，而非由相互作用强度决定。

Abstract: Local measurements transform a many body wavefunction into a statistical ensemble of conditional quantum states. In chaotic systems, the higher moments of such ensembles diagnose the emergence of quantum state randomness, but their structure at equilibrium quantum criticality is largely unknown. Here we show that the projected ensemble of a Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid exhibits a universal nonlinear hierarchy of state overlap moments. Remarkably, the leading scaling exponents are independent of the continuously varying Luttinger parameter. Replica boundary conformal field theory reveals a geometric origin: outcome locking combines the active replica swaps into a single collective rotated sector, while intersecting compact replica branes eliminate the leading interaction dependent zero mode contribution. Matrix product state calculations across the interacting XXZ critical phase and direct free fermion calculations independently confirm the interaction independence and nonlinear hierarchy.

</details>


### [133] [Imaginary Barrier, Real Transfer: Non-Hermitian Dynamical Tunnelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16545)
*Eva-Maria Graefe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了一种非厄米（non-Hermitian）动力隧穿（dynamical tunnelling）现象，其由对称约束势中的局域吸收区域（由虚数势垒描述）诱发，产生有效双阱结构。与实数势垒导致的周期性隧穿振荡不同，该系统缓慢演化至两侧粒子数相等，而吸收区域仅保持弱占据。作者在两种连续势中验证了这一通用机制，通过一个最小3×3矩阵模型捕捉其解析本质，并提出一个现实的光波导（optical waveguide）实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力隧穿研究聚焦于实数势垒下的周期振荡行为，而非厄米系统（如含吸收区域的系统）中的隧穿动力学尚未被充分理解。作者旨在识别并刻画由局域吸收区域诱导的非厄米隧穿现象，为其提供理论框架与实验验证途径。

Method: 作者在对称约束势中引入由虚数势垒描述的局域吸收区域，从而构造有效双阱结构。通过分析两种连续势中的系统演化，并结合一个最小3×3矩阵模型获取解析理解，最终提出一个基于光波导的实验方案用于观测该效应。

Result: 研究发现在非厄米系统中，吸收区域诱导的隧穿不存在周期振荡，而是系统缓慢趋于两侧粒子数相等的状态，同时吸收区域保持低占据。该通用机制在两种连续势中得到验证，其本质可通过3×3矩阵模型解析刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了由局域吸收区域诱发的非厄米动力隧穿作为一种通用机制，并提供了最小矩阵模型的解析框架及光波导实验方案，可为后续实验验证和非厄米隧穿动力学研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We identify a non-Hermitian counterpart of dynamical tunnelling induced by a localised absorbing region in a symmetric confining potential. The absorbing region, described by an imaginary potential barrier, induces an effective double-well structure with a pair of long-lived states that have little population in the barrier. In contrast to the periodic tunnel oscillations through a real barrier, the system slowly evolves towards equal population of the two sides, while the absorbing region remains only weakly populated. We demonstrate this generic mechanism in two continuous potentials, and capture its essence analytically in a minimal $3\times 3$ matrix model. We propose a realistic optical waveguide setup for the experimental observation of the effect.

</details>


### [134] [Quantum Permutations and Beyond Quantum Controlled Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16532)
*Ofek Bengyat,Časlav Brukner,Marios Christodoulou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子排列（quantum permutations，又称魔幻酉算符）从图论等距变换研究引入物理学，提出其在离散背景下推广量子参考系框架（quantum reference frames）的新视角，类似从狭义相对论到广义相对论的过渡。研究发现真正量子排列允许构建非对易量子参考系并对应局域变换叠加，且量子场的非对易性正是实现真正量子排列换帧的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年在图的自同构等距研究中，量子排列被用于识别"真正量子"特征，但尚未被引入物理学。作者希望借此工具为量子参考系框架提供更广泛的推广，并探索量子重力（quantum gravity）等领域的潜在应用。

Method: 作者将标准量子参考系框架映射为数学文献中"经典"类的量子排列，证明其等价于量子受控变换（超位置的经典坐标映射）；进一步分析真正量子排列的性质，证明其构成非对易参考系与局域变换叠加。在首次量子化和第二次量子化形式中给出多个实例：非对易变量的同步控制、双粒子态的局域化对比、伊辛模型对称性扩展及弯曲时空中标量场作用的对称性扩展。

Result: 发现量子场作为参考系统时，其非对易性（non-commutativity）恰好蕴含换帧须通过真正量子排列实现。实例表明真正量子排列可同时控制非对易变量，且存在可用真正量子排列局域化、但常规量子参考系变换（在无需引入额外自由度条件下）无法局域化的双粒子态；伊辛模型和弯曲时空中标量场作用的对称性均可扩展到真正量子排列。

Conclusion: 量子排列为量子参考系框架提供一种类比狭义相对论到广义相对论演变的推广，既适用于量子重力相关应用，也可广泛应用于量子信息领域的多种课题。作者期望该形式体系能在量子信息范围内产生广泛兴趣。

Abstract: Quantum permutations, or magic unitaries, have in recent years been explored in the context of identifying `genuinely quantum' isometries of graphs. Here, we import this tool in physics, showing that quantum permutations yield a generalisation of quantum reference frames in a discrete setting that is reminiscent of the passage from special to general relativity. We show that the typical quantum reference frames framework corresponds to quantum permutations classified as `classical' in the mathematical literature, and which we demonstrate are quantum controlled transformations (superpositions of classical coordinate maps). Genuinely quantum permutations (i) allow to construct \emph{non--commuting} quantum reference frames (ii) correspond to \emph{local}, as opposed to global, superpositions of transformations. Strikingly, we find that the non-commutativity of quantum fields, when used as reference systems, is exactly what implies that the change of frame is achieved through a genuine quantum permutation. We illustrate the above with several examples in both first and second quantization formalism, which demonstrate (a) simultaneous control on non--commuting variables, (b) the existence of bipartite states that can be localized with a genuine quantum permutation and cannot be localized with the usual quantum reference frame transformations (without introducing additional degrees of freedom), (c) extension of the Ising model symmetries to genuinely quantum permutations, and (d) extension of the symmetries of a scalar field action on curved spacetime to genuinely quantum permutations. While we have in mind applications in quantum gravity, we expect our formalism to be of interest in a wide range of topics in quantum information.

</details>


### [135] [Superposition of dynamics, indefinite causal order, and quantum histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16694)
*Charis Anastopoulos,Ntina Savvidou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在历史理论（histories theory）框架下探讨不定因果序（indefinite causal order）的物理意义，论证进程矩阵（process matrix）形式中的不定因果序可被识别为动力学叠加（superposition of dynamics）这一更一般现象在操作受限条件下的实现，并进一步区分了事件序与干预序两种不同的不定序概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进程矩阵形式描述了不假设固定全局因果序的量子过程，但不定因果序的物理含义至今仍未清晰阐明。作者旨在通过历史理论这一同时区分时间排序与动力学演化的框架，为不定因果序提供更基础的物理解释。

Method: 论文在历史理论框架中分析退相干泛函（decoherence functional），证明其允许动力学的相干叠加；在测量情境下，当满足适当的因子化条件时，这些叠加可生成进程矩阵。同时，历史理论还提供了一种独立的运动学意义上的不定序概念，其中物理事件本身排序成为历史观测量。

Result: 研究表明，进程矩阵中的不定因果序是更普遍的动力学叠加现象在操作受限条件下的具体实现；历史理论中还存在事件排序本身作为观测量的独立的运动学不定序概念。由此区分了事件序（event order）与干预序（intervention order）。

Conclusion: 事件序与干预序的区分有助于厘清不定因果序、量子动力学与时空结构三者之间的关系，为理解不定因果序的物理本质提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The process-matrix formalism describes quantum processes without assuming a fixed global causal order, but the physical meaning of indefinite causal order remains open. We address this question within histories theory, where temporal ordering and dynamical evolution are distinct structures. We show that the decoherence functional admits coherent superpositions of dynamics. In measurement settings, under suitable factorization conditions, these generate process matrices. Thus, we identify process-matrix indefinite causal order as an operationally restricted realization of the more general phenomenon of superposition of dynamics. Histories theory also admits a distinct kinematical notion of indefinite order, in which the ordering of physical events is itself a history observable. The resulting distinction between event order and intervention order clarifies the relation between indefinite causal order, quantum dynamics, and spacetime structure.

</details>


### [136] [Equivalence of quantum resources under ergodic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15197)
*Sreemayee Aditya,Xhek Turkeshi,Piotr Sierant*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究量子资源理论（quantum resource theory）在复杂多体动力学中的演化规律，发现在强混沌（chaotic）动力学下，不同的量子资源度量（如相干性、不对称性、数熵等）不再独立演化，而是由少数共同自由度支配，导致量子资源的动力学等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子资源理论刻画了多体量子态中不同形式的非经典性，但尚不清楚这些资源在一般遍历动力学（ergodic dynamics）下是否独立演化。该问题关系到能否简化对复杂多体量子系统动力学行为的描述。

Method: 作者考虑对态二次型（quadratic in the state）的量子资源诊断量，分析Haar随机电路、混沌Floquet系统以及连续时间Hamiltonian动力学下不同资源度量之间的相互关系，并通过纯度（purity）与单一资源见证（resource witness）重构其余资源度量，同时推导精确的相干性—虚部（coherence–imaginarity）关系。

Result: 在Haar随机电路下，纯度与单一资源见证足以重构其余资源度量，并在相干性、多种不对称性及数熵（number entropies）中得到验证；同样的关系在混沌Floquet与连续时间Hamiltonian动力学下亦以良好近似成立。此外，推导出的相干性—虚部精确关系在Haar随机电路之外也具有显著准确性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，足够强的scrambling效应会使多体量子动力学呈现涌现简化（emergent simplification）：看似截然不同的量子资源被约化为少数共同动力学自由度，为理解多体量子系统的资源演化提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Quantum resource theories characterize distinct forms of nonclassicality in many-body quantum states, raising the question of whether these resources evolve independently under generic ergodic dynamics. Considering diagnostics quadratic in the state, we show that the dynamics of different resource measures become mutually interdependent and are governed by a few common degrees of freedom. For Haar-random circuits, the purity together with a single resource witness suffices to reconstruct the remaining resource measures, as we demonstrate for coherence, various asymmetries, and number entropies. The same relations hold, to a good approximation, under chaotic Floquet and continuous-time Hamiltonian dynamics, establishing that this dynamical resource equivalence extends beyond random circuits. We further derive an exact coherence--imaginarity relation, remarkably accurate also beyond Haar-random circuits. Our results reveal an emergent simplification of many-body dynamics, in which sufficiently strong scrambling reduces seemingly distinct quantum resources to a few common dynamical degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [137] [Transitions to super-radiance in ensembles of incoherently pumped emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15329)
*Laszlo Rassaert,Tommaso Roscilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在退相（dephasing）和个体衰减（individual decay）条件下，发射体（emitters）系综发生超辐射（super-radiance）相变的条件。通过置换对称系综的精确求解，作者验证了截断累积量展开方法的有效性，证明超辐射对退相和非辐射衰减具有显著鲁棒性，并建立了从正常辐射到超辐射转变的普适临界标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超辐射是耗散多体系统中区别于独立发射体行为的一种独特相态，类似铁磁与顺磁的区别。作者旨在回答在有退相和个体衰减的现实条件（尤其针对固态发射体）下，超辐射相变是否仍能发生及其临界性质如何。

Method: 采用置换不变（permutationally invariant）发射体系综的简化假设，精确求解脉冲激发后的耗散动力学和非相干泵浦下的稳态；在此基础上，用精确解作为基准来验证截断累积量展开（truncated cumulant expansion）方法，从而可推广至任意大系统尺度的预测。

Result: 超辐射对可观的退相和非辐射衰减速率具有根本性的鲁棒性，这一特性源于超辐射发射相对个体局部环境耦合的集体加速效应。作者确定了正常辐射与超辐射之间转变的普适临界标度律，并发现超辐射相中在达到渐近标度区域之前存在显著的有限尺寸交叉（finite-size crossover）。

Conclusion: 本研究将超辐射视为非平衡多体相变，为未来实验定量表征超辐射的标度性质铺平了道路，尤其在非相干泵浦发射体系综中具有重要意义。

Abstract: Super-radiance is a striking phenomenon resulting from the collective interaction of emitters with light, and it is fundamentally related to the appearance of long-range correlations between the dipole moments of the emitters. As such, it represents a distinct phase of dissipative many-body systems compared to the case of independent emitters, similarly to how ferromagnetism stands out in magnetic materials in contrast to paramagnetism. In this work we address the conditions under which the transition to super-radiance can occur in ensembles of emitters subject to dephasing and individual decay -- a situation which is particularly relevant to the case of emitters in the solid state. The simplifying assumption of an ensemble of permutationally invariant emitters allows for the efficient solution of both the dissipative dynamics after a pulsed excitation, as well as of the steady state under incoherent pumping. This exact solution allows us to benchmark a truncated cumulant expansion approach, which can give predictions for arbitrarily big system sizes. We show that super-radiance is fundamentally robust to sizable dephasing and non-radiative decay rates, both under a pulsed excitation, as well as under continuous pumping. This robustness is the result of the collective acceleration effect of super-radiant emission with respect to the individual coupling to a local environment. We establish the universal critical scaling laws at the transition between normal radiance and super-radiance; and we show that, in the super-radiant phase, significant finite-size crossovers can be observed before reaching the asymptotic scaling regime. Our results pave the way for future experiments to provide a quantitative characterization of the scaling properties of super-radiance, seen as a distinct non-equilibrium many-body phase in ensembles of incoherently pumped emitters.

</details>


### [138] [Postselection-Free Reconstruction of Monitored SPT Flux-Charge Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15477)
*Shuai Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的受监测拓扑不变量（monitored topological invariants）估计方案：以均匀随机化的对称本征态和离线扭曲解码器（offline twist decoder）替代传统需要指数级配后选择（matched-postselection）的扭曲量子实验，通过有限时间响应恒等式实现多项式可扩展的拓扑相测量，并在受监测簇电路（monitored cluster circuits）中验证了该方法的精确性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统受监测拓扑不变量的直接估计需要将普通与对称扭曲的量子轨迹进行配后选择，其匹配轨道在概率上呈指数级稀薄，导致实验采样成本不可承受。作者旨在消除这一实验采样瓶颈，建立一种可观测、可扩展的拓扑不变量估计框架。

Method: 作者用带已知电荷的均匀随机化对称本征态替换扭曲量子实验，结合普通边界监测与离线扭曲解码器，利用两者的关联构造精确的有限时间响应恒等式。通过分析普通电荷锐化条件与普通到磁通形变（ordinary-to-flux deformation）的净读出特征流，将响应收敛至对称保护拓扑相（symmetry-protected topological phase）的投影对易子；在受监测簇电路中，高斯极限（Gaussian limit）下解码器精确且呈多项式复杂度，高斯掉重建（Gaussian-drop reconstruction）用于处理相互作用记录，并通过联合扇区谱学与效应态诊断加以验证。

Result: 该方法消除了指数级配后选择的采样瓶颈；当电荷锐化且形变具有平凡净读出特征流时，响应收敛到对称保护拓扑相的投影对易子，生成子对响应可确定有限阿贝尔上同调类（finite-Abelian cohomology class）。旁观者扇区跨越可能关闭全局李雅普诺夫间隙（global Lyapunov gap）但不改变该特征值型响应。受监测簇电路实验验证了解码器的精确性、多项式复杂度及对相互作用记录的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种无需指数级采样即可精确估计受监测拓扑不变量的实验方案，将拓扑响应与投影对易子及有限阿贝尔上同调类直接关联，并在簇电路系统中充分验证。该框架为量子监测场景下的对称保护拓扑相的表征提供了可扩展且实验可行的途径。

Abstract: A class of monitored topological invariants compares ordinary and symmetry-twisted quantum trajectories with identical stochastic records, so direct estimation requires exponentially costly matched-record postselection. We replace the twisted quantum experiment by uniformly randomized symmetry eigenstates with known charges, ordinary-boundary monitoring, and an offline twist decoder. Their correlation yields an exact finite-time response identity and removes this experimental sampling bottleneck. When the ordinary charge sharpens and the ordinary-to-flux deformation has trivial net readout-character flow, the response converges to the projective commutator of the symmetry-protected-topological phase; generator-pair responses then determine its finite-Abelian cohomology class. Spectator-sector crossings may close the global Lyapunov gap without changing this character-valued response. In monitored cluster circuits, the decoder is exact and polynomial in the Gaussian limit, while Gaussian-drop reconstruction treats interacting records; independent joint-sector spectroscopy and effect-state diagnostics validate the topological deformation.

</details>


### [139] [Tunable Statistics-Induced Caging in the Anyon-Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15739)
*Zenong Zhou,Chaorong Guo,Hongzheng Wu,Qianglin Hu,Xiaobing Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了四格点plaquette上Anyon-Hubbard模型中两个相互作用任意子的量子动力学，揭示了静态阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆（Aharonov-Bohm）笼禁效应在强相互作用极限下出现但在弱相互作用下失效，并提出通过Floquet工程（Floquet engineering）动态恢复该效应，实现对任意子的选择性笼禁和统计相位的识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索任意子（anyon）量子动力学及其统计相位导致的Aharonov-Bohm笼禁效应在不同相互作用强度下的表现，并寻求在弱相互作用区恢复笼禁效应的机制，为任意子的操控与统计相位识别提供有效手段。

Method: 采用四格点plaquette上的Anyon-Hubbard模型研究两个相互作用任意子，该模型可精确映射为Bose-Hubbard模型；通过Floquet工程引入合成Floquet通量，利用统计因子诱导的动力学机制实现笼禁效应的动态恢复。

Result: 仅在特定统计相位下，静态Aharonov-Bohm笼禁在强相互作用极限中涌现，但在弱相互作用下失效；Floquet工程能够动态恢复统计因子诱导的笼禁效应，并扩展至整个统计相位谱的弱相互作用区；外部驱动还实现了任意子的选择性笼禁。

Conclusion: Floquet工程提供了一种在不依赖强相互作用的前提下恢复任意子笼禁效应的通用动力学机制，为任意子操控和统计相位识别提供了高效途径。

Abstract: We study the quantum dynamics of two interacting anyons in the Anyon-Hubbard model on a four-site plaquette, a system that is exactly mappable to a Bose-Hubbard model. We reveal that static Aharonov-Bohm (AB) caging, induced by only specific statistical phases, emerges in the strongly interacting limit but breaks down under weak interparticle interactions. To address this, we demonstrate that statistical-factor-induced AB caging can be dynamically restored via Floquet engineering. This dynamical mechanism, governed by the synthetic Floquet flux and the anyonic statistical phase, extends the caging effect into the weakly interacting regime across the full spectrum of statistical phases. Furthermore, we show that the external drive enables the selective caging of anyons, providing an efficient approach for manipulating anyons and identifying statistical phases.

</details>


### [140] [Longitudinal-Field-Driven Transition in a non-integrable Non-Hermitian Transverse-Field Ising Chain via RBMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15806)
*M. E. Ateuafack,M. R. Kamsap,J. T. Diffo,L. C. Fai,M. N. Jipdi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个受纵向磁场和复数横向磁场作用的非厄米横向场伊辛链的基态性质与量子临界行为，利用基于受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）的实值神经量子态结合变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）采样求解，并识别出由PT（parity-time）对称性破缺引发的非厄米量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米量子系统（non-Hermitian quantum systems）中的多体基态求解与临界现象研究面临计算挑战，需要开发高效且可扩展的数值方法。作者旨在利用神经量子态（neural quantum states）方法探索此类系统中PT对称性破缺与量子相变的内在机制。

Method: 采用基于受限玻尔兹曼机（Restricted Boltzmann Machines, RBM）的实值神经量子态，结合变分蒙特卡洛（Variational Monte Carlo, VMC）采样对非厄米哈密顿量进行基态求解；利用有限链的谱分析揭示与自发PT对称性破缺相关的例外点（exceptional points），并与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）进行基准对比验证。

Result: RBM方法与精确对角化基准对比表明，该方法能准确重现基态能量、磁化强度和自旋-自旋关联。扩展到更大系统尺寸后，识别出以PT对称性破缺和磁序涌现为特征的非厄米量子相变。

Conclusion: 实值神经量子态被证明是研究相互作用非厄米量子系统中临界现象的高效且可扩展的计算框架，为后续非厄米多体物理的理论与数值研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state properties and quantum critical behavior of a non-Hermitian transverse-field Ising chain subjected to longitudinal and complex transverse magnetic fields. To address this interacting many-body problem, we employ real-valued neural quantum states based on Restricted Boltzmann Machines (RBMs), optimized using Variational Monte Carlo (VMC) sampling. Spectral analysis of finite chains reveals exceptional points associated with spontaneous parity-time (PT) symmetry breaking. A real-valued RBM framework is developed to reconstruct the ground-state eigenstates of the non-Hermitian Hamiltonian. Benchmark comparisons with exact diagonalization demonstrate that the RBM approach accurately reproduces the ground-state energy, magnetization, and spin-spin correlations. Extending the analysis to larger system sizes, we identify a non-Hermitian quantum phase transition characterized by PT-symmetry breaking and the emergence of magnetic order. Our results establish real-valued neural quantum states as an efficient and scalable framework for investigating critical phenomena in interacting non-Hermitian quantum systems.

</details>


### [141] [Topologically Protected Learning from Exceptional Point Braiding: Toward Braid Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15829)
*M. N. Jipdi,C. Avomo Mba,A. B. Moubissi,L. C. Fai,M. E. Ateuafack*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于非厄米Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量中超常点（exceptional point, EP）编织（braiding）的拓扑学习框架，通过导出EP超曲面的封闭代数方程预测所有EP的位置，利用EP拓扑不变量实现鲁棒态交换，并将学习重新形式化为对辫群（braid group）的离散搜索，以组合优化替代连续权重上的梯度下降，展示了遗传算法可发现高保真度的辫词（braid word）以复现标准量子门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于连续权重梯度下降的神经网络面临噪声脆弱、灾难性遗忘以及泛化性缺乏数学保证等问题。作者试图探索一种基于拓扑保护的替代计算范式，利用非厄米系统中的超常点编织结构作为鲁棒、可解释且具有固有噪声免疫性的计算基底。

Method: 首先在非厄米Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量框架下导出EP超曲面的封闭代数方程，并通过动量量子化精确预测有限体系中所有EP的数量与参数位置。随后以态交换保真度和归一化Berry相位（Berry phase）两个量化不变量刻画EP拓扑。通过绝热环绕（adiabatic encirclement）实验验证鲁棒态交换并构造通用辫门集合，最后将学习问题重新表述为辫群上的离散搜索（组合优化），并用遗传算法（genetic search）搜索短辫词以复现目标量子门。

Result: 完整的拓扑映射表明所有EP位于特定区域；绝热环绕产生包括Pauli-X、Pauli-Y、Pauli-Z、Hadamard-like门、T门和SWAP操作在内的通用辫门集合，其中a=0的特殊情形还提供额外的相位门。概念验证的遗传搜索成功发现可精确复现标准Hadamard门和H·Z门的短辫词。

Conclusion: 该框架天然具备噪声免疫性、通过组合拼接实现灾难性遗忘的防止，并凭借数学构造保证泛化性，确立了EP编织作为鲁棒、可解释且拓扑保护的神经形态计算基底的潜力。

Abstract: We present a framework for topological learning based on exceptional point (EP) braiding in a non-Hermitian Bogoliubov-de Gennes Hamiltonian. A closed algebraic equation for the EP super-surface is derived; through momentum quantisation in finite systems, it predicts the exact number and parameter positions of all EPs in real space, irrespective of system size. The EP topology is characterised by two quantised invariants the state-swap fidelity and the normalised Berry phase which cannot both be zero for a topological EP. A complete topological map shows that all EPs lie within a specefic region. Adiabatic encirclements confirm robust state swapping and yield a universal set of braid gates, including Pauli-X, Pauli-Y, Pauli-Z, a Hadamard-like gate, the T-gate, and a SWAP operation, with the special case where a is 0, providing additional phase gates. Building on these generators, we reformulate learning as braid programming a discrete search over the braid group that replaces gradient descent on continuous weights with combinatorial optimisation. A proof-of-concept genetic search successfully discovers short braid words that reproduce the standard Hadamard gate and the H.Z gate with perfect fidelity. This paradigm offers inherent noise immunity, catastrophic-forgetting prevention through compositional concatenation, and guaranteed generalisation by mathematical construction, establishing EP braiding as a promising substrate for robust, interpretable, and topologically protected neuromorphic computation.

</details>


### [142] [Half a qubit: an algebraic fractionalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16183)
*Po-Yao Chang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的量子二能级系统分数化（fractionalization）方案，即代数分数化：通过将经典的Székely“半硬币”嵌入非厄米Krein空间，构造了双正交半量子比特（biorthogonal half-qubit），两个这样的对象通过有符号Vandermonde卷积融合为任意纯量子比特，并推广至1/n量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常，量子二能级系统的分数化依赖于基于Majorana费米子对的编码（即操作性分数化）。本文旨在展示一种替代性的代数分数化路径，将经典概率对象嵌入非厄米框架，从而提供一种全新的量子比特分解视角。

Method: 将Székely的经典“半硬币”嵌入非厄米Krein空间，利用$(q+pz)^{1/2}$的系数定义有符号序列，构造归一化的非厄米双正交算子作为双正交半量子比特。通过分析其L1范数、Krein空间中的正定性、ζ2×ζ2子群结构及η-度量的对称性约束，证明融合性质，并将其推广到n次根情形。

Result: 两个半量子比特通过有符号Vandermonde卷积精确融合为任意纯量子比特，且融合在Fock基截断下依然精确。半量子比特的L1范数以闭式给出，随偏置单调递增并在无偏点达到上确界√2。半量子比特携带一个Z2可观测量但无局部SU(2)对称性，完整的Pauli代数仅在融合后才出现。

Conclusion: 代数分数化提供了一种与Majorana编码互补的量子比特分解机制，其融合结构在实验上可通过腔QED或离子阱系统的符号加权光子数统计进行验证，并可自然推广为1/n量子比特。

Abstract: Fractionalizing a quantum two-level system is usually associated with encodings based on pairs of Majorana fermions---an operational fractionalization. We show an alternative algebraic fractionalization by embedding Székely's classical ``half-coin'' into a non-Hermitian Krein space. The coefficients of $(q+pz)^{1/2}$ define a signed sequence and a normalized, non-Hermitian biorthogonal operator describing a biorthogonal half-qubit. We prove that two such objects fuse into an arbitrary pure qubit through the signed Vandermonde convolution that the collective $N\ge2$ vectors are null in Krein space. $L_1$ norm of the half-qubit follows in closed form, $\lVert p\rVert_1 = 2\sqrt{q}-\sqrt{q-p}$. Its $L_1$ norm increases monotonically with the bias and attains its supremum $\sqrt{2}$ precisely at the unbiased point $p=q=1/2$. Interestingly, we identify two structural results as follows. First, number parity and the $η$-metric generate a distinguished commuting $\mathbb Z_2\times\mathbb Z_2$ subgroup. Second, we find the $η$-metric obstructs any local $η$-self-adjoint partner of the parity, so a half-qubit carries a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ observable but no local $SU(2)$. The full Pauli algebra emerges only upon fusion. We then show that the construction survives truncation of the Fock basis: the fused qubit is exact at every cutoff, and the Vandermonde cancellation is visible in sign-weighted photon-number statistics, and can be tested using existing cavity and trapped-ion state-synthesis methods. Finally, we generalize this algebraic fractionalization to a $1/n$-qubit, which can be achieved by replacing the square root with an $n$th root.

</details>


### [143] [Controlling rotations of magnetically levitated superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16184)
*Fynn Köller,Dominik Maile,Joachim Ankerhold,Klaus Hornberger,Witlef Wieczorek,Benjamin A. Stickler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文研究了磁阱中微米级Ⅰ型超导体的旋转动力学，指出抗磁力矩和陀磁耦合（由爱因斯坦-德哈斯效应和巴涅特效应引起）显著影响其转动行为，并利用这些效应实现棒状超导体在阱中心的二维对准，确定了相应的天平动囚禁频率，最后提出了探测陀磁耦合的实验方案及其用于控制粒子旋转的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁捕获Ⅰ型超导体有望用于量子-经典交界处的精密实验和量子传感，但实现其空间对准和旋转控制尚属未解问题。

Method: 基于抗磁力矩和陀磁耦合的理论分析，推导超导体的旋转动力学，并结合数值计算确定天平动囚禁频率；提出具体的实验设计以探测陀磁耦合效应。

Result: 证明了磁阱中微米级超导体的转动明显受抗磁力矩与陀磁耦合影响；实现了非球状超导体在阱中心的二维对准，并获得了相应的天平动囚禁频率。

Conclusion: 磁捕获超导体的旋转控制可用于未来传感应用与量子实验，所提出的实验方案可探测陀磁耦合并用于调控粒子旋转。

Abstract: Magnetically trapped type-I superconductors are promising candidates for precision experiments at the quantum-to-classical borderline, with applications in quantum sensing of forces and accelerations and fundamental tests of quantum physics. Here, we show how the rotational motion of micron-sized superconductors is strongly affected by (i) the diamagnetic torques due to the gradient of the trapping field and by (ii) the gyromagnetic coupling due to Einstein-de Haas and Barnett effects. We show that this allows the three-dimensional alignment of asperhical superconductors in the trap center, as required for future sensing applications and quantum experiments, and determine the resulting librational trapping frequencies. Finally, we propose an experiment to probe gyromagnetic coupling in levitated superconductors and we discuss how it can be used to control the particle rotation.

</details>


### [144] [Dynamical spectral functions from bitstring-sampled quantum subspaces: entanglement, not one-body magic, tracks the sampling cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16436)
*Nicolás Bonilla Vargas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于样本的量子对角化方法扩展范式，将量子采样目标从基态能量转向动力学性质。通过一种仅需计算基测量的比特串采样原语，从采样子空间重构单粒子谱函数和中性扇区动力学结构因子，并在Hubbard链和19个分子上验证精度，同时证明了费米子反平坦度（fermionic anti-flatness）无法预测采样成本，真正的成本由纠缠（最小键维数χ）控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于样本的量子对角化（SQD）和量子选择组态相互作用（QSCI）是在硬件上最具可行性的电子结构方法，但其经典目标——基态能量——已被经典方法追平。作者希望将目标转向动力学性质和资源代价问题，以探索量子方法的真正优势所在。

Method: 核心方法是从一个比特串采样原语出发（浅层实时电路的测量基测量，无阿达玛门或受控酉门），从采样的子空间中，以Lehmann表示在经典端构建四种动力学量：单粒子谱函数A(ω)和A(k,ω)，以及中性扇区动力学结构因子S(q,ω)和S^{zz}(q,ω)。此外，推导了费米子反平坦度F₁在数守恒态下简化为2N_u，并证明其为轨道旋转（高斯）不变量但于采样成本解耦；通过自洽组态恢复改进子空间以应对器件噪声，并与学习的生成模型进行对比基准。

Result: 重构结果在Hubbard链上与精确对角化匹配，A(ω)在19个分子上与完整组态相互作用（FCI）验证至误差小于10⁻⁵ Ha，且在IBM Heron处理器上运行。经验上，采样成本的下界和追踪由纠缠控制——最小键维数χ（Spearman相关系数ρ=0.90），而反平坦度F₁与成本解耦，表明单体magic不可靠地预测代价；证明矩精确性及关于|S|多项式的采样界（与Hilbert空间维数无关）。自洽组态恢复在器件噪声下提升了子空间质量，而学习的生成模型未能超越该经典基线。

Conclusion: 作者证明动力学性质可以从与基态能量相同的采样原语中高效重构，且精度达到相当水平。资源代价的核心由纠缠而非费米子反平坦度控制，因此量子优势的任何真正来源在于高阶体累积量的非高斯性。自洽组态恢复为噪声环境下的稳健方案，而经典生成模型不构成竞争。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) and quantum-selected configuration interaction (QSCI) are the electronic-structure methods with most hardware traction, yet their canonical target -- the ground-state energy -- is where classical methods have caught up. We move the target to dynamics and the resource question. From one bitstring-sampling primitive -- computational-basis measurements of a shallow real-time circuit, with no Hadamard or controlled unitaries -- we reconstruct, from sampled subspaces, the single-particle spectral functions $A(ω)$ and $A(k,ω)$ and the neutral-sector dynamical structure factors $S(q,ω)$ and $S^{zz}(q,ω)$, each built classically in the Lehmann representation from its own subspace. The reconstruction matches exact diagonalization on Hubbard chains and, for $A(ω)$, across nineteen molecules (FCI-verified to $<10^{-5}$ Ha), and runs on the IBM Heron processor. Second, we ask which resource controls the cost -- the determinant support $|\mathcal{S}|$ the sampler must populate. On number-conserving states the fermionic AntiFlatness collapses to one 1-RDM invariant, $\mathcal{F}_1 = 4\,\mathrm{tr}[γ(1-γ)] = 2N_u$. An orbital-rotation (Gaussian) invariant while $|\mathcal{S}|$ is basis dependent, $\mathcal{F}_1$ is provably decoupled from the cost; the cost is instead lower-bounded and tracked by the entanglement -- the minimal bond dimension $χ$ (Spearman $ρ= 0.90$). One-body magic is thus a faithful multireference diagnostic but an unreliable cost predictor; any genuine advantage lives in the non-Gaussianity of the higher-body cumulants. We prove moment exactness and a sampling bound polynomial in $|\mathcal{S}|$, independent of Hilbert-space dimension. Self-consistent configuration recovery improves the subspace under device noise, while a learned generative model does not beat that classical baseline.

</details>


### [145] [Scientific applications of quantum computing: challenges and opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16568)
*Bruno Camino,C. Richard A. Catlow,John Buckeridge,Alin M. Elena,Vladimir V. Gusev,Sarah Harris,Thomas W. Keal,Glenn Jones,Vivien Kendon,Syma Khalid,Phalgun Lolur,Jamal A. Nasir,Matthew J. Rosseinsky,Chris-Kriton Skylaris,Paul A. Warburton,Scott M. Woodley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨量子计算在化学、材料科学和生物化学中可能带来真正科学优势的方向，指出其核心挑战不在于量子比特数量的扩展，而在于能否在考虑全部成本后，获得可验证的化学预测可靠性提升。近期的实用价值更可能来自与传统经典方法的系统性工作流整合，而非全面替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典分子与材料预测模拟虽然影响广泛，但仍受限于电子关联（electronic correlation）、激发态（excited states）和复杂能量景观（energy landscapes）的精密处理成本。量子计算提供了直接编码和操控量子态的全新计算范式，有必要系统评估其在化学科学中可能产生实质性科学优势的具体场景。

Method: 本文采用质性分析与论证性讨论的方法，系统梳理量子计算在化学、材料科学与生物化学中的潜在应用方向，包括关联活性空间（correlated active spaces）的高精度处理、激发态模拟改进以及组合结构空间（combinatorial structure spaces）的加速探索，并区分不同量子计算发展阶段（含噪物理设备、误差缓解实验、早期容错与完全容错量子计算机）的科学前景。

Result: 作者论证了量子计算近期的价值最可能来自与经典方法的纪律性工作流整合，而非对经典方法的全面取代。量子计算要成为科学上有价值的工具，必须在计入态制备（state preparation）、测量（measurement）、误差处理（error handling）及与经典模拟耦合的全部成本之后，仍能显著降低计算能量、速率、光谱或材料稳定性的不确定性。

Conclusion: 量子计算在化学科学中获得科学价值的判断标准，不应仅以量子比特扩展为目标，而应以能否在完整成本核算后提供可论证的化学预测可靠性增益为准。不同量子计算技术成熟度阶段对应不同的科学前景，任何关于"有用性"的主张必须与所讨论的具体技术阶段绑定。

Abstract: The predictive simulation of molecules and materials has had a broad and significant impact. It nevertheless remains constrained by the cost of accurately treating electronic correlation, excited states, and complex energy landscapes. Quantum computing offers a fundamentally different computational paradigm in which quantum states are encoded and manipulated directly rather than approximated on classical hardware. Here we discuss where this approach may provide a genuine scientific advantage in chemistry, materials science, and biochemistry. Promising directions include the high-accuracy treatment of correlated active spaces, improved excited-state simulations, and accelerated exploration of combinatorial structure spaces. The central challenge is therefore not qubit scaling alone, but demonstrably chemically meaningful gains in predictive reliability. We argue that near-term value is most likely to come from disciplined workflow integration rather than wholesale replacement of classical methods. Noisy physical devices, error-mitigated utility experiments, early fault-tolerant devices, and fully fault-tolerant quantum computers offer different scientific prospects, and claims of usefulness must be tied to the specific regime being discussed. Quantum computing will become scientifically valuable when it demonstrably reduces uncertainty in computed energies, rates, spectra, or materials stability after the full costs of state preparation, measurement, error handling, and coupling to classical simulation are included.

</details>


### [146] [Evidence for cavity-induced metallic phase from entropic electron correlation effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16593)
*Jacob Horak,Dominik Sidler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对光学腔中集体强耦合（collective strong coupling）改变分子系综导电性、介电性质乃至触发相变的物理机制，推导出由关联自由能引起的极化率变化的解析表达式，发现特定条件下集体强耦合可通过熵效应电离集体简并的分子轨道，使分子系综金属化，是构建腔介导电子关联效应整体理论的首个重要步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验已揭示光学腔中的集体强耦合可显著改变分子系综的导电性和介电性质，甚至触发突变相变，但底层物理机制一直未知，亟需从理论上加以解释。

Method: 作者基于近期提出的理论框架，该框架将腔诱导的电子关联（cavity-induced electron correlations）类比于自旋玻璃的球型Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型；在此框架下推导出了关联自由能诱导的极化率变化的解析表达式。

Result: 解析结果表明，在特定条件下，集体强耦合可以通过熵效应电离集体简并的分子轨道，从而将分子系综转变为金属态；这一结果可能解释先前实验中观察到的导电性与介电性质的剧变现象。

Conclusion: 该工作为建立腔介导电子关联效应的整体理论（holistic theory）迈出了第一步，为理解实验中观察到的腔致相变和物性变化提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: Experiments have revealed that collective strong coupling in optical cavities can drastically alter the conductivity and dielectric properties of molecular ensembles, or even trigger abrupt phase transitions (in Rayleigh scattering or dispersion force driven conformational equilibrium). Until now, the underlying physical mechanism has remained unknown. A recently proposed theory attributes these collective effects to cavity-induced electron correlations that share properties of the known spherical Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model of a spin glass. As a consequence, entropic effects can trigger a phase transition in the inter-molecular electron correlations that could potentially explain the above experiments. In the following work, we derive analytic expressions for the correlation (free-)energy-induced polarizability changes, which reveal that under certain conditions collective strong coupling can even render our molecular ensemble metallic by entropically ionizing collectively degenerate molecular orbitals. Our results are a first major step towards a holistic theory of cavity-mediated electron correlation effects.

</details>


### [147] [Crossover from Fast Scrambling to Operator Confinement Tuned by an Auxiliary Qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16674)
*Ryan Buechele,Joseph Szabo,Nandini Trivedi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种静态无无序的自旋链哈密顿量，通过调节与辅助量子比特（ancilla qubit）的耦合强度，实现从超弹道传播（super-ballistic scrambling）到亚弹道算子局域化（sub-ballistic operator confinement）的交叉。该模型为混合场伊辛链（mixed-field Ising chain）耦合一个自旋1/2辅助比特，弱耦合时加速算子扩展与纠缠增长，强耦合时将算子投影到冻结子空间并通过有效隐藏对称性实现指数级时间尺度的算子局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机幺正电路（random-unitary-circuit）实现中的星形几何（star geometry）报道了快速的对数时间尺度算子紊乱（scrambling），而时间无关哈密顿量类比重现了算子局域化现象，两者之间的关系此前尚不明确。本文旨在通过单一哈密顿量族和单一无量纲参数统一并解释这两种看似矛盾的观测结果。

Method: 构建混合场伊辛链与自旋-1/2辅助比特耦合的最小模型，并通过两种独立判据定位交叉点：互信息饱和时间在$λ_c N/h \approx 8$附近的发散峰值，以及晚期无序增长算符（OTOC）增长率的指数抑制$\logα\propto 1/λ$。采用Feshbach-Fano投影和Schrieffer-Wolff变换揭示链上的有效隐藏对称性。

Result: 弱耦合时辅助比特介导有效的全对全（all-to-all）相互作用，加速算子扩展与纠缠增长，表现出超弹道动力学；强耦合时辅助比特纠缠快速饱和，将自旋链算子投影到有效冻结子空间，实现对链上算子持续时间随耦合强度指数增长的限制。两种独立判据均定位了该交叉，并发现OTOC增长率呈指数抑制。

Conclusion: 由于辅助比特耦合而产生的有效隐藏对称性将算子指数级时间地约束在链上，这一机制调和了随机幺正电路中的快速对数时间紊乱与时间无关哈密顿量中的算子局域化，证明二者源于同一哈密顿量族以单一无量纲参数为函数的不同极限。

Abstract: We demonstrate a static, disorder-free spin chain Hamiltonian which, by tuning the coupling to an auxiliary qubit, realizes a crossover between super-ballistic, ancilla-accelerated scrambling and sub-ballistic operator confinement. Our minimal model is the mixed-field Ising chain with a spin-1/2 ancilla coupled to its longitudinal magnetization. The ancilla mediates an effective all-to-all interaction which accelerates operator spreading and entanglement growth when weakly coupled, but rapidly saturates its entanglement and projects spin chain operators into effectively frozen subspaces when the ancilla coupling is strong. We locate this crossover independently through both a divergent peak in the mutual-information saturation time near $λ_c N/h \approx 8$ and an exponential suppression of the late-time OTOC growth rate, $\logα\propto 1/λ$. Through a Feshbach-Fano projection and Schrieffer-Wolff transformation, we reveal an effective hidden symmetry on the chain which confines operators on the chain for a time exponential in the coupling strength. This reconciles the fast, $\log(N)$ scrambling reported for random-unitary-circuit realizations of the star geometry with the confinement previously found in its time-independent Hamiltonian analog, showing both emerge from a single Hamiltonian family as a function of one dimensionless parameter.

</details>


### [148] [Time-optimal quantum gates with bang-bang control in multilevel systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16702)
*Valentín Reparaz,Santiago Ferreyra,María José Sánchez,Daniel Domínguez,Leandro Tosi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了实现高保真、快速单量子比特门的最优量子操控协议，证明时间最优脉冲序列为“bang-bang”序列，并在通量量子比特（fluxonium）电路上通过离散序列与连续波形平滑结合，显著优于标准共振控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实现高保真快速单量子比特门时，需要确定最优的量子操控协议，以最小化门时长并降低泄漏误差，尤其在低频架构中实现速度提升。

Method: 首先推导横向驱动二能级系统的最优协议，提取最小门时间的精确解析表达式；随后将该框架推广至一般多能级架构，识别可相干抑制泄漏误差的条件；最后以通量量子比特电路为案例，结合离散bang序列与连续波形平滑优化X/2和Y/2门，并通过开放系统仿真验证性能。

Result: 证明时间最优脉冲序列为“bang-bang”序列（离散的正/负最大幅值或零脉冲）；非绝热bang-bang脉冲序列最小化门时长，为低频架构提供加速；在通量量子比特各参数区域中，该序列优于可公度与共振脉冲方案，即使在1/f磁通噪声和耗散存在下也能比标准共振控制更快实现低误差操控。

Conclusion: 离散bang序列与连续波形平滑的组合方案能在保持近最优执行速度的同时抑制计算子空间外的跃迁，为超导量子电路中的快速高保真单量子比特门操控提供了一种通用且鲁棒的优化框架。

Abstract: We determine the optimal quantum manipulation protocols for implementing high-fidelity, fast single-qubit gates. We demonstrate that the time-optimal pulse sequence is a ``bang-bang'' sequence: discrete pulses of either positive or negative maximum amplitude or zero. The non-adiabatic bang-bang pulse sequence minimizes gate duration providing a speedup for low-frequency architectures. We first derive the protocol for a transversally driven two-level system, extracting exact analytic expressions for minimum gate times. We then extend this framework to general multilevel architectures, identifying conditions that enable the coherent suppression of leakage errors. Using the fluxonium circuit as a representative case study, we optimize $X/2$ and $Y/2$ gates through a combination of discrete bang sequences and continuous waveform smoothing. This approach preserves near-optimal execution speeds while mitigating transitions outside the computational subspace. Open-system simulations demonstrate that these sequences outperform commensurate and resonant pulse schemes across different fluxonium regimes, achieving low-error manipulation significantly faster than standard resonant control, even in the presence of $1/f$ flux noise and dissipation.

</details>

### [149] [No-Go Theorem and Routes towards Cavity-Enhanced Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14784)
*Qing-Dong Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文从最小耦合到量子腔模式的金兹堡-朗道理论出发，证明在被动腔中真空电磁涨落只会抑制而非增强超导，建立了"不可行定理"（no-go theorem）；随后提出两条突破该限制的路径——集体模式增强顺磁交换，或竞争序被腔削弱从而间接增强超导，将限制转化为实用的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的实验报道腔真空调制超导现象，引发了根本性问题：真空电磁涨落在什么条件下才能提高超导转变温度（superconducting transition temperature）？作者希望从理论上澄清这一争议问题。

Method: 作者从最小耦合到量子化腔模的金兹堡-朗道理论（Ginzburg–Landau theory）出发，推导腔诱导的超导自由能重整化。该修正包含正的反磁（diamagnetic）贡献和负的顺磁交换（paramagnetic exchange）贡献，在此基础上证明后者在被动腔中不能超过前者。

Result: 建立了不可行定理：在最小腔电动力学（minimal cavity electrodynamics）框架内，真空涨落抑制而非增强超导。随后识别出两条突破路径：集体模式路线通过腔活性激发放大顺磁贡献；竞争序路线通过腔削弱与超导竞争的序来间接增强超导。

Conclusion: 这些结果将不可行定理转化为实用设计原则：腔增强超导需要额外的腔耦合材料模式，该模式要么加强顺磁交换，要么抑制竞争序。

Abstract: Recent experiments reporting cavity-vacuum-modified superconductivity raise a fundamental question: under what conditions can vacuum electromagnetic fluctuations increase a superconducting transition temperature? Starting from a Ginzburg--Landau theory minimally coupled to a quantized cavity mode, we derive the cavity-induced renormalization of the superconducting free energy. This correction comprises a positive diamagnetic contribution and a negative paramagnetic exchange contribution. We prove that, in a passive cavity, the latter cannot exceed the former, establishing a no-go theorem: within minimal cavity electrodynamics, vacuum fluctuations suppress, rather than enhance, superconductivity. We then identify two routes beyond this constraint, both involving additional collective degrees of freedom. In the collective-mode route, a cavity-active excitation amplifies the attractive paramagnetic contribution. In the competing-order route, the cavity weakens an order that competes with superconductivity, thereby indirectly enhancing superconductivity. Together, these results turn the no-go theorem into a practical design principle: cavity superconductivity enhancement requires an additional cavity-coupled material mode that either strengthens paramagnetic exchange or suppresses a competing order.

</details>


### [150] [Rapid supercurrent decay in Mn$_5$Si$_3$ Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14842)
*Arjun Sapkota,Kurt Lorenzen,Tyler Kuhn,Juan Gomez,Demet Korucu,Robert M. Klaes,Reza Loloee,Norman O. Birge,Nathan Satchell*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了含金属交变磁性（altermagnetic）Mn₅Si₃势垒的Nb/Pt/Mn₅Si₃/Pt/Nb约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）。临界电流随势垒厚度以单指数衰减，衰减长度ξ=0.31±0.03 nm，未观测到0-π转变。作者将未观测到可分辨转变归因于势垒微结构、不确定的磁相及快速衰减限制的窄厚度窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论预测含金属交变磁性势垒的约瑟夫森结应显示临界电流随势垒厚度和温度变化的0-π转变，且超流的衰减与振荡周期取决于晶轴相对于输运方向（transport direction）的取向。作者旨在通过实验检验这一预测。

Method: 实验制作并测量了Nb/Pt/Mn₅Si₃/Pt/Nb约瑟夫森结，改变Mn₅Si₃势垒厚度；随后测量临界电流随厚度变化的衰减行为、势垒的电流垂直平面（current-perpendicular-to-plane）电阻率，以及临界电流随温度的变化关系。

Result: 临界电流以单指数形式衰减超过四个数量级，衰减长度ξ_{Mn₅Si₃}=0.31±0.03 nm，短于含金属反铁磁体（antiferromagnet）FeMn、Cr和NiMn的约瑟夫森结报告值；势垒的垂直平面电阻率估计为320±10 μΩ·cm。在所采样的势垒厚度下未分辨出0-π转变，1 nm势垒结的临界电流温度依赖平滑且单调。

Conclusion: 未观测到可分辨的0-π转变可能源于势垒的微结构、其不确定的磁相，以及快速衰减导致的可探测厚度窗口过窄。作者指出，具有明确晶体取向（well-defined crystalline orientation）的势垒是未来检验交变磁性约瑟夫森物理的关键要求。

Abstract: Theoretical work predicts that Josephson junctions containing metallic altermagnetic barriers should display $0$-$π$ transitions of the critical current as a function of both barrier thickness and temperature, with the decay and oscillation period of the supercurrent depending on the orientation of the crystal axes relative to the transport direction. Motivated by these predictions, and by reports of a compensated magnetic phase attributed to altermagnetism in epitaxial Mn$_5$Si$_3$ thin films, we fabricate and measure Nb/Pt/Mn$_5$Si$_3$/Pt/Nb Josephson junctions varying the thickness of the Mn$_5$Si$_3$ barrier. The critical current decays as a single exponential over more than four orders of magnitude with decay length $ξ_{\text{Mn}_5\text{Si}_3} = 0.31 \pm 0.03$ nm, shorter than reported for Josephson junctions containing the metallic antiferromagnets FeMn, Cr, and NiMn. The Mn$_5$Si$_3$ barrier has an estimated current-perpendicular-to-plane resistivity of $320 \pm 10 μΩ\,$cm. No $0$-$π$ transition is resolved at the sampled barrier thicknesses, and the temperature dependence of the critical current of a junction with a 1 nm barrier is smooth and monotonic. We discuss the absence of resolvable transitions in terms of the microstructure of the barrier, its uncertain magnetic phase, and the narrow thickness window imposed by the rapid decay, and identify barriers with well-defined crystalline orientation as the key requirement for future tests of altermagnetic Josephson physics.

</details>


### [151] [Anharmonicity and Nonadiabaticity in Hydride Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14949)
*Shashi B. Mishra,Francesco Belli,Eva Zurek,Elena R. Margine*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过非谐声子（anharmonic phonons）、电子-声子顶点修正（electron-phonon vertex corrections）以及全带宽Eliashberg理论（full-bandwidth Eliashberg theory），系统研究了代表性氢化物（hydrides）的超导电性，并提出两个材料特异性诊断参数来判断超越谐波绝热Migdal-Eliashberg理论的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压氢化物超导体和非环境压力体系的超导电性预测往往依赖谐波、绝热Migdal-Eliashberg理论，但该理论在某些体系中可能失效，需要辨明何时必须引入更高阶修正。

Method: 采用非谐声子、电子-声子顶点修正及全带宽Eliashberg理论，对H3S、YH6、YH9、PdH/PdD/PdT系列和LaBeH8等代表性氢化物进行系统计算，并引入非谐重整化参数A_λ和顶点比率R_V作为材料特异性诊断指标。

Result: 高压二元氢化物H3S、YH6、YH9必须同时考虑非谐和非常势（nonadiabatic）修正；常压PdH/PdD/PdT系列强非谐但保持绝热（adiabatic），无需显著顶点修正即可复现逆同位素效应（inverse isotope effect）；LaBeH8非谐性较弱，顶点修正使临界温度（Tc）降低约4 K，预测Tc仍高于实验值。

Conclusion: 不同氢化物超导体对谐波绝热Migdal-Eliashberg理论的偏离程度各异，A_λ和R_V可作为有效诊断工具，判定何时需要超越谐波绝热框架的处理。

Abstract: We study superconductivity in representative hydrides using anharmonic phonons, electron-phonon vertex corrections, and full-bandwidth Eliashberg theory. The high-pressure binary hydrides H3S, YH6, and YH9 must be treated with both anharmonic and nonadiabatic corrections, whereas the ambient-pressure PdH/PdD/PdT series is strongly anharmonic but remains adiabatic, reproducing the inverse isotope effect without sizable vertex contributions. LaBeH8 exhibits weak anharmonicity, while vertex corrections reduce the critical temperature (Tc) by approximately 4 K, leaving the predicted Tc above experiment. To identify when treatments beyond harmonic, adiabatic Migdal-Eliashberg theory are required, we introduce the anharmonic renormalization $A_λ$ and the vertex ratio $R_{V}$ as material-specific diagnostics.

</details>


### [152] [Josephson network as a~model for high-temperature superconductor and beyond: Macroscopic quantum coherence probed by microwave absorption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15441)
*Adam Rycerz*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文简要综述了用于解释高临界温度超导体（high-temperature superconductors）磁调制微波吸收（MMMA）谱的若干理论模型，重点聚焦于具有随机电阻、电容和电感参数的约瑟夫森结三维阵列模型，该模型能再现实验中观察到的特征吸收异常，并讨论了其对约瑟夫森结量子比特中微波吸收研究的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自从Stankowski等人对YBa₂Cu₃O₇₋ₓ的开创性实验以来，磁调制微波吸收已成为检测非均匀陶瓷化合物中超导转变的重要技术。然而，低于临界温度（T_c）时微波吸收的上升伴随多种低场异常，这些异常通常归因于超导颗粒间的弱连接（展示约瑟夫森效应）或Abrikosov-Josephson涡旋的动力学，需要建立理论模型来系统解释这些实验谱。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法，回顾若干用于合理化高临界温度超导体微波吸收谱的理论模型，并聚焦于一个三维约瑟夫森结阵列（3-dimensional array of Josephson junctions）模型，其中每个结包含随机的电阻、电容和电感参数，以模拟非均匀陶瓷化合物中颗粒间弱连接的多样性。

Result: 该三维随机参数约瑟夫森结阵列模型能够展现实验中观察到的特征吸收异常，包括临界温度以下微波吸收的上升及相关的低场异常现象。此外，文中还讨论了这些理论框架对当前研究的约瑟夫森结量子比特（Josephson junction qubits）中微波吸收现象的启示。

Conclusion: 基于随机参数的约瑟夫森结三维阵列模型为理解高临界温度超导体中磁调制微波吸收谱提供了合理的理论解释，其对微波吸收异常的特征重现验证了该模型的有效性，同时该框架对约瑟夫森结量子比特系统的微波吸收研究具有延伸应用价值。

Abstract: Since the seminal experiment by Stankowski et al. [Phys. Rev. B 36, 7126 (1987)] on YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{7-x}$, Magnetically-Modulated Microwave Absorption (MMMA) has become an important technique for detecting the superconducting transition in inhomogeneous ceramic compounds. The rise of microwave absorption below $T_c$ is accompanied by several low-field anomalies, usually attributed to the weak links between superconducting grains showing the Josephson effect, or to the dynamics of Abrikosov-Josephson vortices. In this article, we briefly review selected theoretical models rationalizing MMMA spectra for high-temperature superconductors, focusing on the $3$-dimensional array of Josephson junctions with random parameters including the resistivity, capacity and inductance of each junction, showing characteristic absorption anomalies observed in the experiment. The implications for recently studied microwave absorption in Josephson junction qubits are also discussed.

</details>


### [153] [Superconducting Hydride Mg2RhH6 Experimentally Achieved at Lower Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15745)
*Linjing Wu,Zelong Wang,Guiqi Liu,Yuanhao Su,Runteng Chen,Hongyu Liu,Jun Zhang,Wenmin Li,Sijia Zhang,Jingcheng Zhu,Jianfa Zhao,Zheng Deng,Shaomin Feng,Jing Song,Qingqing Liu,Haozhe Liu,Panpan Kong,Xiancheng Wang,Changqing Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究首次实验合成了氢化物超导体Mg₂RhH₆，在显著降低的30 GPa压力下实现了超导转变（Tc=24 K，53 GPa时升至29 K），为在可及压力下寻找高温超导体开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多氢化物超导体领域虽已取得巨大进展，但实现高临界温度超导仍依赖于极端高压条件，寻找在较低压力下即可实现超导的氢化物具有重要科学意义。

Method: 采用两步合成法获得Mg₂RhH₆：(1) 制备含共价稳定氢原子的Mg₂RhH₅前驱体；(2) 在超过30 GPa压力下补充氢，使电子填充至反键轨道，伴随RhH₅四方锥向RhH₆八面体的结构相变。

Result: Mg₂RhH₆在30 GPa下实现超导，Tc为24 K，在53 GPa下Tc增强至29 K；电阻急剧降为零且在外加磁场下Tc受到特征性抑制，验证了超导性；实验证明该材料在30 GPa以上热力学稳定。

Conclusion: Mg₂RhH₆是首个在易于达到的压力下实现约30 K超导转变温度的案例，为在声子介导的BCS框架内合理设计和发现高温超导体提供了极具前景的新路径。

Abstract: Although tremendous progress has been made in recent years in the field of polyhydride superconductors, the realization of high critical temperature superconductivity still relies on formidable high pressures. Searching for superconducting hydrides at lower pressures is of particular importance. Here we report the first experimental synthesis of the Mg2RhH6, which achieves superconductivity under a significantly reduced pressure of 30 GPa. The synthesis of Mg2RhH6 proceeds via a two step process (1) preparation of the Mg2RhH5 precursor containing hydrogen atoms stabilized by covalent bonding, followed by (2) hydrogen supplementation resulting in the filling of electrons into anti bonding orbitals above 30 GPa, which was accompanied by the structural transition from RhH5 square pyramid to RhH6 octahedron. Superconductivity is achieved at 30 GPa with a Tc of 24 K, which is further enhanced to 29 K at 53 GPa, evidenced by a sharp drop of resistivity to zero and characteristic suppression of Tc under applied magnetic fields. Our experiments prove the Mg2RhH6 superconductor to be thermodynamically stable above 30 GPa, making it the first case exhibiting a Tc of approximately 30 K at a readily accessible pressure. This study pioneers a highly promising pathway for the rational design and discovery of high temperature superconductors within phonon mediated BCS framework.

</details>


### [154] [External identification and electrothermal modeling of persistent-current-switch operating states in a Siemens MRI magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15911)
*Volodymyr Pupkov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了MRI磁体持续电流开关（persistent current switch, PCS）的非二元行为，发现加热器关闭后焦耳自热可维持电阻态，并通过专用实验分离出两种电阻状态，建立了统一的电热关系，可用于预测持续电流精度和自持边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCS控制通常被视为二元系统（加热器开=电阻态，关=超导态），但实际电流电压日志显示指令并不能唯一确定PCS状态，传统假设与实际行为存在偏差。需要精确建模PCS电阻行为以提升MRI磁体持续电流控制的准确性。

Method: 基于Siemens MAGNETOM Avanto磁体的MPS2电源电流电压日志，设计专用机动（maneuvers）分离两种电阻态：加热器开启态和关闭后的自持态；拟合统一的电热关系$R_{sw}=C(P_h h+V_{node}^2/R_{sw})^a$；通过18个维修会话和动态模拟验证模型。

Result: 加热器开启时支路电阻在0.5V以下近似恒定为1.86-2.19Ω；关闭后自持态满足$R_{sw}=0.65|V_{node}|^{1.5}$，在0.4V处比加热态低12倍；统一电热关系在690倍电阻范围内成立；跨会话中位数保持时间为13τ，残余支路电流为线圈电流的8 ppm；自持边界估计0.062V介于观测的重陷（0.06V）与保持（0.07V）之间；动态模拟中双特征曲线将电流RMSE从1.449降至0.154 A（降9.4倍）。

Conclusion: PCS行为并非二元，其状态由电热关系、外部电路负载线和保持时间共同决定；两系数特征模型显著提升动态模拟精度，为PCS控制策略优化和MRI磁体持续电流管理提供了定量基础。

Abstract: A persistent current switch (PCS) closes the superconducting loop of an MRI magnet; a heater makes it resistive so an external supply can change the magnet current. PCS control is usually treated as binary: heater on--resistive, heater off--superconducting. External current and voltage logs from an MPS2 service power supply (Digit Systems) show for a Siemens MAGNETOM Avanto magnet that the command does not uniquely determine the PCS state: after heater switch-off, Joule self-heating can sustain the resistive state. Purpose-designed maneuvers separate two resistive regimes. With the heater energized, the branch resistance is nearly constant at 1.86--2.19 $Ω$ below 0.5 V. After switch-off, the self-sustained regime follows $R_{sw}=0.65|V_{node}|^{1.5}$ ($R_{sw}$ in ohms, $V_{node}$ in volts) and is 12-fold below the heater-on resistance at 0.4 V. Both regimes obey one electrothermal relation, $R_{sw}=C(P_h h+V_{node}^2/R_{sw})^a$, with $h$ the binary heater command, over a 690-fold resistance range. First, branch resistance and coil inductance set the redistribution time constant, $τ=L/R_{sw}$; final-hold duration therefore controls persistent-current accuracy. Across 18 service sessions on other Siemens magnets, analyzed without transferring the Avanto coefficients, the median hold was $13τ$ and the median residual branch current was 8 ppm of coil current. Second, the intersection of the PCS characteristic and external-circuit load line sets the self-sustainment boundary. The cross-session estimate of 0.062 V lies between observed retrapping at 0.06 V and retention at 0.07 V. In dynamic simulation, the two fixed-coefficient characteristics $R(V,h)$ reduce current RMSE from 1.449 to 0.154 A (9.4-fold); omitting explicit heater input gives no improvement over the inductance-only model.

</details>


### [155] [Superconducting $T_\mathrm{c}$ up to 20.6 K in bulk YSi$_2$ and YSi$_2$/Si superlattices due to chemical flattening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16062)
*Ding-qing Li,Chong Tian,Juan Du,Jun-jie Shi,Pei-song He,Deng-hui Xu,Hong-xia Zhong,Yao-hui Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与硅晶格匹配的AlB₂型超导体YSi₂，作为约瑟夫森结中Al的替代材料。通过求解Migdal-Eliashberg方程预测其超导转变温度T_c约为20.6 K（含非谐效应时为17.2 K），远超液态氦沸点，并系统验证了其热力学、动力学和力学稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流量子计算机的基本构成单元是约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction），其核心材料通常为硅晶圆上的超导铝（Al）。然而Al的体相超导转变温度T_c（~1.1 K）低于液态氦的沸点（~4.2 K），这成为其大规模应用面临的挑战之一，因此需要寻找替代材料。

Method: 通过求解各向异性（或含非谐效应的各向同性）Migdal-Eliashberg方程计算超导转变温度；采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算验证材料的热力学、动力学和力学稳定性；分析Y原子对Si六角蜂巢层产生的"化学压平"（chemical flattening）效应；通过施加应变压缩Si蜂巢层来改善弹性刚度常数并匹配实验晶格常数；提出YSi₂(0001)/Si(111)超晶格结构以实现所需应变。

Result: 各向异性（不含非谐效应）和各向同性（含非谐效应）Migdal-Eliashberg方程分别给出T_c约20.6 K和17.2 K，处于硅化物（silicides）中的最高水平。超导电性主要来源于被Y原子"化学压平"的Si蜂巢层。通常方法计算得到的负弹性刚度常数C₆₆在Si蜂巢被压缩至阈值以下后变为正，即使不考虑零点能亦然，该应变还能使计算晶格常数与实验一致。所提出的YSi₂(0001)/Si(111)超晶格结构可实现该应变，同时维持T_c高于7.0 K并增强整体稳定性。

Conclusion: YSi₂作为与硅晶格匹配的AlB₂型超导体，其T_c远高于液态氦沸点，且有实验可实现的应变方案来确保力学稳定性，是有前景的Al替代候选材料，对约瑟夫森结量子计算器件的扩展应用具有意义。

Abstract: Currently, the fundamental building blocks of leading quantum computers are Josephson junctions, whose core is usually the superconducting Al on Si wafers. However, the transition temperature $T_\mathrm{c}$ of bulk Al ($\sim1.1$ K) is below the boiling point of liquid helium ($\sim4.2$ K), which is one of the challenges to its widespread application. Here, we propose a Si-matched AlB$_2$-type superconductor YSi$_2$ as a promising alternative to Al. The solution of anisotropic (isotropic) Migdal-Eliashberg equation without (with) anharmonicity gives $T_\mathrm{c}\sim20.6$ K ($17.2$ K), which is at the highest level in silicides. Its excellent superconductivity can be attributed mainly to the Si honeycombs, which become plane here due to the 'chemical flattening' effects of the Y atoms instead of being buckled in most silicides. We tested its thermodynamical, kinetic, dynamical, and mechanical stability by first-principles calculations. Particularly, the negative elastic stiffness constant $C_{66}$ calculated by usual methods turns positive even without the zero-point energy once the Si honeycombs are compressed below a threshold. This strain can also make its calculated lattice constants agree with the experimental ones. We propose structures to realize this strain, i.e., YSi$_2$(0001)/Si(111) superlattices, which can also strengthen the overall stability of YSi$_2$ while maintaining its $T_\mathrm{c}$ above $7.0$ K.

</details>


### [156] [Theory of Magnetic Excitations in the Heavy-Fermion Spin-Triplet Superconductor UTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16350)
*Koki Shimura,Shuntaro Sumita,Yusuke Kato*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用混合维度周期性安德森模型（mixed-dimensional periodic Anderson model）研究UTe₂的动力学自旋响应。在BCS-RPA框架下，作者发现正常态中f电子与巡游电子的杂化在Q_Y=(0,π,0)处增强自旋响应；在超导态中，仅B₂u对称性的奇宇称自旋三重态序参量在该波矢处产生自旋共振模式，这一现象源于B₂u序参量在由Q_Y连接的f电子主导的费米面区域间变号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTe₂是一种备受关注的重费米子自旋三重态超导体，其超导配对对称性尚未明确。理解其磁激发行为与超导序参量的关系，有助于揭示其配对机制和超导态的性质。

Method: 采用混合维度周期性安德森模型描述UTe₂中局域f电子与巡游导带电子的杂化，并在BCS-RPA（Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer–random phase approximation）形式主义下计算正常态和超导态的自旋响应函数。对四种奇宇称不可约表示A_u、B_1u、B_2u和B_3u的自旋三重态序参量分别进行分析，并考察d矢量平行方向的自旋磁化率的超导诱导变化。

Result: 正常态中，f电子与导带电子的有限杂化产生杂化能隙（hybridization gap），并在Q_Y=(0,π,0)处增强自旋响应，该磁激发源于跨杂化能隙的粒子-空穴散射。超导态中，仅B₂u对称性序参量在d矢量平行分量上于Q_Y处产生显著的自旋共振，这是因为B₂u序参量在k_z=π附近由Q_Y连接的f电子主导费米面区域之间保持有限并变号。

Conclusion: 提出一个适用于三维重费米子多带超导体的符号变号判据（sign-change criterion），其成立条件为：(i)费米面存在由嵌套矢量连接的低维部分；(ii)f电子特征占主导；(iii)对应费米面部分上超导能隙幅值有限。该判据可推广用于识别其他重费米子超导体中自旋共振的出现条件。

Abstract: We study the dynamical spin response of UTe$_2$ by using a mixed-dimensional periodic Anderson model. Within the BCS-RPA formalism, we examine how the $f$-orbital character of the quasiparticles affects magnetic excitations in both the normal and superconducting (SC) states. In the normal state, finite mixing between localized $f$ electrons and conduction electrons produces a hybridization gap and enhances the spin response at $\mathbf{Q}_{\mathrm{Y}} = (0,π,0)$, indicating that the magnetic excitation originates from particle--hole scattering across the hybridization gap. In the SC state, we compare four odd-parity irreducible representations, $A_u$, $B_{1u}$, $B_{2u}$, and $B_{3u}$, for the spin-triplet order parameter. We find that, for the component of the spin susceptibility parallel to the $\mathbf{d}$ vector, a pronounced superconductivity-induced spin resonance appears at $\mathbf{Q}_{\mathrm{Y}}$ only in the $B_{2u}$ state. This behavior arises because the $B_{2u}$ order parameter remains finite and changes its sign between the relevant $f$-electron-dominated Fermi-surface regions connected by $\mathbf{Q}_\mathrm{Y}$ near $k_z=π$. The sign-change criterion is applicable to multiband superconductors in three-dimensional heavy-fermion systems, in the presence of (i) low-dimensional portions of the Fermi surface connected by a nesting vector, (ii) the dominance of the $f$-electron character, and (iii) the finite amplitude of SC gap on the portions.

</details>

### [157] [Evidence for parity violation in gravitational fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14703)
*V. Gharibyan,V. Adler,P. D. Allfrey,M. A. Bell,B. D. Belusic,A. Block,Y. Bozhko,J. A. Coughlan,R. K. Dementiev,A. Deshpande,J. Ferencei,R. Gonçalo,K. H. Hiller,R. Kaiser,R. Kammering,B. Krause,B. -Q. Ma,M. C. K. Mattingly,S. Padhi,A. Perieanu,D. Protopopescu,P. Ryan,J. Schaffran,P. Schmid,M. Seebach,E. Sombrowski,G. Susinno,M. Wang,K. Wick,M. Wobisch,A. Zichichi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了对HERA康普顿偏振计（Compton polarimeter）在2004–2007年间获取的314896个光谱的分析结果。通过激光偏振态标记分离电荷（C）和空间宇称（P）贡献，测量到由激光螺旋度翻转引起的康普顿边能量不对称度高达(4.9±0.5)×10⁻⁵，对应的螺旋度依赖引力势差异为(1.7±0.2)×10⁻¹⁴，这可能指示引力系统中的高能CP破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力中的分立对称性（discrete symmetries）仅在低能、非相对论物质中得到了验证，确认了完全对称的广义相对论（general relativity）。近期在HERA康普顿偏振计的电子束与正电子束测量的两个光谱中找到了引力场中高能CP破坏（CP violation）的线索。本文旨在通过更多光谱数据的系统分析，验证并量化这一潜在的引力宇称破坏效应。

Method: 对同一台HERA康普顿偏振计在2004–2007年间采集的314896个光谱进行分析。利用激光偏振态标记来分离电荷（C）和空间宇称（P）的贡献，通过测量由激光螺旋度翻转诱发的康普顿边（Compton edge）能量不对称度，计算螺旋度依赖的引力势差异。

Result: 测量到的康普顿边能量不对称度高达(4.9±0.5)×10⁻⁵，对应的螺旋度依赖引力势差异为(1.7±0.2)×10⁻¹⁴。若观测到的异常耦合能量无关，则自旋不对称引力将对银河系旋转曲线（galactic rotational curves）产生贡献。

Conclusion: 进一步的后续分析与计算将可以确定所观测到的引力宇称破坏（gravitational parity violation）量级是否足以将这一著名现象与暗物质理论（dark matter theory）区分开来。该结果表明引力中可能存在此前未被验证的高能CP破坏效应。

Abstract: Discrete symmetries in gravity have only been tested for low energy, non-relativistic matter, confirming the perfectly symmetric general relativity. A hint for high energy $\cal{CP}$ violation in gravitational fields has recently been found in the HERA Compton polarimeter's two spectra, measured with electron and positron beams. Here we report results of the analysis of the same polarimeter's 314896 spectra, acquired during 2004--2007 and tagged by laser polarization states allowing the separation of charge ($\cal C$) and space parity ($\cal P$) contributions. The measured Compton edge energy asymmetry, induced by the laser helicity flips, is as high as $(4.9\pm0.5)\cdot10^{-5}$ which corresponds to a helicity-dependent difference in the gravitational potentials of $(1.7\pm0.2)\cdot10^{-14}$. In the case of the observed anomalous coupling's energy independence, the spin asymmetric gravity will contribute to the galactic rotational curves. Further analysis and calculations can determine whether the observed magnitude of the gravitational parity violation is sufficient for detaching this famous phenomena from the dark matter theory.

</details>


### [158] [Measurement of $e^{+} e^{-}\to p\bar{p}$ around $ψ(2S)$ with scan method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15074)
*The BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用积分亮度为495 pb⁻¹、质心能量范围3.58–3.71 GeV的BESIII实验数据，首次测量了ψ(2S)共振附近的质子–反质子（p p̄）对产生截面与有效形状因子，填补了该能区实验空白，并提取了强子振幅与电磁振幅之间的相对相位Φ_{g,γ}=(105.9±9.4)°或(−105.3±9.1)°，对应得到两种ψ(2S)→p p̄衰变分支比解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ψ(2S)共振附近，质子–反质子对的实验测量结果此前存在空白，需要对强作用与电磁作用振幅之间的相对相位进行提取，以检验相位的普适性并理解其深层机制。

Method: 利用积分亮度为495 pb⁻¹、质心能量范围3.58 GeV至3.71 GeV的BESIII实验数据，测量ψ(2S)附近e⁺e⁻→p p̄反应的截面与有效形状因子（effective form factor），并通过截面线形（cross-section line shape）提取相对相位。

Result: 结果的实验空白被填补且与以往BESIII和BaBar结果一致；提取的相对相位为Φ_{g,γ}=(105.9±9.4)°或(−105.3±9.1)°；对应的两种ψ(2S)→p p̄分支比分别为(3.21±0.13)×10⁻⁴和(3.47±0.19)×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持相对相位的普适性（universality），并为理解其深层机制提供了新的视角。

Abstract: This paper uses the data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $495$~pb$^{-1}$ in the center-of-mass energy region from 3.58~GeV to 3.71~GeV to study the relative phase between strong and electromagnetic amplitudes in the $ψ(2S)\to p\bar{p}$ decay. The cross sections and the effective form factors around $ψ(2S)$ of proton-antiproton pairs are presented thereby filling the gap in the experimental results around the $ψ(2S)$ resonance for the first time, and the results are consistent with the previous BESIII and BaBar results. The relative phase is extracted from the cross-section line shape as $Φ_{g,γ}=(105.9\pm9.4)^\circ$ or $Φ_{g,γ}=(-105.3\pm9.1)^\circ$. Correspondingly, two solutions for the $ψ(2S)\to p\bar{p}$ branching fractions are obtained as $\mathcal{B}=(3.21\pm0.13)\times10^{-4}$ and $\mathcal{B}=(3.47\pm0.19)\times10^{-4}$. These results support the universality of the phase and provide new insights into its underlying mechanism.

</details>
