Peres,J. Pienaar,M. Pierre,G. Plante,T. R. Pollmann,F. Pompa,A. Prajapati,L. Principe,J. Qin,D. Ramírez García,A. Ravindran,A. Razeto,L. Sanchez,J. M. F. dos Santos,I. Sarnoff,G. Sartorelli,M. T. Schiller,P. Schulte,H. Schulze Eißing,M. Schumann,L. Scotto Lavina,M. Selvi,F. Semeria,F. N. Semler,P. Shagin,X. Shen,S. Shi,H. Simgen,Z. Song,A. Stevens,C. Szyszka,A. Takeda,Y. Takeuchi,P. -L. Tan,D. Thers,G. Trinchero,C. D. Tunnell,K. Valerius,S. Vecchi,S. Vetter,G. Volta,B. von Krosigk,C. Weinheimer,D. Wenz,C. Wittweg,V. H. S. Wu,Y. Xing,D. Xu,Z. Xu,M. Yamashita,J. Yang,L. Yang,J. Ye,M. Yoshida,L. Yuan,G. Zavattini,Y. Zhao,M. Zhong,T. Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究利用XENONnT实验2.1吨·年的数据，搜索磁性（magnetic）和自旋无关（spin-independent）非弹性暗物质（inelastic dark matter），报告了GeV/c²至TeV/c²质量范围内的90%置信水平上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非弹性暗物质模型预言了核反冲后伴随延迟去激发光子发射的独特信号特征，需要专门的实验搜索。已有实验数据在此类模型约束上仍存在空白，尤其需要对磁性和自旋无关两种相互作用形式进行系统性约束。

Method: 利用XENONnT实验的2.1吨·年曝光数据，同时考虑单站点（single-site）和双站点（double-site）事件拓扑结构，以初始核反冲后伴随延迟去激发光子的独特信号为目标。该方法引入了基于激发态暗物质粒子在散射与衰变站点之间推断速度与方向的新型方向性（directional）和运动学（kinematic）选择条件以压制背景。

Result: 收集的数据与背景期望一致，未发现超出预期的信号。对磁性和自旋无关两种非弹性暗物质模型，在GeV/c²至TeV/c²质量范围内均报告了90%置信水平的上限约束。

Conclusion: XENONnT实验在该质量区间内未观测到非弹性暗物质信号，所报告的上限约束为上述两种模型提供了新的实验限制，填补了该参数空间内的搜索空白。

Abstract: We present a search for Magnetic and Spin-Independent inelastic Dark Matter using 2.1 tonne-years of data from the XENONnT experiment. We consider both single- and double-site event topologies, targeting the unique signature of an initial nuclear recoil followed by a delayed de-excitation photon. To suppress backgrounds, we introduce novel directional and kinematic selections based on the inferred speed and direction of the excited dark matter particle between the scatter and decay sites. We find that the collected data are consistent with background expectations, and report 90% C.L. upper limits for both models across the GeV/c2-TeV/c2 mass range.

</details>


### [160] [Deep Earth imaging through neutrino and seismic tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15231)
*Sanjib Kumar Agarwalla,Arjun Datta,Amol Dighe,Vinod Kumar Gaur,Anuj Kumar Upadhyay*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了2026年2月23–24日在印度浦那科学教育与研究所（IISER Pune）举行的"地球深部中微子+地震成像与层析成像"（DENSITY 2026）小型研讨会。该研讨会由IISER Pune与布巴内什瓦尔物理研究所（IOP）联合组织，汇聚了地球科学和中微子物理学的研究人员，旨在探索研究地球深部内部结构的多学科方法（multipronged approaches）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球深部研究涉及地震学、地球化学、矿物物理学和中微子物理学等多个学科，各学科研究人员缺乏跨学科共识。本次研讨会旨在通过跨学科交流，整合多元方法以更全面地研究地球内部结构。

Method: 研讨会采用系列综述性报告（overview talks）的形式，向所有参与者介绍各领域的基本概念，并阐明这些概念如何应用于地球深部研究，以促进来自不同学科背景的参与者之间的相互理解。

Result: 本次会议成功组织了一场跨学科研讨会，来自地球科学与中微子物理领域的研究人员共同参与，并设有覆盖地震学、地球化学、矿物物理学和中微子物理学等领域的概述性讲座。

Conclusion: 该研讨会通过提供各学科概述报告，有效促进了地震学与中微子物理等多学科参与者之间的交流，为后续运用多学科方法研究地球深处内部结构奠定了基础。

Abstract: This article is a report on the Deep Earth Neutrino + Seismic Imaging and TomographY (DENSITY 2026) mini-workshop, held on 23--24 February 2026 in the Department of Earth and Climate Science at the Indian Institute of Science Education and Research (IISER), Pune. The workshop was jointly organised by IISER Pune and the Institute of Physics (IOP), Bhubaneswar. Researchers from Earth Sciences and Neutrino Physics participated in the workshop to explore multipronged approaches for studying the deep interior of the Earth. Since the participants came from diverse scientific disciplines (seismology, geochemistry, mineral physics, and neutrino physics), the programme featured a series of overview talks introducing all participants to the basic concepts of each field and highlighting how these concepts may be applied to the study of the deep Earth.

</details>


### [161] [First measurement of the one-point charge correlator in $e^+e^-$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 91.2$ GeV with DELPHI Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15357)
*Jingyu Zhang,Yi Chen,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Cristian Baldenegro,Hannah Bossi,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了强子Z衰变中电荷关联函数（charge correlator）的首次测量，利用1994-1995年记录的61 pb⁻¹ DELPHI归档数据（√s=91.2 GeV），通过两步探测器效应修正后，测量结果展现出宇称奇性的sin(2θ)调制，并与PYTHIA 8.3预测一致，验证了该观测量的实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z耦合的手征结构在强子Z衰变中产生宇称奇的电荷流印记，相关的前后不对称性（forward-backward asymmetries）是电弱精密测量计划中的关键观测量，已在LEP和SLC上通过喷注重构测量。单点电荷关联函数提供了一条互补路径，可直接测量相对于入射电子束轴极角的强子电荷流，无需依赖喷注或重构夸克方向，但此前尚未被实现测量。

Method: 利用1994-1995年记录的DELPHI归档开放数据（61 pb⁻¹，√s=91.2 GeV）开展首次测量。探测器效应分两步修正：第一步基于全模拟样本推导，第二步通过e⁺e⁻→τ⁺τ⁻事件中的测量来约束数据与模拟之间残余的电荷重构差异。

Result: 测得的电荷关联函数展现出特征性的宇称奇性sin(2θ)调制，并与PYTHIA 8.3的预测一致。

Conclusion: 该测量证明了该观测量的实验可行性，建立了控制相关探测器效应的策略，为在归档e⁺e⁻数据及未来对撞机上的新电荷通量（charge flux）测量计划铺平了道路。

Abstract: The chiral structure of the $Z$ couplings imprints a parity-odd flow of electric charge on hadronic $Z$ decays. The related forward-backward asymmetries, a key set of observables in the electroweak precision program, were measured at LEP and SLC using the jet charge. The one-point charge correlator offers a complementary route, measuring the hadronic charge flow directly as a function of polar angle relative to the incoming electron-beam axis, without reference to jets or a reconstructed quark direction, following the formalism developed in a companion paper. We report its first measurement, using $61~\mathrm{pb}^{-1}$ of archival DELPHI Open Data recorded at $\sqrt{s} = 91.2$~GeV in 1994 and 1995. Detector effects are corrected in two stages. The first is derived from fully simulated samples, and the second bounds the residual charge-misreconstruction difference between data and simulation using a measurement in $e^+e^-\toτ^+τ^-$ events. The measured charge correlator exhibits the characteristic parity-odd $\sin(2θ)$ modulation and agrees with the \textsc{PYTHIA}~8.3 prediction. The measurement demonstrates the experimental feasibility of the observable and establishes strategies for controlling associated detector effects, paving the way for a new program of charge-flux measurements, both in archival $e^+e^-$ data and at future colliders.

</details>


### [162] [Analysis note: one-point charge correlator with DELPHI Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15367)
*Jingyu Zhang,Yi Chen,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Cristian Baldenegro,Hannah Bossi,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用1994-1995年LEP-1对撞机在质心能量√s = 91.2 GeV下记录的DELPHI开放数据，首次测量了强子末态中的单点电荷关联子（one-point charge correlator），即电荷角通量。经探测器效应校正后的数据呈现清晰的sin(2θ)调制，与Z玻色子耦合手性结构所造成的宇称破缺强子电荷流一致，证明了该可观测量的实验可行性，并为后续电荷通量测量计划奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在首次实验测量强子末态中的单点电荷关联子（电荷角通量），验证该可观测量的实验可行性，并建立控制相关探测器效应的策略，从而推动电荷通量测量这一新研究方向的开展。

Method: 利用DELPHI在LEP-1上于1994-1995年采集的开放数据，在质心能量√s = 91.2 GeV下测量强子末态的电荷角通量，并对探测器效应进行校正以恢复真实物理信号。

Result: 经过探测器效应校正后的数据展现出清晰的sin(2θ)调制，该调制与Z玻色子耦合的手性结构在末态上留下的宇称破缺强子电荷流一致。

Conclusion: 该测量证明了电荷通量这一可观测量的实验可行性，建立了一套控制探测器效应的策略，从而为开展电荷通量测量的新实验计划提供了动机；本文档详细记录了支撑配套实验论文和理论与实验联合快报的实验细节。

Abstract: We present the first measurement of the one-point charge correlator, the angular flux of electric charge in hadronic final states, using DELPHI Open Data collected at LEP-1 at $\sqrt{s} = 91.2$~GeV during 1994 and 1995. The data, corrected for detector effects, exhibit a clear $\sin(2θ)$ modulation, consistent with the parity-violating hadronic charge flow that the chiral structure of the $Z$ couplings imprints on the final state. The measurement demonstrates the experimental feasibility of the observable and establishes strategies for controlling associated detector effects, thereby motivating a new program to measure charge-flux observables. This note documents the experimental details supporting the companion experimental paper and the joint theory--experiment Letter.

</details>


### [163] [Measurement of reconstructed final-state kinematics in charged-current interactions on water using the J-PARC $ν_μ$ beam and NINJA emulsion detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16063)
*NINJA Collaboration,A. Kasumi,S. Han,E. Abad Díaz,S. Aoki,D. Barčot,C. Bronner,T. Fukuda,Y. Furuta,M. Ghosh,T. Hayakawa,Y. Hayasaka,Y. Hayato,L. Halić,D. Hirata,H. Hotta,M. Jakkapu,C. Jesús-Valls,T. Katori,H. Kawahara,T. Kawahara,T. Kikawa,B. Kilček,H. Kobayashi,R. Komatani,M. Komatsu,B. Kovač,T. Matsuo,S. Mikado,A. Minamino,K. Mizuno,Y. Morimoto,K. Morishima,Y. Nakamura,T. Nakano,T. Nakaya,T. Nishikiori,H. Oaira,T. Odagawa,S. Ogawa,H. Oshima,N. Otani,G. Pintaudi,H. Rokujo,O. Sato,H. Shibuya,S. Shimizu,K. Sugimura,Y. Suzuki,S. Takeshita,K. Yasutome,S. Yamamoto,M. Yoshimoto*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究基于NINJA核乳胶探测器记录的J-PARC ν_μ聚焦束流在水中产生的带电电流中微子相互作用事件，分析了带电粒子多重数以及缪子、质子和带电π介子的运动学分布，并与T2K实验使用的相互作用模型进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水靶上的中微子相互作用（neutrino interactions）是许多中微子振荡实验中重要的系统不确定性来源，特别是质子运动学信息对理解中微子-核反应机制至关重要，但此前在核乳胶实验中对质子动量的低阈值敏感度尚未得到充分利用。

Method: 利用NINJA核乳胶探测器暴露于J-PARC ν_μ聚焦束流，选取通量积分（flux-integrated）的带电电流（charged-current）ν_μ全包含事例样本；命中4.63×10^20质子靶暴露量，从3.9 kg fiducial水质量（占总靶质量5%）中选出82个事例；将重构的运动学分布与基于T2K相互作用模型的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）预测进行对比。

Result: 重构分布总体上与模型预测在估计不确定度范围内一致，但质子角分布与预测存在一定张力；当前结果受统计不确定度限制。该分析首次提供了对低至200 MeV/c质子动量的敏感度的水靶ν_μ聚焦束流质子运动学研究。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了基于核乳胶数据开展水靶质子运动学测量的分析框架，为将来利用更大规模NINJA数据样本开展精确中微子-水相互作用研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a study of charged-particle multiplicities and the kinematic distributions of muons, protons, and charged pions in a flux-integrated sample of charged-current inclusive $ν_μ$ interactions on water recorded with the NINJA nuclear emulsion detector exposed to the J-PARC $ν_μ$-focused beam. The data correspond to an exposure of $4.63\times10^{20}$ protons on target, with a neutrino energy spectrum peaked at 0.7 GeV. In this analysis, 82 events are selected from a fiducial water volume with a mass of 3.9 kg, corresponding to 5% of the total water target mass exposed during the run. The reconstructed distributions are compared with Monte Carlo predictions based on the interaction model used in the T2K experiment. The observed distributions are generally consistent with the predictions within the estimated uncertainties, although the proton angular distribution exhibits some tension with the prediction. The results are currently limited by statistical uncertainties. This analysis provides the first study of proton kinematics with sensitivity to proton momenta as low as 200 MeV/$c$ with a $ν_μ$-focused beam on a water target using nuclear emulsion data and establishes the analysis framework for future measurements with substantially larger NINJA data samples.

</details>

H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上收集的100亿J/ψ事例，首次实验研究了Σ^0→Λe^+e^-的Dalitz衰变（Dalitz decay），测得绝对分支比为(6.34 ± 0.25_stat. ± 0.23_syst.) × 10^-3，并确定了跃迁磁矩（transition magnetic moment）为(1.74 ± 0.03_stat. ± 0.09_syst.) μ_N。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Σ^0→Λe^+e^-的Dalitz衰变此前从未被实验研究过，该过程对于理解Σ^0超子的内在结构与跃迁磁矩具有重要价值。首次对其实验研究可以检验理论预言并与粒子数据组（PDG）中的理论计算进行对比。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞器上采集的100亿J/ψ事例数据样本，通过J/ψ→Σ^0\barΣ^0过程产生Σ^0超子，并采用双标记法（double-tag method）对衰变事件进行识别与分析。

Result: 测得的绝对分支比为B(Σ^0→Λe^+e^-)=(6.34±0.25_stat.±0.23_syst.)×10^-3，该结果与PDG中引用的理论计算存在2σ的偏差。同时确定跃迁磁矩为μ=(1.74±0.03_stat.±0.09_syst.) μ_N，其中μ_N表示核子磁矩。

Conclusion: 该研究首次实验确定了Σ^0→Λe^+e^-的分支比，为Σ^0超子的结构提供了新视角；结果与理论预言的2σ偏差提示现有理论可能需要进一步修正，而测得的跃迁磁矩为理解Σ^0的内在结构提供了有价值的实验依据。

Abstract: Based on a data sample of 10 billion $J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, the Dalitz decay $Σ^0 \rightarrow Λe^+e^-$ is studied experimentally for the first time. The $Σ^0$ hyperons are produced through the process $J/ψ\rightarrow Σ^0\barΣ^0$ and analyzed using a double-tag method. The absolute branching fraction is measured to be $\mathcal{B}(Σ^0 \rightarrow Λe^+e^-) = (6.34 \pm 0.25_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.23_{\rm syst.}) \times 10^{-3}$. This result shows a $2σ$ discrepancy from the theoretical calculation quoted in the PDG, where the uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. In addition to the branching fraction, the transition magnetic moment $μ$ is determined to be $(1.74 \pm 0.03_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.09_{\rm syst.})\,μ_N$, where $μ_N=e/(2m_p)$ represents the nucleon magnetic moment, providing valuable insight into the intrinsic structure of the $Σ^0$ hyperon.

</details>

Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集到的大样本数据，首次观测到强子衰变 $J/ψ\to Σ^{0}\barΣ^{0}η$ 和 $ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0}\barΣ^{0}η$，测量出其分支比并检验了12%规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的大统计量$J/ψ$和$ψ(3686)$数据样本，探索粲偶素（charmonium）强子衰变中新的衰变模式，并通过分支比比值检验12%规则（12%-rule）的适用性。

Method: 基于$(10087 \pm 44) \times 10^6$个$J/ψ$事例和$(2712 \pm 14) \times 10^6$个$ψ(3686)$事例，通过BESIII探测器测量两个衰变道的分支比，并利用比值 $Q$ 与12%规则进行一致性检验；同时检查了$\Sigma^0(\barΣ^{0})η$和$\Sigma^0\barΣ^{0}$不变质量谱中是否存在中间共振态或阈效应（threshold enhancement）。

Result: 首次观测到这两个衰变道，测得 $B(J/ψ\to Σ^{0}\barΣ^{0}η)=(7.5\pm0.3\pm0.8)\times10^{-5}$ 和 $B(ψ(3686)\toΣ^{0}\barΣ^{0}η)=(1.3\pm0.1\pm0.1)\times10^{-5}$，比值 $Q=(17.3\pm1.5\pm1.7)\%$，与12%规则在3.0σ内一致；两者不变质量谱中未发现显著的中间态或阈增强。

Conclusion: 两个衰变道的分支比测量为首次报道，其比值与12%规则在3.0σ置信水平内一致；未观察到中间共振态或阈增强，表明这些衰变可能直接通过树图或非共振过程发生。

Abstract: Based on $(10087 \pm 44) \times 10^6$ $J/ψ$ and $(2712 \pm 14) \times 10^6$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, the hadronic decays $J/ψ\to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η$ and $ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η$ are observed for the first time. The corresponding branching fractions are measured to be $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0}η)= (7.5 \pm 0.3 \pm 0.8) \times 10^{-5}$ and $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0}η)= (1.3\pm 0.1 \pm 0.1) \times 10^{-5}$, respectively, where the first uncertainties are statistical, and the second systematic. The ratio $\text{Q} \approx \frac{\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η)}{\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η)}$ is determined to be $(17.3 \pm 1.5 \pm 1.7)\%$, which is con sistent with the 12\%-rule within 3.0$σ$.~No significant intermediate states or threshold enhancements are observed in the $Σ^0$($\barΣ^{0}$)$η$ and $Σ^0$$\barΣ^{0}$ invariant mass spectra.

</details>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [The Boltzmann structure of sampling: Intrinsic $p$-value and its emergent closed-form expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14608)
*Orestis Loukas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究观测变量在有限类别中受限取值的条件下，基于前向采样过程构造内在离散的p值。通过鞍点技术推导连续极限下的概率质量密度近似，并利用大N球对称性，通过卡方分布（χ²分布）和KL散度实现p值的半解析高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当观测变量（observable）在采样数据中的实现受限于有限可区分类别时，需要一种直接从采样过程本身定义而非依赖观测额外概率结构的离散p值。同时，处理大规模组合学计数在计算上不可行，需要有效的近似方法。

Method: 利用组合构造定义离散p值，将d+k个任意函数的样本均值指定为线性数据集族，其概率质量通过加权求和解码多面体内整数格点获得。采用鞍点技术（saddle-point technique）在多项式普适类（multinomial universality class）的连续极限中近似概率质量密度，并结合信息几何与球面对称性，通过拉普拉斯近似（Laplace approximation）和χ²分布高效计算p值。

Result: 得到半解析表达式：p值统计量为2N乘以结构族与观测族信息投影（information projection）之间的Kullback-Leibler散度，该统计量服从χ²_k分布，且投影可通过标准数值例程高效计算。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种无需额外概率结构、直接从采样过程构造离散p值的框架，并通过鞍点近似和卡方分布实现了在大样本极限下p值的可计算性，适用于条件采样等典型场景。

Abstract: We consider observables $X$ whose realizations in a sampled dataset are restricted, for example by measurement resolution, to a finite set of distinguishable categories within their possibly infinite theoretical domain. Given the probabilities of observable categories, we study the coarse-grained probability mass of families of possible datasets generated through a forward sampling process. The combinatorial construction induces an intrinsically discrete $p$-value defined directly from the sampling process rather than through additional probabilistic structure on observables.
  Specifying the sample means of $d+k$ arbitrary functions $g_α(X)$ defines a linear family of datasets whose probability mass is obtained as a weighted sum over integer lattice points contained within the associated polyhedron. To overcome the intractable large-$N$ combinatorics, we derive via saddle-point techniques a density approximating these probability masses in the continuum limit of forward sampling within the multinomial universality class. As a demonstration, we consider conditional sampling, where $d$ structural means are fixed while $k$ means vary over admissible datasets. The information geometry emerging from the saddle-point density, together with the spherical symmetry arising at large $N$ from the intrinsic $p$-value construction, enables efficient computation of the $p$-value in the Laplace approximation via the $χ^2_k$ distribution. The resulting statistic is given by the semi-analytic expression $2N$ times the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the information projections associated with the corresponding structural and observed linear families. These projections can be computed efficiently via standard numerical routines converging for sufficiently well-behaved sample means.

</details>

### [167] [Observing Macroscopic Consequences of Electroweak Anomalies with Archival DELPHI Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14779)
*Jingyu Zhang,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Yi Chen,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出电荷相关函数（charge correlator）可作为标准模型电弱反常（electroweak anomaly）的宏观体现，并利用DELPHI实验存档数据首次测量了强子衰变中的一点电荷相关函数，干净地观测到了反常的宏观痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷通量（charge flux）中的不对称性为电弱反常提供了宏观表现，但传统的正反不对称（forward-backward asymmetry）测量在强子衰变精度研究中存在长期张力，需要新的可观测量来替代或补充。

Method: 提出利用电荷相关函数作为新的观测手段，重新表述正反不对称测量；基于DELPHI实验的存档数据，对电磁电荷通量在强子上的单点电荷相关函数进行了首次测量。

Result: 首次在实验上干净地观测到了标准模型电弱反常的宏观印记，验证了电荷相关函数作为精密电弱可观测量（precision electroweak observable）的可行性。

Conclusion: 电荷相关函数有望助力解决强子不对称测量中长期存在的张力问题，值得在后续实验中投入更多努力加以研究。

Abstract: In this Letter, we emphasize that asymmetries in charge flux produced in the decays of on-shell Z-bosons provide a macroscopic manifestation of electroweak anomalies in the Standard Model (SM). We propose that these can be cleanly observed using charge correlators, providing a new formulation of forward-backward asymmetry measurements that is particularly well suited for precision studies of hadronic decays. Using archival DELPHI data, we perform a first measurement of the one-point charge correlator of electromagnetic charge flux on hadrons, and cleanly observe the macroscopic imprint of the underlying anomaly. Our analysis illustrates the potential of charge correlators as precision electroweak observables, and motivates a renewed effort to resolve longstanding tensions in hadronic asymmetry measurements.

</details>


### [168] [Energy Correlators in $V + X$ as a Benchmark Observable for Precision QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14793)
*Terry Generet,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Rene Poncelet,Xiaoyuan Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在大型强子对撞机（LHC）上以Z/γ玻色子反冲的QCD辐射上测量的投影能量关联子（projected energy correlators）作为精密QCD基准观测量。利用Z/γ作为硬标度避免了对喷注算法（jet algorithm）的需求，简化了微扰与非微扰修正。作者建立了结合先进固定阶振幅、高阶求和（resummation）和普适非微扰矩阵元的框架，并给出了NLO+NNLL阶的完整匹配预测数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC上进行精密QCD（precision QCD）测量需要高精度的基准观测量，而传统方法通常依赖喷注算法，给微扰和非微扰修正带来复杂性。本文旨在通过能量关联子这一无需喷注算法的观测量，为LHC上的QCD精密测量提供更简洁且易于计算的框架。

Method: 利用Z/γ作为硬标度定义反冲辐射上的投影能量关联子，从而免除喷注算法的需求。构建了一个结合最先进的固定阶振幅、高阶求和以及普适非微扰矩阵元的统一框架，基基于数值计算的包含硬函数（inclusive hard functions），允许灵活处理不同物理过程并纳入现实实验截断条件；在此基础上进行NLO+NNLL（次领头阶+次次领头对数）阶的匹配计算。

Result: 完成了NLO+NNLL阶的详细数值研究，验证了该计算框架的稳定性；得到了LHC上能量关联子在该阶次下的首批完整匹配预测结果，并展示了对更高阶扩展的前景。

Conclusion: 本文提出的投影能量关联子及其计算框架为LHC上的精密QCD研究提供了有效工具，为后续推进到NNLO计算给出了清晰的实现路径，有望成为LHC物理中新的基准观测量。

Abstract: We propose projected energy correlators measured on the recoiling QCD radiation of a $Z/γ$ as a benchmark observable for precision QCD at the LHC. Using the $Z/γ$ as a hard scale prevents the need for a jet algorithm, simplifying both the perturbative and non-perturbative corrections. We develop a framework to combine state-of-the-art fixed-order amplitudes, high order resummation, and universal non-perturbative matrix elements. Our approach is based on numerically computed inclusive hard functions, allowing flexibility in the process and the inclusion of realistic experimental cuts. We perform detailed numerical studies of the projected energy correlators at next-to-leading order + next-to-next-to-leading logarithm (NLO+NNLL) to verify the stability of our setup. We present numerical results at NLO+NNLL, which are the first complete matched predictions for energy correlators at the LHC at this order. We discuss the prospects for extensions to higher orders, outlining a path to NNLO calculations of energy correlators at the LHC.

</details>


### [169] [Leading order quark-antiquark-W-boson vertex in the presence of a magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14890)
*Alejandro Ayala,Alejandro Lopez,Ana Julia Mizher,Manuel Emiliano Monreal Cancino,Norberto Scoccola*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了恒定均匀磁场中场论中夸克-反夸克-W玻色子顶点的最低阶修正，具体针对u夸克和反d夸克情形，推导出任意朗道能级（Landau level）间跃迁的选择定则，并展示当初态粒子处于最低朗道能级时W+玻色子也在最低朗道能级产生且具有与磁场对齐的单偏振态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强磁场环境下（如中子星、早期宇宙磁化等离子体），量子电动力学与弱相互作用顶点的修正具有重要意义；理解磁化介质中夸克—W玻色子耦合的朗道能级结构与选择定则，是构建磁化环境中弱过程理论的基础。

Method: 在常且均匀的外磁场背景下，直接计算u夸克与反d夸克到W玻色子顶点的领头阶（leading order）修正，利用朗道量子化描述带电费米子与规范玻色子的态，推导一般朗道能级间的跃迁选择定则，并通过低朗道能级的算例验证一般结论，同时建立W玻色子偏振矢量与夸克、反夸克偏振态之间的对应关系。

Result: 推导出u、反d与W玻色子占据任意朗道能级时跃迁所满足的选择定则；特别地，当初态夸克与反夸克均处于最低朗道能级时，W+也仅在最低朗道能级产生，且其偏振方向唯一地平行于外磁场（single polarization aligned with the magnetic field）；通过对低朗道能级间跃迁的示例计算，展示了W玻色子偏振矢量与夸克—反夸克偏振态之间的显式对应。

Conclusion: 在强均匀磁场中，W玻色子与最低朗道能级夸克对的耦合被严格约束于最低朗道能级且有确定偏振方向，这为磁化环境中弱相互作用过程（如β衰变、中微子发射）的建模提供了明确的顶点修正与选择定则依据。

Abstract: We compute the leading order modification to the quark-antiquark-$W$-boson vertex in the presence of a constant and uniform magnetic field, specifically for the case where the quark and antiquark are the $u$ and $\bar{d}$, respectively. We find the selection rules for transitions where the $u$, $\bar{d}$, and $W$-boson occupy arbitrary Landau levels. We show that, for the particular case where the initial particles each occupy the lowest Landau level, the $W^+$ is also produced in the lowest Landau level with a single polarization aligned with the magnetic field. We also illustrate the general findings by computing the case of transitions between low lying Landau levels and show the correspondence between the polarization vectors for the $W$-boson and the polarization states for the quark and the antiquark.

</details>


### [170] [Dark Charge Conjugation and Complementary Constraints from $D^0$ Mixing and Rare Decays with Massless Dark Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15275)
*X. Zhong,X. Q. Shen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究暗电荷共轭（dark charge conjugation，$\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm D}$）如何组织涉及无质量暗光子（dark photon）的稀有 $D^0$ 衰变，并揭示这些衰变与中性粲介子混合（neutral charm mixing）在约束普适味改变相互作用时具有对称性分辨的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解无质量暗扇区中味改变相互作用的探测途径，并阐明稀有粲介子衰变与中性介子混合在约束此类相互作用中的互补关系。

Method: 在包含实赝标量媒介子（real pseudoscalar mediator）的简化框架中，分析 $D^0\to\gamma_{\mathrm D}\gamma_{\mathrm D}$（受 $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm D}$ 守恒门户控制）与 $D^0\to\gamma\gamma_{\mathrm D}$（受 $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm D}$ 破缺门户控制）两个衰变道；利用两衰变道与 $D^0$ 混合共享相同味归一化和媒介子传播子的特点，解析地消除味依赖性，将混合约束转化为不可见衰变率的直接上限。

Result: 结合混合、不可见衰变和单暗光子通道的现有约束，在媒介子参数空间中识别出由混合主导与由不可见衰变主导区域的转变；在味归一化固定于混合和不可见衰变允许的最大值时，单暗光子搜索给出 $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm D}$ 破缺门户的条件性上限。

Conclusion: 结果表明，中性介子混合与稀有粲衰变在探测涉及无质量暗扇区的味改变相互作用方面展现出对称性分辨的互补性。

Abstract: We investigate how dark charge conjugation organizes rare $D^0$ decays involving a massless dark photon and how these decays complement neutral charm mixing in constraining a common flavor changing interaction. Within a simplified framework with a real pseudoscalar mediator, $D^0\toγ_{\mathrm D}γ_{\mathrm D}$ probes the portal that preserves $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm D}$, whereas $D^0\toγγ_{\mathrm D}$ probes the portal associated with $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm D}$ breaking. Because the two decay channels and $D^0$ mixing share the same flavor normalization and mediator propagator, the flavor dependence can be eliminated analytically, converting the mixing constraint into a direct upper bound on the invisible decay rate. Combining current constraints from mixing, invisible decay, and the single dark photon channel, we identify a transition between regions dominated by mixing and regions dominated by invisible decay in mediator parameter space. The single dark photon search then yields conditional upper limits on the portal associated with $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm D}$ breaking once the flavor normalization is fixed at its maximal value allowed by mixing and invisible decay. These results demonstrate a symmetry resolved complementarity between neutral meson mixing and rare charm decays in probing flavor changing interactions with a massless dark sector.

</details>


### [171] [Exploring charmonia $η_c(6S,7S)$ in the $Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$ invariant mass spectrum of $B^0 \to K_S^0Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15524)
*Cheng-Xi Liu,Jun-Zhang Wang,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了LHCb实验观测到的$B^0 \to K_S^0 Λ_c^+ \barΛ_c^-$衰变过程，针对4.63 GeV处的质量增强现象，使用包含非共振项、两个已知$Ξ_c$态及高激发粲偶素态（charmonium states）的振幅模型进行研究，并分别检验了固定质量下的$η_c(6S)$和$η_c(7S)$贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb实验近期观测到$B^0 \to K_S^0 Λ_c^+ \barΛ_c^-$衰变，其中$Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$不变质量谱在4.63 GeV附近出现显著的积聚（accumulation），其来源的性质尚不明确。该研究旨在通过振幅分析（amplitude analysis）理解这一增强结构的物理起源。

Method: 构建包含非共振项、两个已确立的$Ξ_c$态（$Ξ_c(2923)$和$Ξ_c(2939)$）以及$Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$道中高激发粲偶素态的振幅模型。以屏蔽Godfrey–Isgur势和夸克对产生（quark-pair-creation）计算结果为指导，固定$η_c(6S)$在(4629.00, 24.20) MeV和$η_c(7S)$在(4718.84, 22.84) MeV的质量与宽度进行拟合；同时通过强子圈（hadron-loop）机制评估$η_c(nS) \to Λ_c^+ \barΛ_c^-$重子衰变，并在朴素因子化（naive factorization）框架下估算$B^0 \to K_S^0 η_c(nS)$产生率。

Result: 在窄宽度近似（narrow width approximation）下，拟合给出$\mathcal{B}(B^0\to K_S^0η_c(6S)) \cdot \mathcal{B}(η_c(6S)\toΛ_c^+\barΛ_c^-) \approx 6.2 \times 10^{-6}$。而因子化和强子圈计算给出的速率比上述拟合比低了约一到三个数量级。$η_c(6S)$贡献改善了4.63 GeV区域的描述，$η_c(7S)$贡献则改善了谱的较高质量区域。

Conclusion: 模型与计算之间的显著差异表明，需要更精确的实验数据和完整的振幅分析来确定线形（line shape），并澄清高质量区域增强贡献的性质。

Abstract: We analyze the $B^0 \to K_S^0 Λ_c^+ \barΛ_c^-$ decay recently observed by the LHCb Collaboration. The $Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$ invariant-mass spectrum exhibits an accumulation near 4.63~GeV, which we investigate together with the accompanying $Λ_c^+ K_S^0$ spectrum constrained by the known $Ξ_c(2923)$ and $Ξ_c(2939)$ states. Our amplitude model includes a nonresonant term, the two established $Ξ_c$ contributions, and high-lying charmonium states in the $Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$ channel. Guided by screened Godfrey--Isgur and quark-pair-creation calculations, we test the 4.63~GeV enhancement with a fixed $η_c(6S)$ amplitude at $(4629.00, 24.20)$~MeV, {which improves the description of the 4.63~GeV region}, and examine a fixed $η_c(7S)$ contribution at $(4718.84, 22.84)$~MeV, which improves the higher-mass region of the spectrum. We also evaluate the $η_c(nS) \to Λ_c^+ \barΛ_c^-$ baryonic decays via a hadron-loop mechanism and estimate the $B^0 \to K_S^0 η_c(nS)$ production in naive factorization. Within the narrow width approximation, the fit yields a $\mathcal B(B^0\to K_S^0η_c(6S))
  \mathcal B(η_c(6S)\toΛ_c^+\barΛ_c^-)$ of about $6.2 \times 10^{-6}$, while the factorization and hadron-loop calculations give rates roughly one to three orders of magnitude lower than the above fitted ratio. These results highlight the need for more precise data and a full amplitude analysis to determine the line shapes and clarify the nature of the higher-mass contribution.

</details>


### [172] [Probing 5.49 MeV Solar Axions at Xenon Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15699)
*Ruofei Feng,Shao-Feng Ge,Oleg Titov,Yongchao Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了在暗物质直接探测实验中利用氙靶搜寻单色5.49 MeV太阳轴子（solar axion）的可行性，该轴子由等矢量耦合g_{3aN}诱导产生。研究发现，下一代氙实验可超越现有Borexino实验的约束，在200和1000吨·年的曝光量下，对|g_{3aN}g_{ae}|的探测灵敏度分别可达1.59×10^-14和7.12×10^-15（90%置信水平），对|g_{3aN}g_{aγ}|的灵敏度可达6.76×10^-12 GeV^-1和3.02×10^-12 GeV^-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳轴子作为暗物质与粒子物理交叉领域的重要研究对象，其由等矢量耦合诱导产生的5.49 MeV单色信号可在现有暗物质直接探测实验中进行搜寻，本文旨在评估相关轴子耦合常数的探测前景。

Method: 针对轴子质量m_a < 1 MeV的氙靶情形，通过考虑轴子-电子耦合g_{ae}，分析轴子诱导的e^+e^-对产生过程作为主导信号，并利用该过程截面随原子序数平方Z^2增强的特性来评估下一代氙实验的探测灵敏度。

Result: 在200和1000吨·年的曝光量下，|g_{3aN}g_{ae}|可分别被探测至1.59×10^-14和7.12×10^-15（90% C.L.）；若轴子与光子耦合，|g_{3aN}g_{aγ}|的预计灵敏度分别可达6.76×10^-12 GeV^-1和3.02×10^-12 GeV^-1。

Conclusion: 下一代氙基暗物质直接探测实验凭借Z^2增强效应，有望超越现有Borexino约束，并达到与JUNO和Hyper-Kamiokande等大型中微子探测器相当甚至更具竞争力的灵敏度。

Abstract: The monochromatic 5.49 MeV solar axions induced by the isovector coupling $g_{3aN}$ can be searched for at the dark matter direct detection experiments. In this paper we estimate the prospects of the relevant axion couplings for axion mass $m_{a} <$ 1 MeV with xenon targets. Given the axion-electron coupling $g_{ae}$, the signal is dominated by the axion-induced $e^+ e^-$ pair production whose cross section is largely enhanced when the axion mass approaches twice of the electron mass. Furthermore, the cross section depends on the atomic number squared $Z^2$. This allows the next-generation xenon experiments to surpass the current Borexino constraints and provide sensitivities competitive with those of the large neutrino detectors such as JUNO and Hyper-Kamiokande. With an exposure of 200 and 1000 ton$\cdot$yr, the couplings $|g_{3aN} g_{ae}|$ can be probed down to $1.59\times10^{-14}$ and $7.12\times10^{-15}$ at 90% C.L., respectively. If the axion couples to photons, the projected sensitivities on $|g_{3aN}g_{aγ}|$ can touch down to $6.76\times10^{-12}$ GeV$^{-1}$ and $3.02\times10^{-12}$ GeV$^{-1}$, respectively.

</details>


### [173] [Probing T and CP Violation at DUNE and T2HK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15960)
*Sabya Sachi Chatterjee,Sudhanwa Patra,Thomas Schwetz,Kiran Sharma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了DUNE和T2HK长基线实验中中微子振荡对时间反演（T）对称性破坏的灵敏度，提出了一种不依赖交换初末味态的新方法，即利用固定能量下ν_μ→ν_e跃迁概率对基线长度L的依赖关系来探测T破坏，结果显示两实验联合可达到约4σ显著性，且两者在CP相位搜索上互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究中微子振动中时间反演（T）破坏的方法需要交换初始和末态中微子味态，本文旨在提出一种替代性的探测T破坏的新方案。此外，作者希望评估DUNE与T2HK在搜索CP相位δ_CP时各自的优势和互补性。

Method: 本文不采用交换初末味态的传统途径，而是在标准三味框架（standard three-flavour framework）下，仅使用中微子数据，通过固定中微子能量E_ν时ν_μ→ν_e跃迁概率对基线长度L的依赖关系来搜索L奇性（L-odd）分量，从而探测时间反演对称性破坏。研究中利用DUNE和T2HK的联合数据分析，并特别关注DUNE的第二个振荡极大（second oscillation maximum）的作用。

Result: DUNE与T2HK联合可在E_ν∈[0.68, 0.92] GeV能量区间达到约4σ显著性，用以确立振荡概率中L奇性分量的存在。研究还发现，DUNE在中微子-only模式下对T破坏更敏感，而T2HK则在传统的中微子与反中微子比较中提供更好的灵敏度，两者在CP相位δ_CP搜索中具有互补性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种仅基于中微子数据探测时间反演对称性破坏的新方法，并通过DUNE与T2HK的联合分析表明其可行性与互补性优势，为未来中微子实验中CP相位δ_CP的搜寻提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We study the sensitivity of the DUNE and T2HK long-baseline experiments to time reversal (T) violation in neutrino oscillations. Rather than the conventional approach of exchanging initial and final neutrino flavors, we search for T violation through the $L$-dependence of the $ν_μ\to ν_e$ transition probability at fixed neutrino energy using neutrino data only. Within the standard three-flavour framework, we show that the DUNE and T2HK together can establish the presence of an $L$-odd component in the oscillation probability at up to $\sim 4σ$ significance, with the optimal sensitivity in the energy range $E_ν\in [0.68, 0.92]$ GeV. The second oscillation maximum of DUNE plays a crucial role in this analysis. We further show that DUNE is more sensitive to T violation that is running in neutrino-only mode, whereas T2HK provides better sensitivity in the conventional neutrino versus anti-neutrino comparison, making the two experiments complementary to each other in search of the CP phase $δ_{\rm CP}$.

</details>


### [174] [Assessing Parameter Redundancy in Transformers for Jet Tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16061)
*Huitong Cheng,Yabo Dong,Jun Fan,Kun Wang,Haijun Yang,Jingya Zhu,Yifan Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过引入沙漏（hourglass）结构和轻量级粒子嵌入层，将Particle Transformer（ParT）和MIParT两种Transformer基喷注标记器的参数量分别减少至原模型的约48%和39.7%，同时保持相当的标记性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer基喷注标记器（如ParT和MIParT）虽能通过利用喷注组成部分间的关联获得优异判别性能，但通常需要比早期深度学习标记器更多的可训练参数，因此本文旨在探究能否以大幅减少的参数实现相当的判别能力。

Method: 提出沙漏结构，替换注意力块中的前馈网络（feed-forward networks，FFNs），同时保持粒子相互作用注意力不变；并引入轻量级粒子嵌入层以替代原密集嵌入网络。将这两种修改分别应用于ParT和MIParT，得到沙漏变体ParT-HG和MIParT-HG。

Result: 在top标记和夸克-胶子判别基准数据集上，两种变体在固定信号效率下的背景拒绝率等标记性能与基线相当，而参数量仅为基线的大约48%和39.7%。在更大规模的JetClass数据集上，准确率和AUC下降不超过1%，部分信号类别的背景拒绝率也有下降。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种减少Transformer喷注标记器参数数量的替代途径，同时在大体上保留其标记性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based jet taggers, such as the Particle Transformer (ParT) and the More-Interaction Particle Transformer (MIParT), achieve excellent discrimination by exploiting correlations among jet constituents, but often require more trainable parameters than earlier deep-learning taggers. In this paper, we investigate whether comparable discriminating power can be achieved with substantially fewer parameters. We introduce an hourglass structure that replaces the feed-forward networks (FFNs) in the attention blocks while leaving the particle-interaction attention unchanged. We also introduce a lightweight particle-embedding layer to replace the original dense embedding network. Applying both modifications to ParT and MIParT yields the hourglass (HG) variants ParT-HG and MIParT-HG, respectively. We evaluate both models on benchmark datasets for top tagging and quark-gluon discrimination. Both variants retain comparable tagging performance, including background rejection at fixed signal efficiencies, while using only approximately 48% and 39.7% of the parameters of their respective baselines. On the larger JetClass dataset, accuracy and AUC decrease by less than 1%, and background rejection also decreases for several signal classes. Overall, our approach provides an alternative way to reduce the parameter count of Transformer jet taggers while largely retaining their tagging performance.

</details>


### [175] [Extending the Constituent Gluon Model to Heavy-Flavour Hybrids: A Unified Study of $c\bar{c}g$ Mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16250)
*Qi Huang,Zhe-Tao Miu,Rui Chen,Xiao-Huang Hu,Yue Tan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在组分胶子（constituent gluon）模型框架下研究了基态粲偶素混杂子（charmonium hybrids）的质量谱和两体强衰变性质，仅引入一个新参数（组分胶子质量$m_g$=450 MeV），其他参数直接取自普通介子谱的成功描述，计算结果与格点QCD、势模型和其他方法高度一致，并针对$1^{-+}$和$2^{+-}$等奇特量子数态给出实验搜索建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粲偶素混杂子的质量和衰变性质尚缺乏统一且一致的描述，需要一种能够在单一理论框架内同时准确预测其能谱和强衰变宽度的模型，以指导未来实验探测。

Method: 基于非微扰QCD赋予胶子有效质量的假设，在手性夸克模型（chiral quark model）框架中加入组分胶子自由度，引入唯象参数$m_g=450$ MeV（取自轻混杂子研究），其余参数直接沿用描述普通介子谱的成功取值；系统计算不同量子数下基态粲偶素混杂子的能谱和两体强衰变宽度。

Result: 计算的粲偶素混杂子能谱与格点QCD（lattice QCD）、势模型（potential model）及其他方法的结果保持一致，对应的衰变宽度也处于合理范围。体系建议实验优先搜索奇特态$1^{-+}$和$2^{+-}$，它们分别显著衰变到$D\bar{D}_1$和$D\bar{D}_2^*$通道；普通量子数中的$0^{-+}$、$2^{-+}$和$1^{+-}$态因显著衰变到轨道激发粲介子也值得关注。

Conclusion: 该模型为粲偶素混杂子提供了统一而自洽的描述，并为未来实验确认这些态给出了明确指引。

Abstract: We investigate the mass spectra and two-body strong decay properties of ground charmonium hybrids within the framework of a constituent gluon model. Based on the assumption that non-perturbative QCD endows the gluon with an effective mass, we extend the chiral quark model by introducing a single new parameter, the constituent gluon mass $m_g=450$~MeV, which is fixed from previous studies of light hybrids, while other parameters are taken directly from successful descriptions of ordinary meson spectra. We systematically compute the spectra for various quantum numbers and find good agreement with results from lattice QCD, potential models, and other approaches. The corresponding decay widths are also reasonable. For experimental searches, we recommend focusing on the exotic $1^{-+}$ and $2^{+-}$ states, which decay prominently into $D\bar{D}_1$ and $D\bar{D}_2^*$ channels, respectively. Among ordinary quantum numbers, the $0^{-+}$, $2^{-+}$, and $1^{+-}$ states with significant decays into orbitally excited charm mesons are also suggested. Our results provide a unified and consistent description of charmonium hybrids and offer clear guidance for future experimental identification.

</details>


### [176] [Amplified Isospin Breaking from Coupled-Channel Dynamics Near Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16266)
*Xiang-Kun Dong,Feng-Kun Guo,Christoph Hanhart,Teng Ji,Ulf-G. Meißner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别了近阈值态（near-threshold states）中放大同位旋破缺（isospin breaking）的动力学起源。通过基于极点留数（pole-residue）的度量而非衰变观测量，作者证明仅靠阈值接近不够，需要伴随同位旋通道中存在可观的相互作用。将该判据应用于X(3872)，观测到的大同位旋破缺指向邻近的伙伴极点W_{c1}，并揭示了其在同位旋极限下与纯I=1本征态及影极点的连续连接关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作指出近阈值态中同位旋破缺可能被放大，但其动力学起源尚未阐明；同时，传统基于衰变观测量的度量受最终态运动学（final-state kinematics）影响，无法纯粹反映极点耦合中的同位旋破缺效应。

Method: 作者引入一种基于极点耦合直接定义的极点-留数度量，避免衰变观测量中不同最终态运动学的干扰；通过比较阈值邻近性（threshold proximity）与伴随通道相互作用强度，系统考察放大的同位旋破缺所需的条件，并将其应用于具体态X(3872)的分析。

Result: 研究发现仅靠极点接近阈值并不足以产生大的同位旋破缺，还必须依赖伴随同位旋通道中显著的相互作用。将该判据应用于X(3872)后，观测到的大同位旋破缺指向一个邻近的伙伴极点W_{c1}；此外，W_{c1}极点在物理情形下主要影响带电阈值附近的线形，但在同位旋极限下并不成为纯I=1本征态，该本征态与一个更远的影极点（shadow pole）连续相连。

Conclusion: 同位旋破缺的放大并非单纯由近阈值几何效应决定，而是由近邻通道相互作用的动力学强度所控制；X(3872)的数据支持存在W_{c1}伙伴极点，且模型在I=1通道展现出非平凡的本征态拓扑结构。

Abstract: We identify the dynamical origin of amplified isospin breaking in near-threshold states. Using a pole-residue-based measure defined directly from the pole couplings, rather than from decay observables that are also affected by different final-state kinematics, we show that threshold proximity of the pole alone is insufficient: large isospin breaking requires a sizable interaction in the companion isospin channel. Applied to the $X(3872)$, the observed large isospin breaking points to a nearby partner pole, $W_{c1}$, as predicted in previous studies. In addition, we find that the $W_{c1}$ pole, which predominantly affects the line shapes near the charged threshold in the physical case, does not evolve into the pure $I=1$ eigenstate in the isospin limit; instead, that eigenstate is continuously connected to a more distant shadow pole.

</details>


### [177] [Exotics with gluonic excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16784)
*Jozef J. Dudek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文回顾了量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics）中胶子强自相互作用所支撑的强子态——胶球（glueball）与杂交态（hybrid）——的当代理论理解，并讨论了在实验强子谱中明确识别这些态的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子色动力学中胶子的强自耦合暗示可能存在胶子场起关键作用的强子态，包括无需夸克参与的胶球以及激发胶子场与夸克耦合的杂交态；对这些态的理论理解与其实验识别的难度构成研究动机。

Method: 基于当代理论框架（如格点QCD等）综述胶球与杂交态的性质，并系统分析在实验强子谱中鉴定这些态所面临的理论与实验挑战。

Result: 本章呈现了当前关于这类强子态的理论图景，同时指出在其与实验强子谱对应、从而确切识别方面仍存在显著困难。

Conclusion: 胶球与杂交态是量子色动力学理论的重要推论，但其在实验中的确定性甄别仍是尚未完全解决的开放问题。

Abstract: That the gluons of quantum chromodynamics self-interact with a strong coupling suggests the possibility of hadrons in which the gluonic field plays an essential role: Glueballs in which gluons stick together to produce a hadron in which no quarks are required, and hybrids in which an excited gluonic field couples to quarks. This chapter presents our contemporary theoretical understanding of such states, and the challenges associated with definitively identifying their presence within the experimental spectrum of hadrons.

</details>


### [178] [First Order Phase Transition Induced Graviton Bremsstrahlung: A Multi Peak Gravitational Wave Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14782)
*Dipendu Bhandari,Rajat Kumar Mandal,Arunansu Sil*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类全新的引力波（gravitational waves, GWs）微观产生机制：在一级相变（first order phase transitions, FOPTs）中，驱动相变的标量场衰变过程中因引力子轫致辐射（graviton bremsstrahlung）会额外产生随机引力波背景，该信号与传统的泡碰撞、声波和等离子体湍流来源互补，并可能呈现多峰谱结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，一级相变产生的引力波被认为来源于泡碰撞、声波和等离子体湍流等宏观过程；本文旨在探索标量场衰变过程中由引力子轫致辐射产生的新微观引力波源，以补充并深化对一级相变引力波物理的理解。

Method: 作者指出，在存在标量场与轻费米子的汤川相互作用（Yukawa interactions）的前提下，由于引力对能量-动量张量的普适耦合，标量场衰变必然伴随引力子发射。基于这一普适性论证，推导出这些引力子在一级相变时期产生的额外随机引力波背景及其谱形特征。

Result: 该机制产生的引力波谱可呈现特征性的多峰结构（multi-peaked structure），与传统的泡碰撞、声波和湍流信号叠加互补，共同构成一级相变的完整引力波信号。

Conclusion: 与传统场景中不同引力波分量来自不同宇宙学时期不同，本文所述的所有引力波贡献均来源于同一级相变的动力学过程，从而为同时探测标量场的微观粒子动力学与宏观等离子体演化提供了独特的探针。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) from first order phase transitions (FOPTs) are conventionally sourced by bubble collisions, sound waves, and plasma turbulence. We propose a novel microscopic GW source arising from graviton bremsstrahlung during the decay of the scalar field driving the FOPT. In the presence of Yukawa interactions with light fermions, the scalar decay is inevitably accompanied by graviton emission due to the universal coupling of gravity to the energy-momentum tensor. We show that these gravitons generate an additional stochastic GW background during the phase-transition epoch, complementing the conventional FOPT signal. The resulting GW spectrum can exhibit a characteristic multi-peaked structure. Unlike scenarios in which different GW components originate from distinct cosmological epochs, all contributions considered here emerge from the dynamics of the same FOPT, offering a unique probe of both its microscopic particle dynamics and macroscopic plasma evolution.

</details>


### [179] [Uncovering Hidden Leptonic Correlations with Flow Matching and Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15042)
*Haruto Kitagawa,Satsuki Nishimura,Hajime Otsuka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用生成式人工智能方法（flow matching）对Type-I seesaw机制中的Yukawa矩阵和Majorana质量进行全局搜索，生成大量与实验测得的轻子扇区中微子质量平方差和混合角一致的解，并运用自编码器（autoencoder）揭示出涉及中微子质量与CP相位的新非线性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解轻子扇区质量层级和混合模式（mixing patterns）的起源，是粒子物理中代际结构（flavor structure）研究的核心挑战。传统解析方法难以在Type-I seesaw机制庞大的参数空间中高效搜索同时满足实验约束的解，亟需新的计算工具。

Method: 首先采用flow matching——一种生成式人工智能（generative AI）方法——在Type-I seesaw机制的Yukawa矩阵和Majorana质量参数空间中进行全局搜索，生成大量能复现实验测得的中微子质量平方差和混合角的解；随后应用自编码器（autoencoder）这一机器学习方法，对生成解的轻子扇区物理量进行降维分析，以揭示非平凡的相关性。

Result: 分析发现了涉及中微子质量与CP相位之间新的非线性关系（non-linear relations），这些关系此前未被传统方法识别。

Conclusion: 这些新发现的非线性关系可能有助于阐明质量层级（mass hierarchies）和代际混合模式（mixing patterns）的起源，为理解轻子扇区的代际结构提供新的理论洞见。

Abstract: We perform a global search for values of the Yukawa matrices and Majorana masses in the Type-I seesaw mechanism. Using flow matching, which is a generative artificial intelligence (generative AI) method, we generate a broad set of solutions reproducing the experimentally measured values of the neutrino mass-squared differences and the mixing angles. Then, a machine learning method known as an autoencoder is applied to uncover non-trivial correlations among physical quantities in the lepton sector. Our analysis reveals new non-linear relations involving neutrino masses and CP phases. These findings may contribute to elucidating the origins of the mass hierarchies and mixing patterns among generation structure.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [On a class of 3D second-order integrable Lagrangians and their dispersive deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15155)
*Lingling Xue,E. V. Ferapontov,M. V. Pavlov*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究三维二阶拉格朗日量密度 f(u_xy, u_xt, u_yt) 对应的欧拉-拉格朗日方程的可积性。研究表明恰好存在四种不同类型的此类拉格朗日密度，其中第一种为 f=√(u_xy u_xt u_yt)，第二种和第三种更复杂但可用初等函数表示，第四种最一般，需用洛巴切夫斯基函数（Lobachevsky function）表达，揭示了与球面/双曲三角学及Schläfly型公式的联系。论文还构造了无色散Lax对及可积色散形变；第一类的色散形变与经典达布（Darboux）系统拉格朗日量一致，其余三类分别提供半离散和全离散达布系统的拉格朗日表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维二阶拉格朗日密度 f(u_{xy},u_{xt},u_{yt}) 对应欧拉-拉格朗日方程的可积性（integrability），旨在系统分类此类系统并揭示其与球面/双曲三角学及离散可积系统的深层联系。

Method: 通过分析形式为 ∫f(u_{xy},u_{xt},u_{yt})dxdydt 的三维二阶拉格朗日量，分类可能的拉格朗日密度 f；构造相应欧拉-拉格朗日方程的无色散Lax对（dispersionless Lax pairs）及可积色散形变，并将其与达布系统建立联系。

Result: 得到恰好四类拉格朗日密度：f=√(u_{xy}u_{xt}u_{yt})，两个以初等函数表示的更复杂形式，以及一个需用洛巴切夫斯基函数表达的最一般形式。第一类拉格朗日密度的色散形变恰好是 R³ 中三重正交坐标系理论中经典达布系统的拉格朗日量；其余三类的色散形变分别给出具有一个、两个和三个离散变量的半离散及全离散达布系统的拉格朗日表述。

Conclusion: 四类拉格朗日密度均对应可积的欧拉-拉格朗日方程，且其可积色散形变以统一的拉格朗日框架刻画了经典达布系统及其半离散和全离散版本，揭示了连续与离散可积系统之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We investigate integrability of Euler-Lagrange equations associated with 3D second-order Lagrangians of the form \begin{equation*} \int f(u_{xy},u_{xt},u_{yt})\ \text{d}x\text{d}y\text{d}t. \end{equation*} It is demonstrated that there are exactly four different types of such Lagrangian densities $f$: the first one is given by the formula $f=\sqrt{u_{xy}u_{xt}u_{yt}}$, the second and the third are more complicated (although still representable in elementary functions), whereas the most generic fourth one is expressible in terms of the Lobachevsky function, revealing unexpected links to spherical/hyperbolic trigonometry and Schläfly-type formulas. Dispersionless Lax pairs and integrable dispersive deformations of the corresponding Euler-Lagrange equations are also constructed.Remarkably, dispersive deformation of the first Lagrangian density coincides with the Lagrangian of the classical Darboux system arising in the theory of triply-orthogonal coordinate systems in $\mathbb{R}^3$. Dispersive deformations of the three other cases provide Lagrangian formulation of semi-discrete and fully discrete versions of the Darboux system, with one, two and three discrete variables, respectively.

</details>


### [181] [Poisson structures for a similarity reduction of the Drinfeld-Sokolov hierarchy of type $A$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15484)
*Takao Suzuki*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究A型Drinfeld-Sokolov（德林菲尔德-索科洛夫）层级及其相似性约化（similarity reduction），将其表示为两类哈密顿系统，并探讨其与等单值变形系统（isomonodromy deformation system）之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在深入理解A型Drinfeld-Sokolov层级的相似性约化结构，并通过哈密顿系统形式化表达，探索其与等单值变形系统之间的内在关联。

Method: 考虑A型Drinfeld-Sokolov层级及其相似性约化，将约化表述为两类哈密顿系统——一类带有泊松括号（Poisson bracket），另一类带有正则坐标（canonical coordinates）的泊松括号，并研究约化与等单值变形系统之间的联系。

Result: 将相似性约化成功表达为两种类型的哈密顿系统，并建立了相似性约化与等单值变形系统之间的关联。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了A型Drinfeld-Sokolov层级相似性约化的哈密顿结构，及其与等单值变形系统的联系，为相关可积系统理论提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In this article we consider the Drinfeld-Sokolov hierarchy of type $A$ and its similarity reduction. Then the similarity reduction is expressed as $2$ types of Hamiltonian systems, one is with a Poisson bracket and another a Poisson bracket of canonical coordinates. We also investigate a connection between the similarity reduction and the isomonodromy deformation system.

</details>


### [182] [Novel lump solutions of the modified Kadomtsev-Petviashvili-I equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16296)
*Tianwei Qiu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文通过广义长波极限法（generalized long-wave limit method）结合谱扰动，首次构造了修正Kadomtsev-Petviashvili-I（mKP-I）方程的二阶和三阶怪波解（lump solutions），并通过渐近分析揭示了其与KP-I方程在碰撞动力学上的关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前mKP-I方程尚未有高阶怪波解的研究报道，构造此类解并进行动力学分析有助于理解修正型可积系统与标准可积系统之间相互作用机制的差异。

Method: 采用广义长波极限法结合谱扰动（spectral perturbations），通过调节孤子解中的相位参数（phase parameters）获得高阶怪波解，并对其进行详细的渐近分析（asymptotic analysis）。

Result: 获得了mKP-I方程的二阶和三阶怪波解，渐近分析显示这些退化怪波表现出与KP-I方程类似的异常散射（anomalous scattering）行为；但与KP-I情形不同，碰撞后不发生完整的能量均分（energy equipartition），两个怪波在渐近振幅的次主导项（subleading terms）中仍保持可区分性。

Conclusion: mKP-I方程与KP-I方程的怪波碰撞动力学存在本质差异，这凸显了修正型与标准型KP-I方程相互作用动力学的不同。

Abstract: We construct novel higher-order lump solutions of the modified Kadomtsev-Petviashvili-I (mKP-I) equation by applying the generalized long-wave limit method with spectral perturbations. By tuning the phase parameters in the soliton solution, we obtain second- and third-order lump solutions, which, to the best of our knowledge, are reported here for the first time for the mKP-I equation. A detailed asymptotic analysis reveals that these degenerate lumps exhibit anomalous scattering analogous to that in the KP-I equation. However, in contrast to the KP-I case, no complete energy equipartition occurs after the collision, and the two lumps remain distinguishable in the subleading terms of their asymptotic amplitudes. This distinction highlights a difference in the interaction dynamics between the modified and standard KP-I equations.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Trimer Thouless Pump: Topology, Symmetries, and Multigap Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14745)
*Rittwik Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了一个最小三带推广模型——三聚体 Thouless 泵（Trimer Thouless Pump, TTP），通过循环绝热调制一维三格点系统的跳跃振幅和反对称在位势，实现了具有两个独立体隙的多间隙拓扑输运，并揭示了中间能带作为几何中介者在上下能带之间交换量子化 Berry 通量的关键角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的拓扑电荷泵通常基于两带系统（如 Rice-Mele 模型），这类模型本质上只能支持单一独立的泵浦通道。作者旨在突破这一限制，探索多间隙（multigap）拓扑输运的最小实现。

Method: 引入三格点（三能带）紧束缚模型，对跳跃振幅和反对称在位势实施循环绝热调制，构建参数环面上的 Berry 曲率与陈数分析，并通过谱流和相图计算验证拓扑性质。最后提出基于超冷原子在相位控制光学超晶格中的实验方案。

Result: 发现量子化电荷输运由参数环面上高度局域化的 Berry 曲率热点驱动，对应有效两能级 Dirac 回避交叉。中间能带作为几何中介，促成上下能带间 Berry 通量交换而自身陈数净值为零；边界局域边缘态的谱流跨越多个间隙，验证了体拓扑；相图显示中心格点失谐诱导拓扑不变量的带选择性转移。

Conclusion: TTP 提供了多间隙拓扑输运的最小三带实现，其机制（中间带的几何中介作用）拓展了拓扑电荷泵的理论框架，并通过超冷原子实验方案为多带拓扑泵浦的观测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Topological charge pumping is paradigmatically understood through two-band systems such as the Rice-Mele model, which are intrinsically restricted to a single independent pumping channel. In this work, we introduce the Trimer Thouless Pump (TTP), a minimal three-band generalization that exhibits genuinely multigap topological transport. By subjecting a one-dimensional three-site lattice to cyclic adiabatic modulations of its hopping amplitudes and antisymmetric onsite potentials, we explore a topological regime characterized by two independent bulk gaps. We show that the quantized charge transport is driven by highly localized Berry curvature hotspots corresponding to effective two-level Dirac avoided crossings on the parameter torus. Crucially, we demonstrate that the middle energy band acts as a geometric mediator: it facilitates the exchange of quantized Berry flux between the upper and lower bands while maintaining a net zero Chern number itself. This bulk topology is corroborated by the spectral flow of boundary-localized edge states traversing multiple gaps. Furthermore, we map the topological phase diagram as a function of central-site detuning, illustrating a band-selective transfer of topological invariants across discrete phase transitions. Finally, we propose a concrete experimental protocol to realize the TTP and observe its multigap charge transport using ultracold atoms in phase-controlled optical superlattices.

</details>


### [184] [Spectral preservation under momentum-dependent similarity transformations in non-Hermitian lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14775)
*Ye Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了在开放边界条件（open boundary conditions, OBC）下，动量依赖的相似变换（momentum-dependent similarity transformations）保持晶格哈密顿量谱性质的充分与必要条件。研究证明了进行非对角化约化所需的常变换条件，提出了广义布里渊区（generalized Brillouin zone）与变换行列式零点之间的“两半径条件”（two-radius condition），并厘清了变换的两侧性（two-sidedness）仅影响有限个边界本征值而非体态谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷系统中动量依赖的相似变换能精确保持系统谱，但在有限系统中，由于实空间逆变换的长程性质带来了复杂性，因此需要严格判定有限开放边界条件下谱保持的具体条件。

Method: 对于一般的无迹2×2哈密顿量，推导了通过常数相似变换约化为斜对角形式（skew-diagonal form）的充要条件并给出显式变换矩阵；在此基础上建立了动量依赖变换保持体态谱的严格条件。

Result: 证明了体态谱保持需要满足两个条件：广义布里渊区最大值小于$\det S(z)$的最小零点（即$r_{\mathrm{GBZ}}^{\max}<z_{\min}$，两半径条件），以及$H$本身谱稳定（无非临界非厄米趋肤效应，non-Hermitian skin effect）。同时发现$S(z)$的两侧性仅改变有限数量的边界本征值，不影响体态。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了在变换坐标系下计算的体态拓扑不变量（bulk topological invariants）能否可靠预测边界物理的条件，对非厄米系统、光子晶体及其他仅在适当基变换后才出现手性（chiral）或隐藏对称性的平台具有重要启示。

Abstract: We investigate the conditions under which momentum-dependent similarity transformations preserve spectral properties of lattice Hamiltonians with open boundary conditions (OBC). While such transformations exactly preserve spectra in infinite systems, their application to finite systems introduces subtleties due to the long-range nature of the inverse transformation in real space. For general traceless $2\times 2$ Hamiltonians, we derive necessary and sufficient conditions for reduction to skew-diagonal form via constant similarity transforms, providing explicit transformation matrices for all cases. We then establish rigorous conditions for bulk spectral preservation under momentum-dependent transformations: the generalized Brillouin zone of $H$ must lie inside the smallest zero of $\det S(z)$ (the two-radius condition $r_{\mathrm{GBZ}}^{\max}<z_{\min}$), together with a spectral-stability (no critical non-Hermitian skin effect) condition on $H$. Two-sidedness of $S(z)$ governs only the modification of a finite number of boundary eigenvalues, not the bulk. Our results establish when bulk topological invariants computed in transformed coordinates reliably predict boundary physics, with implications for non-Hermitian systems, photonic crystals, and other platforms where chiral or hidden symmetries emerge only after appropriate basis changes.

</details>


### [185] [Interaction driven charge transfer transitions in closely spaced graphene double layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14805)
*Kenneth A. Lin,Unmesh Ghorai,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Emanuel Tutuc,Rafi Bistritzer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过隧穿谱（tunneling spectroscopy）实验和理论对比，揭示了紧密间距石墨烯双层量子霍尔铁磁体（quantum Hall ferromagnet）中的电荷转移并非传统的单粒子过程，而是由电容充电与库仑交换相互作用竞争所驱动的系列相互作用诱导的相变序列。研究发现层内交换倾向于整条自旋-谷子带（spin-valley subband）的突跃式转移，而层间交换则稳定了实现渐进电荷转移的相干中间相。该电荷转移会重构准粒子谱（quasiparticle spectrum），并将层间隧穿确立为探测相互作用电子系统的一种强有力手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为导体间的电荷转移是由静电学和能带对齐（band alignment）决定的单粒子过程，但该框架无法描述强关联体系中的电荷转移行为。本文旨在揭示紧密间距石墨烯双层量子霍尔铁磁体中电荷转移的真实物理机制，并建立相应的实验探测手段。

Method: 采用隧穿谱方法对紧密间距的石墨烯双层量子霍尔铁磁体进行实验测量，并将实验结果与理论计算进行对比分析。通过识别相互作用驱动的电荷转移相变的谱学特征，确定了该过程中层内与层间交换相互作用的竞争机制。

Result: 实验与理论对比识别出相互作用驱动电荷转移跃迁的光谱学特征，并表明该电荷转移重构了准粒子谱。发现层内交换作用（intralayer exchange）促使整个自旋-谷子带在层间突跃式转移，而层间交换作用（interlayer exchange）则稳定了能够实现渐进电荷转移的相干中间相。

Conclusion: 研究确立了层间隧穿（interlayer tunneling）作为探测相互作用电子系统的强有力探针，特别是在准粒子谱本身依赖于偏压（bias）的系统中。该工作表明紧密间距石墨烯双层中的电荷转移由相互作用驱动的相变序列主导，而非传统的单粒子静电过程。

Abstract: Charge transfer between two conductors is conventionally viewed as a single-particle process governed by electrostatics and band alignment. Using tunneling spectroscopy, we show that charge transfer in closely spaced graphene double layer quantum Hall ferromagnets instead proceeds through a sequence of interaction driven phase transitions governed by the competition between capacitive charging and Coulomb exchange interactions. A comparison of experimental data and theoretical calculations identifies spectroscopic signatures of the interaction driven charge transfer transitions, and reveals that this charge transfer reconstructs the quasiparticle spectrum. While intralayer exchange favors abrupt transfer of entire spin-valley subbands between the layers, interlayer exchange stabilizes coherent intermediate phases that enable gradual charge transfer. Our results establish interlayer tunneling as a powerful probe of interacting electronic systems whose quasiparticle spectrum is itself bias dependent.

</details>


### [186] [Symmetry-Tunable Skyrmions and Merons in Magnetic Nanodisks via Spatially Engineered Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14983)
*X. D. Wang,J. F. Oliveira da Silva,Z. H. Tao,H. M. Dong,K. Chang,M. V. Milošević*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明，即使在缺乏Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）的情况下，通过对纳米盘进行空间工程化磁各向异性设计，仍可稳定斯格明子（skyrmion）和梅隆（meron）自旋纹理。研究通过约束解析模型和微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulation）展示了竞争性垂直与面内各向异性能够产生非共线拓扑纹理，并进一步表明DMI和偶极相互作用可破除螺旋度简并、选择特定手性构型。此外，各向异性工程化斯格明子阵列可通过操控其涡度排列来控制自旋波传输，为基于非手性拓扑纹理的可重构磁子学（magnonics）元件提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，斯格明子等拓扑自旋纹理的稳定依赖于手性磁性体系中的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）。本文旨在探索在无DMI的非手性磁性系统中，如何通过空间工程化的磁各向异性设计来稳定这类拓扑自旋纹理，从而拓展拓扑磁结构的实现手段与应用平台。

Method: 作者结合约束解析模型（constrained analytical model）与微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulation）方法，系统考察了竞争性垂直磁各向异性（perpendicular anisotropy）和面内磁各向异性（in-plane anisotropy）对纳米盘中拓扑自旋纹理形成的影响；同时引入DMI和偶极相互作用（dipolar interaction）来研究它们对螺旋度（helicity）简并的破除效应及手性构型的选择。

Result: 研究发现，在无DMI条件下，空间工程化各向异性足以在磁性纳米盘中稳定斯格明子和梅隆自旋纹理；DMI及偶极相互作用能够破除螺旋度简并并选择优势手性构型，微磁模拟确认了物理稳定态的存在。进一步研究表明，各向异性工程化斯格明子阵列可通过涡度（vorticity）排列的操控实现对自旋波（spin-wave）传输的控制。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了各向异性图案化纳米盘作为研究无DMI拓扑自旋纹理及其可调控磁响应的有效平台，并提出了基于非手性拓扑纹理构建可重构磁子学元件的可能性，为拓扑磁结构与磁子传输调控提供了新的物理途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that spatially engineered magnetic anisotropy can stabilize skyrmion and meron spin textures in magnetic nanodisks even in the absence of Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI). Using a constrained analytical model and micromagnetic simulations, we show that competing perpendicular and in-plane anisotropies can generate non-collinear topological textures in non-chiral magnetic systems. We further show that DMI and dipolar interactions lift the helicity degeneracy and select preferred chiral configurations; micromagnetic simulations were used to identify physically stable states. These results establish anisotropy-patterned nanodisks as a platform for studying DMI-free topological spin textures and their controllable magnetic response. We also show that arrays of anisotropy-engineered skyrmions can control spin-wave transmission by manipulating their vorticity arrangement, pointing to reconfigurable magnonic elements based on non-chiral topological textures.

</details>


### [187] [Temperature-Dependent Spin-Orbit Torque Generation in Perpendicularly Magnetized Topological Insulator-Magnetic Multilayer Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15232)
*Soumyarup Hait,Benjamin A. Brereton,Ahmet Yagmur,Satoshi Sasaki,Gavin Burnell,Christopher H. Marrows*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究通过温度依赖的二阶谐波霍尔测量，系统探究了拓扑绝缘体/磁性金属多层异质结构中的自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT）生成机制。含有Bi2Se3的样品在15 K时自旋轨道矩可达约12 mT/(10^12 A m^-2)，约为不含拓扑层样品的5倍，且2 nm Ta缓冲层结构表现出最强增强效应；而无Bi2Se3仅含重金属（Ta和Pt）的样品力矩显著较小（约2.5 mT/(10^12 A m^-2)），突显了拓扑表面态自旋动量锁定电流的主导作用及其对界面结构的敏感依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator, TI）界面处自旋轨道矩的生成机制及其随温度变化的规律，对于开发高效的磁性存储与自旋电子学器件具有重要意义。已有研究缺乏对TI/磁性金属异质结构在宽温度范围内自旋轨道矩行为的系统表征。

Method: 制备具有垂直磁化金属多层膜生长在拓扑绝缘体外延层上的异质结构，采用温度依赖的二阶谐波霍尔测量（second-harmonic Hall measurement）系统表征各样品在不同温度下的自旋轨道矩大小，并对比不同缓冲层（2 nm Ta与10 nm Ta）及有无Bi2Se3的样品差异。

Result: 含Bi2Se3的样品在15 K时自旋轨道矩约12 mT/(10^12 A m^-2)，约为不含拓扑层样品的5倍；采用2 nm Ta薄缓冲层的结构增强最显著且在低温下增幅明显，而10 nm Ta间隔层样品力矩效率下降；不含Bi2Se3但含Ta和Pt两种重金属的样品力矩仅约2.5 mT/(10^12 A m^-2)。

Conclusion: 拓扑绝缘体诱导的自旋动量锁定（spin-momentum-locked）电流是产生大阻尼类自旋轨道矩的主要机制，其效率强烈依赖于界面结构与缓冲层厚度；较厚的Ta缓冲层会部分衰减自旋传输，而传统自旋霍尔源产生的力矩远小于拓扑表面态的贡献。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive temperature-dependent investigation of spin-orbit torque (SOT) generation in heterostructures comprising a perpendicularly magnetized metallic multilayer grown on top of a topological insulator (TI) epilayer. Temperature-dependent second-harmonic Hall measurements reveal distinct trends in the magnitude of the spin-orbit torque across the studied heterostructures. Samples incorporating Bi2Se3 exhibit torques reaching approximately 12 mT/(10^12 A m^-2) at 15 K, around 5 times larger than those in a multilayer without the topological layer. The structure with a thin 2-nm Ta buffer for the multilayer shows the strongest enhancement and a pronounced increase at low temperatures, highlighting efficient spin-current generation from the topological surface states. In contrast, the sample with a 10 nm-thick Ta spacer exhibits reduced torque efficiency, consistent with partial attenuation of spin transmission through the buffer. Systems lacking Bi2Se3 but containing two heavy metals (Ta and Pt) yield significantly smaller torques, around 2.5 mT/(10^12 A m^-2), despite the presence of conventional spin Hall sources. These observations underscore the dominant role of TI-derived spin-momentum-locked currents in driving large damping-like torques and their sensitivity to interfacial structure and buffer-layer thickness.

</details>


### [188] [Field-Gated Programming of Nested Skyrmion Bags by Spatially Selective Skyrmion Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15293)
*Shuhuang Lin,Shirong Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种场门控纳米接触（field-gated nanocontact）协议，利用微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）实现对双层嵌套斯格朗子袋（nested skyrmion bags）S(m,S(n))的确定性、区域选择性逐次写入，为多级自旋电子信息编码提供了可编程嵌套拓扑态的构建途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套斯格朗子袋是复合磁性孤子（composite magnetic solitons），其内部占据数可提供扩展的状态空间以实现多级信息编码；然而，向嵌套袋中确定性地、区域选择性地写入单个斯格朗子（skyrmion）仍然是一大挑战。

Method: 通过微磁模拟提出一种场门控纳米接触协议。该协议利用具有反平行磁背景的袋区域对磁场的相反响应，采用空间分段的垂直磁场选择性地扩展外区域或内袋，使其与纳米接触重叠；随后施加合适极性的自旋极化电流脉冲（spin-polarized current pulse），在所选区域成核一个斯格朗子，再通过场驱动的收缩使写入的斯格朗子远离接触位点并恢复写入位置以供重复操作。

Result: 从空嵌套袋S(0,S(0))出发，该协议能够受控构建S(m,S(0))、S(0,S(n))以及一般S(m,S(n))状态。模拟显示，在顺序写入过程中总拓扑电荷（total topological charge）以单位步长变化，证实了逐个且区域选择性的控制。

Conclusion: 这些结果为面向多级自旋电子信息编码的可编程嵌套拓扑态提供了一条可行的实现路径。

Abstract: Nested skyrmion bags are composite magnetic solitons whose internal occupation numbers provide an expanded state space for multilevel information encoding. However, deterministic and region-selective writing of individual skyrmions into nested bags remains challenging. Here, using micromagnetic simulations, we propose a field-gated nanocontact protocol for generating and programming double-layer nested skyrmion bags S(m,S(n)). The protocol exploits the opposite field responses of bag regions with antiparallel magnetic backgrounds. Spatially segmented perpendicular magnetic fields selectively expand either the outer region or the inner bag until it overlaps with a nanocontact. A spin-polarized current pulse of the appropriate polarity then nucleates one skyrmion in the selected region, while subsequent field-driven contraction moves the written skyrmion away from the contact and restores the writing site for repeated operation. Starting from an empty nested bag S(0,S(0)), this procedure enables the controlled construction of S(m,S(0)), S(0,S(n)), and general S(m,S(n)) states. The simulated total topological charge changes in unit steps during sequential writing, confirming one-at-a-time and region-selective control. These results provide a possible route toward programmable nested topological states for multilevel spintronic information encoding.

</details>


### [189] [A cold-insertable scanning probe microscope for dry dilution refrigerators with picometer stability and ultra-low electron temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15495)
*Yizhou Wei,Berthold Jäck*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy, AFM）的扫描微波阻抗显微镜，集成于标准冷插型探头上，通过机械刚度与内部弹簧悬挂相结合的设计，在无液氦稀释制冷机中实现了低于10皮米的相对针尖-样品位移稳定性，同时维持样品电子温度不高于60毫开尔文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子材料的微观机制需要高分辨率的扫描探针技术，但AFM在无液氦稀释制冷机中实施时面临脉冲管机械噪声（约10^-6 米/√Hz）以及低于100毫开尔文条件下压电位移台热平衡这组根本性权衡的挑战，亟需一种兼顾机械稳定性与低温热性能的方案。

Method: 采用双管齐下的设计策略：耦合一个机械刚度高的AFM模块与一个磁兼容、临界阻尼的内部弹簧悬挂系统，以隔离脉冲管振动；同时优化热界面、定制铜连接带并实施综合射频滤波，以弥补机械解耦带来的热路径损失。

Result: 实现了极低的相对针尖-样品振动噪声密度（<10^-11 米/√Hz），对应的谱积分相对位移约10皮米，较近期快速加载型干式扫描探针装置提升了超过100倍的稳定性；同时保持局部样品电子温度不高于60毫开尔文，且无需对主制冷机进行永久性结构改造。

Conclusion: 该模块化架构为在干式低温恒温器中适配其他扫描探针技术提供了通用框架，无需改动宿主系统的结构，有助于在皮米级稳定条件下加速探索脆弱的量子相。

Abstract: Investigating the microscopic mechanisms of quantum materials requires high-resolution scanning probe techniques, often based on atomic force microscopy (AFM). However, implementing AFM in cryogen-free dilution refrigerators with picometer stability is challenged by intense pulse tube mechanical noise ($S_{\rm PT}(f)\approx10^{-6}\,\text{m}/\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$) and the fundamental trade-off with thermalizing piezoelectric motion stages below 100\,mK. Here, we demonstrate an AFM-based scanning microwave impedance microscope integrated onto a standard cold-insertable probe that successfully resolves this bottleneck. By employing a two-pronged design strategy---coupling a mechanically stiff AFM module with a magnetic-field-compatible, critically damped internal spring-suspension---we achieve an extremely low relative tip-sample vibration noise density of $S_{\rm AFM}(f)<10^{-11}\,\text{m}/\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$. This yields a spectrally integrated relative tip-sample displacement of $Δz\approx10\,\text{pm}$, representing a greater than 100-fold stability improvement over recent fast-loading dry SPM setups. Simultaneously, optimized thermal interfaces, customized copper strapping, and comprehensive RF filtering enable a local sample electron temperature of $T_{\rm e}\leq 60\,$mK, circumventing the thermal penalties of mechanical decoupling. By avoiding permanent structural modifications to the host cryostat, this robust, modular architecture provides an accessible framework for adapting other scanning probe techniques, accelerating the exploration of fragile quantum phases in dry cryostats with picometer stability.

</details>


### [190] [Weak localization in magnetic Euler bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15529)
*Doh Young Kim,Seung Hun Lee,Akira Furusaki,Bohm-Jung Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维具有非零欧拉类（Euler class）的脆弱拓扑能带中无序对电导率的量子修正，发现具有自旋轨道耦合和面内磁化的欧拉能带在显式破坏物理时间反演对称性的条件下，仍表现出正交对称类（orthogonal symmetry class）的弱局域化（weak localization）行为，其根源在于由晶格对称性和时空反演对称性组合而成的涌现有效时间反演对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯中两个狄拉克节点具有相反涡度，而具有单位欧拉数的两个能带的两个狄拉克点具有相同涡度，这可能影响安德森局域化（Anderson localization）行为。该差异促使作者研究无序对高欧拉类拓扑能带的量子修正。

Method: 采用对称性分析（symmetry analysis）和图表法计算（diagrammatic calculations），具体研究自旋自由度和磁性欧拉能带中无序引起的电导量子修正及其局域化行为。

Result: 在存在自旋轨道耦合和面内磁化、物理时间反演对称性被明确破坏的条件下，系统仍表现出正交对称类的弱局域化行为。该反常现象源于由晶格对称性与时空反演对称性组合构成的涌现有效时间反演对称性，其能够取代标准的局域化行为。

Conclusion: 有效晶体对称性可以从根本上改变无序系统的普适类（universality class），使局域化行为不依赖于狄拉克节点的具体涡度构型。这揭示了晶体对称性在无序拓扑体系中的深层作用。

Abstract: We study the quantum correction to the conductivity due to disorder in two-dimensional fragile topological bands with nonzero Euler class. Contrary to graphene where two Dirac nodes have opposite vorticities, two bands with a unit Euler number possess two Dirac points with the same vorticity, which may affect the Anderson localization. Most notably, we report an anomalous localization behavior in spinful magnetic Euler bands based on symmetry analysis and diagrammatic calculations. Despite the presence of spin-orbit coupling and an in-plane magnetization that explicitly breaks physical time-reversal symmetry, the system exhibits weak localization behavior characteristic of the orthogonal symmetry class. We demonstrate that this counter-intuitive phenomenon originates from an emergent effective time-reversal symmetry composed of crystalline and spacetime inversion symmetries, allowing it to supersede the standard localization behavior. Our findings reveal that the effective crystalline symmetries can fundamentally alter the universality class of disordered systems, rendering the localization behavior independent of the specific vorticity configuration of Dirac nodes.

</details>


### [191] [Topological insulator realization induced by fermionic interaction through BF mediators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16660)
*G. B. de Gracia,R. da Rocha,A. A. Nogueira,M. A. C. de Barcelos*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明通过BF媒介子（BF mediator）相互作用的普通费米子（fermion）在辐射修正（radiative corrections）作用下会形成具有非平凡拓扑性质的重整化结构。研究将其与真实三维拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators, TIs）单狄拉克锥附近的性质进行类比，发现该拓扑结构属于一类修正的狄拉克方程（modified Dirac equations），从而保证螺旋无隙近边界模式的存在，并进一步推导了包含轴向有限尺寸效应的准粒子间相互作用有效势，揭示了自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）和时间反演不变性（time-reversal invariance）的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索普通费米子通过BF媒介子相互作用后能否形成具有拓扑性质的物理结构，并通过拓扑绝缘体的既有图像来理解和约束这一重整化费米子的行为。

Method: 基于BF媒介子互作用费米子模型，参照真实三维拓扑绝缘体的晶格常数和能隙典型量级来约束模型参数；将辐射修正导致的拓扑结构归类为修正的狄拉克方程，以分析其边界态和相互作用性质。

Result: 证明辐射诱导的拓扑结构保证近边界存在螺旋无隙模式（helical gapless near-boundary modes）；推导出包含轴向（axial）有限尺寸效应的准粒子间相互作用有效势，该势呈现自旋轨道耦合和时间反演不变性的特征；发现不同自旋的粒子不通过特征自旋轨道耦合相互作用，而是产生一种非中心的自旋相关力。

Conclusion: BF媒介子互作用的费米子系统在辐射修正下可映射为三维拓扑绝缘体的有效描述，并表现出独特的自旋相关相互作用特征；系统在薄膜极限（thin-film limit）下的相变行为也得到了讨论。

Abstract: This paper demonstrates that ordinary fermions, interacting via a BF mediator, form a renormalized structure with non-trivial topological properties. We explore the analogy between the renormalized fermion and a subset of real three-dimensional topological insulators (TIs) in the vicinity of their single Dirac cones. Using the typical magnitude of TI lattice and gaps, we constrain model parameters. The topological structure induced by radiative corrections enters a class of modified Dirac equations, ensuring the existence of helical gapless near-boundary modes. We derive an effective potential for inter-quasi-particle interactions that incorporates finite-size effects in the axial direction, revealing signatures of spin-orbit coupling and time-reversal invariance. Remarkably, particles with different spins do not interact via the characteristic spin-orbit coupling, and a non-central spin-dependent force arises. We also address the behavior of the system in the phase transition associated with the thin-film limit.

</details>


### [192] [Observation of nonlocal ferron-drag thermoelectricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15596)
*Takuma Itoh,Takamasa Hirai,Ping Tang,Hossein Sepehri-Amin,Ryo Iguchi,Yusuke Kozuka,Takao Shimizu,Soshi Akita,Shunsuke Mori,Gerrit E. W. Bauer,Ken-ichi Uchida*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究通过主动热成像技术，在无结均质金属靠近铁电绝缘体时观测到了热吸收与释放信号，证实了铁子拖曳效应（ferron-drag effect）的预言，并揭示了金属中传导电子对铁电有序集体激发——铁子（ferron）的非局域激发机制。研究还发现了温度变化信号对电极化方向的依赖及其随金属厚度增加而反常增大的现象，表明金属/铁电混合结构中存在额外的电子-声子-铁子相互作用，这对高性能集成电路和热电装置设计具有潜在影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕尔帖效应（Peltier effect）驱动的固态热泵需要在不同导体界面处实现冷热转换，但本研究旨在探索无需结（junction-free）的均质金属中是否也能产生热吸收与释放，即在单导体中实现类似的热管控效应，从而拓展热电装置的设计空间。

Method: 作者采用主动热成像（active thermographic imaging）技术，在紧邻铁电绝缘体的均质金属中测量热吸收和释放信号，系统考察了金属厚度变化对信号的影响，并分析了温度变化信号与铁电极化方向之间的依赖关系。

Result: 实验证实了铁子拖曳效应的理论预言：传导电子可非局域地激发铁电有序的集体激发——铁子。温度变化信号依赖于电极化方向，且随金属厚度增加而反常增大，超出了电荷屏蔽长度（charge screening length）的预期范围，表明存在额外的电子-声子-铁子相互作用。

Conclusion: 金属与铁电体之间通过远程铁子实现的串扰（crosstalk）效应，既可能对采用铁电阻挡材料的超高集成度电路构成干扰，也为热电装置的设计架构革新提供了新机遇。

Abstract: The Peltier effect induces a heat current when a charge current passes through a conductor. Since a charge current is conserved at the junction between two different conductors, the difference between the heat flowing in both conductors for the same charge current leads to heating or cooling of the interface, providing an operating mechanism of solid-state heat pumps. Here, we report observation of heat absorption and release signals even in a junction-free, homogeneous metal when placed in proximity to a ferroelectric insulator. Our experiments using active thermographic imaging techniques confirm the prediction of the ferron-drag effect, i.e., the nonlocal excitation of ferrons, the collective excitation of the ferroelectric order, by conduction electrons in the adjacent metal. We reveal the electric-polarization-direction dependence of the temperature change signals and their unexpected increase with the metal thickness beyond the charge screening length, uncovering additional electron-phonon-ferron interactions in the metal/ferroelectric hybrid structure. The discovery of crosstalk between metals and ferroelectrics via remote ferrons could become both a nuisance and an opportunity for highly integrated circuits with ferroelectric barrier materials and revolutionize the design architecture of thermoelectric devices.

</details>


### [193] [Electrode-tunable nonlocal Rashba-Edelstein effect and layer-selective chirality switch in WSe$_2$-intercalated bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15622)
*Marko Milivojević,Juraj Mnich,Martin Gmitra*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将单层WSe₂插入双层石墨烯之间，在费米能级处形成合成双层石墨烯结构，发现跨层非局域Rashba-Edelstein效应和层选择性手性开关等自旋电子学行为，并证明其对扭转角与电场具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单界面近邻体系中，层间耦合与自旋轨道相互作用的能量尺度差异限制了可访问的自旋电子学行为；本文旨在通过设计新材料结构拓展近邻体系可达到的物理状态。

Method: 将WSe₂单层插层进入双层石墨烯之间，形成由WSe₂介导波函数重叠从而杂化两石墨烯层的合成体系；构造四电极器件，分别激活顶层/底层或跨层电极以探测不同輸运和自旋响应，并考察扭转角与电场的影响。

Result: 跨层源漏电极激活时观测到非局域Rashba-Edelstein效应，即电荷注入一层石墨烯可在空间分离的另一层产生自旋积累，且该效应在扭转角和电场变化下保持稳定；顶层或底层电极激活时分别产生符号相反的局域Rashba-Edelstein信号，实现层选择性手性开关，源于即便净Rashba分裂被禁止时仍存在的隐藏Rashba效应。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了超出常规单界面近邻体系的新物理状态和器件架构，为自旋电子学应用提供了新的可能方向。

Abstract: We show that intercalating a WSe$_2$ monolayer into bilayer graphene creates a synthetic bilayer graphene at the Fermi level, in which WSe$_2$-mediated wavefunction overlap hybridizes the two graphene layers, giving rise to states delocalized across both layers. Because the interlayer coupling is comparable in energy to the proximity-induced spin-orbit interaction, the resulting spintronic behavior goes beyond what is typically accessible in conventional single-interface proximity systems. A simple four-electrode device, with source and drain electrodes on both the top and bottom graphene layers, gives access to distinct regimes depending on which electrodes are activated. The most prominent feature is a nonlocal Rashba-Edelstein effect, activated via cross-layer source and drain electrodes, in which a charge current injected into one graphene layer generates a spin accumulation in the spatially separated opposite layer. This effect is robust to the twist angle modulation between graphene and WSe$_2$ and to the applied electric field, suggesting that a moderate degree of structural asymmetry along the vertical direction does not destroy it. In addition, activating the source and drain electrodes on either the top or bottom graphene layer reveals local Rashba-Edelstein signals of opposite sign in the two layers, thereby realizing a layer-selective chirality switch. This is a consequence of the hidden Rashba effect, which is present even when a net Rashba splitting is forbidden. Our results suggest new physical regimes and device architectures that could be useful for spintronic applications.

</details>


### [194] [The Hodge structure of Berry-phase transport: topology, geometry, and noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15789)
*Zhi-Wei Wang,Samuel L. Braunstein*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用Berry曲率的Hodge-de Rham分解，将布洛赫能带的输运响应及其涨落统一组织在一个几何结构中：矢量（harmonic）扇区承载拓扑反常霍尔电导，精确（exact）扇区承载费米面几何与Berry曲率偶极子，余精确（co-exact）扇区承载手征反常；在涨落噪声中，矢量扇区被消音，从而使电流噪声谱能够区分全局带拓扑与局部带几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究分别处理了布洛赫带的平均输运响应与噪声涨落，但缺乏将二者统一起来的几何框架。本文旨在通过Berry曲率的数学分解，揭示拓扑输运与噪声抑制之间可能存在的内在关联。

Method: 作者将Berry曲率按L²-正交分解为调和（harmonic）、精确（exact）与余精确（co-exact）三个扇区，并分别建立它们与电流矩的对应关系。对于涨落部分，采用满足涨落耗散定理（FDT）的粒子数守恒随机Boltzmann方程进行分析。

Result: 证明了调和扇区在噪声中的空空间保护具有维度无关性；余精确（单极）扇区不携带守恒律，在热采样测度下与精确扇区出现O(1)量级的混合，无法提供干净的噪声可观测量的。噪声分离因此是双向的：在二维和三维中均实现拓扑与几何的区分。

Conclusion: 揭示了一个普适结论：拓扑输运必然无噪声，而几何输运必然产生场致噪声。电流噪声谱提供了一种"拓扑与几何分离"的二维/三维通用的诊断工具。

Abstract: We show that the Hodge-de Rham decomposition of the Berry curvature organises the transport of a Bloch band and its fluctuations within a single geometric structure. Splitting the curvature into $L^2$-orthogonal harmonic, exact, and co-exact sectors yields a dictionary for both moments of the current. In the mean response the harmonic sector carries the topological anomalous-Hall conductivity, the exact sector the Fermi-surface geometry and the antisymmetric Berry-curvature dipole of polar metals, and, in three dimensions, the co-exact sector the chiral anomaly, quantised by the Weyl-node charges. In the fluctuations, described by a particle-conserving stochastic Boltzmann equation constrained by the fluctuation-dissipation theorem (FDT), the harmonic sector is silent, so topological transport is noiseless, while the field-driven noise is sourced by the geometric sectors (solely the exact sector in two dimensions); current-noise spectroscopy therefore separates global band topology from local band geometry. We prove that the harmonic null-space protection is dimension-independent, and we settle the remaining sector: the co-exact (monopole) sector carries no conservation law and, under the thermal sampling measure, mixes with the exact sector at $\mathcal{O}(1)$, so it furnishes no clean noise observable. The separation the noise provides is therefore two-way, topology versus geometry, in both two and three dimensions.

</details>


### [195] [Morphology-Guided Deterministic Fabrication of Low-Noise High-Temperature Superconducting Quantum Interference Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15846)
*Bingke Xiang,Wanjuan Tang,Shiqun Liu,Lingtong Hou,Geming Zhang,Yibo Wang,Ruonan Wang,Zhiqiang Cao,Jiaqi Wei,Xueshen Wang,Xueying Zhang,Xiaoyang Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy）的位点选择性（site-selective）制备流程，通过预先表征晶界形貌、剔除孔隙密集区段并写入对准标记，将晶界局域异质性从不可控的变异性来源转变为可调控的制备基础，最终在液氮温度下实现了40 fT·Hz^(-1/2)的磁场噪声水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重复制备的双晶高温超导量子干涉器件（HTS SQUID）长期受限于约瑟夫森结所在晶界的局域变异性，这已成为制约器件均匀性和规模化制造的关键瓶颈。

Method: 开发了一套位点选择性制备工作流程：在光刻前利用原子力显微镜绘制目标结区形貌，量化表观晶界宽度（apparent grain-boundary width），剔除富含孔隙的区段，并在附近写入配准标记以实现位点特异性的图形对准。此外，在该流程中引入迭代优化策略以进一步提升结与器件性能。

Result: 表观晶界宽度可作为实用的形貌度量指标，较窄的晶界区域一致性地产生更大的临界电流（critical current）和特征电压（characteristic voltage）；经迭代优化后，器件在液氮温度下达到40 fT·Hz^(-1/2)的磁场噪声水平。

Conclusion: 该策略将局域晶界异质性从不可控的变异源转变为位点选择性制备的依据，为实现低噪声、高均匀性HTS SQUID的可规模化制造提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reproducible bicrystal high-temperature superconducting quantum interference devices remain limited by local variability along the grain boundaries that form the Josephson junctions. Here, we develop a site-selective fabrication workflow in which atomic force microscopy maps the intended junction region before lithography, quantifies an apparent grain-boundary width, rejects pore-rich segments, and writes a nearby registration mark for site-specific pattern alignment. The apparent grain-boundary width provides a practical morphology metric, with narrower regions consistently yielding larger critical currents and characteristic voltages. Iterative optimization within this workflow further improves junction and device performance, reaching a liquid-nitrogen-temperature field-noise level of 40 fT Hz^(-1/2). This strategy turns local grain-boundary heterogeneity from an uncontrolled source of variability into a basis for site-selective fabrication, providing a route towards scalable manufacturing of low-noise HTS SQUIDs with high uniformity.

</details>


### [196] [Electrically Switchable Spintronics in a Multiferroic Altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15953)
*Martin Latorre*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一个二维晶格的最小模型，通过自发对称性破缺同时实现交变磁序（altermagnetic order）、有限电极化（electric polarization）和自旋极化输运响应，三者均由外电场控制并动态耦合，为设计兼具补偿磁体与铁电优势的下一代自旋电子逻辑与纯自旋电流存储器件提供了物理蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋电子器件在速度与功耗方面存在瓶颈，而铁电极化与磁有序的共存与耦合机制尚不清晰。作者希望通过一个最小模型阐明三者（交变磁序、电极化、自旋极化输运）之间的动态纠缠关系，为多功能器件设计提供理论基础。

Method: 构建一个二维晶格的最小模型，将二聚化交变磁体（dimerized altermagnet）与外电场耦合，分析自发对称性破缺后各序参量（order parameter）的行为与相互耦合关系。

Result: 研究表明三个序参量不仅相容，而且动态纠缠：切换其中一个（例如通过电场反转极化）必然重构另外两个。模型给出了可实现的物理蓝图，用于设计下一代自旋电子逻辑和纯自旋电流存储器件（memdevice）。

Conclusion: 该模型将补偿磁体（compensated magnets）的无杂散场（stray-field-free）与超快优势同铁电体的低功耗切换架构相结合，为多功能自旋电子器件提供了清晰的设计路径。

Abstract: We introduce a minimal model of a two-dimensional lattice that, upon spontaneous symmetry breaking, simultaneously develops altermagnetic order, a finite electric polarization, and a spin-polarized transport response, all of which are controlled by an external electric field. By coupling a dimerized altermagnet to an external electric field, we show that the three order parameters are not merely compatible but dynamically entangled, so that switching one (for instance, reversing the polarization with an electric field) necessarily reconfigures the other two. We show that this model offers a clear physical blueprint for designing next-generation spintronic logic and pure spin current memdevices that merge the ultrafast, stray-field-free advantages of compensated magnets with the low-power switching architectures of ferroelectrics.

</details>


### [197] [Chiral Damping-Induced Chirality Switching and Control of Domain Walls in Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16057)
*Collins Ashu Akosa,Mu-Kun Lee,Aurélien Manchon,Masahito Mochizuki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究系统考察了手性阻尼（chiral damping, CD）对反铁磁体（antiferromagnets, AFMs）中电流驱动畴壁（domain-wall, DW）动力学的影响。研究发现CD可在缺乏Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）的条件下实现Néel型和Bloch型畴壁之间的手性切换，并提出了一种基于相对论洛伦兹收缩（Lorentz contraction）效应的实验方案来提取CD贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性阻尼作为磁性系统中的关键耗散参数，其对反铁磁畴壁动力学的影响尚未被系统揭示，尤其是CD能否作为独立于DMI的控制参量来调控磁织构仍属开放问题。
