### [229] [On the relations between several notions of symmetry for the second order linear differential equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16879)
*David Blázquez-Sanz,Santiago Alexis Aguirre Agudelo*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究二阶线性常微分方程中三类无穷小对称性（Lie点对称、垂直（规范）对称、算子对称）之间的显式对应关系，并系统刻画了切触系统上的无穷小切触变换的Lie代数结构。作者给出了Lie代数的典范分解、切触向量场是对称的充要条件，并证明了对称代数总由两个任意二元函数参数化，但分解的各阶分量永远无法捕获完整对称代数。最后精确给出了切触变换下特征标量的变换律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶线性微分方程文献中存在多种不等价的无穷小对称概念（Lie点对称、垂直对称、算子对称、无穷小切触对称与Lie–Bäcklund算子），这些概念间的关系缺乏显式刻画；同时切触变换的Lie代数结构及其与对称问题之间的联系尚未被系统理解。

Method: 作者建立了前三类无穷小对称（Lie点对称、垂直（规范）对称、算子对称）之间的显式对应关系；随后在微分域$\mathbb U$（由$x,y$的函数构成）上构造切触系统$\Omega=\langle dy-y'\,dx\rangle$的无穷小切触变换Lie代数$\mathcal L_\Omega(\mathbb U)$，给出其典范分解$\mathcal L_\Omega(\mathbb U)=\prod_{k\ge0}\mathcal L_\Omega^k\mathbb U$为$\mathbb C$-向量空间的直积，并在此坐标下计算Lie括号的代数微分公式。基于此结构，推导切触向量场成为对称的充要条件及特征标量的变换律。

Result: 证明了切触向量场（生成函数$W$）是对称当且仅当$A^{2}W=aW+b\,AW$（其中$A$为对应方程的jet空间向量场），等价地当且仅当$W=F_1(u_1,u_2)\varphi_1+F_2(u_1,u_2)\varphi_2$，其中$F_1,F_2$为$A$的两个首次积分的任意函数，$\varphi_1,\varphi_2$为解空间的基。对称代数总由两个任意二元函数参数化。分解$\mathcal L_\Omega(\mathbb U)$的各阶分量永远无法捕获完整对称代数：当$a\neq0$时梯度部分退化仅为点对称；对$y''=0$则为无穷维但仍是真子空间，两种情况均不构成Lie子代数。

Conclusion: 三类无穷小对称概念之间的显式对应已建立，切触变换Lie代数的完整结构已刻画。对称代数的特征表明其不能被该Lie代数的分次分解所完全覆盖，这揭示了切触对称性与上述分解之间的本质不一致性。最后给出了切触变换（共形因子$\mu$）下特征标量的变换律$W\mapsto\mu^{-1}(W\circ\varphi)$，该律式支配演化型表示（evolutionary representatives）的输运。

Abstract: There are several non-equivalent notions of infinitesimal symmetry in the literature of second order linear differential equations: Lie point symmetries, vertical (gauge) symmetries, operator symmetries, infinitesimal contact symmetries, and Lie--Bäcklund operators. We construct an explicit correspondence among the first three, We then describe the Lie algebra $\mathcal L_Ω(\mathbb U)$ of infinitesimal contact transformations of the contact system $Ω=\langle dy-y'\,dx\rangle$, with coefficients in a differential field $\U$ of functions of $x$ and $y$. We obtain a canonical decomposition $\mathcal L_Ω(\mathbb U)=\prod_{k\ge0}\mathcal L_Ω^k\mathbb U$ into $\mathbb C$-vector spaces, each parametrized by $\mathbb U$ (by $\mathbb U\oplus\mathbb U$ for $k=0$), and we compute the algebraic differential formulae for the Lie bracket in these coordinates. Applied to the symmetry problem, we prove that a contact vector field with generating function $W$ is a symmetry of if and only if $A^{2}W=aW+b\,AW$, where $A$ is the vector field in the jet space corresponding to the equation; equivalently, if and only if $W=F_1(u_1,u_2)φ_1+F_2(u_1,u_2)φ_2$ for arbitrary functions $F_1,F_2$ of the two first integrals of $A$ and a basis $φ_1,φ_2$ of solutions. The symmetry algebra is always parametrized by two arbitrary functions of two variables. It also shows that the decomposition of $\Lom(\U)$ never captures the whole symmetry algebra: for $a\neq0$ the graded part reduces to the point symmetries, while for $y''=0$ it is an infinite dimensional but still \emph{proper} subspace, and in neither case is it a Lie subalgebra. Finally we make precise the transformation law $W\mapstoμ^{-1}(W\circ\varphi)$ for characteristics under a contact transformation with conformal factor $μ$, which governs the transport of evolutionary representatives.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Galois descents of certain multiple polylogarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16735)
*Yajun Zhou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了K. C. Au关于多重多对数（multiple polylogarithms）求和中分圆层级下降（descents of cyclotomic levels）的若干猜想，并通过回答Au在特定情形下提出的问题，确证了Broadhurst关于偶数权重荣誉多重zeta值（honorary multiple zeta values）的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重多对数与多重zeta值（multiple zeta values）是数论与代数几何中的重要研究对象，其中涉及分圆层级和荣誉多重zeta值的结构性问题长期缺乏严格证明，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 本文通过对多重多对数求和中分圆层级的下降性分析，构造证明框架来验证Au提出的猜想；同时针对Au提出的若干特殊情形问题给出解答，以此作为验证Broadhurst猜想的关键路径。

Result: 成功证明了K. C. Au关于分圆层级下降的若干猜想，并且在特定情况下回答了Au的问题，进而确证了Broadhurst关于偶数权重荣誉多重zeta值的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文的研究成果为分圆层级下降结构与荣誉多重zeta值的理论研究提供了严格证明，推进了多重对数和多重zeta值领域相关猜想体系的完善。

Abstract: We prove some conjectures of K. C. Au concerning the descents of cyclotomic levels in certain sums over multiple polylogarithms. Via answers to a question of Au in some particular cases, we also confirm Broadhurst's conjecture on honorary multiple zeta values at even weights.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Driven Time Crystal in Low-Symmetry ENZ Conductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15208)
*Mario G. Silveirinha*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出低对称性导体（low-symmetry conductors）可作为透明导电氧化物（如ITO）的替代方案，利用ENZ约束与强反常速度非线性（anomalous-velocity nonlinearity）的组合，使亚波长纳米颗粒在光泵浦下产生时间晶体（time-crystalline）响应，并在强泵浦下克服耗散损耗实现参量放大（parametric amplification），极端情况下可使探测光消光为负、实现探针光束放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，epsilon-near-zero（ENZ）材料在非线性光学领域受到广泛关注，因其兼具场增强与强超快非线性特性。但现有方案大多依赖有效χ^{(3)}调制，即材料主导响应由光泵浦包络（envelope）控制，需要探索更自然的、具有光学周期调制能力的替代方案。

Method: 作者提出利用低对称性导体的强反常速度非线性（anomalous-velocity nonlinearity），结合ENZ约束效应，在光泵浦下使亚波长纳米颗粒自发产生时间晶体响应；同时通过调节泵浦强度来调控弱探测光的散射与消光行为。

Result: 研究表明：在足够强的泵浦下，时间晶体响应能够克服耗散损耗（dissipative losses）并导致参量放大；泵浦可强烈调控弱探测光的散射与消光，在极端情况下可使消光变为负值，此时受驱动的纳米颗粒等效地对探针光束进行放大。

Conclusion: 低对称性导体为透明导电氧化物（transparent conducting oxides）提供了一种有趣且更自然的替代方案，能够实现光学系统中具有光学周期调制（optical-cycle modulation）的时间晶体行为，并在亚波长尺度实现探针光束的参量放大。

Abstract: In recent years, epsilon-near-zero (ENZ) materials have attracted a great deal of attention in nonlinear optics, as they combine field enhancement with strong, ultrafast nonlinearities. In particular, transparent conducting oxides, such as ITO, have emerged as a promising class of materials and have been extensively exploited to achieve temporal optical responses varying on the femtosecond scale using an optical pump. Most of the solutions discussed so far in the literature rely on effective $χ^{(3)}$ modulations, wherein the dominant material response is controlled by the envelope of the optical pump. Here, it is shown that low-symmetry conductors can provide an interesting alternative to transparent conducting oxides and a more natural implementation of time-crystalline behavior in optical systems with optical-cycle modulation. I demonstrate that ENZ confinement, combined with the strong anomalous-velocity nonlinearity of low-symmetry conductors, enables a subwavelength nanoparticle to develop a time-crystalline response under optical pumping. For sufficiently strong pumping, this response can overcome dissipative losses and lead to parametric amplification. Furthermore, the pump can strongly tailor the scattering and extinction of a weak probe and, in extreme cases, render the extinction negative. In this regime, the driven nanoparticle effectively amplifies the probe beam.

</details>


### [232] [Transport-defined photodetection through electrically selectable nonequilibrium carrier transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16047)
*Jiaxuan Cai,Yan Xie,Ning Yang,Weidong Chu,Peng Bai,Lianhe Li,Edmund H. Linfield,Hanbing Wang,Meng Chen,Guanchen Li,Yingxin Wang,Ziran Zhao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由输运(transport)定义的光电探测（photodetection）方案，利用电学偏压选择光激发载流子的再分布、逃逸与收集路径，在同一活性区域内实现互补响应函数。在GaAs/AlGaAs半导体棘轮（semiconductor ratchet）中，0V时响应由光驱动棘轮输运决定，-2V时表现为场辅助热载流子输运（hot-carrier transport），覆盖0.4–94.5 μm光谱范围，并演示了中红外与太赫兹波段的直接光频拍频探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带光场在强度和光谱分布上均可变化，但固定响应探测器只能将这种演化信息映射为单一电信号，缺乏灵活的重构能力。作者旨在将光激发后的载流子输运作为设计变量，以补充现有结构定义、场调谐和光学编码的可重构光电探测方法。

Method: 采用GaAs/AlGaAs半导体棘轮结构，通过电学偏压（0 V与-2 V）选择不同的载流子输运机制——光驱动棘轮输运与场辅助热载流子输运——生成互补的响应函数。在5 K和30 K下测量光谱响应，并通过光频拍频实验验证光学到电学的直接转换。

Result: 在5 K下测得0.4–94.5 μm的光谱覆盖范围，获得状态依赖的计算探测下限（detection floor），并支持30 K下的联合红外工作。在中红外7.432 GHz和太赫兹17.103 GHz频段直接观测到光频拍频信号，且共路输出可恢复施加的空间温度梯度及激光激发石墨的瞬态有效辐射温度。

Conclusion: 光激发后输运（post-photoexcitation transport）可作为半导体光电探测器的功能定义设计变量，补充现有的结构定义、场调谐和光学编码途径，为可重构光电探测（reconfigurable photodetection）提供新范式。

Abstract: Broadband optical fields can change in both intensity and spectral distribution, but a fixed-response detector maps this evolving information onto a single electrical signal. Here we demonstrate transport-defined photodetection, in which electrical bias selects how photoexcited carriers are redistributed, escape and are collected, creating complementary response functions within one shared active region. In a GaAs/AlGaAs semiconductor ratchet, light-driven ratchet transport defines the response at 0 V, whereas the spectral evolution at -2 V is consistent with field-assisted hot-carrier transport. The overlapping states span a measured spectral range of 0.4-94.5 μm at 5 K, provide state-dependent calculated detection floors and support joint infrared operation at 30 K. Direct optical beat notes at 7.432 GHz in the mid-infrared and 17.103 GHz in the terahertz demonstrate optical-to-electrical conversion. Their common-path outputs recover an imposed spatial temperature gradient and the transient field-of-view-integrated effective radiation temperature of laser-excited graphite. These results establish post-photoexcitation transport as a function-defining design variable for semiconductor photodetectors, complementing structure-defined, field-tuned and optically encoded approaches to reconfigurable photodetection.

</details>


### [233] [In-situ adjoint protocols for nonlinear PT-symmetric self-optimizing machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16056)
*Zheming Li,Lucas J. Fernández-Alcázar,Zin Lin,Tsampikos Kottos*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文证明非线性宇称-时间（parity-time, PT）对称系统能够克服伴随方法（adjoint method）在物理实现中的障碍——即无需反向时间演化、雅可比转置和终值约束，即可通过正向时间演化与受控注入在实验中精确求取伴随梯度，从而在非线性PT对称谐振子链中实现自主优化，自动发现可达成指定时空功能的参数配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随方法为基于梯度的优化提供了强大路径，但在一般非线性系统中其物理实现受到阻碍，因为伴随动力学需要反向时间演化、雅可比转置和终值约束，这些条件在实验中难以直接满足。因此研究者寻求能够克服这些物理实现障碍的系统结构或对称性资源。

Method: 利用一类非线性非厄米谐振子网络，建立对称性关系（symmetry relations），将形式伴随动力学（formal adjoint dynamics）映射到实验可访问的正向时间演化并辅以受控注入。该方法无需显式反向传播或矩阵转置，即可在实验中原位精确求取伴随梯度。

Result: 在非线性PT对称谐振子链中，该优化协议自主发现能够实现预设时空功能的参数配置，包括均匀能量再分配（uniform energy redistribution）和预设时间窗口内的定向波输运（targeted wave transport）。

Conclusion: 研究结果将PT对称性确立为在物理系统中实现计算灵敏度（computational sensitivities）的资源，并为自优化非线性机器（self-optimizing nonlinear machines）的构建开辟了路径。

Abstract: Adjoint methods provide a powerful route for gradient-based optimization, but their physical implementation is obstructed in generic nonlinear systems because the adjoint dynamics requires backward-time evolution, Jacobian transposition, and terminal-value constraints. Here we show that nonlinear parity-time ($\mathcal{PT}$)-symmetric systems overcome this obstruction. Using a class of nonlinear non-Hermitian resonator networks, we establish symmetry relations that map the formal adjoint dynamics onto experimentally accessible forward-time evolutions supplemented by controlled injections. This construction enables exact in-situ evaluation of adjoint gradients without requiring explicit backward propagation or matrix transposition. We demonstrate the approach in nonlinear $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric resonator chains, where the resulting optimization protocol autonomously discovers parameter configurations that realize prescribed spatio-temporal functionalities, including uniform energy redistribution and targeted wave transport at predefined time windows. Our results identify $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry as a resource for implementing computational sensitivities within physical systems and establish a route toward self-optimizing nonlinear machines.

</details>


<div id='math.GT'></div>

# math.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [$\mathrm {U}_{q\tilde q}\mathfrak{sl}(2;\mathbb R)$ Turaev-Viro invariants for cusped $3$-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16560)
*Tianyue Liu,Shuang Ming,Xin Sun,Baojun Wu,Tian Yang*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 该论文定义了一族针对带尖点末端（cusp ends）的双曲三维流形（hyperbolic 3-manifolds）的Turaev-Viro型不变量，推广了文献[LMSWY]中针对完全测地边界（totally geodesic boundary）流形提出的不变量，并证明了这些不变量以指数方式衰减，衰减率由流形的双曲体积（hyperbolic volume）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LMSWY等人的工作中，Turaev-Viro型不变量已被推广到带完全测地边界的双曲三维流形，但带尖端（cusp）的流形（即带锥形退化边界的情形）尚未被处理。该论文旨在填补这一空白，将此类不变量扩展至更广泛的流形类别，并研究其指数衰减行为与双曲体积之间的关联。

Method: 论文定义了一族Turaev-Viro型不变量，其构造基于作者所称的“理想U_qq̃sl(2;ℝ)-6j符号”（ideal U_qq̃ sl(2;ℝ)-6j symbols），这些符号是与U_q sl(2;ℝ)模对偶（modular double）的正表示（positive representations）相关联的6j符号（6j-symbols）的变体。

Result: 证明了所构造的不变量以指数形式衰减，且指数衰减率由流形的双曲体积决定。

Conclusion: 该工作将Turaev-Viro型不变量推广至带尖点末端的双曲三维流形，并建立了其指数衰减与双曲体积之间的定量关系，为量子不变量与几何量之间的联系提供了新的例证。

Abstract: We define a family of Turaev-Viro type invariants for hyperbolic $3$-manifolds with cusp ends, extending the invariants introduced in \cite{LMSWY} for hyperbolic $3$-manifolds with totally geodesic boundary. These invariants are constructed from what we call the ideal $\mathrm{U}_{q\tilde q}\mathfrak{sl}(2;\mathbb R)$-$6j$ symbols, which are variants of the $6j$-symbols associated with the positive representations of the modular double of $\mathrm{U}_q\mathfrak{sl}(2;\mathbb R)$. We also prove that these invariants decay exponentially, with the exponential decay rate determined by the hyperbolic volume of the manifold.

</details>

### [235] [Physics-Informed Symbolic Regression for Predicting the Glass Transition Temperature of Alkali Borate Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14853)
*Leonardo dos Santos Vitoria,Marcio Luis Ferreira Nascimento,Susana de Souza Lalic,Daniel Roberto Cassar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本工作将物理启发的符号回归（physics-informed symbolic regression）应用于碱金属硼酸盐玻璃的Tg预测，推导出具有明确物理意义的闭式解析表达式，均方根误差仅14-16 K，并发现基于刚性单元堆积分数（RUPF）的模型优于传统原子堆积分数（APF）。通过蒙特卡洛不确定性量化，识别出预测误差最大的区域与硼反常（boron anomaly）结构转变相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碱金属硼酸盐玻璃的玻璃化转变温度（Tg）高度依赖组分，且由于硼网络的结构复杂性，难以从第一性原理直接预测。作者希望开发一种透明、可解释的替代黑箱模型的方法来预测Tg。

Method: 采用物理启发的符号回归（physics-informed symbolic regression），即结合进化搜索（evolutive search）与物理意义明确的描述符，在xM₂O·(100-x)B₂O₃玻璃体系（M=Li、Na、K，x为摩尔百分比）中推导Tg的闭式解析表达式，并外推至Rb和Cs体系。模型中显式捕捉了Tg、结构解离能（structural dissociation energy）与网络堆积之间的相互作用。

Result: 模型达到14-16 K的均方根误差，且具有清晰的物理可解释性。基于Rigid Unit Packing Fraction（RUPF）的模型比基于传统Atomic Packing Fraction（APF）的模型产生更真实的Tg预测，因为APF在中等组分区间高估了结构刚性。拟合的解离能与修正的Makishima-Mackenzie模型交叉验证一致。蒙特卡洛不确定性量化显示，预测不确定性在与硼反常相关的组分区域最高。

Conclusion: 物理启发的符号回归可作为玻璃体系中性质预测的黑箱模型的一个透明、可解释的替代方案。模型在硼反常区域的预测局限直接关联于已知的结构转变，表明了该方法在揭示物理机理方面的潜力。

Abstract: The glass transition temperature ($T_{g}$) of alkali borate glasses is strongly composition-dependent and difficult to predict from first principles due to the structural complexity of the boron network. Here, we apply physics-informed symbolic regression (combining evolutive search with physically meaningful descriptors) to derive an interpretable closed-form expression for $T_{g}$ in the $x\mathrm{M}_2\mathrm{O}\cdot(100-x)\mathrm{B}_2\mathrm{O}_3$ glass family, with M = Li, Na, and K and $x$ expressed in mol%, and subsequently extrapolate it to M = Rb and Cs. The resulting model achieves a root-mean-square error of 14-16 K while maintaining clear physical interpretability, explicitly capturing the interplay among $T_{g}$, structural dissociation energy, and network packing. Critically, models built on the Rigid Unit Packing Fraction (RUPF) yield substantially more realistic $T_{g}$ predictions than those using the conventional Atomic Packing Fraction (APF), as APF overestimates structural rigidity at intermediate compositions. The fitted dissociation energies are further validated against the revised Makishima-Mackenzie model, confirming that the inferred parameters are physically consistent, not merely statistically effective, within the alkali borate family. Finally, Monte Carlo uncertainty quantification reveals that prediction uncertainty is highest in the compositional regions associated with the boron anomaly, directly linking model limitations to a known structural transition in these glasses. This result highlights the potential of physics-informed symbolic regression as a transparent and interpretable alternative to black-box models for property prediction in glass systems.

</details>


### [236] [ESPResSo++: A Fast and Extensible Molecular Simulation Package for Coarse-Grained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14911)
*Zhen-Hao Xu,James Vance,Nikita Tretyakov,Sebastian Eibl,Pavel Kus,Jakub Krajniak,Tristan Bereau,Horacio V. Guzman,Bin Song,Markus Rampp,Torsten Stuehn,Christoph Junghans*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: ESPResSo++ 是一个开源分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟软件包，专注于软物质系统的粗粒化（coarse-grained, CG）建模，采用 C++ 编写并提供灵活的 Python 接口，支持通过 MPI 实现大规模并行模拟，可用于聚合物、膜、胶体和复杂流体的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软物质系统（如聚合物、膜、胶体等）的分子动力学模拟在复杂体系研究中具有重要价值，需要一个针对粗粒化模型设计的开源、高性能、可扩展的模拟工具包，以满足高分辨率计算环境下的需求。

Method: 该软件以 C++ 为核心实现高性能计算，并提供灵活的 Python 接口以降低用户使用门槛；通过 MPI 支持大规模并行计算，适配高性能计算（high-performance computing, HPC）环境；配备多种相互作用模型与先进算法以覆盖广泛的模拟场景。

Result: 该软件包能够支持聚合物、膜、胶体及复杂流体的模拟，并集成多种相互作用模型和先进算法，实现大规模并行化的粗粒化分子动力学模拟。

Conclusion: ESPResSo++ 作为开源软件，为软物质粗粒化分子动力学模拟提供了一个兼具灵活性与高性能可扩展性的解决方案，尤其适合在 HPC 环境中执行大规模并行模拟任务。

Abstract: ESPResSo++ is an open-source software package for molecular dynamics (MD) simulations with a particular emphasis on coarse-grained (CG) models of soft matter systems. Written in C++ with a flexible Python interface, it is designed for high-performance computing (HPC) environments and supports massively parallel simulations through MPI. The package enables simulations of polymers, membranes, colloids and complex fluids with a wide range of interaction models and advanced algorithms.

</details>


### [237] [Shear effects in active models of normal and cancer cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15010)
*Souvik Sadhukhan,Rajsekhar Das,Lin Zhao,Wolfgang Losert,D. Thirumalai*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究利用三维基于智能体的模型（agent-based model）模拟正常与癌变组织的剪切响应，发现正常上皮的屈服应力遵循Herschel-Bulkley形式，且剪切率依赖性与弹性依赖变化可通过适当缩放落入主曲线（master curve）；随着活性增强、刚度降低的癌细胞比例增加，集体运动从仿射（affine）向非仿射（non-affine）转变；Gaussian Mixture Model（GMM）与平均场理论可定量解释模拟与实验结果，表明刚度与活性的异质性决定正常与癌变组织中的非仿射运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物组织的力学性质在发育和癌症转移等过程中至关重要，但细胞在剪切等机械形变下的动力学响应及其流变学性质尚未被充分表征，亟需系统研究。

Method: 采用三维基于智能体的模型分别模拟正常与癌变组织对简单剪切的响应，以细胞刚度和随机活性力（stochastic active forces）为变量；在正常上皮中以均匀活性力强度考察屈服应力与剪切率的关系；在癌变模型中选取特定比例（N_p）的细胞赋予增强活性和降低刚度，并模拟嵌入正常组织的刚性实体瘤；结合Gaussian Mixture Model（GMM）与平均场理论对模拟及实验数据进行定量分析。

Result: 正常上皮中屈服应力对剪切率的依赖符合Herschel-Bulkley形式，且剪切率依赖及弹性相关的屈服应力变化经适当缩放后落入主曲线；随N_p增大，细胞集体运动程度下降，从仿射向非仿射运动转变，与成像实验一致；刚性实体瘤半径R_s增大时屈服应力升高且细胞迁移更趋于集体化；GMM与平均场理论可定量解释癌性、非癌性及混合组织的模拟与实验结果。

Conclusion: 刚度与活性的异质性（heterogeneity in stiffness and activity）决定了正常与癌变组织中的非仿射运动，理论模拟与实验相结合的综合研究确立了这一结论。

Abstract: Mechanical properties of biological tissues, driven by passive and active forces, play a vital role in several processes ranging from development to cancer metastasis. However, the dynamical responses of cells in tissues, subject to mechanical deformations such as shear and the associated rheological properties, are not well characterized. Here, we use three-dimensional agent-based models for normal and cancer tissues to investigate their responses to simple shear as a function of cell stiffness and stochastic active forces. In the normal epithelium, with uniform strength of active force, the yield stress as a function of shear rate follows the Herschel-Bulkley form over a range of cell volume fraction. Strikingly, the shear rate dependence and the elasticity-dependent changes in the yield stress fall on master curves upon suitable scaling. To model cancer-like behavior, a certain fraction ($N_p$) of cells was chosen to have enhanced activity and decreased stiffness. As $N_p$ increases, the extent of collective cell movement decreases, transitioning from affine (collective) to non-affine (individualistic) movement, a finding that is in accord with imaging experiments. Simulations of a model of a stiff solid tumor, with radius $R_s$ embedded in normal tissue, show that as $R_s$ increases, the yield stress increases. Interestingly, the cells migrate collectively as $R_s$ increases. A Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) and a mean field theory quantitatively account for the simulation as well as experimental results on cancerous, non-cancerous, and a mixture of these two types. The combined theoretical and experimental study establishes that heterogeneity in stiffness and activity determines non-affine movements in normal and cancer tissues.

</details>


### [238] [Time-resolved sedimentation of dense potato-starch suspensions measured by optical coherence tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15067)
*T. Saiki,H. Katsuragi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出将光学相干断层扫描（optical coherence tomography, OCT）用作测量高浓度不透明悬浮液的实验技术，通过同时追踪沉降速度和粒子浓度，实现了单次沉降过程中的连续动态测量，结果与Krieger–Dougherty模型及Richardson–Zaki定律一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光学方法无法探测高浓度不透明悬浮液内部，因而缺乏直接测量这类体系内部动力学过程的手段。作者旨在利用OCT突破这一局限，实现对稠密悬浮液沉降过程中多个关键物理量（沉降速度与有效体积分数）的同步连续测量。

Method: 利用OCT分辨约20微米的马铃薯淀粉颗粒作为独立散射体，即使悬浮液对肉眼不透明（opaque）仍可成像。在同一条OCT图像序列中同时追踪颗粒承载层的垂直质心位置⟨Z⟩(t)与上清液边界Z_sup(t)，从而同时获得瞬时沉降速度V(t)和随时间演化的有效体积分数φ_eff(t)。实验改变初始体积分数φ₀（0.30–0.50）和溶剂密度ρ_L（1.0–1.3×10³ kg m⁻³）进行系统测量。

Result: 将归一化速度V/V_Stokes对有效体积分数φ_eff作图，数据在φ_eff≈0.30–0.52范围内塌缩为一条共同趋势线，与Krieger–Dougherty模型及Richardson–Zaki定律均一致，验证了该方法捕获了物理上合理的受阻沉降（hindered-settling）行为。这是首次将沉降速度与粒子浓度合并为单次沉降运行中的一条连续轨迹。

Conclusion: 上述结果表明OCT是一种可行的工具，可用于探测此前光学测量无法触及的稠密悬浮液内部动力学。该方法为高浓度悬浮体系提供了新的实验途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate optical coherence tomography (OCT) as a measurement technique for dense, optically opaque suspensions. Conventional optical methods cannot access the interior of such suspensions. OCT resolves individual potato-starch particles (${\sim}20~\mathrm{μm}$) as distinct scatterers, even though the suspension appears opaque to the eye. By tracking the vertical centroid position of the particle-laden layer $\langle Z \rangle(t)$ and the supernatant boundary $Z_\mathrm{sup}(t)$ in the same OCT image sequence, we obtain the instantaneous settling velocity $V(t)$ and the time-evolving effective volume fraction $φ_\mathrm{eff}(t)$ simultaneously and continuously in time. To our knowledge, this is the first measurement to combine settling velocity and particle concentration into a single continuous trajectory within one sedimentation run. Conventional batch measurements yield only one velocity value per run. We applied this method to dense potato-starch suspensions, varying the initial volume fraction $φ_0$ from 0.30 to 0.50 and the solvent density $ρ_\mathrm{L}$ from 1.0 to $1.3{\times}10^3~\mathrm{kg~m^{-3}}$ using aqueous sodium polytungstate solutions. The normalized velocity $V/V_\mathrm{Stokes}$ plotted against $φ_\mathrm{eff}$ collapses onto a common trend consistent with both the Krieger--Dougherty model and the Richardson--Zaki law over $φ_\mathrm{eff} \simeq 0.30$--$0.52$, confirming that the method captures physically reasonable hindered-settling behavior. These results establish OCT as a viable tool for probing internal dynamics in dense suspensions that were previously inaccessible to optical measurement.

</details>


### [239] [Skimming transition in flexible granular sweeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15069)
*Y. Ochi,H. Katsuragi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了柔性板扫过颗粒床时引发的惯性颗粒-结构相互作用（IGSI），发现更快的扫掠速度导致更少的颗粒喷射，并通过引入无量纲数Sk（板弹性时间尺度与扫掠时间尺度之比）统一描述了该行为，将其类比为流固耦合（FSI）的颗粒对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性体置于稳定流体中会弯曲以减小阻力（即自流线化），这是流固耦合（FSI）的标志性现象。颗粒-结构相互作用在自然界同样普遍，但对其理解仍十分有限，因此亟需对惯性颗粒-结构相互作用（IGSI）进行系统研究。

Method: 通过实验方法考察柔性板扫过颗粒床时诱发的颗粒喷射现象，系统改变扫掠速度以观测喷射质量的差异，并引入无量纲数Sk（定义为板弹性时间尺度与扫掠时间尺度之比）来刻画其物理机制。

Result: 实验发现更快的扫掠导致更少的颗粒喷射，尤其对柔性板而言。当Sk≲1时，板的偏转遵循FSI的自流线化规律并引发显著颗粒喷射；当Sk≳1时，板掠过床面，喷射颗粒质量急剧减少。

Conclusion: 无量纲数Sk成功组织并描述了IGSI的整体行为，使其成为FSI在颗粒介质中的对应物（granular counterpart），为该领域提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: A flexible body placed in a steady flow bends to reduce drag. This self-streamlining is a hallmark of fluid-structure interaction (FSI). Granular-structure interaction is equally ubiquitous in nature. However, it remains poorly understood. Thus, we investigate inertial granular-structure interaction (IGSI). Specifically, ejection induced by a flexible plate sweeping a granular bed is experimentally examined. We find that a faster sweep results in less ejection, particularly for a flexible plate. To understand the underlying physics of this behavior, a dimensionless number Sk is introduced as the ratio of the plate elastic timescale to the sweep timescale. At $\mathrm{Sk} \lesssim 1$, the plate deflection follows the self-streamlining law of FSI and induces substantial ejection. At $\mathrm{Sk} \gtrsim 1$, on the other hand, the plate skims the bed and the mass of ejected grains decreases sharply. Sk organizes IGSI as the granular counterpart of FSI.

</details>


### [240] [Growth-Induced Transitions in Viscoelastic Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15320)
*Valentin Slepukhin,Oskar Hallatschek*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究增殖物质（proliferating matter）的黏弹性力学行为，发现当生长率与应力松弛速率相当时，系统由单一无量纲参数 gτ 控制，且在 gτ ~ 1 时出现两个极限行为（纯黏性、纯弹性）均不存在的全新的力学跃迁动力学，并提出了生长兼容的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生物组织（从生物被膜到肿瘤）同时表现出弹性和黏性行为，但现有描述通常将生长材料的应力-形变响应仅视为纯弹性或纯黏性，忽视了黏弹性对增殖物质力学的关键控制作用，特别是在生长率与应力松弛速率相近的情况下。

Method: 以生长弹性梁为原型，分析其动力学由无量纲参数 gτ（g 为生长率，τ 为黏弹性松弛时间）控制，考察 gτ→0 与 gτ→∞ 两个极限及中间区间的行为；随后发展了一个生长兼容的理论框架，其中无约束生长内在地无应力，并将分析推广到其他原型几何并支持更真实生物材料的模拟。

Result: gτ→0 与 gτ→∞ 分别恢复纯黏性与纯弹性行为；中间区间并非两者间的平滑过渡，而是在 gτ ~ 1 处涌现定性全新的动力学，包括两个极限区间均不存在的亚稳态之间的快速跃迁。还发现当指数生长产生的应力累积快于黏弹性松弛的耗散时，会出现尖锐的力学转变。

Conclusion: 黏弹性响应在生长率与应力松弛速率可比时对增殖物质力学起到关键调控作用；所提出的生长兼容理论框架能够捕捉这一机制，并为研究更真实的生长生物材料提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Growth is a fundamental process in living systems. Although the stress-deformation response of growing materials is often described as either purely elastic or purely viscous, many biological tissues, from biofilms to tumors, exhibit both elastic and viscous behavior. Here, we show that this viscoelastic response can crucially control the mechanics of proliferating matter when the growth rate becomes comparable to the rate of stress relaxation. Focusing first on the prototypical case of a growing elastic beam, we find that the dynamics are governed by a single dimensionless parameter, $g τ$, where $g$ is the growth rate and $τ$ is the viscoelastic relaxation time. While the limits $g τ\to 0$ and $g τ\to \infty$ recover purely viscous and purely elastic behavior, respectively, the intermediate regime is not merely a smooth crossover between them. Instead, qualitatively new dynamics emerge at $g τ\sim 1$, including rapid transitions between metastable states that occur in neither limiting regime. We then develop a general, growth-compatible theoretical framework in which unconstrained growth is intrinsically stress-free, extending the analysis to other prototypical geometries and enabling simulations of more realistic growing biological materials. Within this framework, sharp mechanical transitions arise when stress generated by exponential growth accumulates faster than it can be dissipated by viscoelastic relaxation.

</details>


### [241] [Motile Bacteria Modify Salt Precipitation Patterns in Dried Sessile Droplet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15378)
*Yumeng Zhao,Boyoung Jeong,Markus C. Noll,Sheng C. Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验与多尺度建模相结合的方法，揭示了大肠杆菌（Escherichia coli）的运动能力可以改变蒸发液滴中盐结晶的形貌模式：运动细菌能克服早期阶段蒸发诱导的流动，作为成核位点调控结晶形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上蒸发液滴中的溶质沉积遵循"咖啡环效应"（coffee-ring effect），但作者发现运动细菌的存在能够显著改变盐结晶的分布模式，这一现象尚未被系统研究，其背后机制也缺乏定量解释。

Method: 实验上观察不同细菌浓度下盐结晶的分布与形态变化；理论上采用基于Stokes的分析模型估算内部流场演化并与细菌运动性对比，同时建立有限体积模型描述细菌与盐的输运和吸附，并结合随机模型模拟盐成核过程。

Result: 在高细胞密度下细菌分布趋于均匀；有细菌存在时液滴中心出现孤立晶体且其数量随细菌浓度增加，外围晶体则呈径向延伸的树枝状（dendritic）形态；多尺度模型成功再现了实验中观察到的结晶模式。

Conclusion: 细菌的运动性能够克服早期阶段蒸发诱导的流动，使细菌细胞充当成核位点从而改变最终结晶形态；这项工作凸显了运动微生物主动调控蒸发结晶的潜力，对多孔介质流动和微流控沉积过程具有启示意义。

Abstract: Motile Escherichia coli bacteria can alter salt crystallization patterns during the evaporation of sessile droplets. In dilute bacterial suspensions in deionized water, dried bacteria cells predominantly accumulate at the droplet periphery, consistent with the classic "coffee-ring" effect. At higher cell densities, however, the bacterial distribution becomes more uniform. In the absence of bacteria, pure Phosphate Buffered Saline also forms salt crystals in a coffee-ring pattern. When bacteria are present alongside the salt solute, additional isolated crystals appear near the droplet center, with their abundance increasing with bacterial concentration, while crystals at the periphery adopt dendritic morphologies that extend radially. To investigate these phenomena, we used a Stokes-based analytical model to estimate the evolution of internal flow fields and compare them with bacterial motility. Then a finite volume model is implemented for bacteria and salt transport and adsorption, and a stochastic model for salt nucleation was developed, which successfully explains the crystallization pattern seen in the experiments. Our results show that bacterial motility can overcome evaporation induced flow during early stage, enabling bacteria cells to serve as nucleation sites and thereby altering the final crystalline morphology. This work highlights the potential of motile microorganisms to actively control evaporative crystallization, with implications for porous media flow and microfluidic deposition processes.

</details>


### [242] [Why the Multi-Sphere Shape Generator Works: Medial-Axis Placement of Spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15457)
*Arash Moradian,Felix Buchele,Thorsten Poeschel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文证明了多球形状生成器（Multi-Sphere Shape Generator, MSS）中残差场的每个局部极大值都位于目标形状的中轴（medial axis）上，从而首次为MSS的球体放置策略提供了数学基础。数值实验表明，与精确中轴的偏差不超过体素分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSS通过基于特征增强残差场放置球体来生成形状，取得了良好效果，但该策略的几何依据此前未知。作者旨在揭示MSS球体放置位置背后的数学原理，为其精度提供理论解释。

Method: 作者通过数学证明的方式，论证残差场的每个局部极大值必位于目标形状的中轴上，表明MSS实际上将球体放置于最大内切球（maximal inscribed sphere）的中心，而无需显式进行骨架提取；随后通过数值实验验证该结论。

Result: 理论证明成立，数值测试显示MSS球体位置与精确中轴的偏离仅限于体素分辨率（voxel resolution）的量级。

Conclusion: 该结果为MSS的球体放置策略提供了严格的数学解释，阐明了其高准确性的内在机理，即MSS隐含地实现了基于中轴的几何放置。

Abstract: The Multi-Sphere Shape Generator (MSS) [1] places spheres according to a feature-enhanced residual field, but the geometric basis of this strategy has remained unknown. We prove that every local maximum of the residual field lies on the medial axis of the target shape, implying that MSS places spheres at the centers of maximal inscribed spheres without explicit skeleton extraction. Numerical tests show that deviations from the exact medial axis are limited to the voxel resolution. This result provides a mathematical explanation for the placement strategy that underlies the accuracy of MSS.

</details>


### [243] [GEMSS: A C++ Library for Multi-Sphere Modeling in DEM Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15463)
*Arash Moradian,Felix Buchele,Thorsten Poeschel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: GEMSS是一个基于多球体（multi-sphere）算法的头文件式C++库，可将三维表面网格或体素栅格转换为颗粒的多球体表示，并计算离散元方法（DEM）所需的物理属性（体积、质心、主惯性矩和惯性轴），已集成到MercuryDPM中支持仿真过程中实时生成多球体颗粒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散元方法等仿真框架需要将任意形状的颗粒表面网格或体素表示转换为便于动力学计算的多球体表示，同时需要精确获取颗粒的关键物理属性。

Method: GEMSS采用最近发表的MSS算法（generator of multi-sphere shapes），将三维表面网格（surface meshes）或体素栅格（voxel grids）转换为颗粒的多球体表示，并计算体积、质心、主惯性矩及惯性轴等物理属性；实现为头文件式（header-only）C++库以简化集成。

Result: GEMSS已成功集成到离散元仿真框架MercuryDPM中，能够在仿真循环内实现多球体颗粒的即时（on-the-fly）生成，无需预先离线转换。

Conclusion: GEMSS作为一种轻量级、易集成的库，适用于DEM和一般多体动力学（multibody dynamics）仿真框架；其在MercuryDPM中的集成验证了其在实际仿真工作流中的实用性与即用能力。

Abstract: GEMSS (GEnerator of Multi-Sphere Shapes) converts 3D surface meshes or voxel grids into multi-sphere representations of granular particles using the recently published MSS algorithm. It computes key physical properties required for discrete element method (DEM) and general multibody dynamics simulations, including particle volume, center of mass, and principal moments and axes of inertia. Implemented as a header-only C++ library, GEMSS is easily integrated into DEM and molecular dynamics frameworks. The library has been integrated into MercuryDPM, which enables on-the-fly generation of multi-sphere particles directly within the simulation loop.

</details>


### [244] [Complex nonlinear dynamics of area-preserving, active vesicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15610)
*Reiner Kree,Annette Zippelius*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究了低雷诺数（low Reynolds number）下具有局部不可伸展膜（locally inextensible membrane）的活性驱动准球形囊泡（vesicle）的非线性形状动力学与自主推进。研究表明，全局面积约束作为唯一非线性源，可使原本线性的动力学系统展现出丰富的非线性行为，包括同步、相位滑移、模式锁定及准周期不变环面等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活性驱动软物质系统在低雷诺数条件下的形状动力学与推进机制，探索几何约束如何将线性动力学系统转化为非线性动力学系统。

Method: 从斯托克斯流体力学（Stokes hydrodynamics）、线性化膜弹性和谐波主动强迫出发，利用球谐变形模态（spherical harmonic deformation modes）建立约化描述。通过分析不同数量主动模态下的动力学行为，并运用递归诊断（recurrence diagnostic）和周期平均推进涨落来表征系统。

Result: 局部不可伸展性所强加的全局面积约束是唯一动态非线性来源，将动力学限制在形状空间的紧致流形上。自主推进由形状空间轨迹方向的定向面积几何决定；两模态系统表现为周期驱动相位方程，呈现同步、相位滑移和模式锁定；引入第三模态则产生准周期不变环面与共振周期循环。

Conclusion: 对于活性驱动囊泡，几何约束足以将线性动力学系统转化为具有丰富非线性动力学的系统，周期平均推进的涨落可作为实验上可观测的形状动力学特征信号。

Abstract: We investigate the nonlinear shape dynamics and autonomous propulsion of actively driven quasi-spherical vesicles with locally inextensible membranes at low Reynolds number. Starting from Stokes hydrodynamics, linearized membrane elasticity, and harmonic active forcing, we derive a reduced description in terms of spherical harmonic deformation modes. The global area constraint enforced by local inextensibility is the sole source of dynamic nonlinearity. It confines the dynamics to compact manifolds in the space of possible shapes. Autonomous propulsion arises through nonlinear mode coupling and is determined geometrically by the oriented area swept by the trajectories in shape space. For two active modes, the dynamics reduces to a periodically driven phase equation exhibiting synchronization, phase slips, and mode locking. Introducing a third active mode fundamentally changes the dynamics, giving rise to quasiperiodic invariant tori and resonant periodic cycles. A recurrence diagnostic reveals the resulting resonance structure, while fluctuations of the cycle-averaged propulsion provide an experimentally accessible signature of the underlying shape dynamics. Our results demonstrate that, for actively driven vesicles, a geometric constraint is sufficient to transform an otherwise linear dynamical system into one exhibiting rich nonlinear dynamics.

</details>


### [245] [Finite strain homogenization of periodic rod networks with application to semi-flexible biopolymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16117)
*Vinayak,Prashant K. Purohit,Ajeet Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究采用有限应变计算均质化（finite strain computational homogenization）方法，基于8-链和14-链周期网络模型表征半柔性生物聚合物网络（semi-flexible biopolymer networks）的力学响应，纤维采用几何精确的特殊Cosserat杆理论建模，成功再现了应变硬化、压缩软化、反向Poynting效应等生物网络独特现象，并与实验数据取得良好一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半柔性生物聚合物网络展现出诸多不同于传统材料的独特力学现象，需要一个能在大变形条件下准确描述其微观纤维力学行为的计算框架。现有建模方法在捕获非仿射变形（non-affine deformation）、纤维弯曲与屈曲等非线性机制方面存在不足。

Method: 采用几何精确的特殊Cosserat杆理论（special Cosserat rod theory）对微观纤维进行建模，构建8-链和14-链周期网络模型；同时执行宏观应变驱动和应力驱动的均质化计算，涵盖单轴拉伸、压缩与简单剪切工况；采用无缺陷、非线性的路径跟踪（path following）方法求解后屈曲解并验证其稳定性。

Result: 均质化结果复现了生物聚合物网络的独特现象，包括单轴拉伸下的应变硬化（strain-stiffening）与体积收缩、压缩下的软化（softening）以及简单剪切下的反向Poynting效应（reverse Poynting effect）；微观尺度的非线性与非仿射变形、特别是纤维弯曲与屈曲在这些现象中起关键作用；与实验数据对比呈良好一致性；将8-链单元中的纤维替换为螺旋杆后可捕获柔性超材料（compliant metastructures）中的伸长行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的有限应变均质化框架能有效模拟半柔性生物聚合物网络在大变形下的复杂力学响应，微观纤维的弯曲与屈曲是宏观非线性现象的关键微观机制；本方法还具备扩展到螺旋纤维构型以模拟超材料行为的潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we adopt a finite strain computational homogenization approach to characterize the response of semi-flexible biopolymer networks modeled as idealized 8- and 14-chain periodic networks. We use the geometrically exact special Cosserat rod theory to model the microscale fibers forming these 8- and 14-chain networks. This allows us to capture arbitrarily large microscale deformations. Both macroscopic strain- and stress-driven homogenization are performed to study the macroscopic uniaxial tension, compression and simple shear responses. Several phenomena unique to biopolymer networks are recovered such as strain-stiffening and volume shrinkage under uniaxial tension, softening under compression and reverse Poynting effect under simple shear. We find that nonlinearity and non-affine deformation at microscale, especially bending and buckling of microscale fibers, plays an important role in these phenomena. We obtain the postbuckled solutions of the homogenization problem using a nonlinear, imperfection-free path following approach and also check for their stability. We further compare our homogenization results with experimental data for uniaxial tension and compression of biofilament networks and find good agreement. When the fibers are replaced by helical rods in the 8-chain unit cell, we are also able to capture the enlarged stretching behaviour as shown in recently fabricated compliant metastructures.

</details>


### [246] [Direct inference of viscoelastic memory from chirp rheometry via physics-informed Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16306)
*Isaac Y. Miranda-Valdez,Juha Koivisto,Mikko J. Alava*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息的高斯过程（Gaussian process）框架，直接从单个啁啾（chirp）流变实验的原始时域数据中推断软材料的本构律（constitutive law），无需中间信号处理步骤，并能泛化预测未见过的变形历史响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软材料会记住其变形历史，从实验中识别这种记忆对预测材料在实际载荷下的行为至关重要。虽然啁啾流变测量可快速产生海量应力-应变数据，但标准流程将此记录压缩为少数频域估计后才拟合本构模型，大量信息被丢弃。

Method: 提出物理信息高斯过程框架，直接在单个啁啾实验的原始时域记录中推断材料本构律，在候选记忆核（memory kernel）中进行选择并参数化，无需中间信号处理。框架推断的是记忆核而非训练时的具体波形。

Result: 该框架能够预测材料对从未见过的变形历史的响应而无需重新训练；此外，它还能在单个啁啾实验内直接在时域中解析材料演化（material evolution）。

Conclusion: 通过绕过频域压缩直接利用原始时域数据，该方法保留了啁啾流变测量的信息密度，实现了无需重训的本构泛化预测以及单次测量内的材料演化解析。

Abstract: Soft materials remember their deformation history, and identifying that memory from experiments is essential for predicting how these materials behave under real-world loading conditions. Chirp rheometry has recently emerged as a way to accelerate this characterization, compressing hours of conventional measurement into seconds and yielding thousands of stress-strain pairs per experiment. That density is then largely discarded: the standard pipeline reduces the record to a handful of frequency-domain estimates before any constitutive model is fitted. We introduce a physics-informed Gaussian process framework that infers the material's constitutive law directly from the raw time-domain record of a single chirp, selecting among candidate memory kernels and parametrizing the selected one without any intermediate signal processing step. Because the framework infers the memory kernel rather than the specific waveform used during training, it predicts the response to deformation histories it never saw, without retraining. The method also resolves material evolution within a single chirp directly in the time domain.

</details>


### [247] [Experimental evidence of an Apolar Biaxial Smectic-A Phase Comprised of Bent-Core Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16462)
*Susovan Bhandary,Abhijith K,Arun Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究对一种由不对称弯核分子（asymmetric bent-core molecules）构成的化合物所展现的双轴近晶A相（biaxial smectic-A phase）进行了结构与物理性质表征，通过偏振光学显微、X射线衍射、极化反转电流及介电谱等方法，证实了该相的双轴性而无层极化，并发现其平面排列样品表现出无需层结构重组的显著电光响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双轴近晶A相在液晶领域具有重要的基础物理意义，但迄今被明确研究的实例仍然较少，特别是由不对称弯核分子形成的情况尚未被充分探索，因此需要对该相的结构与物理性质进行系统性实验表征。

Method: 采用偏振光学显微（polarized optical microscopy）、X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）、极化反转电流（polarization reversal current）测量、介电谱（dielectric spectroscopy）以及电光响应实验，对降温过程中的相变序列和近晶A相的双轴性、层间距、缺陷结构及弛豫行为进行了综合分析。

Result: 观测到降温相序为各向同性相（Isotropic, 403.9 K）→ 双轴近晶A相（359.8 K）→ 晶体。双轴近晶A相已被证实无层极化；层间距在整个温度范围内接近分子长度；垂直取向样品呈现±1/2和±1向错（defects），显示双轴性；介电弛豫为德拜型（Debye-type），弛豫时间随温度符合阿伦尼乌斯方程（Arrhenius equation）；平面取向样品表现出显著电光响应，且无需层结构重组。

Conclusion: 不对称弯核分子能够形成稳定的无层极化双轴近晶A相，其双轴性由多种独立实验手段交叉验证，且该相具有有趣的电场响应特性，为深入理解弯核液晶中双轴近晶相的物理机制提供了新的实验依据。

Abstract: We report structural and physical investigations on the biaxial smectic-A phase exhibited by a compound consisting of asymmetric bent-core molecules. Upon cooling from the isotropic phase, the compound exhibits the following phase sequence: Isotropic (403.9 K) $\rightarrow$ biaxial Smectic-A (359.8 K) $\rightarrow$ Crystal. The polarized optical microscopy, X-ray diffraction, and polarization reversal current measurements clearly establish the biaxial nature of the smectic-A phase without any layer polarization. The measured layer spacing in the entire temperature range of the smectic-A phase is close to the molecular length. The schlieren texture in a homeotropically aligned sample shows both $\pm \frac{1}{2}$ and $\pm 1$ defects which indicate the biaxial nature of this smectic-A phase. The dielectric spectroscopy studies on the samples revealed Debye-type relaxation processes with the relaxation time following the Arrhenius equation with temperature. Interestingly, the observed biaxial smectic-A phase exhibits a remarkable electro-optic response for a planar-aligned sample without any reorganization of the smectic layer structure.

</details>


### [248] [Approximation of anisotropic pairwise interactions for charged objects using multivariate polynomials and a multipole expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16782)
*Mohammadreza Fakhraei,Dylan McElheny,Chris A. Kieslich,Michael P. Howard*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息驱动的数据方法（physics-informed data-driven method），用于在存在长程静电作用（long-ranged electrostatics）的条件下建模各向异性成对相互作用。方法将总相互作用分解为偶极矩截断的多极展开（multipole expansion）近似的长程静电部分，以及用多元切比雪夫多项式（multivariate Chebyshev polynomials）拟合短程残差部分，并在苯、苯腈和苯氧负离子等芳香族分子序列上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合成和生物材料中，带电体系内的各向异性成对相互作用建模长期受限于长程静电效应的处理难度，传统方法难以在有限构型数据下同时兼顾精度与计算成本。

Method: 方法将总相互作用分解为两部分：长程静电相互作用通过截断至偶极水平的多极展开近似；短程残差相互作用则利用多元切比雪夫多项式对有限数量构型的测量数据进行拟合。

Result: 在苯（benzene）、苯腈（benzonitrile）和苯氧负离子（phenoxide）序列的芳香族分子测试中，该方法在采用适度的短程相互作用截断距离（cutoff distance）时即可获得令人满意的结果。

Conclusion: 该方法为含电荷的合成和生物材料中复杂相互作用的建模以及动力学模拟（dynamic simulations）提供了一种可行的途径。

Abstract: We formulate a physics-informed data-driven method for modeling anisotropic pairwise interactions in the presence of long-ranged electrostatics. The method separates the total interaction into a long-ranged electrostatic interaction that is approximated using a multipole expansion truncated at the dipole level and a short-ranged residual interaction that is approximated using multivariate Chebyshev polynomials fit to measurements from a limited number of configurations. We assess the approach on a sequence of aromatic molecules (benzene, benzonitrile, and phenoxide), finding that it produces satisfactory results using a modest cutoff distance for the short-ranged interaction. This method has applications for modeling complex interactions for, and conducting dynamic simulations of, synthetic and biological materials with charge.

</details>


### [249] [Size matters more than packing in bimodal colloidal gel compositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16874)
*Robert A. Campbell,Ziye Zhuang,Ali Mohraz,Safa Jamali*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用大规模水动力学解析模拟（hydrodynamically resolved simulation）研究大小比为1:2的双峰（bimodal）胶体耗竭凝胶，发现增加大颗粒比例主要改变凝胶的介观结构与弹性模量所依赖的有效长度尺度，而非改变刚性网络的局部拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际胶体凝胶配方常含多种粒径的颗粒，但现有模型多为单分散粒子网络，缺乏对多分散体系中粒径效应与局部堆积效应的系统性理解。

Method: 采用大规模、水动力学解析的胶体耗竭凝胶（depletion gel）模拟，在大小比1:2的双峰体系中系统改变大颗粒占比，隔离粒径与局部堆积的影响，并结合Cauchy-Born理论估算弹性模量。

Result: 增加大颗粒比例引入了新的异型角构型（heterotypic angular motif），并显著提高参与四面体（tetrahedral）结构的键分数，在中间组成处达到最大值；但平均配位数与特征聚集体尺寸几乎不随组成变化，而空隙与团簇尺寸分布在归一化后按有效粒子长度尺度折叠。

Conclusion: 对于中等粒径差异，颗粒尺寸对双峰胶体凝胶的结构尺度与预测力学响应的影响强于增强的局部堆积效应，组成变化主要重标度凝胶架构而非产生截然不同的刚性网络拓扑。

Abstract: Colloidal gels are frequently modeled as monodisperse particle networks, although practical formulations commonly contain particles with multiple characteristic sizes. Here, we use large-scale, hydrodynamically resolved simulations of colloidal depletion gels to isolate the effects of particle size and local packing in bimodal systems with a small-to-large size ratio of 1:2. Increasing the large-particle fraction introduces new heterotypic angular motifs and substantially increases the fraction of bonds participating in tetrahedral structures, with a maximum at intermediate composition. However, these additional rigid motifs do not reorganize into larger or more highly connected tetrahedral aggregates. The mean coordination and characteristic aggregate size remain nearly composition independent. By contrast, the void and cluster-size distributions coarsen systematically as the large-particle fraction increases. These mesoscale distributions largely collapse when normalized by a composition-dependent particle length scale, indicating that changes in composition primarily rescale gel architecture rather than producing distinct rigid-network topologies. An elastic modulus estimated using Cauchy-Born theory similarly follows this effective length scale more closely than the abundance of local tetrahedral motifs. These results show that, for moderate size disparity, particle size controls the structural scale and predicted mechanical response of bimodal colloidal gels more strongly than enhanced local packing.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [MacDowell-Mansouri $σ$-model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14596)
*Egor Pluzhnikov,Stanislav Alexeyev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过将MacDowell-Mansouri模型的李代数（Lie algebra）扩展为李代数胚（Lie algebroid），提出了一种标量耦合拓扑项（scalar-coupled topological terms）的涌现机制，将其作为宇宙暴胀（cosmological inflation）的来源。该理论可归约为Horndeski类标量-张量理论（scalar-tensor theory），并再现满足Planck/BICEP/Keck和GW170817等现有约束的暴胀动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的前几何（pre-geometric）机制能够复现ΛCDM模型，但缺乏暴胀的自然来源；本文旨在弥补这一不足，为暴胀提供前几何框架下的理论起源。

Method: 将MacDowell-Mansouri模型的李代数扩展为李代数胚，从而在理论中涌现出标量耦合拓扑项——这类项在有效弦作用量（effective string actions）中被普遍视为暴胀的来源。由此将理论约化为属于Horndeski类的标量-张量理论。

Result: 所构建的理论能够再现暴胀动力学，且满足现有观测约束，包括Planck/BICEP/Keck的宇宙微波背景观测数据以及来自GW170817引力波事件的约束。

Conclusion: 该研究在前几何框架内实现了暴胀机制的自然涌现，为其提供了与观测相容的理论来源，弥补了标准前几何机制缺乏暴胀起源的不足。

Abstract: Standard pre-geometric mechanisms reproduce the ΛCDM model but lack a natural source for inflation. We propose a mechanism for the emergence of scalar-coupled topological terms by extending the Lie algebra of a MacDowell-Mansouri model to a Lie algebroid. These terms are commonly regarded as a source for cosmological inflation in effective string actions. The theory reduces to the scalar-tensor theory belonging to the Horndeski class, and reproduces inflationary dynamics that meet existing constraints (Planck/BICEP/Keck and GW170817).

</details>


### [251] [Catastrophic Inflation in the Axiverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14780)
*Naomi Gendler,Oliver Janssen,Matthew Kleban,Cameron Norton*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在IIB型弦论（type IIB string theory）于Calabi-Yau三流形的O3/O7 orientifold紧化背景下，识别出特定几何中支持小场暴胀（small-field inflation）的轴子势（axion potential）区域。通过调节Calabi-Yau体积使其处于特定值，轴子势的多个临界点在"突变"（catastrophe）中合并，产生缓滚暴胀（slow-roll inflation）所需的小梯度和小的Hessian行列式条件，从而在轴子场范围远小于普朗克质量（f/M_Pl ≪ 1）的情况下仍能暴胀数千e-folds。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦论紧化中轴子暴胀模型通常依赖对齐机制（alignment）或大场范围来获得足够的e-folds，但这类方案在弦景观中实现难度较大。作者旨在证明无需这些特殊机制，仅依靠弦论自身结构中临界点合并形成的"突变"现象，即可在自然构造中实现小场暴胀。

Method: 论文假设Calabi-Yau总体体积可被稳定在特定调谐值，在此条件下分析轴子势的多临界点合并结构。作者设计了一个算法来系统搜索具有"突变"结构的显式几何例子，该突变区域具有小梯度和小的Hessian行列式，满足缓滚暴胀的必要条件，并进一步计算暴胀的观测预言。

Result: 算法找到了多个显式例子，其轴子场范围满足 f/M_Pl ≪ 1，却能暴胀数千e-folds。部分例子正确复现了观测到的标量谱指数（spectral tilt），但无法匹配标量扰动振幅（amplitude）；另一些例子能复现振幅，但其谱指数略偏红。所有例子中张量扰动（tensor perturbations）可忽略，缓滚阶段生成的显著非高斯性（non-Gaussianity）为零。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，弦论轴子（string theory axions）可以成为宇宙暴胀的来源，而不需要对齐机制或大场范围，前提是Kähler模（Kähler moduli）能被稳定在体积的特定特殊值。这一发现为小场暴胀模型提供了一条源自弦论的现实构建路径。

Abstract: In the landscape of compactifications of type IIB string theory on O3/O7 orientifolds of Calabi--Yau threefolds, we identify regions of the axion potential in specific geometries that support small-field inflation, under the assumption that the overall Calabi--Yau volume can be stabilized at certain tuned values. At the tuned volume multiple critical points of the axion potential merge in a ``catastrophe'', creating a region with small gradient and small Hessian determinant --- necessary conditions for slow-roll inflation. We describe an algorithm that identifies several explicit examples of these catastrophes that have small field range $f / M_{\rm Pl} \ll 1 $ yet inflate for thousands of efolds. Some examples correctly reproduce the observed spectral tilt but not the amplitude of scalar perturbations, while others reproduce the observed amplitude but with a tilt that is slightly too red. All our examples have negligible tensor perturbations and no significant non-Gaussianity generated during the slow-roll phase. Our results demonstrate that string theory axions can be the source of cosmic inflation without alignment or large field ranges, provided that the Kähler moduli can be stabilized at special values of the volume.

</details>


### [252] [What is the simplest holographic matrix model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14781)
*Harish Murali,Pedro Vieira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了对J. Hoppe在1989年提出的双矩阵模型（two-matrix model）的推广。在强耦合和大N极限下，该模型展现出涌现的面积保持微分同胚对称性（area preserving diffeomorphism symmetry），使得模型在领头阶可完全求解。所有n点连通关联函数（connected correlator）的解可表示为在涌现的二维对偶时空（dual spacetime）中的Witten图展开。作者最后讨论了该对偶模型的量子化猜想及其在高维推广和全息原理（holography）方面的潜在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过对Hoppe双矩阵模型的推广，探索强耦合大N极限下涌现的对称性和对偶结构，以期揭示矩阵模型与时空涌现及全息原理之间的深层联系。

Method: 在强耦合和大N极限下分析推广后的双矩阵模型，识别其涌现的面积保持微分同胚对称性，并利用该对称性在领头阶完全求解模型；将n点连通关联函数的解构造为二维涌现时空中的Witten图展开。

Result: 该模型在强耦合大N极限下涌现出面积保持微分同胚对称性，使模型可在领头阶完全求解。所有n点连通关联函数均可用Witten图展开形式在二维对偶时空中表示。

Conclusion: 作者推测了该对偶模型的量子化方案，并讨论了将其推广到更高维度以及与更广义全息框架建立联系的可能性。

Abstract: We study a generalization of a two-matrix model proposed by J. Hoppe in '89. At strong coupling and large $N$, we unveil an emergent area preserving diffeomorphism symmetry of this model, which allows us to completely solve it at leading order. The solution for all $n$-point connected correlators takes the form of a Witten diagram-like expansion in an emergent two-dimensional dual spacetime. We conclude with speculations about the quantization of this dual model and comment on its extensions to higher dimensions and relations to holography more broadly.

</details>


### [253] [Universal Emergence of Bosonic and Fermionic Algebras in a Deterministic Proper-Time Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14816)
*Alessio Maiezza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在先前提出的确定性离散固有时间（proper-time）框架基础上，赋予其动力学与算符实现，并将构造推广到费米子自由度。通过宏观分辨率的改变推导出重整化群方程（Renormalization Group Equation）的结构，并证明在粗粒化后，玻色子与费米子扇区共享同一个由微观计数得到的有效普朗克常数（effective Planck constant），从而将微观演化与涌现的量子代数联系起来。文章最后探讨了该框架对高能圈振幅和有效霍金辐射（effective Hawking radiation）的可能意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先前的论文中，量子场论被描述为具有运行普朗克常数（running Planck constant）的有效红外描述，其有效量子化尺度由粗粒化未解析的微观多重度推断而来。然而，该框架尚缺乏明确的动力学和算符实现，且未扩展到费米子自由度，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文首先从宏观分辨率变化下的一致性要求出发，推导出重整化群方程的结构，并阐明运行普朗克常数如何控制向确定性（deterministic）领域的交叉。其次，通过场构型空间中的有限差分平移来表示可逆微观动力学，并在粗粒化后验证算符值正则对易关系（canonical commutation relations）再现了统计微观态计数所得的量化尺度。最后，将同一更新表示在格拉斯曼代数（Grassmann algebra）中，得到相应的正则反对易关系（canonical anticommutation relations），使费米子扇区继承与玻色子扇区相同的有效普朗克常数。

Result: 本文成功实现了确定性离散固有时间框架的动力学与算符化构造，并将之推广至费米子自由度。结果表明，粗粒化后的正则对易关系精确再现了此前由统计微观态计数导出的量化尺度，且玻色子与费米子扇区通过共享的格拉斯曼表示共同继承同一有效普朗克常数，无需引入独立的费米子更新或量化尺度。

Conclusion: 该框架将微观演化动力学与涌现的正则代数建立了直接联系，为量子场论作为有效低能理论的理解提供了统一图景。文中还对高能圈振幅和有效霍金辐射的可能影响进行了初步讨论，但具体的物理后果有待后续研究。

Abstract: This work develops the deterministic, discrete proper-time framework introduced in our previous work, Eur.\ Phys.\ J.\ C \textbf{86} (2026) 829, in which quantum field theory emerges as an effective infrared description characterized by a running Planck constant. There, the effective quantization scale was inferred from the microscopic multiplicity unresolved by coarse-graining. Here, we provide its dynamical and operatorial realization and extend the construction to fermionic degrees of freedom. First, consistency under changes of macroscopic resolution leads to the structure of the Renormalization Group Equation, while the running Planck constant governs the crossover toward the deterministic regime. Second, we represent the reversible microscopic dynamics through finite-difference translations in field-configuration space. After coarse-graining, the resulting operator-valued canonical commutation relations reproduce the same quantization scale previously obtained from statistical microstate counting, thereby linking microscopic evolution to the emergent canonical algebra. Third, representing the same update within a Grassmann algebra yields the corresponding canonical anticommutation relations. The bosonic and fermionic sectors thus inherit a common effective Planck constant without introducing an independent fermionic update or quantization scale. Finally, we discuss possible implications for high-energy loop amplitudes and effective Hawking radiation.

</details>


### [254] [Applications of Flux Compactifications in F-Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14844)
*Shing Yan Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文研究带通量（fluxes）的四维F-理论紧致化及其与粒子物理的联系。作者发展了利用纵向通量（vertical flux）和余切通量（remainder flux）破缺刚性规范群的通用形式体系，可实现标准模型规范群与手征物质的实现；详细研究了刚性E_7模型的唯象学，发现其自然兼容质子衰变抑制等许多类标准模型性质；最后展示了通过竖向通量将刚性非阿贝尔群破缺至U(1)规范群时，矢量样物质可携带高达657的超大电荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦理论（string theory）紧致化如何产生标准模型（Standard Model）的规范群与手征物质谱，是粒子物理唯象学中的核心问题。论文旨在系统探索F-理论紧致化中通量诱导的规范对称性破缺机制，并考察其能否自然产生类标准模型特征以及极端电荷等奇异现象。

Method: 论文建立了通量破缺刚性规范群的通用形式体系：纵向通量可在破缺规范群的同时诱发手征物质，余切通量可辅助破缺。据此通过刚性E_7、E_6规范群经中间SU(5)规范群的破缺来实现标准模型规范群与手征物质；随后从群论与F-理论几何结构出发分析E_7模型的唯象性质；最后利用竖向通量将刚性非阿贝尔群破缺为一U(1)规范群以产生超大电荷。

Result: 研究表明有三代手征物质的实现概率相当高，并给出了显式构造示例。刚性E_7模型中，维度4与维度5质子衰变普遍被抑制，这源于E_7群论结构及其F-理论几何导出的一组近似全局对称性，导致不期望耦合被指数级压低。对于阿贝尔规范对称性构造，给出了独立的四维F-理论构造，其中矢量样物质携带高达657的电荷，远超此前已知的F-理论电荷界。

Conclusion: 刚性E_7模型的上述特征暗示了一类基于E_7群及其弦理论构造的新大统一理论（GUT）候选方案。超大电荷结果被启发式地论证可能为F-理论景观（landscape）中解耦U(1)规范群下轻场电荷提供上界。

Abstract: This thesis studies 4D F-theory compactifications with fluxes and their connection to particle physics, accompanied with a review of F-theory.
  First, we develop a general formalism for gauge symmetry breaking with fluxes. Vertical and remainder fluxes can be used to break a rigid gauge group, with the former simultaneously inducing chiral matter, even when the unbroken gauge group has no complex representations. We show that it is fairly likely to have three generations of chiral matter. We focus on the realization of the Standard Model gauge groups and chiral matter by breaking rigid $E_7,E_6$ gauge groups with an intermediate SU(5) gauge group, and provide examples of explicit constructions.
  Then, the phenomenological aspects of rigid $E_7$ models are studied in detail. We find that many other Standard-Model like features are naturally compatible with the $E_7$ models. For example, dimension-4 and dimension-5 proton decay are ubiquitously suppressed. Many of these features are due to the group-theoretic structure of $E_7$ and its F-theory geometry. In particular, a set of approximate global symmetries descends from the $E_7$ group, leading to exponential suppression of undesired couplings. These features suggest a new set of grand unified theories based on the $E_7$ group and its string theory construction.
  Finally, we study constructions of abelian gauge symmetries with exotic charges. By breaking a rigid nonabelian group to a U(1) gauge group using vertical flux, very large charges can arise in the massless or light spectrum. We give an explicit construction in 4D F-theory in which the vector-like matter carries charges as large as 657, which is much larger than the previously known bounds in F-theory. We heuristically argue that this result may provide an upper bound on charges for light fields under decoupled U(1) gauge groups in the F-theory landscape.

</details>


### [255] [On M2-, M5-, and D3-branes from 12d anomalies: an equivariant perspective on duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14932)
*Kiril Hristov,Pedro Vicente Marto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了传统上用于刻画M5膜世界体理论的十二维反常多项式及其对应的D3膜版本，证明这些构造自然归属于等变化积分（equivariant integration）框架，并将反常流入（anomaly inflow）公式完全改写为等变化体积（equivariant volumes）形式。作者进一步将反常构造推广至同时包含电荷和磁荷的膜，从而纳入M2膜及D3膜的对偶表述，提供了统一框架来解释若干经典结果和近期发展，并将M2/M5对偶重新诠释为AGT型对应关系，同时给出拓扑弦理论（topological string theory）中常数映射贡献的M理论反常解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期已知的十二维反常多项式控制着M5膜世界体理论，但其与近期提出的D3膜对应物之间的关系尚不清晰。作者旨在将这些看似独立的结构统一到等变化积分框架中，并探索其更深层的物理含义。

Method: 利用等变化欧拉类积分的数学框架，通过Bott-Cattaneo公式将全球角形式（global angular form）在$S^{2k}$上的幂次积分与$\mathbb{C}^k$的等变化欧拉类积分建立联系，并在球面南北极以反足方式粘连两份拷贝。在此视角基础上，将反常构造推广至同时包含电性和磁性荷的膜系统。

Result: 反常流入公式可完全以等变化体积形式重写；构造被推广至包含M2膜及D3膜对偶表述的统一框架。获得的统一框架涵盖多项经典结果和近期发展，并为近期提出的M2/M5对偶（arxiv:2604.06343）提供了AGT型对应关系的自然诠释；此外，拓扑弦理论中的常数映射（constant-map）贡献被重新解释为M理论反常的体现。

Conclusion: 等变化积分框架为M5/M2及D3膜相关的反常构造提供了统一且自然的数学基础，揭示了弦/M理论中若干看似独立现象之间的深层联系，并建立了异常理论与拓扑弦理论之间的新关联。

Abstract: We revisit the twelve-dimensional anomaly polynomial that has long been known to govern M5-brane worldvolume theories, together with its more recently proposed D3-brane counterpart. We show that these constructions naturally fit into the framework of equivariant integration, allowing the anomaly inflow formulas to be rewritten entirely in terms of equivariant volumes. In particular, we use the fact that the Bott--Cattaneo formula for the integral of powers of the global angular form on $S^{2k}$ emerges from the equivariant Euler-class integration of $\mathbb{C}^k$, with two copies glued antipodally at the north and south poles of the sphere. Building on this perspective, we generalize the anomaly construction to include both electrically and magnetically charged branes, thereby incorporating M2-branes as well as a dual formulation of D3-branes. This provides a unified framework encompassing a number of classical results together with several recent developments, and offers a natural interpretation of the recently proposed M2/M5 duality, arxiv:2604.06343, in terms of an AGT-like correspondence. Remarkably, it also leads to a direct reinterpretation of constant-map contributions in topological string theory as manifestations of M-theory anomalies.

</details>


### [256] [History-Dependent Mode Selection in a Driven Holographic Superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14997)
*Bo-Xuan Ge*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息超流体（holographic superfluid）中研究了具有有限速率（finite-rate）的驱动下，竞争性朗道不稳定模（Landau-unstable modes）之间的模式选择问题。研究表明，被选择的模式并非一般意义上的瞬时最快增长模式，而驱动背景上的时间相关线性演化能够完全重現系统动力学。即使当前驱动条件完全相同，不同历史驱动协议可导致截然相反的模式排序，揭示系统对先前驱动的记忆。这种记忆可由竞争模式的准正则模（QNM）增长率累积差定量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性/动态环境中模式选择机制的一般规律，特别是当系统中存在多个竞争性不稳定模式时，何种模式最终胜出取决于哪些因素。

Method: 在全息超流体框架下研究有限速率驱动过程中朗道不稳定模式间的竞争，通过对比时间依赖线性演化与瞬时最快增长模式判断，并利用竞争模式的准正则模增长率累积差来量化驱动历史的记忆效应。

Result: 被选中模式不一定对应瞬时最快增长的模；时间依赖线性演化在驱动背景上可完整再现系统动力学；即使在相同当前驱动条件下，先前驱动历史的不同可导致相反的模式排序，且该记忆效应可被竞争模式QNM增长率差的累积量定量描述。

Conclusion: 模式选择本质上依赖于驱动历史而非仅由瞬时状态决定，时间依赖的线性演化提供了完整的动力学描述，QNM增长率累积差为这种历史记忆提供了定量的刻画指标。

Abstract: We study finite-rate mode selection among competing Landau-unstable modes in a holographic superfluid. The selected mode is not generally the instantaneous fastest-growing one, while time-dependent linear evolution on the driven background reproduces the full dynamics. Protocols with identical present driving conditions can exhibit opposite modal ordering, revealing memory of the preceding drive. This memory is quantitatively captured by the accumulated difference between the QNM growth rates of the competing modes.

</details>


### [257] [Spinning Conformal Correlators from Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15001)
*Manas Dogra,James Halverson,Joydeep Naskar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于神经网络与嵌入形式（embedding formalism）构造自旋共形场（spinning conformal fields），在示例中计算其二点、三点和四点关联函数，并在特定独立同分布（i.i.d.）神经元系综下，于无限宽极限（infinite-width limit）中重现四维麦克斯韦共形场论（4d Maxwell CFT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标量共形场（scalar conformal field）神经网络技术的基础上，将方法推广到自旋场的构造与关联函数计算，以探索神经网络与共形场论之间的深层联系。

Method: 结合神经网络架构与嵌入形式，扩展标量共形场技术框架以构造自旋共形场，并利用特定i.i.d.神经元系综推导二点、三点及四点关联函数。

Result: 在无限宽极限下，特定独立同分布神经元系综的神经网络配置成功恢复出四维麦克斯韦共形场论。

Conclusion: 神经网络框架可有效构造自旋共形场并复现已知共形场论（如4d麦克斯韦CFT），表明该方法具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We construct spinning conformal fields from neural networks and the embedding formalism, computing their two-, three- and four-point functions in examples, building on scalar conformal field techniques introduced in \cite{Halverson:2024axc}. For a particular ensemble of i.i.d. neurons we recover the 4d Maxwell CFT in the infinite-width limit.

</details>


### [258] [Quasinormal frequencies and greybody factors for axial perturbations of dilaton-Euler-Heisenberg de Sitter black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15111)
*Ming Zhang,Guo-Xin Chen,Xufen Zhang,Cheng-Fu Feng,Sheng-Yuan Li,Rui-Hong Yue,De-Cheng Zou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究弦启发欧拉-海森堡引力中胀子-欧拉-海森堡（dEH）de Sitter黑洞的准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）和灰体因子（greybody factors），发现轴向引力与电磁扰动解耦，且QNM谱对磁荷、宇宙学常数和非线性耦合参数高度敏感，并出现拓扑反常现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解弦理论启发的欧拉-海森堡引力框架下de Sitter黑洞的动力学与辐射特性，探究其准正则模和灰体因子对关键参数的依赖关系。

Method: 利用渐近迭代法（asymptotic iteration method, AIM）与六阶WKB近似（sixth-order WKB approximation）两种独立方法计算准正则频率；由于轴向引力与电磁扰动的解耦，对两种扰动分别独立处理并对两种方法的结果进行交叉验证。

Result: 两种计算方法所得准正则频率高度一致；QNM谱对磁荷、宇宙学常数以及非线性耦合参数敏感，电磁频率轨迹中出现显著的拓扑反常；较大的磁荷或多极数会抑制波动透过率，而电磁扇区中取值为1的非线性耦合参数表现出反常响应。

Conclusion: 该工作为dEH de Sitter黑洞的线谱特征与波传播性质提供了系统的数值分析，揭示了电磁扇区中与非线性耦合相关的反常拓扑行为，并强调磁荷与多极数对灰体因子的抑制作用。

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) and greybody factors of dilaton-Euler-Heisenberg (dEH) de Sitter (dS) black holes in string-inspired Euler-Heisenberg gravity. Since the axial gravitational and electromagnetic perturbations decouple, we treat them independently. By applying the asymptotic iteration method (AIM) alongside a sixth-order WKB approximation, we compute the quasinormal frequencies and find excellent agreement between the two approaches. We also find that the QNM spectra depend sensitively on the magnetic charge $Q_{\text{m}}$, cosmological constant $Λ$, and nonlinear coupling $ε$, with a notable topological anomaly appearing in the electromagnetic frequency trajectories. Additionally, larger values of $Q_{\text{m}}$ or the multipole number $l$ generally suppress wave transmission, while the electromagnetic sector with $ε=1$ exhibits an anomalous response.

</details>


### [259] [Topological Field Theory and Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15220)
*Igor V. Ovchinnikov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了随机微分方程（SDE）的超对称表述与Witten型拓扑场论（TFT）之间表面上的矛盾，并通过超对称随机动力学（STS）理论框架阐明这一矛盾。论文进一步指出，在该理论中，动力学混沌可视为拓扑超对称性的自发破缺，1/f噪声可由Goldstone定理解释，而蝴蝶效应则可能隐藏着拓扑结构，并借助Ginzburg-Landau方法与AdS/CFT对偶加以推测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Baulieu和Grossman在1980年代末证明了Parisi-Sourlas提出的Langevin随机微分方程超对称表述属于Witten型拓扑场论家族，但这一结论看似矛盾：拓扑场论不含局域自由度，而随机微分方程却具有局域涨落。作者致力于在STS框架下解决这一表面矛盾。

Method: 论文在超对称随机动力学（STS）理论框架内进行分析，该理论是Parisi-Sourlas-Baulieu-Grossman方法向任意形式随机微分方程的推广。作者通过将动力学混沌与拓扑超对称性的自发破缺相联系、将1/f噪声与Goldstone定理相联系来建立理论解释，并对蝴蝶效应的有效场论描述进行推测性讨论，基于Ginzburg-Landau方法和AdS/CFT对偶的洞见展开。

Result: 文章阐明STS框架可化解TFT与SDE之间关于局域自由度的表面矛盾。在STS中，动力学混沌被认定为拓扑超对称性的自发破缺，1/f噪声被视为Goldstone定理的自然结果；蝴蝶效应则可能对应具有隐藏拓扑结构的有效场论描述。

Conclusion: STS理论为理解随机动力学中的涌现现象提供了统一的场论视角：混沌、噪声与蝴蝶效应均可纳入拓扑超对称框架下的结构性或自发破缺机制予以解释，其中蝴蝶效应的潜在拓扑结构仍属推测，有待在Ginzburg-Landau及AdS/CFT方向进一步探索。

Abstract: In the late 1980s, Baulieu and Grossman demonstrated that the supersymmetric formulation of Langevin stochastic differential equations (SDEs), proposed earlier by Parisi and Sourlas, belongs to the family of Witten-type topological field theories (TFTs). From a certain angle, this finding may appear puzzling: TFTs have no local degrees of freedom, whereas SDEs do exhibit local fluctuations. In this paper, we address this apparent contradiction in the context of the supersymmetric theory of stochastic dynamics (STS), a generalization of the Parisi-Sourlas-Baulieu-Grossman approach to SDEs of arbitrary form. We further discuss how, within STS, dynamical chaos can be identified with the spontaneous breakdown of the corresponding topological supersymmetry, while 1/f noise can be understood as a consequence of the Goldstone theorem. The butterfly effect, in turn, calls for an effective field-theoretic description that may itself possess a hidden topological structure, as we speculate on the basis of the Ginzburg-Landau approach and insights from AdS/CFT duality.

</details>


### [260] [Higher-order analogues of colour-kinematics duality in first-order Yang-Mills Feynman diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15393)
*Leron Borsten,Dimitri Kanakaris,Hyungrok Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证了颜色-运动学对偶（colour-kinematics duality）在标准杨-米尔斯理论（Yang-Mills theory）拉格朗日量中并非显然，但通过高阶微分算子可得到其弱形式。作者证明一阶杨-米尔斯作用量的超空间（superspace）表述在Batalin-Vilkovisky形式体系中，于任意时空维度都存在六阶微分算子，从而使费曼图展开自动满足弱形式的颜色-运动学对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使在树级（tree level），颜色-运动学对偶在标准杨-米尔斯理论的拉格朗日量中也不是显式的，因为通常的费曼图（Feynman diagram）展开不遵循运动学雅可比恒等式（kinematic Jacobi identities）。作者希望寻找一种拉格朗日量表述，使这些恒等式能够自然涌现。

Method: 作者利用BV□-代数（BV□-algebra）的概念，指出颜色-运动学对偶的显式实现依赖于一个二阶微分算子$\mathsf{b}$作用在颜色剥离场的代数上。他们进一步论证高阶（大于二阶）微分算子的存在意味着较弱但非平凡的雅可比恒等式碎片，并证明一阶杨-米尔斯作用量在超空间表述中于任意维度都具备六阶微分算子。

Result: 研究表明，一阶形式的杨-米尔斯理论中，树级和圈级散射振幅的费曼图展开自动享有弱形式的颜色-运动学对偶。这一结果由超空间表述中六阶微分算子的存在性所保证。

Conclusion: 颜色-运动学对偶的弱形式可以在比完全对偶更宽松的条件下实现，为更普遍地探索颜色-运动学对偶的物理起源提供了新途径。

Abstract: Colour-kinematics duality is, even at tree level, not manifest from the standard Lagrangian of Yang-Mills theory in that the usual Feynman-diagram expansion does not follow the kinematic Jacobi identities. For a Lagrangian manifesting colour-kinematics duality, the kinematic Jacobi identities of the Feynman-diagram expansion follow from the existence of a second-order differential operator $\mathsf{b}$ acting on the algebra of colour-stripped fields that forms part of the data of a BV$^\square$-algebra. We argue that the existence of differential operators of order greater than two implies weaker but nontrivial fragments of kinematic Jacobi identities. We further show that a superspace formulation of the first-order Yang-Mills action in the Batalin-Vilkovisky formalism admits a differential operator of order six in every spacetime dimension. Therefore, the Feynman-diagram expansion of tree and loop scattering amplitudes of the first-order formulation of Yang-Mills theory automatically enjoy a weak form of colour-kinematics duality.

</details>


### [261] [Quantum Information in SYK Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15589)
*Chen-Te Ma,Jeff Murugan,Masaki Tezuka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从量子信息视角研究SYK模型中的体-边界对应（bulk-boundary correspondence），通过量子混沌与纠缠诊断探测全息原理，并考察模型变形以阐明量子信息结构与体引力动力学之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型为大N、低能极限下近AdS₂时空中的全息术（holography）与涌现时空提供了最小化研究框架，作者旨在通过量子信息工具厘清边界量子混沌与体半经典引力之间的具体联系，并揭示时空涌现的机制。

Method: 从量子混沌与纠缠两个维度探测全息原理：早时域中检验Lyapunov指数是否饱和普适上界，晚时域中分析能谱统计是否符合随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）；同时分析量子纠缠与算子代数（operator algebra）结构，研究von Neumann代数的转变及其对涌现几何的启示；并通过修正物质耦合与替代随机分布构造SYK模型变形。

Result: 早时域中SYK模型饱和Lyapunov指数的普适上界，呈现与半经典黑洞动力学一致的最大混沌（maximal chaos）；晚时域中能谱统计由随机矩阵理论支配，反映强混沌量子系统的普适特征。这些动力学性质确立了边界量子混沌与体半经典引力之间的具体联系。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明量子信息论结构编码了体引力动力学，为时空涌现机制提供了洞见；von Neumann代数的转变与纠缠结构共同承载了涌现几何的信息。

Abstract: We investigate the bulk-boundary correspondence in the SYK model from a quantum information perspective. The SYK model describes a system of Majorana fermions with random all-to-all interactions, whose disorder average-typically taken over a Gaussian ensemble-admits a dual description in terms of JT gravity in the large-$N$, low-energy limit. This framework provides a minimal setting for exploring holography and emergent spacetime in nearly AdS$_2$. We probe the holographic principle through diagnostics of quantum chaos and entanglement. In the early-time regime, the SYK model saturates the universal bound on the Lyapunov exponent, signaling maximal chaos consistent with semiclassical black hole dynamics. In the late-time regime, its spectral statistics are governed by random matrix theory, reflecting universal features of strongly chaotic quantum systems. These dynamical properties establish a concrete link between boundary quantum chaos and bulk semiclassical gravity. In parallel, we analyze quantum entanglement and the structure of operator algebras to investigate transitions in the associated von Neumann algebras and their implications for emergent geometry. To explore the robustness of these phenomena, we consider deformations of the SYK model through modified matter couplings and alternative random distributions. Our results clarify how quantum information-theoretic structures encode bulk gravitational dynamics and provide insight into the mechanism of spacetime emergence.

</details>


### [262] [Positive Singularities and Volumes in Scattering Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15606)
*Elia Mazzucchelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论文以正几何（positive geometry）为统一框架，系统研究散射振幅的解析与几何结构，重点围绕 Amplituhedron 展开三条研究路径：对偶体积表示与正性、圈级振幅的奇异性分类，以及 Landau 奇异性与丛代数的猜想关系，最终将规范形式（canonical form）、体积与奇异性统一在正几何语言下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期进展表明，某些量子场论中的散射振幅可借助正几何（positive geometry）进行几何表述，其中微分形式的边界结构反映了局域性、幺正性与因子化等物理原理。本文旨在对这一框架进行自包含的导引性论述，并深入发展正性（positivity）、体积（volumes）与奇异性（singularities）三者之间的内在联系。

Method: 论文围绕三个方向展开方法论建构：其一，通过对偶体积表示（dual volume representation）研究规范形式的正性性质，将多面体情形扩展至非线性正几何，揭示非负超越测度（non-negative transcendental measures）与完全单调性（complete monotonicity）的联系；其二，结合 Amplituhedron 几何与 Landau 分析，对带拉格朗日插入的 Wilson 圈的领先奇异性（leading singularities）进行分类并约束可能奇点轨迹；其三，利用动量大阶梯（momentum-twistor）与 Grassmannian 方法，探索 Landau 奇异性、正性与丛代数（cluster algebras）之间的猜想性关系，识别跨圈阶的递归结构并证明若干无限族情形。

Result: 论文取得了三方面的主要进展：建立了从多面体到非线性正几何的对偶体积表示推广，并发现了与完全单调性相关的非负超越测度；通过整合 Amplituhedron 与 Landau 分析，系统分类了带拉格朗日插入的 Wilson 圈的领先奇异性；利用动量大阶梯和 Grassmannian 方法识别了 Landau 奇异性与丛代数之间的递归结构，证明了多个无限族情形，并提出了朝向一般猜想的策略。

Conclusion: 整体而言，本文论证了正几何能为散射振幅的解析与几何组织提供统一语言，从规范形式与体积出发，涵盖圈积分后涌现的奇异性结构，为振幅的几何化理解奠定了更完整的理论框架。

Abstract: Recent advances have revealed that scattering amplitudes in certain quantum field theories admit a geometric formulation in terms of positive geometries. In this framework, amplitudes are encoded by differential forms whose boundary structure reflects physical principles such as locality, unitarity, and factorization.
  This thesis gives a self-contained introduction to positive geometries, with particular emphasis on the Amplituhedron, which describes amplitudes in planar maximally supersymmetric Yang--Mills theory through its canonical form. We develop three related directions connecting positivity, volumes, and singularities.
  First, we study positivity properties of canonical forms through dual volume representations. For polytopes, canonical functions compute volumes of dual polytopes; we extend this picture to nonlinear positive geometries, uncovering non-negative transcendental measures and links with complete monotonicity. Second, we investigate loop-level amplitudes via their singularity structure. Combining Amplituhedron geometry with Landau analysis, we classify leading singularities of the Wilson loop with Lagrangian insertion and constrain the possible singular loci. Third, we examine conjectural relations between Landau singularities, positivity, and cluster algebras. Using momentum-twistor and Grassmannian methods, we identify recursive structures across loop orders, prove several infinite families of cases, and propose a strategy toward the general conjectures.
  Overall, the thesis develops the perspective that positive geometry provides a unifying language for the analytic and geometric organization of scattering amplitudes, from canonical forms and volumes to the singularities that emerge after loop integration.

</details>


### [263] [Strong CP Problem in Type IIA Toroidal Orientifold String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15611)
*Yang Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个全局自洽的Type IIA弦论方向模型（orientifold），通过四形式通量机制（four-form flux mechanism）动态解决强CP问题，并给出通量参数与场论参数的一一对应字典、反D6膜背反应定量估计、沼泽地（swampland）约束窗口分析以及与其他构造方案的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强CP问题（strong CP problem）是粒子物理标准模型中的未解之谜，弦论构造有望为其提供自洽的动力学解答。作者此前已给出原理验证型构造，本文旨在将该构造完善为定量、全局自洽的模型，并验证其满足各种约束条件及实验可观测性。

Method: 基于Type IIA弦理论在T^6/(Z_2×Z_2)奇点（orientifold）上的相交D6膜（intersecting D6-branes）构造，利用四形式通量机制求解强CP问题。构造满足所有RR图尾（RR tadpole）、K理论（K-theory）与超对称（SUSY）条件，稳定所有模（moduli），并通过反D6膜（anti-D6-brane）实现dS真空提升。文中给出四形式参数与弦通量之间的一一映射字典（含通量量子化条件）、反D6背反应的定量估计、沼泽地约束的参数窗口分析，以及与Type IIB LVS和杂化弦（heterotic）解的比较。

Result: 定量估计显示反D6膜背反应造成的修正满足|δθ̄|<10^-11，验证了构造的鲁棒性。沼泽地约束仅在特定参数窗口内能同时满足。预测的轴子衰变常数（axion decay constant）f_a与质量m_a处于下一代实验的可探测范围内。

Conclusion: 该构造为强CP问题的弦论动力学求解提供了全局自洽且定量可控的模型，通量参数映射与背反应估计使其具备理论可检验性，且预测的轴子参数窗口有望被下一代实验观测所检验。

Abstract: We present a globally consistent Type IIA $T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2)$ orientifold with intersecting D6-branes that dynamically solves the strong CP problem via the four-form flux mechanism. The construction satisfies all RR tadpole, K-theory, and SUSY conditions, stabilizes all moduli, and uplifts to dS vacuum with an anti-D6-brane. Four new results beyond our earlier proof-of-principle \cite{Liu:2025tnx} are given: (i) an explicit one-to-one dictionary between field-theory four-form parameters and string fluxes, including flux quantization constraints; (ii) a quantitative anti-D6 backreaction estimate showing $|δ\barθ|<10^{-11}$; (iii) an analysis of swampland constraints that are simultaneously satisfied only in a parameter window; (iv) a comparison with Type IIB LVS and heterotic solutions. The predicted axion decay constant $f_a$ and mass $m_a$ are within reach of next generation observations.

</details>


### [264] [Bezoutian Decoupling for Conformal Yang--Mills Multiplets in $(A)dS$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15628)
*Weiqi Jiang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文延续Metsaev对共形杨-米尔斯理论（conformal Yang–Mills theory）在高偶数维（A）dS时空中的研究，识别并对角化了一种全N阶的Bezout连续延拓（Bezoutian continuation），该延拓由三个通用Gram形式唯一确定，其向量与根式Gram形式的对角化恒等式对所有D=2N+4维成立，并为逆、行列式和惯性指标给出闭式公式，同时将归一化权重识别为Johnson图重数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Metsaev此前给出了共形杨-米尔斯理论在(A)dS_6、(A)dS_8和(A)dS_10中的通用与解耦表述，并猜想其在更高偶数维中的相应关系。本文旨在通过在任意偶数维中构造并对角化自然的Bezout连续延拓，验证这一猜想并提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 通过附录C中低维矩阵的启发，构造一个自然的全N阶Bezout连续延拓来统一三个通用的Gram形式。该延拓由首一的Hankel截止、附录C中的求值模式和猜想的质量节点唯一确定，并借由Bezout矩阵（Bezout matrix）及其单根处的Vandermonde同余进行对角化分析。

Result: 该连续延拓的向量与根式Gram形式对角化恒等式对所有D=d+1=2N+4且N≥1的维数成立；推导出逆、行列式和惯性（inertia）的闭式公式，归一化权重被识别为Johnson图重数（Johnson-graph multiplicities）。在N=4时，按照结合律、Frobenius不变性和固定平坦特殊化等代数约束，仍存在一个单参数族的成对互异乘积。

Conclusion: 该结果在任意偶数维中完成了Gram形式的对角化并运送（transport）了通用表述中已指定的非线性系数代数，但并未在任意维中构造这样的代数；在N=4时，即使施加结合律、Frobenius不变性和固定平坦特殊化约束，仍存在一个单参数族的非平凡乘积结构。

Abstract: Metsaev exhibited generic and decoupled formulations of conformal Yang--Mills theory in $(A)dS_6$, $(A)dS_8$, and $(A)dS_{10}$, and conjectured the corresponding relations in higher even dimensions. Motivated by the low-dimensional matrices in Appendix C, we identify and diagonalize a natural all-$N$ Bezoutian continuation of the three generic Gram forms. The monic Hankel cutoff, the evaluation pattern in Appendix C, and the conjectured mass nodes determine this continuation uniquely. Its vector and radical Gram-form diagonalization identities hold for every $D=d+1=2N+4$, $N\geq1$. The continuation is the Bezout matrix of $z\prod_{i=1}^N(z-ρi(2N+1-i))$ and $1$; evaluation at its simple roots produces the Vandermonde congruence appearing in the field redefinition. The same calculation gives closed formulas for the inverse, determinant, and inertia, and identifies the normalization weights with Johnson-graph multiplicities. Any nonlinear coefficient algebra already specified in the generic formulation is transported by this change of basis. The result does not construct such an algebra in arbitrary dimension. At $N=4$, we impose associativity, Frobenius invariance, and a fixed flat specialization; these algebraic constraints still admit a one-parameter family of pairwise distinct products in a fixed generic basis.

</details>


### [265] [Renormalization-group locking of finite anisotropic propagation cones in Yukawa networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15661)
*Shuai Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究有限图上的局域Yukawa相互作用能否通过重整化群（renormalization group）动力学使不同场规范几何（propagation geometry）在红外极限下收敛到一个共同的空间锥，即使在驱动锁定的相互作用变得边缘无关（marginally irrelevant）的情况下依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同场规范可以具有有限各向异性的传播几何，其主轴不必重合。本文旨在回答一个关键问题：即便驱动锁定的相互作用的耦合常数在红外变为边缘无关，这些相互作用是否仍能通过重整化群流动使所有场共享一个普遍的空间锥。

Method: 保留空间动力学矩阵（spatial kinetic matrices）之间的完全有限失配（finite mismatch），推导单圈（one-loop）非线性矩阵流动方程，并证明其Thompson直径全局非扩张（globally nonexpansive）。在连通图上，持久的归一化边活动将其升级为一致有限窗口收缩；进一步通过显式的双通道Pauli–Dirac Yukawa-quartic补全在弱耦合开集上实现该持久条件。

Result: 在连通图上所有相互作用扇区渐近地共享一个相对传播几何。在四维时空中Yukawa耦合在红外消失，但其累积相互作用时间发散，最终得到具有共同空间锥的高斯端点（Gaussian endpoint）。对于受控的双平台极值类（two-plateau extremal class），图距离还决定了直径减少的首个非零短时阶数。

Conclusion: 共同的空间锥可以由重整化群动力学集体选择产生，而非必须作为微观共同的几何结构被预先设定。

Abstract: Different field species can begin with finite anisotropic propagation geometries whose principal axes need not coincide. We ask whether local Yukawa interactions on a finite graph can nevertheless generate one common infrared spatial cone, even when the interactions that drive the locking become marginally irrelevant. Retaining the full finite mismatch between positive-definite spatial kinetic matrices, we derive the one-loop nonlinear matrix flow and show that its Thompson diameter is globally nonexpansive. On a connected graph, persistent normalized edge activity upgrades this to a uniform finite-window contraction, so all interacting sectors asymptotically share one relative propagation geometry. An explicit two-channel Pauli-Dirac Yukawa-quartic completion realizes the persistence conditions on an open weak-coupling set of finite-mismatch initial data. In four dimensions the Yukawa couplings vanish in the infrared, yet their accumulated interaction time diverges, leaving a Gaussian endpoint with a common spatial cone. For a controlled two-plateau extremal class, graph distance also fixes the first nonzero short-time order of the diameter decrease. Thus a common cone can be collectively selected by renormalization-group dynamics rather than imposed as a microscopic common structure.

</details>


### [266] [Bootstrapping bilinear relations of discrete Painlevé systems from 5d gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15756)
*Minsung Kim,Xin Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过5维N=1超对称场论与椭圆型和q-潘勒韦方程（q-Painlevé equations）的对应关系研究其tau函数（tau-functions），提出了一种基于Ω背景（Ω-background）上配分函数的bootstrap方法，可从有效预势（effective prepotential）和微扰贡献及全局对称性出发确定双线性关系（bilinear relations），并将该方法成功应用于SU(2)规范理论与N_f≤8个基本超多重态耦合的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆型和q-Painlevé方程与超对称场论之间存在深刻联系，但如何系统性地从场论数据构造这些方程所对应的tau函数是一个关键问题。作者希望建立一种通用的bootstrap方法，使得仅从场论的已有关键信息（有效预势、微扰贡献和全局对称性）即可确定tau函数的双线性关系。

Method: 作者通过5维N=1超对称场论与椭圆型和q-Painlevé方程的对应关系，将tau函数定义为Ω背景上的配分函数（partition function）。在此基础上提出bootstrap方法：利用有效预势和微扰贡献，结合理论的全局对称性，推导出tau函数满足的双线性关系。

Result: 将该方法应用于5维SU(2)规范理论耦合N_f≤8个基本超多重态的情形，成功获得了椭圆型和q-Painlevé方程的双线性关系。该方法不限于SU(2)规范理论，可推广至一般的5维超对称场论。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种从超对称场论数据出发系统构造tau函数并确定其双线性关系的bootstrap方法，为椭圆型和q-Painlevé方程与5维场论对应关系的研究提供了新的框架，并具有向更一般规范理论推广的广阔前景。

Abstract: We study tau-functions of elliptic and $ q $-Painlevé equations through their correspondence with 5d $ \mathcal{N}=1 $ supersymmetric field theories.
