Defining tau-functions associated with 5d field theories in terms of partition functions on the $ Ω$-background, we propose a bootstrap method that determines their bilinear relations from the effective prepotential and the perturbative contributions, together with the global symmetry of the theory. Applying the method to 5d $ \mathrm{SU}(2) $ gauge theories coupled to $ N_f \leq 8 $ fundamental hypermultiplets, we obtain the bilinear relations of the elliptic and $ q $-Painlevé equations. This method can be applied to general 5d supersymmetric field theories beyond $ \mathrm{SU}(2) $ gauge theories.

</details>


### [267] [Holographic Entanglement and Emergent Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15862)
*Mohammad Ali-Akbari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息对偶（holographic duality）框架下，通过边界纠缠熵重建体视几何（bulk geometry）。作者基于热1+1维共形场论（CFT₂）与BTZ黑洞的对偶，计算四种全息纠缠度量，并通过对这些纠缠熵对边界间隔和极值面转向点的求导，在不引入爱因斯坦方程的情况下成功重建了完整的BTZ度规，包括径向分量gₓₓ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"全息涌现"（holographic emergence）的核心命题，即边界量子信息结构是否足以唯一决定体视引力背景，从而为重力源于量子纠缠这一思想提供具体实现。

Method: 考虑与BTZ黑洞对偶的热1+1维CFT₂，计算四种全息纠缠度量：标准类空纠缠熵（spacelike HEE）及其补类空量HEE^s、类时纠缠熵（timelike HTEE）及其补类时量HEE^t；这些度量分别探测黑洞视界的外部和内部区域。随后对这些纠缠熵关于边界间隔和极值面转向点（turning point）求导，重建BTZ度规的全部分量。

Result: 通过求导方法成功重建了完整BTZ度规，包括径向分量gₓₓ，且无需假设爱因斯坦方程，重建结果在所有几何区域均正确。在零温极限下，结果一致地退化到纯AdS₃度规和熟悉的真空CFT₂纠缠熵。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵数据本身足以唯一确定对偶引力背景，为全息涌现提供了具体实现，表明重力可以从边界的量子信息结构中涌现。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of bulk geometry from boundary entanglement entropy in the context of holographic duality. Considering a thermal $1+1$-dimensional conformal field theory dual to the BTZ black hole, we compute four distinct holographic entanglement measures: the standard spacelike HEE, its complementary spacelike counterpart HEE$^s$, the timelike HTEE and its complementary timelike counterpart HEE$^t$. These measures probe both the exterior and interior regions of the black hole horizon. Remarkably, by taking appropriate derivatives of these entanglement entropies with respect to the boundary intervals and the turning point of the extremal surface, we reconstruct the full BTZ metric, including the radial component $g_{zz}$, without imposing the Einstein equations. The reconstruction yields the correct metric components in all regions of the geometry. We further show that in the zero-temperature limit, our results consistently reduce to the pure AdS$_3$ metric and the familiar vacuum CFT$_2$ entanglement entropies. Our findings demonstrate that entanglement entropy data alone contains sufficient information to uniquely determine the dual gravitational background, offering a concrete realization of holographic emergence where gravity arises from the quantum information structure of the boundary theory.

</details>


### [268] [Lagrangian Schur index and Bethe ansatz type formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15878)
*Yutong Li,Yiwen Pan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以初等方法计算一般 $\mathcal{N}=2$ 拉格朗日理论Schur指标（Schur index）闭合形式的方案，将积分问题转化为求解简单差分方程，最终结果以含扭曲Eisenstein级数的拟Jacobi形式（quasi-Jacobi forms）有限和表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于Bethe ansatz类型公式推导 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形指标的方法存在缺陷：积分过程中周期性（periodicity property）丧失以及忽略环形区域之外的奇点会带来错误，因此需要一种更可靠的闭合形式计算方法。

Method: 受 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形指标（superconformal index）的Bethe ansatz类型公式启发，识别并规避原始推导中的两个问题（周期性丧失与环外极点遗漏），将积分转化为求解简单差分方程（difference equation），最终得到关于扭曲Eisenstein级数（twisted Eisenstein series）的拟Jacobi形式有限和表达式。

Result: 方法在多种理论中得到了验证，包括BCD型 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 理论以及多种 $\mathcal{N}=2$ 箭袋规范理论（quiver gauge theories）。

Conclusion: 该方法为一般 $\mathcal{N}=2$ 拉格朗日理论提供了系统且初等的Schur指标闭合形式计算途径，纠正了原始推导中的核心缺陷。

Abstract: We propose a surprisingly elementary method to compute the Schur index in closed-form for general $\mathcal{N} = 2$ Lagrangian theories. The method is inspired by the Bethe ansatz type formula for $\mathcal{N} = 1$ superconformal index. We identify issues underlying the original derivation: the loss of periodicity property upon integration and the omitted poles outside of the annulus region. We circumvent the problems and transform integration into solving a simple difference equation. The final result is expressed as a finite sum of quasi-Jacobi forms involving twisted Eisenstein series. We test our method on different types of theories, including BCD-type $\mathcal{N} = 4$ theories and various $\mathcal{N} = 2$ quiver gauge theories.

</details>


### [269] [Gravitational wave signatures from area metric gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16046)
*Bianca Dittrich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究线性化面积度量（area metric）理论的引力波解及其观测效应，证明通过干涉仪中偏振光的双折射现象（birefringence）可原则上测定Barbero-Immirzi参数，但基于普朗克质量非长度激发的信号极其微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面积度量理论在自旋泡沫、弦论和全息等量子引力（quantum gravity）途径中均被提出作为有效描述，且允许非拓扑的字称破坏项，从而为观测测定Barbero-Immirzi参数提供了可能性。

Method: 对Lorentz号差的镜对称版本线性化面积度量理论进行分析，考查其类引力波解，并通过与电磁学耦合的分析推导管在引力波探测器中的信号特征。

Result: 利用干涉仪中的偏振光可测量面积度量诱导的双折射，从而原则上确定Barbero-Immirzi参数；但若面积度量中的激发为普朗克质量的非长度激发，则所产生的信号极其微弱。

Conclusion: 面积度量理论为观测检验量子引力途径提供了独特窗口，但实验探测面临信号极弱的现实挑战。

Abstract: Area metric theories have been proposed as effective descriptions in a number of quantum gravity approaches: as continuum limit of effective spin foams, as an effective description for string theory and in the context of holography. Area metric actions allow for non-topological parity violating terms, which opens the possibility of observationally determining the Barbero-Immirzi parameter. We consider the shift symmetric version of linearized area metric theory in Lorentzian signature, which arises in particular from spin foams and modified Plebanski theory, and avoids ghost and tachyonic instabilities in the graviton sector. We discuss the gravitational wave like solutions of this theory and through an analysis of the coupling to electro-magnetism, deduce the signals in gravitational wave detectors. Using polarized light in such interferometers allows in principle to measure area metric induced birefringence and to determine the Barbero-Immirzi parameter. Assuming Planck massive non-length excitations in the area metric the resulting signal is however extremely weak.

</details>


### [270] [Positive Tensor-Network Kähler Metrics on gCICY Threefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16083)
*Juntao Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一种用于计算Calabi-Yau流形上Ricci平坦Kähler度量的正张量网络参数化方法，通过矩阵乘积分解替代高次代数度量的大规模Hermitian系数矩阵。该方法在固定局部维数和键维数下，参数数量随代数次数线性增长，且度量在所有参数值下全局正定。在三个广义完全交集Calabi-Yau三维流形上的测试表明，张量网络在多个配对实验中优于参数匹配的神经势场和直接优化的无约束Hermitian度量，并观察到度数与键维数上的饱和效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Calabi-Yau流形上Ricci平坦Kähler度量的数值构造依赖于高次代数度量对应的大规模Hermitian系数矩阵，其参数数量随次数快速增长且难以保证全局正定性。本文旨在开发一种参数高效且全局正定的新参数化方案，以更有效地逼近满足Kähler度量的Calabi-Yau度量。

Method: 方法核心是将高次代数度量的Hermitian系数矩阵替换为矩阵乘积因子分解（matrix-product factorization），形成正张量网络参数化，确保度量在所有参数值下全局正定。在浸入源空间上，参数数量随代数次数线性增长。构造逐图（chart by chart）的广义截面、全纯体积形式与采样测度来定义和训练度量，并在三个广义完全交集Calabi-Yau三维流形上进行系统测试。

Result: 在三个gCICY三维流形上，张量网络从共同低次度量出发，在每对配对实验中均优于参数匹配的神经势场，同时降低了体积误差与1%尾部条件均值。相较于同次数自由Hermitian度量的直接优化，张量网络达到了显著更低的误差。在第二个几何上，参数更少的高次张量网络大幅降低了与低次无约束Hermitian基线相同的样本误差。此外观察到饱和行为：固定键维数时，增加次数最终进入平台期；键维数增加在固定优化预算下无已解析增益；结果强烈依赖于初始化和优化路径。

Conclusion: 正张量网络参数化提供了一种参数高效且全局正定的替代方案，在数值精度上优于同参数规模的神经势场和直接优化的Hermitian度量。然而，其性能受到键维数、次数以及初始化与优化路径的显著影响，存在明显的饱和现象，提示需要进一步优化初始化策略和网络架构以提高实际计算效率。

Abstract: We develop and implement a positive tensor-network parameterization for computing Ricci-flat Kähler metrics on Calabi-Yau manifolds. It replaces the large Hermitian coefficient matrix of a high-degree algebraic metric by a matrix-product factorization. For the immersed source spaces used here, the resulting metric is globally positive for every parameter value and, at fixed local and bond dimensions, its number of parameters grows only linearly with the algebraic degree. We test the construction on three generalized complete-intersection Calabi-Yau (gCICY) threefolds, constructing chart by chart the generalized sections, holomorphic volume forms and sampling measures that define and train the metric there. From a common low-degree metric, the tensor network outperforms a parameter-matched neural potential using the same section data, reducing both bulk errors and the one-percent tail conditional mean in every paired run. It also reaches a substantially lower error than direct optimization of an unrestricted Hermitian metric of the same degree from the same start, with both methods optimized to validation convergence under their respective schedules. On a second geometry, a higher-degree network with fewer parameters than a lower-degree unrestricted Hermitian baseline substantially reduces the same-sample errors. We further observe saturation within the tested calculations: at fixed bond dimension, increasing the degree eventually plateaus; increasing the bond dimension at fixed optimization effort gives no resolved gain; and the outcome depends strongly on initialization and optimization path.

</details>


### [271] [Quantum-Corrected Black Holes in Higher Dimensions: From Gravitational Collapse to Remnants, WGC-Like Behavior and Accretion Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16369)
*Saeed Noori Gashti,Umair Anwar,Abdul Jawad,Behnam Pourhassan,İzzet Sakallı,Sanjar Shaymatov,Aram Bahroz Brzo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维时空中带圈量子引力（loop quantum gravity）修正的均匀尘埃球引力坍缩，发现该过程在有限视界半径处温度消失、蒸发停止并留下残骸（remnant）。在把量子参数α视为有效电荷后，表现出与弱引力猜想（Weak Gravity Conjecture, WGC）相似的行为，尽管模型中不存在U(1)规范场。论文在D=4,5,6,7维中得到残骸半径和质量的闭式表达式，检验了无量纲组合的不变性，并研究了Bondi吸积对应的爱丁顿光度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在研究高维时空中圈量子引力修正下的引力坍缩终态，特别是残骸形成及其随维度的标度行为。通过将α视为有效电荷，作者希望检验该量子修正模型是否呈现与弱引力猜想相似的结构，并澄清此前使用质量展开方法在残骸端点附近失效的问题。

Method: 采用修正Friedmann方程引入圈量子引力效应，通过解退化条件（degeneracy conditions）并使用未截断的度规函数（untruncated metric function）而非在端点附近不均匀的质量展开，得到D=4,5,6,7维中残骸半径和质量的闭式解。进一步引入量子参数变形α→α+ε，计算固定熵导数在残骸极限下的行为，并研究Bondi吸积以获得各维度的爱丁顿光度。

Result: 残骸残骸的未标度比值α/M²仅在四维中为常数，在其他维度按α^(4-D)增长但具有L^(8-2D)量纲。无量纲组合R̃=(α/M²)r₀^(2D-8)在每一维度都收敛至有限的与α无关的数，从四维的27/64递减到七维的13824/(15625π⁴)。由于(∂M/∂r)_α在残骸端点处消失，固定熵导数精确约化为固定半径导数，组合U在每维有限但在D≠5时依赖ε。不变的量是乘积R·U·M_remnant=(D-3)/2，经数值验证成立。

Conclusion: 该量子修正模型在所有考虑维度中均产生稳定的残骸，且无量纲不变量R·U·M_remnant揭示了该模型的维度无关结构。尽管不存在规范场，其行为仍与弱引力猜想相似，暗示了量子引力修正与WGC之间存在更深层的共性。

Abstract: We study the gravitational collapse of a homogeneous dust sphere in higher-dimensional spacetime, with the loop quantum gravity correction entered through a modified Friedmann equation. Its most striking feature is an endpoint: at a finite horizon radius the temperature vanishes and evaporation stops, leaving a remnant. Reading \(α\) as an effective charge, we find behavior reminiscent of the Weak Gravity Conjecture (WGC), although no \(U(1)\) gauge field is present in the model. Solving the degeneracy conditions with the untruncated metric function, rather than with a mass expansion that is not uniform near the endpoint, we obtain closed-form expressions for the remnant radius and mass in \(D=4,5,6,7\). The unscaled ratio \(α/M_{\text{remnant}}^2\) is constant only in four dimensions and grows as \(α^{4-D}\) elsewhere, but it carries dimension \(L^{8-2D}\), so that growth is an artifact of the units. The dimensionless combination \(\tilde{\mathcal{R}} = (α/M^2) r_0^{2D-8}\) settles instead on a finite \(α\)-independent number in every dimension, decreasing from \(27/64\) in four dimensions to \(13824/(15625π^4)\) in seven. We also deform the quantum parameter, \(α\to α+ \varepsilon\), and evaluate \(-T(\partial S/\partial \varepsilon)_M\) in the remnant limit. Because \((\partial M/\partial r)_α\) vanishes there, the fixed-entropy derivative reduces exactly to a fixed-radius one, and the resulting combination \(\mathcal{U}\) is finite in each dimension but depends on \(\varepsilon\) except for \(D=5\). What is invariant is the product \(\mathcal{R}\,\mathcal{U}\,M_{\text{remnant}} = (D-3)/2\), which we verify numerically. Lastly, we study Bondi accretion onto the quantum-corrected black hole and obtain the Eddington luminosity for each dimension.

</details>


### [272] [Quantum Stability of the Fuzzy Sphere Black Hole Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16374)
*Chong-Sun Chu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在大N矩阵量子力学（matrix quantum mechanics）框架下，建立了模糊球黑洞视界（fuzzy-sphere black-hole horizon）的微扰稳定性。通过大N计数论证，主导量子修正来自平面玻色子单圈贡献；经典谱中的快子模与边际模均获得正量子曲率而被稳定化，且Hessian矩阵的因子化结构保证了量子曲率非负。在满足光滑性与非饱和假设条件下，足够大但有限的N值下量子涨落谱的严格正性得以确立，且这一稳定性与不可微扰的磁单极隧穿（monopole tunneling）过程相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞视界的量子稳定性是量子引力与弦理论研究中的核心问题。作者旨在证明在弦理论的矩阵模型框架中，模糊球黑洞视界作为经典解在量子层次上不会因快子不稳定性而坍塌，从而为该视界几何在量子动力学的合法性提供理论支撑。

Method: 采用大N计数（large-N counting）分析量子修正的相对阶数，确定平面玻色子单圈（planar bosonic one-loop）贡献为主导项。通过分析Hessian矩阵的因子化结构（factorization structure）来计算量子曲率，并结合经典曲率与量子效应在角动量空间中的竞争关系，辅以有限N数值检验来验证相关假设。

Result: 大N计数表明主导量子修正是平面玻色子单圈贡献，而更高圈玻色子效应与费米子单圈贡献在参数上被压低。经典谱中的快子$(1,2)$模与边际$(2,3)$模均被稳定化，且一般谱的量子曲率通过Hessian因子化结构保持非负。低角动量模由量子效应主导稳定化，高角动量模由经典曲率主导，两者在$L\sim\sqrt{N}$的交叉区域（crossover regime）变得可比。在光滑性与非饱和假设下，足够大但有限的N具有严格正的量子涨落谱。

Conclusion: 模糊球黑洞视界的局部曲率稳定性在足够大但有限的N下得以确立，且该稳定性与不可微扰的磁单极隧穿兼容。值得注意的是，$L\sim\sqrt{N}$介观角动量范围同时主导隧穿过程，暗示黑洞视界量子动力学中存在一个显著的IR–UV交叉（cross-over）机制。

Abstract: We establish the perturbative stability of the fuzzy-sphere black-hole horizon in the large-$N$ matrix quantum mechanics of \cite{Chu:2024qil}. Large-$N$ counting shows that the leading quantum correction to the fluctuation spectrum is the planar bosonic one-loop contribution, while higher-loop bosonic effects and the leading fermionic one-loop contribution are parametrically suppressed. The classical spectrum contains a tachyonic $(1,2)$ mode and a marginal $(2,3)$ mode; we show that both acquire positive quantum curvature and are stabilized. For the general spectrum, we find that a factorization structure of the Hessian controls the leading quantum curvature and renders it non-negative. Quantum effects dominate the stabilization of generic low-angular-momentum modes, whereas classical curvature dominates at high angular momentum. The two effects become comparable in the crossover regime $L \sim \sqrt{N}$, where both must be retained. Under smoothness and non-saturation assumptions supported by finite-$N$ numerical tests, positivity of the quantum fluctuation spectrum is thus established for sufficiently large but finite $N$. This local curvature stability is compatible with non-perturbative monopole tunneling. Intriguingly, the same $L\sim\sqrt{N}$ mesoscopic angular momentum range also dominates the tunneling process, suggesting a distinguished IR--UV crossover regime in the quantum dynamics of the black-hole horizon.

</details>


### [273] [Effective field equations with stochastic initial conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16486)
*Ian G. Moss*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论将截断维格纳（Truncated Wigner）技术从一种近似数值方法扩展为完整的有效场方程（effective field equation）方法，纳入高圈和高动量修正，以更精确地追踪非平衡量子态中场期望值的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截断维格纳技术能够实时数值追踪初始非平衡量子态中场期望值的演化，并可处理诸如假真空衰变（false vacuum decay）等非微扰量子现象且结果良好；然而它是一种近似方法，会受到量子圈修正（quantum loop corrections）的限制，因此需要向更完整的有效场方程方法推进。

Method: 本文讨论截断维格纳技术向完整有效场方程方法的扩展途径，具体包括纳入来自高圈（higher loops）和高动量（higher momenta）的修正项。

Result: 文中给出了将截断维格纳方法推广至完整有效场方程框架的讨论，该框架能够弥补原有近似方法的不足。

Conclusion: 将截断维格纳技术扩展为完整的有效场方程方法，可将高圈与高动量修正纳入其中，从而提升其在追踪非平衡量子演化中的精度与适用范围。

Abstract: The evolution of quantum field expectation values from an initial non-equilibrium quantum state can be followed numerically using the Truncated Wigner technique. It can track non-perturbative quantum phenomena, such as false vacuum decay, in real time with promising results. Nevertheless, it is an approximate method subject to quantum loop corrections. The extension of Truncated Wigner into a fully fledged effective field equation approach is discussed here, including corrections from higher loops and higher momenta.

</details>


### [274] [Flat space limits of symmetric Taub-NUT instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16487)
*Matthew Dales*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过弓构造（bow construction）在具有一般完整群（generic holonomy）的Taub-NUT空间上构造了具有U(1)对称性的SU(2)瞬子（instanton），涵盖三类瞬时子数与磁荷组合，并展示了它们与平坦R^4瞬子及R^3×S^1上热量子（caloron）之间的极限关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Taub-NUT空间上的瞬子与平坦空间及定域环上瞬子之间的对应关系尚不完整，尤其是在一般完整群条件下尚未被系统构造。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过统一框架构造带有U(1)对称性的SU(2)瞬子，并澄清其与已知极限情形的联系。

Method: 采用弓构造（bow construction），这是一种类Nahm的非线性变换（Nahm-like nonlinear transformation），将Taub-NUT空间上的瞬子编码为一组弓数据（bow data），这些数据满足一个可积常微分方程（integrable ODE）以及区间上的一系列代数关系。通过分析弓数据在不同参数极限下的行为，恢复对应的平坦R^4瞬子与R^3×S^1热量子的数据，并验证存在无法通过极限到达R^4瞬子或热量子的Taub-NUT瞬子。

Result: 成功构造了完整群（holonomy）一般的Taub-NUT空间上具有U(1)对称性的SU(2)瞬子，具体涵盖瞬时子数与磁荷(k,m)=(2,0),(1,1),(2,-1)三种组合。弓数据在适当极限下可恢复对应平坦R^4瞬子和R^3×S^1热量子的数据，并证明了存在某些Taub-NUT瞬子不与R^4瞬子或热量子有极限对应。

Conclusion: 弓构造为Taub-NUT空间上的对称瞬子提供了统一的构造框架，揭示了一部分Taub-NUT瞬子不能由R^4瞬子或热量子通过极限得到，表明Taub-NUT几何本身承载了超出平坦空间与紧致环空间的信息。

Abstract: We construct $U(1)$-symmetric $SU(2)$ instantons on the Taub-NUT space with generic holonomy, and instanton number and magnetic charge $(k,m)=(2,0)$, $(1,1)$, and $(2,-1)$. We construct these instantons via the bow construction, a Nahm-like non-linear transformation which identifies them with a set of bow data solving an integrable ODE and a series of algebraic relations on an interval. We show how the bow data recovers the data for all corresponding $U(1)$-symmetric instantons on flat $\mathbb{R}^4$ and calorons on $\mathbb{R}^3\times S^1$ in various limits, and we demonstrate the existence of Taub-NUT instantons that do not limit to $\mathbb{R}^4$ instantons nor calorons.

</details>


### [275] [Metric Reconstruction from Timelike Entanglement Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16517)
*Hao Feng,Tian-Shun Chen,Shao-Feng Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究利用类时纠缠熵（timelike entanglement entropy, TEE）作为洛伦兹边界探针来重建体几何度规的反问题。作者在复数弱极值曲面（CWES）方案下，对条形TEE数据进行了度规重建方法研究，分别在BTZ黑洞和渐近AdS背景中给出了解析和数值重建方案，并用基准度规进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类时纠缠熵提供了一个洛伦兹型的体几何边界探针，但其用于度规重建的效果依赖于全息方案（holographic prescription）的选择以及该方案所选定的极值曲面分支。作者希望厘清这些依赖关系，并发展系统的度规重建方法。

Method: 在复数弱极值曲面方案中，以TEE对时间宽度（time-width）的依赖性作为Abel密度输入，通过BTZ黑洞的奇点端点固定径向原点实现解析重建；随后发展复数坐标方案的数值前向方法，将其推广为渐近AdS_{d+1}（d≥2）背景的主要重建方案——在选定复分支上，TEE的时间宽度依赖提供Abel输入以确定TEE可及密度，并需附加一个几何锚点将密度转换为确定的径向度规轮廓。

Result: 以UV或视界尺度锚定并结合从重建复路径样本进行的有理延拓，该方法能够复现基准的BTZ、四维Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström（RN）黑洞的黑化因子。但Gubser-Rocha例子表明，当存在非平凡的时空翘曲因子（warp factor）时，单一条形观测量只能确定度规函数的一个函数组合。

Conclusion: TEE条形数据在选定极值曲面分支下可有效重建径向度规轮廓，但该重建本质依赖于全息方案的选择和几何锚点的设定；在一般带翘曲因子的背景中，单一条形观测量提供的信息不足以完全确定度规。

Abstract: Timelike entanglement entropy (TEE) provides a Lorentzian boundary probe of bulk geometry, but its use for metric reconstruction depends on the holographic prescription and on the extremal-surface branch selected by that prescription. We study this inverse problem for strip-shaped TEE data and make these dependencies explicit. In the complex-valued weak extremal surface (CWES) prescription, the time-width dependence of TEE determines an Abel density $H(W)$ on a selected real branch; for Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black holes this gives an analytic reconstruction of the blackening factor once the singularity endpoint fixes the radial origin. After developing a forward numerical method for the complex-coordinate prescription, we formulate it as the main reconstruction scheme for the asymptotically $AdS_{d+1}$ backgrounds with $d\geq 2$. On a chosen complex branch, the time-width dependence of TEE supplies the Abel input that determines the TEE-accessible density, while one additional geometric anchor is required to convert that density into a definite radial metric profile. With UV or horizon-scale anchoring and rational continuation from the reconstructed complex-path samples, the method reproduces the benchmark BTZ, four-dimensional Schwarzschild, and Reissner-Nordström (RN) blackening factors. However, the Gubser-Rocha example shows that a single strip observable with a nontrivial spatial warp factor determines only one functional combination of the metric functions.

</details>


### [276] [7-branes and $Γ_0(2)$ Duality in Type 0B String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16522)
*Naoto Kan,Masashi Kawahira,Hiroki Wada*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了type 0B弦论中的7-膜，在Q对称分支上利用自对偶群Γ₀(2)的阿贝尔化构造了两类独立7-膜荷算子，并通过受限Weierstrass模型构建了由八个Z₄ 7-膜组成的紧致背景，其中轴子-膨胀子被固定为常数τ=(1+i)/2，恰好对应M理论描述中的不稳定de Sitter临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期Baykara、Dudas和Vafa提出的将type 0B弦论与M理论联系起来的方案，为研究type 0B弦论中的7-膜提供了新的动机，旨在厘清其强耦合结构以及新的7-膜类型。

Method: 在Q对称分支上，利用type 0B弦论的自对偶群Γ₀(2)及其阿贝尔化Z×Z₄来推导两独立7-膜荷的存在性；以模形式（modular forms）构造显式荷算子，并在引力解中识别对应的带电客体——普通D7-膜和携带非平凡Z₄荷的7-膜；采用受限Weierstrass模型构造由八个Z₄ 7-膜组成的紧致背景，并分析Q奇扇区的涨落。

Result: 成功构造了两类7-膜荷，对应的带电客体分别为普通D7-膜和携带非平凡Z₄荷的7-膜；在由八个Z₄ 7-膜构成的紧致背景中，轴子-膨胀子被固定为τ=(1+i)/2，与M理论描述中提出的不稳定de Sitter临界点一致；尽管该背景中Q奇扇区运动方程全局良定义，但闭弦快子（tachyon）仍然不稳定。

Conclusion: 这些结果具体实现了type 0B弦论中的新型7-膜，并阐明了它们与其强耦合结构的关系，为理解type 0B弦论与M理论的联系提供了新的几何与动力学证据。

Abstract: We investigate 7-branes in type 0B string theory, motivated by the recent proposal of Baykara, Dudas, and Vafa, which connects type 0B string theory with M-theory. On the $Q$-symmetric branch, the self-duality group of type 0B string theory is $Γ_0(2)$, whose Abelianization is $\mathbb{Z}\times\mathbb{Z}_4$. This implies the existence of two independent 7-brane charges. We construct explicit charge operators in terms of modular forms and identify the corresponding charged objects in gravity solutions. One is the ordinary D7-brane, while the other is a 7-brane carrying a nontrivial $\mathbb{Z}_4$ charge. We further construct a compact background consisting of eight such $\mathbb{Z}_4$ 7-branes using a restricted Weierstrass model. Remarkably, in this background the axio-dilaton is fixed to the constant value $τ=(1+\mathsf{i})/2$, which coincides with the unstable de Sitter critical point proposed in the M-theory description of type 0B string theory. We also analyze fluctuations in the $Q$-odd sector and find that, although the corresponding equations of motion are globally well-defined in this background, the closed string tachyon remains unstable. Our results provide a concrete realization of new 7-branes in type 0B string theory and clarify their relation to its strong-coupling structure.

</details>


### [277] [Chiral Soliton Lattices under Magnetic Fields and Rotation: a Holographic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16579)
*Markus A. G. Amano,Minoru Eto,Muneto Nitta,Shin Sasaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息QCD（holographic QCD）框架下研究了旋转QCD物质在外加磁场中的手性孤子晶格（chiral soliton lattice, CSL），证明旋转可作为背景规范场纳入，CSL在旋转物质中成为引力对偶中的基态，并给出了在磁场和旋转下CSL的膜（brane）解释。通过求解体方程推导出有效哈密顿量，其中介子衰变常数$\tilde{f}$表现为各向异性且依赖场的矩阵，同时讨论了旋转物质基态的热力学性质和状态方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强子物理中，理解旋转和强磁场共同作用下QCD物质的基态结构具有重要理论意义，尤其是在重离子碰撞等极端环境中检验手性孤子晶格是否能够形成稳定的基态。

Method: 采用全息QCD方法，将旋转纳入有效理论作为背景规范场，在引力对偶中研究CSL基态；通过求解体方程（bulk equations）推导有效哈密顿量，并利用膜（brane）几何给出CSL的直观解释。

Result: 得到旋转QCD物质中CSL的基态结构，推导出含各向异性、依赖场的介子衰变常数矩阵的有效哈密顿量，并获得了旋转物质基态的热力学性质和状态方程。

Conclusion: 旋转效应可自然纳入全息框架，旋转与磁场共同作用下的CSL构成稳定的引力对偶基态，其物理性质可通过各向异性有效哈密顿量系统和状态方程加以完整描述。

Abstract: We study the chiral soliton lattice (CSL) in rotating QCD matter under a background magnetic field within holographic QCD. We show that rotation can be incorporated as a background gauge field and that the CSL in rotating matter becomes a ground state in the gravity dual. We also provide a brane interpretation of the CSL under a magnetic field and rotation. Solving the bulk equations, we derive an effective Hamiltonian where the meson decay constant $\tilde{f}$ emerges as an anisotropic, field-dependent matrix. Furthermore, we discuss the thermodynamic properties of the ground state in rotating matter and study its equation of state.

</details>


### [278] [The linear dilaton in cosmology and particle physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16772)
*Eugenio Megias,Mariano Quiros*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了一族由实参数ν参数化的五维（5D）背景时空，推广了Randall-Sundrum的AdS背景（ν=0）和线性胀子（linear dilaton）背景（ν=1），并探讨了后者在粒子物理和膜世界宇宙学（Brane World Cosmology）中的应用，包括通过体场传播子的连续谱（continuum）联系非粒子（unparticles），以及提出暗物质（dark matter）候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1999年Randall和Sundrum提出的五维AdS翘曲额外维可解决规范层级问题（gauge hierarchy problem），并具有四维共形场论（conformal field theory）全息对应。本文旨在展示一种更一般的参数化五维背景族，统一AdS和线性胀子两种理论，并探索其物理应用。

Method: 作者引入由实参数ν参数化的五维背景族，其中ν=0对应AdS，ν=1对应线性胀子（LD）。在五维理论框架下，作者研究了LD背景在粒子物理和膜世界宇宙学中的应用，分析了体场传播子结构，并考察了由辐射修正产生的连续谱与孤立共振。

Result: 在所有情况下，体传播场均存在带隙连续谱（gapped continuum），与unparticles有联系。在膜世界宇宙学中，存在一种无压全息流体，可通过冻结注入机制（freeze-in mechanism）在暴胀后产生，仅具有极微弱（引力）相互作用，可作为暗物质候选者。此外，还提出长寿命弱相互作用大质量引力子（feebly interacting massive graviton）作为另一暗物质候选。

Conclusion: 该参数化五维背景族统一了AdS与线性胀子理论，其物理应用揭示了通过连续谱联系unparticles的可能性，并提供了两类与标准模型（SM）热浴退耦、具有微弱引力相互作用的暗物质候选者。

Abstract: A warped extra dimension in a five-dimensional (5D) anti de Sitter (AdS) background was introduced in 1999 by Lisa Randall and Raman Sundrum to solve the gauge hierarchy problem in particle physics. As a bonus, a holographic interpretation in terms of four dimensional (4D) conformal field theories (CFT) was found. Interestingly enough, another 5D background, the linear dilaton (LD), was found to have a holographic interpretation in terms of Little String Theory. In this review we will show how a set of 5D backgrounds, parametrized in terms of a real parameter $ν$, generalizes both theories and gives rise, in particular, to AdS for $ν=0$ and to LD for $ν=1$. Furthermore, working in the 5D theory, we will consider applications of the LD background to: i) Particle Physics, so that the 5D Planck scale can be lowered to sub-Planckian values, ii) Brane World Cosmology (BWC), based on the appearance of an extra vacuum characterized by a 5D black hole. In all cases we find a gapped continuum for bulk propagating fields, which makes connection with unparticles. In the case of BWC we also point out on the existence of a pressureless holographic fluid which could play the role of dark matter (DM), with feeble (gravitational) interactions to the Standard Model (SM), decoupled from the thermal SM bath, and generated by a freeze-in mechanism after inflation. We also point out the additional possibility of identifying DM with a long-lived feebly interacting massive graviton, as an isolated resonance generated by radiative corrections to the continuum graviton propagator self-energy.

</details>


### [279] [Higher spin dynamics in Weyl reference frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16781)
*Gianfranco De Simone*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在位于有限距离的任意零超曲面（null hypersurface）上导出了w_{1+\infty}代数。通过在Weyl参考系中直接积分演化Bianchi恒等式，提取了（线性）高自旋荷的括号结构，并发现与无穷远渐近情形不同，有限距离下的角度量随时间非平凡演化，导致括号中出现非局域项。作者通过将非局域性（编码了角度量历史）吸收进高自旋荷的定义来消除这些项，并对有限距离下的记忆效应提出了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人研究多集中于空零无穷远（asymptotic null infinity）处的对称代数结构，而有限距离下零超曲面上的相应结构尚未被系统地推导。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立有限距离情形下w_{1+\infty}代数的严格构造。

Method: 作者在有限距离的任意零超曲面上，选取一种称为Weyl参考系的动力学参考框架，对演化形式的Bianchi恒等式进行直接积分，从而提取（线性）高自旋荷（higher spin charge）的对易括号。面对有限距离下角度量随时间非平凡演化所导致的括号中非局域项，作者将这些非局域项吸收进高自旋荷的定义之中，实现荷括号的局域化。

Result: 在Weyl参考系中成功提取了有限距离下线性高自旋荷括号，其形式与渐近无穷远情形类似，但存在由角度量历史编码的非局域项。通过重新定义高自旋荷来吸收非局域性后，括号变为局域形式。此外，论文提出了一种理解有限距离记忆效应（memory effect）的新视角。

Conclusion: 该构造方法同样可推广至渐近（A）dS_4时空，在存在非零宇宙学常数的情况下，需要引入一种相应的宇宙学参考系（cosmological reference frame）来处理。

Abstract: In this manuscript we derive the $w_{1+\infty}$ algebra on an arbitrary null hypersurface located at finite distance. A straightforward integration of the evolution Bianchi identities is performed in a suitable dynamical reference frame, dubbed the Weyl reference frame. In this reference frame, the extraction of the (linear) higher spin charge bracket is similar to the asymptotic null case, except that the corner metric evolves non trivially in time. In other words, boundary degrees of freedom that are frozen at null infinity due to the boundary conditions now become part of the dynamics. This feature is reflected in the appearance of non-local terms in the brackets. In order to avoid the presence of these non-local terms in the charge bracket, we absorb this non-locality - which encodes nothing other than the history of the corner metric - into the definition of the higher spin charges. Moreover, a new perspective on the memory effect at finite distance is suggested. In conclusion, we believe that this treatment can also be applied to asymptotically (A)dS$_{4}$ spacetimes, where the presence of a non-vanishing cosmological constant leads to the introduction of a cosmological reference frame.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [On the kinetic Fokker--Planck equation in curved geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14904)
*Cédric Villani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本笔记系统研究紧致黎曼流形（compact Riemannian manifold）切丛上的动力学Fokker–Planck方程（kinetic Fokker–Planck equation），覆盖了全局次椭圆光滑化（hypoelliptic C∞ regularization）与次协调指数平衡（hypocoercive exponential equilibration）两大核心主题，并在L²与L¹设定下分别建立了多种插值不等式、谱隙（spectral gap）与熵不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机来自两个方向：其一是相对论气体动力学理论（relativistic kinetic theory of gases，源自Debbasch），其二是几何分析（geometric analysis，源自Bismut）。作者旨在为缺乏几何分析背景的读者提供一份基本自足的综述与拓展，同时简化和统一既有结果并引入新结果。

Method: 论文先回顾动力学方程（kinetic equations），随后建立函数分析与谱分析（functional and spectral analysis）的基本工具箱，在此基础上研究全局次椭圆C∞正则化与次协调指数平衡，并在L²与L¹两个框架下分别进行讨论。方法涉及多种插值不等式、谱隙与熵不等式（entropic inequalities）的建立与使用。

Result: 论文对若干已有结果进行了简化与统一，并提出了新的结果；同时指出了若干未来研究方向。具体提出的是在紧致黎曼流形切丛上，对动力学Fokker–Planck方程建立了全局光滑化与指数收敛的完整理论框架。

Conclusion: 作者总结认为，该笔记为动力学Fokker–Planck方程在几何分析视角下提供了统一且简化的处理框架，涵盖L²与L¹两种设定，并对未来的研究路径提出了展望。

Abstract: These notes are devoted to the kinetic Fokker--Planck equation on the tangent bundle of a compact Riemannian manifold, with motivations coming from relativistic kinetic theory of gases (à la Debbasch) and geometric analysis (à la Bismut). They are developed in particular from collaboration with Fabrice Debbasch and Yann Ollivier. The presentation is mostly self-contained, intended for readers without much prior familiarity with geometric analysis. Once the kinetic equations are reviewed, I expand the basic toolbox from functional and spectral analysis. Then the main focus is on global hypoelliptic $C^\infty$ regularization and hypocoercive exponential equilibration, both of which are addressed both in $L^2$ and $L^1$ settings. Various types of interpolation inequalities, spectral gap and entropic inequalities are established and used. Several existing results are simplified and unified, along with new ones. Some directions of research are mentioned.

</details>


### [281] [Resonant Geometry and Well-Posedness for the 2D Boussinesq Wave Kinetic Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15340)
*Haoling Xiang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Shavit–Bühler–Shatah从二维Boussinesq内波系统导出的波动力学方程（wave kinetic equation, WKE），针对其各向异性和矢量值特性、双分支符号可变色散关系以及共振流形上的角度退化，引入自然的角度截断，证明了截断碰撞算子的有界性，并在加权L∞空间中建立了局部适定性，提供了首个针对Boussinesq动力学导出的各向异性矢量值WKE的严谨解析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该WKE具有各向异性、矢量值、双分支符号可变色散关系、耦合传播分支和符号不定伪动量不变量等复杂结构，且其共振流形存在角度退化，阻碍了对碰撞算子的标准解析处理，因此亟需建立适当的解析框架。

Method: 论文引入一种与这些角度退化自然匹配的角度截断（angular cut-off），在此基础上证明截断碰撞算子（collision operator）的有界性，并在加权L∞空间中建立局部适定性（local well-posedness）。方法还考察了在保留零频截断的同时移除|cosθ|=1/2附近截断的情形。

Result: 证明了截断碰撞算子的有界性，在加权L∞空间中建立了局部适定性；同时证明，即使保留零频截断，移除|cosθ|=1/2附近的截断会使碰撞算子在加权空间中无界。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了目前已知的第一个针对Boussinesq动力学导出的各向异性矢量值WKE的严谨解析框架，同时揭示了共振退化点处截断的移除将导致算子无界这一关键障碍。

Abstract: We study the wave kinetic equation (WKE) derived by Shavit--Bühler--Shatah from the two-dimensional Boussinesq system of internal waves. The equation is anisotropic and vector-valued, with a two-branch sign-changing dispersion relation, coupled propagation branches, and a sign-indefinite pseudo-momentum invariant. The resonant manifold has angular degeneracies that obstruct the standard analytic treatment of the collision operator. We introduce a natural angular cut-off adapted to these degeneracies, prove boundedness of the cut-off collision operator, and establish local well-posedness in weighted $L^\infty$ spaces. We also show that, even with the zero-frequency cut-off retained, removing the cut-off near $|\cosθ|=\tfrac12$ makes the collision operator unbounded on these weighted spaces. This provides, to our knowledge, the first rigorous analytic framework for an anisotropic vector-valued WKE arising from Boussinesq dynamics.

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [282] [Assessing the Projector Augmented-Wave Method for Stopping Power Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16389)
*Bryn Lloyd,Dirk O. Gericke,Gilles Rodway-Gant,Gianluca Gregori*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density functional theory, TDDFT）研究带电粒子的阻止本领（stopping power），重点评估投影缀加波（projector augmented-wave, PAW）方法在该类非绝热模拟中的适用性，并提出了一套系统的PAW数据集生成与筛选流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算资源和第一性原理数值实现的进步，TDDFT已成为模拟带电粒子阻止本领的可行工具，但实际模拟中广泛使用的PAW近似对非绝热停止模拟的影响尚缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 研究者开发了一套针对停止本领模拟的PAW数据集生成与选择工作流程，能够系统优化缀加半径（augmentation radius）和投影子构造（projector construction），并将其应用于面心立方（FCC）铝中质子阻止本领的模拟。

Result: 研究表明数据集设计会显著影响停止本领预测结果，并利用该方法探究了晶体沟道效应对带电粒子输运的影响。

Conclusion: PAW近似在TDDFT停止本领模拟中的适用性得到验证，所提出的数据集优化工作流为相关第一性原理模拟提供了可靠基础，并可用于研究晶体结构效应对带电粒子输运的影响。

Abstract: The stopping power of charged particles is investigated using time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT). Such simulations are made possible by recent advances in computational resources and numerical implementations of this first-principles method. In practice, DFT simulations widely employ the projector augmented-wave (PAW) method to approximate all-electron behaviour, but the implications of the PAW approximation for non-adiabatic stopping simulations remain insufficiently explored. Here, the suitability of the PAW method for stopping power simulations is evaluated. A workflow for generating and selecting PAW datasets tailored to these simulations is developed, enabling systematic optimisation of augmentation radii and projector constructions. The approach is applied to proton stopping in FCC aluminium, demonstrating how dataset design influences stopping predictions, and enabling an investigation of crystal channelling effects on charged-particle transport.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [Prospects for Direct Detection of Ultralight Dark Matter candidates in deci-Hz Band with IndIGO-D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15240)
*Md. Emanuel Hoque,Andrew L. Miller,Arunava Mukherjee*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了拟议中的天基分赫兹引力波干涉仪IndIGO-D对不同类型超轻暗物质的探测灵敏度，覆盖标量（膨胀子）、矢量（暗光子）和(类)标量（轴子）暗物质，展示了其在跨越现有地基与未来天基实验的耦合参数空间中开辟新探测窗口的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地基和天基引力波干涉仪之间存在频率覆盖空白，IndIGO-D（一种拟议的天基分赫兹引力波干涉仪）可探测约0.01–10 Hz频段，这一频段能够触及超出现有仪器能力的超轻暗物质质量范围，因此有必要系统评估其探测潜力。

Method: 考虑两类互补信号：由具有质量m_DM~10^-17–10^-14 eV的相干振荡标量场（膨胀子）、矢量场（暗光子，含U(1)_B和U(1)_{B-L}规范群）和张量场所诱导的干涉仪位移；以及由质量m_a~10^-12 eV的赝标量轴子暗物质所诱导的激光偏振变化。对位移类信号，采用交叉关联（cross-correlation）与BSD过剩功率（BSD excess-power）两种管道，并考虑L型和三角形干涉仪布局及三种代表性噪声功率谱密度（S1、S2、S3），每种配置连续观测两年。

Result: 不同搜索管道和干涉仪几何构型下的投影灵敏度在数量级因子以内一致。IndIGO-D可在m_DM~10^-16–10^-14 eV范围内为矢量和张量暗物质开辟此前未受约束的耦合参数空间，衔接空间与地面实验的灵敏度。对轴子暗物质，以光子散粒噪声为主的激光偏振测量可在m_a~10^-12 eV范围内探测轴子-光子耦合g_{aγ}，其水平可能优于其他未来实验且不损失引力波探测灵敏度。

Conclusion: IndIGO-D作为分赫兹频段的天基引力波干涉仪，能够同时实现引力波探测与多种超轻暗物质搜寻，并在矢量、张量及轴子暗物质耦合参数空间中提供超越现有及部分未来实验的覆盖能力，展现了多信使（multi-messenger）探测的科学价值。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of IndIGO-D, a proposed space-based decihertz gravitational-wave interferometer, to different classes of ultralight dark matter. IndIGO-D will probe the $\sim0.01$--$10~\mathrm{Hz}$ frequency band between those accessible to current ground- and future space-based gravitational-wave interferometers, providing access to ultralight dark-matter masses beyond the reach of existing instruments. We consider two complementary signatures: interferometric displacements induced by coherently oscillating scalar (dilaton), vector (dark-photon, $U(1)_B$ and $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge groups), and tensor fields with masses $m_{\rm DM}\sim10^{-17}$--$10^{-14}~\mathrm{eV}$; and changes in laser polarization induced by pseudoscalar axion dark matter at higher masses, around $m_a\sim10^{-12}~\mathrm{eV}$. For the dilatons, dark photons and tensors, we compute the expected sensitivities using two pipelines -- cross-correlation and BSD excess-power -- assuming L-shaped and triangular interferometer layouts and three representative noise power spectral densities (S1, S2, S3), each for two years of continuous observation. We find that the projected sensitivities agree to within a factor of order unity across the search pipelines and interferometer geometries. In particular, we show that IndIGO-D could open previously unconstrained coupling parameter space for vector and tensor dark matter across $m_{\rm DM}\sim10^{-16}$--$10^{-14}~\mathrm{eV}$, bridging the sensitivity of space- and ground-based experiments. For axion dark matter, we demonstrate that a complementary detection for laser light polarization shifts, limited primarily by photon shot noise, could probe the axion-photon coupling $g_{aγ}$ at masses around $m_a\sim10^{-12}~\mathrm{eV}$ at a level potentially better than that of other future experiments without degrading sensitivity to gravitational waves.

</details>


### [284] [Testing a Sign-Switch Cosmological Model with Curvature through Latest Planck 2018, DESI DR2 and PantheonPlus\&SH0ES Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15167)
*Archana Dixit,Manish Yadav,Anirudh Pradhan,M. S. Barak*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文在符号切换暗能量（sign-switch dark energy）框架下扩展Λ_sCDM模型，引入自由曲率参数Ω_k，并利用Planck 2018、DESI DR2 BAO和PantheonPlus&SH0ES超新星数据联合约束宇宙空间曲率。结果显示完整数据集下Ω_k=0.0001±0.0014，宇宙与空间平坦性高度一致；模型比较表明Λ_sCDM相对ΛCDM仅获得微弱观测支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学模型对空间曲率（spatial curvature）的约束仍存在不确定性，且近年暗能量状态方程和哈勃常数张力（Hubble tension）等问题促使研究者探索扩展模型。引入符号切换暗能量并同时放开曲率参数，有助于检验物理参数间的关联和模型稳健性。

Method: 将近期提出的Λ_sCDM模型扩展为包含自由曲率参数Ω_k的框架，其中有效宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）在特征红移z_†处由负值跃迁为正值。采用Planck 2018 CMB、DESI DR2 BAO及PantheonPlus&SH0ES Ia型超新星数据，进行联合贝叶斯参数估计，并通过AIC与贝叶斯证据（Bayesian evidence）进行模型比较。

Result: Planck数据单独在Λ_sCDM+Ω_k和ΛCDM+Ω_k框架下均轻微偏好封闭宇宙，但空间平坦性仍在误差范围内。加入低红移探针后曲率约束显著收紧，完整数据集Pk18+DR2+PP&SH0ES给出Ω_k=0.0001±0.0014，表明宇宙极其接近平坦。此外，曲率与符号切换暗能量的引入有助于稳定H_0和S_8等参数估计。模型比较显示Λ_sCDM相对ΛCDM仅获得不确定性/微弱的观测支持。

Conclusion: 在Λ_sCDM框架下，宇宙空间曲率与平坦性高度一致；符号切换暗能量的引入虽能稳定参数估计并与现有观测兼容，但相对标准ΛCDM模型并未获得显著统计优势。

Abstract: We investigate the spatial geometry of the Universe within the framework of a sign-switch dark energy scenario by extending the recently proposed $Λ_{\rm s}$CDM model to include a free curvature parameter $Ω_k$.In this framework, the effective cosmological constant undergoes a transition from a negative to a positive value at a characteristic redshift $z_{\dagger}$. Using the latest Planck 2018 cosmic microwave background (CMB) data, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) measurements, and the PantheonPlus\&SH0ES Type Ia supernova sample, we derive joint constraints on the spatial curvature parameter $Ω_k$ and other cosmological parameters. We find that Planck data itself slightly favors a closed universe within both the $Λ_{\rm s}$CDM$+Ω_k$ and $Λ$CDM$+Ω_k$ frameworks, although spatial flatness remains well within the allowed uncertainties. When low-redshift probes were included, the curvature constraints were significantly tightened. In particular, the full Pk18+DR2+PP\&SH0ES dataset yields $Ω_k = 0.0001 \pm 0.0014$ for the $Λ_{\rm s}$CDM model, indicating a universe that is remarkably consistent with spatial flatness. We further analyzed the correlations between $Ω_k$, $H_0$, and $S_8$, finding that the inclusion of curvature and a sign-switch dark energy component helps stabilize cosmological parameter estimates while remaining compatible with current observational constraints. Model comparison using AIC and Bayesian evidence shows that the $Λ_{\rm s}$CDM model receives inconclusive/weak observational support relative to $Λ$CDM.

</details>


### [285] [Redshift dependence and dipolar velocity corrections in cosmographic reconstructions through Type Ia supernova samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15858)
*Anna Chiara Alfano,Youri Carloni,Orlando Luongo,Francesco Pace*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用 Pantheon+&SH0ES、DES-SNY5 和 Union3 三个 Ia 型超新星编目，通过三阶泰勒展开和 Padé (1,2) 近似对光度距离进行宇宙学图谱分析，并引入局域本动速度场的偶极修正，研究了宇宙学参数（哈勃常数、减速参数和加加速度参数）对红移区间的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的宇宙学几何学（cosmography）分析通常依赖于单一的超新星样本和固定的红移范围，难以评估参数推断对样本选择和红移区间的稳健性。本文旨在通过多种编目和递进红移区间来系统检验宇宙学参数的稳定性，并考察本动速度场对参数估计的影响。

Method: 使用 Pantheon+&SH0ES、DES-SNY5 和 Union3 三个 Ia 型超新星编目；对光度距离采用三阶泰勒展开和 Padé (1,2) 近似两种参数化方案；直接在观测红移上推断宇宙学参数，随后加入与本动速度场（peculiar velocity field）相关的偶极修正，约束其振幅和方向；通过逐步提高样本最大红移来研究参数对红移区间的依赖性；对 Pantheon+&SH0ES 使用造父变星校准直接确定哈勃常数，对另外两个编目则固定 H0 以设定绝对距离尺度。

Result: 从 Pantheon+&SH0ES 得到的哈勃常数在两种参数化下均与此前的测定结果一致；减速参数 q0 和加加速度 j0 与 ΛCDM 值的吻合程度取决于所选编目，且主要是在 Pantheon+&SH0ES 中随红移区间扩大而改善；三个样本重建的偶极参数在红移区间内保持稳定，速度振幅约为 300 km s⁻¹；将偶极参数固定为宇宙微波背景（CMB）值会导致 q0 和 j0 相对基准 ΛCDM 值出现轻微偏移。

Conclusion: 宇宙学参数对超新星编目和红移区间存在依赖，其中 Pantheon+&SH0ES 在大红移区间下与 ΛCDM 的相容性最佳；本动速度场的偶极效应在所有样本中均稳健，但其取值对 q0 和 j0 的推断有一定影响，提示在超新星宇宙学分析中需谨慎处理局域速度场修正。

Abstract: We perform a cosmographic analysis of Type Ia supernovae using the Pantheon+\&SH0ES, DES-SNY5 and Union3 compilations. We consider different redshift intervals, analyzed through a third-order Taylor expansion and a Padé $(1,2)$ approximation of the luminosity distance. First, we first infer the cosmographic parameters directly from the observed supernova redshifts. Afterwards, we extend the analysis by including a dipole correction associated with the local peculiar velocity field, constraining both its amplitude and direction. For Pantheon+\&SH0ES, the Cepheid calibration allows a direct determination of the Hubble constant, whereas for DES-SNY5 and Union3 we fix $H_0$ to set the absolute distance scale. By progressively increasing the maximum redshift of the sample, we study how the inferred cosmographic parameters depend on the adopted redshift interval. We find that the Hubble constant obtained from Pantheon+\&SH0ES remains consistent with previous determinations for both cosmographic parameterizations. Instead, the agreement of the deceleration $q_0$ and jerk $j_0$ parameters with the $Λ$CDM values depends on the adopted compilation and improves mainly for Pantheon+\&SH0ES as the redshift interval is enlarged. Moreover, the reconstructed dipole parameters remain stable across the redshift intervals considered, with velocity amplitudes of order $300\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$, for all three supernova samples. Finally, fixing the dipole parameters with the cosmic microwave background values leads to a slight shift from the fiducial $Λ$CDM values of the deceleration $q_0$ and jerk $j_0$ parameters.

</details>


### [286] [Cosmology without the cosmological principle: A study of the large-scale structure effects in the background universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15983)
*Erick Pastén*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文综述了在ΛCDM模型面临观测矛盾背景下，放宽宇宙学原理（cosmological principle）假设所带来的后果，系统介绍了非均匀、各向异性以及倾斜宇宙学（tilted cosmologies）等替代模型，并将这些理论框架应用于SNIA数据和本地本动速度场（peculiar velocity field）以约束关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期多项研究表明，ΛCDM模型可能不再是宇宙学的权威标准模型，因为不同宇宙学观测之间出现了日益加剧的矛盾，甚至被部分学者描述为"宇宙学危机"。作者旨在通过放宽ΛCDM模型赖以成立的基本假设（各向同性和哥白尼原理），探索解决这些矛盾的替代路径。

Method: 论文首先系统性综述了多种描述非均匀和各向异性宇宙的模型，包括不同的度规理论（metric theories）、微扰分析（perturbative analyses）、平均化效应（averaging effects）、倾斜场景及宇宙学图景方法（cosmographic approaches）；随后将这些理论框架应用于SNIA数据和本地本动速度场的分析，以约束关键模型参数。

Result: 论文提出了利用现有宇宙学数据来研究非均匀、各向异性及倾斜宇宙学效应的分析方法，并通过应用SNIA数据和本地本动速度场对关键参数进行了约束尝试。

Conclusion: 通过放宽宇宙学原理的假设，可以为解决ΛCDM模型面临的观测矛盾提供替代的理论框架，同时这些框架可以利用现有观测数据在参数空间中进行检验和约束。

Abstract: Recent studies strongly suggest that the $Λ$CDM model may no longer serve as the definitive standard model in cosmology, given the increasing tensions between different cosmological observables. Some researchers have described this situation as a "crisis in cosmology." The $Λ$CDM model relies on two fundamental assumptions: the isotropy of the universe (supported by observational evidence) and the Copernican principle (a philosophical postulate). Together, these assumptions lead to the well-known Cosmological Principle, with the homogeneity of the universe emerging as a direct consequence. In this thesis, I review the cosmological consequences of relaxing some of these assumptions, exploring inhomogeneous and anisotropic universe models, as well as tilted cosmologies, which consider observers in motion relative to the Hubble flow. We examine methods to study these effects using existing cosmological data. First, we explore a variety of models that describe inhomogeneous and anisotropic universes, including different metric theories, perturbative analyses, averaging effects, tilted scenarios, and cosmographic approaches. We then apply this theoretical framework to analyze SNIA data and the local peculiar velocity field, aiming to constrain key parameters.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [Deformation of scalar generalized bi-Hamiltonian structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15538)
*Zhe Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有半单（semisimple）首项的标量广义双哈密顿结构（generalized bi-Hamiltonian structures）的分类问题，并以此为应用，证明了对于任何给定的黏性中心不变量（viscous central invariant），均存在可积黏性守恒律（integrable viscous conservation laws）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义双哈密顿结构是数学物理中研究可积系统的重要框架，其分类问题对理解系统的可积性结构具有重要意义。同时，黏性守恒律与中心不变量之间的关系尚未得到系统刻画，需要建立完整的理论结果。

Method: 本文采用分类方法对具有半单首项的标量广义双哈密顿结构进行系统性归类，并在此基础上构建可积黏性守恒律，将其与给定的黏性中心不变量建立明确联系。

Result: 研究获得了具有半单首项的标量广义双哈密顿结构的分类结果，并证明了存在性结论：对于任意给定的黏性中心不变量，均存在对应的可积黏性守恒律。

Conclusion: 论文建立了广义双哈密顿结构与可积黏性守恒律之间的桥梁，表明黏性中心不变量可以完整刻画相应的可积黏性守恒律的存在性。

Abstract: We study the classification of scalar generalized bi-Hamiltonian structures with semisimple leading terms. As an application, we prove the existence of integrable viscous conservation laws for any given viscous central invariant.

</details>


### [288] [Invariant connections in geometric mechanics: reduction, nonlocality, and curvature effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16561)
*Qiao Huang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了不变联络（invariant connections）在几何力学（geometric mechanics）中的作用，发展了一套在左右不变联络下约化的完整理论，并引入了一种新的依赖于联络的变分原理，由此导出了具有时间非局部性的积分-微分Euler--Poincaré方程，最终在Heisenberg群和旋转群上对理论进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何力学中不变联络在约化过程中的具体作用尚未被系统阐明，理解联络如何影响Euler--Poincaré和Lie--Poisson方程的结构具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 首先建立李群上左、右不变联络下的约化理论；随后引入一种新的依赖于联络的变分原理，使拉格朗日量依赖于沿曲线平行传输回初始点的速度；利用该框架推导出Cartan--Schouten联络下的积分-微分Euler--Poincaré方程，并进一步将其重构为两点边值问题（two-point boundary value problem），设计了两层数值方案。

Result: 证明Euler--Poincaré和Lie--Poisson方程与联络无关；在Cartan--Schouten联络下导出的更新方程呈现两类时间非局域性来源：由平行传输编码的路径依赖项和由曲率积分产生的未来依赖项；该方程在Heisenberg群上因幂零性（nilpotency）而简化，在旋转群上保留完整的积分-微分结构。

Conclusion: 不变联络在几何力学中不仅可作为简化工具，还能通过引入联络依赖的变分原理产生新的、具有非局域结构的动力学方程，为处理完整的联络效应提供了统一的数值与解析框架。

Abstract: We present a systematic study on the role of invariant connections in geometric mechanics. We first develop a comprehensive theory of reduction under left- and right-invariant connections on Lie groups, showing that the Euler--Poincaré and Lie--Poisson equations are independent of the connection. We then introduce a novel connection-dependent variational principle, where the Lagrangian depends on the velocity parallel-transported back to the initial point of the curve. For Cartan--Schouten connections, this leads to an integro-differential Euler--Poincaré equation that exhibits two distinct sources of nonlocality in time: a path-dependent term encoded in the parallel transport and a future-dependent term arising from a curvature integral. We reformulate this equation as a two-point boundary value problem and present a two-level numerical scheme. The general theory is illustrated on the Heisenberg group, where the equations simplify due to nilpotency, and on the rotation group, where the full integro-differential structure is retained.

</details>


### [289] [Atiyah's Minkowski Space Conjecture Fails for Every $n\ge3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16693)
*Ziran Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文否证了Atiyah关于Minkowski时空点构型构造的线性无关性猜想：对任意n≥3，都存在反例；n=3时通过显式构造平面单参数族使系数行列式在特定区间内恰有一个单零点，再利用零平移构造推广至所有n>3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Atiyah提出了一个将n条可许标记世界线构型映射到n个n-1次二元型的构造，并猜想这些二元型总是线性无关的。验证或否证该猜想的正确性是该研究的核心动机。

Method: 对n=3，构造一个显式的平面单参数族，证明其实数系数行列式在指定区间内恰有一个简单零点，此时六个有序天球根互异且系数矩阵秩恰为2；随后利用零平移（null-translation）构造将前三组形式乘以公共因子，将反例推广至所有n>3。

Result: 对每个n≥3均构造出反例；在完全两两不交的类时仿射线构成的类中，线性无关性在n=2时普遍成立，在n≥3时普遍失败；对每个反例，归一化Atiyah–Sutcliffe行列式（normalized Atiyah–Sutcliffe determinant）均有定义且为零。

Conclusion: Atiyah关于二元型线性无关性的猜想对所有n≥3均不成立，其成立范围仅限于n=2的情形。

Abstract: Atiyah's Minkowski-space version of the configuration-of-points construction assigns to an admissible marked configuration of $n$ worldlines a collection of $n$ binary forms of degree $n-1$, whose roots are the ordered retarded celestial directions. He conjectured that these forms are always linearly independent.
  We disprove this conjecture for every $n\ge3$. For $n=3$, an explicit planar one-parameter family yields a real coefficient determinant with exactly one simple zero in a specified interval. At this parameter, all six ordered celestial roots are distinct and the coefficient matrix has rank exactly two. A null-translation construction then multiplies the first three forms by a common factor and produces counterexamples for every $n>3$. Consequently, within the class of complete pairwise disjoint timelike affine lines, universal independence holds at $n=2$ and fails for every $n\ge3$; for each of the counterexamples, the normalized Atiyah--Sutcliffe determinant is defined and vanishes.

</details>


### [290] [Inhomogeneous Einstein metrics on complex projective spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16880)
*Gonzalo Cao-Labora,Alberto Rodríguez-Vázquez*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在复射影空间上首次构造出非齐次爱因斯坦度量（Einstein metric），覆盖复维度3到7的情况，从而肯定地回答了Berger在1965年提出的关于是否存在其他爱因斯坦度量的问题，特别是对复偶维度n=4和n=6给出了肯定答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复射影空间上目前已知的爱因斯坦度量仅有齐次的：Fubini-Study度量（经由Hopf纤维化）以及Ziller度量（在复奇维度上通过四元射影空间上的twistor纤维化的典范变化获得）。1965年Berger证明了Fubini-Study度量是复射影空间上唯一的Kähler-Einstein度量，并提出是否存在其他爱因斯坦度量的开放问题。

Method: 在复维度n为3到7的复射影空间上构造非齐次爱因斯坦度量，这一构造通过不同于已知齐次例子的方式实现。

Result: 成功构造了复维度3≤n≤7上的首个非齐次爱因斯坦度量，并且在复偶维度n=4和n=6的情况下肯定地回答了Berger的问题。

Conclusion: 本文成果表明复射影空间上确实存在非齐次的爱因斯坦度量，推翻了仅依赖两个已知齐次例子的认知，并对Berger的开放问题给出了部分的肯定回答。

Abstract: The only Einstein metrics currently known on complex projective spaces are homogeneous: the Fubini-Study metric, arising via the Hopf fibration; and, in odd complex dimensions, Ziller's metric, obtained as a canonical variation along the twistor fibration over the quaternionic projective space. In 1965, Berger proved that the Fubini-Study metric is the unique Kähler-Einstein metric on the complex projective space, and posed the question of whether other Einstein metrics exist. We construct the first inhomogeneous Einstein metrics on complex projective spaces of complex dimension $n$ for $3\leq n \leq 7$, answering Berger's question affirmatively for the complex even dimensional cases $n=4$ and $n=6$.

</details>

### [291] [Separation of Flexible Enantiomers Using Shear Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14857)
*Minh Nhat Pham,Levi Cherek,J. Daniel Gezelter*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过分子动力学模拟，将分子柔性与分子螺距（molecular pitch）理论框架结合，考察两种药物分子（比卡鲁胺和孟鲁司特钠）在手性剪切流中的对映体分离效果。模拟发现柔性构象变化产生螺距分布，但其均值接近优化气相结构值，且外消旋混合物在10 ns时间尺度上呈现线性分离速率，预测厘米级分离可在数小时内实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对映体的机械分离是对合成方法生产对映纯样品的有吸引力的替代方案。剪切流产生的溶液涡度因手性物体与周围流体间相互作用的张量性质，已被证明可用于微米至纳米尺度的手性物体分离。新发展的分子螺距理论利用阻力张量表征这些相互作用，并能从优化分子几何结构预测剪切诱导的药物类分子分离，但该理论尚未充分考虑分子柔性的影响。

Method: 采用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟方法，将分子灵活性引入分子螺距框架中，研究比卡鲁胺（bicalutamide, Casodex）和孟鲁司特钠（montelukast sodium, Singulair）两种药物分子在真实溶剂环境中的对映体分离潜力，并对不同溶剂环境和剪切过程进行系统评估。

Result: 模拟揭示出柔性诱导的螺距分布，源于真实溶剂环境中发生的构象变化；然而这些分布受溶剂身份和剪切过程的影响较弱，平均标量螺距值接近优化气相结构的结果。尽管分子尺度的平移扩散产生相反效应，柔性对映体的外消旋混合物在10 ns时间尺度上仍呈现线性分离速率，预测厘米级分离可在数小时内实现。此外还提供了泰勒-库艾特装置（Taylor-Couette device）生成层流剪切流的参数估计及未来实验考量。

Conclusion: 分子柔性引入的构象变化会生成螺距分布，但不显著偏离刚性优化结构的预测值，表明分子螺距框架对柔性分子的适用性依然成立。剪切诱导的对映体分离在药物分子尺度上具有实际可行性，厘米级分离可在合理时间窗口内达成，相关实验结果对Taylor-Couette装置参数的估算提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Mechanical separation of enantiomers is an attractive alternative to synthetic methods for producing enantiopure samples. Shear flow that produces solution vorticity has been shown to be a viable means for separating chiral objects on the micro- to nano-scale due to the tensorial nature of the interactions between chiral objects and the surrounding fluid. A recently-developed theory of molecular pitch characterizes these interactions using the resistance tensor and predicts the shear-induced separation of drug-like molecules from their optimized molecular geometries. We present a molecular dynamics study on the effects of incorporating molecular flexibility into the molecular pitch framework. We also evaluate the potential for enantiomeric separation of two drug molecules: bicalutamide (Casodex) and montelukast sodium (Singulair). Simulations reveal the emergence of flexibility-induced pitch distributions that result from conformational changes occurring in a realistic solvent environment. However, these distributions are weakly influenced by the solvent identity and the shearing process, producing mean scalar pitch values that are close to those from optimized gas phase structures. Despite the opposing effect of translational diffusion at the molecular scale, racemic mixtures of flexible enantiomers show linear rates of separation at the 10 ns timescale, and we predict that cm-scale separation can be achieved within hours. Additionally, we provide estimates for parameters of a Taylor-Couette device for generating laminar shear flow, as well as considerations for future experiments.

</details>


### [292] [Gas-generating reactive flows in bicontinuous catalyst support structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15781)
*J. M. P. Beunen,J. Harting*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过带有新中心矩碰撞算子的颜色梯度格子玻尔兹曼（lattice Boltzmann）模拟，首次对双连续界面堵塞乳液凝胶（bijels）这一旋节线衍生架构的反应流进行数值优化研究，并开发了基于模拟数据的解析模型来预测催化剂性能。结果表明该形态可实现极高的转化效率，且可通过超亲水表面涂层进一步优化催化剂性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相催化领域面临催化剂载体结构选择的关键挑战：商用结构易于规模化制造，但其随机性导致化学和输运性质欠佳，尤其在产气反应中多孔结构的不均匀性会造成气泡捕获，阻碍反应物到达催化位点，导致转化效率低下。此前实验表明旋节线衍生架构（spinodally-derived architectures）中的bijels可缓解这些问题，但尚无数值研究来优化此类形态的操作条件。

Method: 使用带有新型中心矩（central moments）碰撞算子的颜色梯度格子玻尔兹曼（color-gradient lattice Boltzmann）方法对反应流进行数值模拟；基于模拟数据开发了用于预测催化剂性能的解析模型。

Result: 该形态可实现非常高的转化效率；催化剂性能可通过超亲水（superhydrophilic）表面涂层进行优化。

Conclusion: 数值模拟验证了bijels形态在异相催化中的优异性能，填补了该领域缺乏数值优化研究的空白，并提出超亲水表面涂层作为一种有效的性能优化途径。

Abstract: A major challenge in the field of heterogeneous catalysis is selecting an optimal catalyst support structure. Commercially available structures can be easily manufactured at scale, but their stochastic nature makes their chemical and transport properties suboptimal. This is particularly relevant for gas-generation reactions, where non-uniformity of a porous structure leads to bubble trapping. Such trapping impedes the flow of reactants to catalyst sites, leading to conversion inefficiencies. Previous experimental work demonstrated that spinodally-derived architectures, in particular bicontinuous interfacially jammed emulsion gels (bijels), can alleviate these issues and deliver superior performance. However, to the best of our knowledge, numerical studies to optimize the operating conditions for such a morphology have not been performed yet. In this work, we aim to close this gap using color-gradient lattice Boltzmann simulations of reactive flows with a novel central moments collision operator. We develop an analytical model to predict catalyst performance based on our simulation data. Our findings show that this type of morphology can achieve very high conversion efficiencies. Moreover, we demonstrate that its catalyst performance can be optimized using superhydrophilic surface coatings.

</details>


### [293] [Extracting a nitrile-centered, ether-assisted motif hierarchy for lithium-battery electrolyte design from billion-scale molecular space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16364)
*Yifeng Xia,Guanghui Wang,Sining Wang,Wenting Chen,Zheng Cheng,Jinzhe Zeng,Qiangqiang Gu*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 作者通过对近十亿个GDB13分子结构的电子-溶剂化（electronic-solvation）描述符进行高通量筛选，在不依赖特定官能团偏好或骨架约束的条件下，揭示了锂离子电池电解液分子设计中以腈基（nitrile）为核心、醚基（ether）为辅助的基序层级（motif hierarchy），并利用生成模型扩展候选空间获得高评分氟化结构，最终通过分子动力学模拟验证了代表性候选分子的弱配位行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂离子电池电解质分子的设计需要在电子稳定性与合适的Li+溶剂化能力之间取得平衡，但跨多种化学结构分子的构效基础尚不明确。现有高通量筛选虽然扩大了搜索空间，但仅排序候选物无法揭示重复出现的结构基序（motif）及其适用边界。

Method: 作者使用电子-溶剂化描述符，对GDB13数据库中的近十亿个结构进行高通量筛选，不采用显式的官能团偏好或骨架约束。通过改变描述符权重分析高排名群体的结构分布，将所得基序层级编码进生成模型以扩展候选空间，并利用显式溶剂分子动力学（explicit-solvent molecular dynamics）模拟验证代表性候选分子的配位行为。

Result: 在覆盖不同描述符权重的条件下，高排名分子群体分离为两个区域：腈基主导区（nitrile-dominant regime）及包含腈基和醚基分子的共存区（coexistence regime），共同定义了以腈基为核心、醚基辅助的基序层级。生成模型产出了高评分的氟化（fluorinated）结构，即使未显式给予氟化奖励。分子动力学模拟显示代表性候选物与Li+形成弱的可交换配位，且不取代碳酸乙烯酯（ethylene carbonate）在Li+第一溶剂化壳层中的主导地位，对离子缔合和传输的影响取决于分子结构和浓度。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种定量、可解释且受物理约束的基序层级框架，将锂离子电池电解液设计中已知的腈基与醚基化学系统化，为该领域的分子设计提供了结构层面的指导原则。

Abstract: Designing electrolyte molecules for lithium batteries requires balancing electronic stability with appropriate Li+ solvation, yet the structural basis remains unclear across chemically diverse molecules. High-throughput screening expands the searchable space, but ranked candidates alone do not reveal recurring motifs or their applicability limits. We searched nearly one billion GDB13 structures using electronic--solvation descriptors without explicit functional-group preferences or scaffold constraints. Across descriptor weights, high-ranking populations separated into a nitrile-dominant regime and a coexistence regime containing substantial fractions of both nitrile- and ether-containing molecules. These regimes together define a nitrile-centered, ether-assisted motif hierarchy: nitrile remains favored across broad weight ranges, whereas ether becomes prominent under stronger electrostatic and polarity constraints. Encoding this hierarchy in a generative model expands the candidate space beyond GDB13 and yields high-scoring fluorinated structures without an explicit fluorination reward. Explicit-solvent molecular dynamics simulations show weak, exchangeable coordination of representative candidates without displacing ethylene carbonate from the dominant first solvation shell around Li+; effects on ion association and transport depend on molecular structure and concentration. These results establish a quantitative, interpretable and physically bounded motif hierarchy that systematizes established nitrile and ether chemistry for lithium-battery electrolyte design.

</details>


### [294] [Complete O(3) Interactions from Wigner-6j Recoupling to Local O(2) Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16592)
*Zemin Xu,Peijun Hu,Wenbo Xie*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部O(2)框架的完整O(3)等变卷积框架，通过广义Wigner-6j卷积支持非共线磁学、电场和磁场等涉及极张量与伪张量同时出现的场景，并将CGTP的计算复杂度从O(L^6)降低到O(L^3)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等变原子模型要么使用Clebsch-Gordan张量积（CGTP），支持一般O(3)表示但高角动量下计算昂贵；要么使用边缘对齐的局部SO(2)运算，但仅设计用于SO(3)或O(3)的自然宇称子集，无法在极张量和伪张量同时出现时提供完整的局部表示。因此需要一种同时兼顾完整性和高效性的O(3)等变卷积方案。

Method: 方法包含三个核心部分：(1) 引入广义Wigner-6j卷积，提供O(3)下的一般卷积方案，可纳入任意节点级等变特征；(2) 基于局部O(2)框架构建完整的O(3)卷积框架，建立全局O(3)不可约表示（irreps）与局部O(2)不可约表示之间的对应关系；(3) 利用该对应关系构造一个封闭算子系统，包括O2Linear、O2TensorProduct和O2Gate三个算子。

Result: 所提出的框架将CGTP的计算复杂度从O(L^6)显著降低到O(L^3)，同时能够完整处理极张量（如电场）与伪张量（如磁场）同时出现的场景，适用于非共线磁性等物理体系。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种更高效且更完整的O(3)等变卷积方案，填补了现有方法在混合宇称特征表示上的空白，为等变原子模型在更广泛物理场景中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Equivariant atomistic models commonly use either Clebsch Gordan tensor product(CGTP) or edge aligned SO(2) operationsCGTP support general O(3) representations but become expensive at high angular degree, while existing local SO(2) frame methods are designed for SO(3) or the natural-parity subset of O(3). They therefore do not provide a complete local representation when polar tensors and pseudotensors occur at the same time. In this work, we introduce a generalized Wigner-6j convolution for non-collinear magnetism, electric fields, and magnetic fields, providing a general convolution scheme under O(3) that can incorporate arbitrary node-wise equivariant features. We further develop a complete O(3) convolution framework based on local O(2) frames, reducing the computational complexity of CGTPs from O(L6) to O(L3). We establish the correspondence between global O(3) irreducible representations (irreps) and local O(2) irreps and use this correspondence to construct a closed operator system comprising O2Linear, O2TensorProduct, and O2Gate.

</details>

### [295] [Statistical validation of calorimeter inpainting with generative diffusion priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14801)
*Himanshu Raj,Roli Esha*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 本文针对相对论重离子碰撞中量能计（calorimeter）探测器局部失效导致数据不完整的问题，提出利用预训练量能计扩散模型（diffusion model）作为先验（prior），以贝叶斯视角在周边测量条件约束下重建缺失信号，并系统比较多种基于扩散的图像修复（inpainting）算法，建立了概率性重建的通用验证策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部探测器无效导致量能计数据不完整，严重限制了高能物理实验中进行精密测量的能力，需要有效的方法重建缺失信号。

Method: 从贝叶斯（Bayesian）视角出发，将预训练量能计扩散模型用作先验，并以周围测量数据为条件重建缺失信号；对多种基于扩散的修复算法进行系统比较，并利用贝叶斯后验诊断（posterior diagnostics）评估能量响应、空间偏差和不确定性校准表现，同时分析重建保真度随碰撞中心度（centrality）和掩蔽区域大小的变化。

Result: 研究系统评估了不同扩散修复算法的重建性能，包括能量响应、空间偏差和不确定性校准三个维度的贝叶斯后验诊断结果，并揭示了碰撞中心度和掩蔽区域大小对重建保真度的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为探测器中缺失信息的概率性重建建立了通用验证策略，有助于提升量能计数据利用率和精密测量的能力。

Abstract: Localized detector inefficiencies produce incomplete calorimeter data that limit the ability to perform precision measurements. We address this problem in relativistic heavy-ion collisions from a Bayesian perspective using pretrained calorimeter diffusion models as priors to reconstruct the missing signal conditioned on surrounding measurements. In this work, we conduct a systematic comparison of several diffusion-based inpainting algorithms, whose performance is evaluated using Bayesian posterior diagnostics of energy response, spatial bias, and uncertainty calibration. The reconstruction fidelity is also analyzed across collision centralities and masked region sizes. This study establishes a general validation strategy for probabilistic reconstruction of missing detector information.

</details>

### [296] [Complex Hadamard Matrices - Quantum Symmetries, Equivalence and Non-Local Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14858)
*Michael Brannan,Daniel Gromada,Roberto Hernández Palomares,Nicholas Priebe*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究复Hadamard矩阵上单项矩阵作用的量子群（quantum group）推广，引入了量子对称性和量子等价概念，证明了最大单项量子群作用下同尺寸Hadamard矩阵均量子等价，并引入s-量子等价概念，通过图形演算方法结合互补蜘蛛（complementary spiders）所生成范畴，证明了所有同尺寸有限量子群量子仿射等价，并提供Kasprzak–Sołtan–Woronowicz定理的简短图形证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者致力于将复Hadamard矩阵的经典对称性与等价性概念推广到量子群框架下，探索量子对称性如何改变Hadamard矩阵的分类结构。

Method: 本文方法主要基于图形演算（graphical calculus），使用由互补蜘蛛生成的范畴作为核心工具，并借助同步非局域博弈（synchronous non-local game）对s-量子等价进行操作化建模。

Result: 证明了在最大单项量子群作用下，给定尺寸的所有Hadamard矩阵均为量子等价；构造了同尺寸同阶Butson矩阵在任意s下均非s-量子等价的例子；证明了所有同尺寸有限量子群均量子仿射等价。

Conclusion: 作者通过图形推理获得了量子群框架下Hadamard矩阵等价性的新分类结果，并证明了有限量子群的量子对称性必定是经典的，为Kasprzak–Sołtan–Woronowicz定理提供了简洁的图形证明。

Abstract: We consider quantum group generalizations of the action of monomial matrices on complex Hadamard matrices. This gives rise to various notions of quantum symmetries and quantum equivalences of Hadamard matrices. We show that if one acts by a certain largest monomial quantum group, then all Hadamard matrices of a given size become quantum equivalent. Taking a more restrictive quantization leads to a notion of $s$-quantum equivalence. We exhibit examples of Butson matrices of the same size and order that are not $s$-quantum equivalent for any choice of $s$. We also show that $s$-quantum equivalence is operationally modeled by a synchronous non-local ``Hadamard equivalence'' game. Our methods are largely graphical calculus based, using categories generated by complementary spiders. We use these same tools to study quantum affine equivalence of quantum groups, and prove that all finite quantum groups of the same size are quantum affinely equivalent. We also provide a short graphical proof of a result of Kasprzak--Sołtan--Woronowicz asserting that quantum symmetries of finite quantum groups must be classical.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [297] [Three-body contact for fermions. II. Non-degenerate limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15324)
*Xavier Leyronas,Félix Werner*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在非简并极限下计算了具有零程相互作用的均匀费米气体的三体接触（three-body contact）$C_3$。在散射长度$a=\infty$时，通过波函数方法得到$C_3$的解析表达式，发现其对温度$T$具有极其缓慢的$1/T^{0.22728}$依赖；对$a<0$的情况，利用费曼图（Feynman diagram）技术，发现正确的三体短距离关联需要二体和三体关联的首阶非平凡消去才能得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三体接触$C_3$是刻画具有零程相互作用的费米气体（Fermi gas）的基本物理量，它决定多个可观测量的行为，包括费米子近邻三重态数量和冷原子实验中的三体损耗率。为此，需要系统计算该量在非简并极限下的数值及其温度依赖规律。

Method: 在非简并极限下，对负散射长度有限值$a<0$和无限散射长度$a=\infty$两种情况分别计算$C_3$。在$a=\infty$时采用波函数方法（wavefunction approach）获得解析表达式；在$a<0$时采用费曼图技术，其中正确的三体短距离关联来自二体和三体关联首阶项的消去，所得到的幂律标度由三体T矩阵（T-matrix）的大波矢尾部贡献。

Result: 在$a=\infty$时，$C_3$的解析表达式显示出对温度$T$异常缓慢的幂律依赖，指数为$1/T^{0.22728}$。对$a<0$的情况，所得幂律标度来源于三体T矩阵的大波矢尾部，其形式受单位极限（unitary limit）下三体问题解析解启发。

Conclusion: 该结果为均匀费米气体三体接触在非简并极限下提供了首阶计算，揭示了$C_3$的温和温度依赖性以及二体与三体关联之间非平凡的消去机制。

Abstract: A fundamental quantity characterizing Fermi gases with zero-range interactions is the three-body contact $C_3$, which determines several observables including the number of nearby triplets of fermions and the three-body loss rate in cold atom experiments, as shown in a companion article. Here, we compute $C_3$ to leading order in the non-degenerate limit for the homogeneous gas with negative or infinite scattering length $a$. At $a=\infty$, using a wavefunction approach, we obtain the analytical expression of $C_3$, which has a remarkably slow $1/T^{0.22728}$ dependence on the temperature $T$. In the Feynman diagram technique, which we use for $a<0$, the correct three-body short-distance correlations emerge only after a non-trivial cancellation between leading order two-body and three-body correlations, and the resulting power-law scaling comes from the large-wavevector tail of the 3-body T-matrix, which we derive inspired by the analytical solution of the three-body problem at the unitary limit.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [298] [A formal framework for higher-order spin models via hypergraphs, polymatroids, and the Tutte polynomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14628)
*Khallil Berrekkal,Joanna A. Ellis-Monaghan,Merijn Moody,Clélia de Mulatier*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文为超图（hypergraph）上的统计力学模型建立了严格的数学框架，将经典的Potts模型配分函数与Tutte多项式之间的对应关系从图推广到超图，并通过多个示例展示了该框架的广泛应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计力学中，图上的Potts模型配分函数与Tutte多项式之间存在深刻联系，但这一联系尚未被系统推广到超图结构。作者旨在建立统一的理论框架，涵盖更广泛的超图模型类别。

Method: 作者定义了超图模型及其配分函数，并扩展到由相互作用函数族诱导的特殊模型类。对布尔型（boolean）相互作用族，证明了配分函数由组合秩函数（combinatorial rank function）决定，并建立了超图删除-收缩递推（deletion-contraction recurrence）成立的充分条件及秩函数定义多拟阵（polymatroid）的充要条件。

Result: 文中分析了Parity Ising、Delta Potts和And Ising三类超图相互作用族。对图的情形，前两类回归经典图形Ising模型，其配分函数恢复多元Tutte多项式。Tutte多项式因此获得至少两种不同的超图推广：超图关联矩阵二元拟阵的Tutte多项式，以及超图多拟阵Poincaré多项式的多元版本；And Ising模型配分函数则是布尔多拟阵Poincaré多项式的多元版本。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了超图模型远超图模型的广阔范围，强调了建立统一理论的必要性，并为超图统计力学与拟阵理论之间建立了系统的桥梁。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous mathematical framework for statistical mechanics models on hypergraphs, and give conditions for lifting the classical connection between Potts model partition functions and the Tutte polynomial from graphs to hypergraphs. We define hypergraphical models and their associated partition functions, and extend these to special classes of models induced by families of interaction functions. For boolean interaction families, whose interaction functions map to $\{0,1\}$, we show that the partition function is determined by a combinatorial rank function, and we establish sufficient conditions for a hypergraph deletion-contraction recurrence and for when the rank function defines a polymatroid. We illustrate the theory by applying it to three hypergraph interaction families: Parity Ising, Delta Potts, and And Ising. The induced hypergraphical models are not isomorphic to each other, but the first two reduce to the same graphical Ising models. We identify three polymatroids naturally associated with hypergraphs for these models: respectively, the binary matroid of the incidence matrix over $\mathbb{F}_2$, the hypergraphical polymatroid, and the boolean polymatroid. For graphs, the first two reduce to the classical graphical matroid, and their partition functions recover the multivariate Tutte polynomial. The Tutte polynomial thus admits at least two distinct generalizations for hypergraphs, both satisfying a deletion-contraction recurrence: the Tutte polynomial of the binary matroid of the hypergraph incidence matrix, and a multivariate version of the Poincaré polynomial of the hypergraphical polymatroid. The partition functions of And Ising models are likewise multivariate versions of the Poincaré polynomial, here of the boolean polymatroid. These examples illustrate the much greater range of hypergraphical models and underscore the need for the unifying theory.

</details>
